单元 1 · 数据表示Unit 1 · Data representationPAPER 1

文字、图片、声音、数字，进到计算机里全都变成 0 和 1 · 这一单元是 Paper 1 的送分区，转换题练熟就是白拿分Text, images, sound and numbers all become 0s and 1s inside a computer · conversion questions here are free marks once drilled

本单元包括什么What this unit covers 9 站 · 约 60 分钟9 stops · about 60 min

  1. 为什么只有 0 和 1 · bit 与 byteWhy only 0 and 1 · bits and bytes
  2. 二进制 ↔ 十进制（含位权表）Binary ↔ denary
  3. 十六进制：给人看的二进制Hexadecimal
  4. 十六进制 ↔ 二进制 ↔ 十进制Converting between all three
  5. 二进制加法与溢出Binary addition and overflow
  6. 逻辑移位：左移乘 2、右移除 2Logical shifts
  7. 补码：负数怎么存Two's complement
  8. 文字、图片、声音怎么存（含文件大小计算）Text, images and sound
  9. 压缩：把重复的省掉Compression

建议顺序：先站 2、4 把转换练到不假思索，再回头看站 6、7 这两个 2026 新版新加的内容。Suggested order: drill stops 2 and 4 until conversion is automatic, then come back to stops 6 and 7 (new in the 2026 syllabus).

1 为什么计算机只认 0 和 1Why computers only use 0 and 1 Bit · Byte位 · 字节
这一站要会的：说出 bit、nibble、byte 分别是什么；记住存储单位的换算；知道为什么计算机用二进制。 Goal: state what a bit, a nibble and a byte are; recall the storage units; explain why computers use binary.
说人话：计算机里面没有「5」这个字，也没有「A」这个字母，只有成千上万个小开关。开关只有两种状态——通电（1）和断电（0）。你写的每一句话、拍的每一张照片，最后都被翻译成一串开关的状态存起来。为什么非要这么麻烦？因为「有电/没电」这两种状态最容易用电路区分，抗干扰、不容易传错；要是做成十种电压来表示 0–9，稍微有点干扰就分不清了。 In plain words: inside a computer there is no symbol "5" and no letter "A" — only millions of tiny switches, each either on (1) or off (0). Everything you type or photograph ends up as a long list of switch states. Why bother? Two states are the easiest thing for a circuit to tell apart reliably: "voltage present" or "voltage absent". Ten different voltage levels for 0–9 would be hopelessly easy to confuse.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • bit（位）：1 个 0 或 1，最小的单位。A bit is a single 0 or 1 — the smallest unit.
  • nibble：4 位；byte（字节）：8 位。A nibble is 4 bits; a byte is 8 bits.
  • 单位换算（都是 1024 进一位）：1 KiB = 1024 B，1 MiB = 1024 KiB，1 GiB = 1024 MiB，1 TiB = 1024 GiB。Units (all ×1024): 1 KiB = 1024 B, 1 MiB = 1024 KiB, 1 GiB = 1024 MiB, 1 TiB = 1024 GiB.
  • MSB 最高位（最左边，身价最大），LSB 最低位（最右边）。MSB = most significant bit (leftmost, worth the most); LSB = least significant bit.
最容易丢分的地方：题目问 KiB/MiB 就必须用 1024；只有明确写 kB/MB（十进制，比如硬盘厂家标称容量）才用 1000。考试不写清楚单位也扣分。 Classic trap: if the question says KiB/MiB you must use 1024. Only kB/MB (decimal, e.g. a hard disk label) uses 1000. Missing units also loses marks.

例题 · 单位换算Example · units

一张照片是 3.5 MiB。它有多少字节？多少位？ A photo is 3.5 MiB. How many bytes? How many bits?
① 先把 MiB 换成 KiB：3.5 × 1024 = 3584 KiB1. MiB → KiB: 3.5 × 1024 = 3584 KiB ② 再把 KiB 换成字节：3584 × 1024 = 3 670 016 字节2. KiB → bytes: 3584 × 1024 = 3 670 016 bytes ③ 1 字节 = 8 位：3 670 016 × 8 = 29 360 128 位3. 1 byte = 8 bits: 3 670 016 × 8 = 29 360 128 bits

练习Practice

先自己算，再点开对答案。Try it yourself first, then open the answer.

① 一台电脑有 8 GiB 内存，等于多少 MiB？多少 KiB？1. A computer has 8 GiB of RAM. How many MiB? How many KiB?

② 一个字节能表示多少个不同的值？两个字节呢？（提示：每一位有两种可能）2. How many different values can one byte represent? Two bytes? (Hint: each bit has two possibilities.)

点开看答案Show answer

① 8 GiB = 8 × 1024 = 8192 MiB；8192 × 1024 = 8 388 608 KiB。1. 8 GiB = 8 × 1024 = 8192 MiB; 8192 × 1024 = 8 388 608 KiB.

② 一个字节 8 位，每位 2 种可能，所以是 2⁸ = 256 个值（0 到 255）。两个字节 16 位，2¹⁶ = 65 536 个值。2. One byte = 8 bits, each with 2 states, so 2⁸ = 256 values (0 to 255). Two bytes = 16 bits, so 2¹⁶ = 65 536.

— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 二进制 ↔ 十进制Binary ↔ denary Conversion进制转换
这一站要会的：8 位二进制 ↔ 十进制，两个方向都要会，不用计算器（考试不让带）。 Goal: convert in both directions between 8-bit binary and denary, without a calculator.
说人话：二进制的每一位都有一个「身价」，从右往左依次是 1、2、4、8、16、32、64、128——每次翻一倍。想知道这串 0/1 是几，就把写着 1 的那些位的身价加起来。反过来，给你一个十进制数，就从最大的身价开始减：128 减得掉就写 1，减不掉就写 0，一直减到 1。 In plain words: each bit has a place value, doubling from right to left: 1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128. To read a binary number, add up the place values of the bits that are 1. To go the other way, subtract place values from largest to smallest: write 1 when the value fits, 0 when it does not.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 8 位位权：128 64 32 16 8 4 2 1（背下来，考试时先在草稿纸上写一行）8-bit place values: 128 64 32 16 8 4 2 1 — learn them and write them on scrap paper first.
  • 二进制 → 十进制：加法（把 1 的位权加起来）Binary → denary: add the place values of the 1s.
  • 十进制 → 二进制：减法（从大到小减位权）或反复除以 2 取余数Denary → binary: subtract place values, or divide by 2 repeatedly and read the remainders.
  • 题目说「8 位」就要写满 8 位，前面的 0 不能省：45 = 0010 1101If the question says 8 bits, write all 8 — keep the leading zeros: 45 = 0010 1101.

例题 · 两个方向Example · both directions

(a) 把 1010 1101 转成十进制。　(b) 把 173 转成 8 位二进制。　(c) 把 45 转成 8 位二进制。 (a) Convert 1010 1101 to denary. (b) Convert 173 to 8-bit binary. (c) Convert 45 to 8-bit binary.
(a) 位权：128(1) 64(0) 32(1) 16(0) 8(1) 4(1) 2(0) 1(1) → 128+32+8+4+1 = 173(a) 128(1)+64(0)+32(1)+16(0)+8(1)+4(1)+2(0)+1(1) = 128+32+8+4+1 = 173 (b) 173 − 128 = 45（写 1）；64 减不掉（写 0）；45 − 32 = 13（写 1）；16 减不掉（写 0）；13 − 8 = 5（写 1）；5 − 4 = 1（写 1）；2 减不掉（写 0）；1 − 1 = 0（写 1）→ 1010 1101(b) 173−128=45 (1); 64 does not fit (0); 45−32=13 (1); 16 no (0); 13−8=5 (1); 5−4=1 (1); 2 no (0); 1−1=0 (1) → 1010 1101 (c) 45：128、64 都不行 → 0 0；45−32 = 13 → 1；16 不行 → 0；13−8 = 5 → 1；5−4 = 1 → 1；2 不行 → 0；1−1 = 0 → 1；结果 0010 1101(c) 45: 128 no (0), 64 no (0), 45−32=13 (1), 16 no (0), 13−8=5 (1), 5−4=1 (1), 2 no (0), 1−1=0 (1) → 0010 1101

练习Practice

草稿纸先写一行位权：128 64 32 16 8 4 2 1Write the place values on scrap paper first: 128 64 32 16 8 4 2 1

① 1001 0110 → 十进制是多少？1. What is 1001 0110 in denary?

② 把 200 写成 8 位二进制。2. Write 200 as 8-bit binary.

点开看答案Show answer

① 128 + 16 + 4 + 2 = 1501. 128 + 16 + 4 + 2 = 150

② 200−128 = 72（1）；72−64 = 8（1）；32、16 都不行（0 0）；8−8 = 0（1）；4、2、1 都是 0 → 1100 10002. 200−128=72 (1); 72−64=8 (1); 32 no (0), 16 no (0); 8−8=0 (1); 4, 2, 1 all 0 → 1100 1000

— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 十六进制：给人看的二进制Hexadecimal: binary for humans Hex十六进制
这一站要会的：认全 0–F 对应的十进制值；说出用十六进制的三个理由；举出两个实际用途。 Goal: know 0–F in denary; give reasons for using hexadecimal; name two real uses.
说人话：二进制写给机器看很完美，写给人看很痛苦：1010110011110011 这么长一串，抄错一位谁也看不出来。于是人们把二进制每 4 位打包成一个符号，4 位能表示 0–15 共 16 种情况，正好用 0–9 加 A–F 这 16 个符号表示——这就是十六进制。它不是新的数，只是二进制的简写。 In plain words: binary is perfect for machines and painful for humans — 1010110011110011 is easy to mistype and impossible to check. So we bundle binary into groups of four bits; four bits give 16 possible values, which map neatly onto the 16 symbols 0–9 and A–F. Hex is not a new kind of number — it is shorthand for binary.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • A=10、B=11、C=12、D=13、E=14、F=15。A=10, B=11, C=12, D=13, E=14, F=15.
  • 为什么用它：① 比二进制短得多，好读好抄；② 与二进制转换极快（4 位一组）；③ 能直接表示内存地址、机器码这类「本来就是 2 的幂」的东西。Why use it: 1. far shorter than binary, easier to read and copy; 2. converts to binary instantly (4 bits per digit); 3. it suits values that are naturally powers of two, like addresses and machine code.
  • 实际用途：HTML 颜色（#FF8800）、MAC 地址、内存地址、机器码与汇编、调试时看内存内容。Real uses: HTML colours (#FF8800), MAC addresses, memory addresses, machine code and assembly, debugging memory dumps.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：题目常问「为什么用十六进制而不是二进制？」标准答法：更短、更不容易抄错、与二进制转换方便。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: for "why use hexadecimal rather than binary?" the expected answers are: it is shorter, easier to read/less prone to copying errors, and quick to convert to binary. Hexadecimal is easier for humans to read and remember than binary, it is much shorter, and each hex digit maps directly onto four bits so conversion is quick.

例题 · 十六进制转十进制Example · hex to denary

把 3C 转成十进制；把 2F 转成十进制。 Convert 3C and 2F to denary.
3C：左边一位是「十六位」，右边是「个位」→ 3 × 16 + 12 = 48 + 12 = 603C: 3 × 16 + 12 = 48 + 12 = 60 2F：2 × 16 + 15 = 32 + 15 = 472F: 2 × 16 + 15 = 32 + 15 = 47 （跟十进制一个道理：十位 ×10 + 个位，十六进制是 ×16）(Exactly like denary, where the second column is ×10 — in hex it is ×16.)

练习Practice

记住 A=10 起头那一串。Remember the run starting at A=10.

① 把 B2 转成十进制。1. Convert B2 to denary.

② 十六进制 F 等于十进制几？1F 呢？2. What is hex F in denary? And hex 1F?

点开看答案Show answer

① B = 11，所以 11 × 16 + 2 = 176 + 2 = 1781. B = 11, so 11 × 16 + 2 = 176 + 2 = 178

② F = 15；1F = 1 × 16 + 15 = 312. F = 15; 1F = 1 × 16 + 15 = 31

— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 十六进制 ↔ 二进制 ↔ 十进制Converting between all three 4-bit groups四位一组
这一站要会的：二进制 ↔ 十六进制（4 位一组）；十六进制 ↔ 十进制（×16）；三个进制之间随便转。 Goal: binary ↔ hex (4-bit groups); hex ↔ denary (×16); move freely between all three.
说人话：二进制转十六进制不用先转成十进制——把 8 位二进制从中间劈成两组，每组 4 位各自翻译成一个符号就行了。左边 0111 = 7，右边 1101 = 13 = D，拼起来就是 7D。反过来十六进制转二进制也一样：一个符号摊成 4 位。「先转十进制再转十六进制」是绕远路，容易算错。 In plain words: you do not need to go via denary. Split the 8 bits into two groups of four and translate each group into one symbol: 0111 = 7, 1101 = 13 = D, giving 7D. Hex to binary is the same in reverse: expand each symbol into four bits. Going via denary is the long way round and an easy place to slip up.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 「从右往左」每 4 位一组，最左边不足 4 位就在前面补 0。Group from the right; pad the leftmost group with zeros if it is short.
  • 十六进制 → 十进制：左位 ×16 + 右位（三位就是 ×256 + ×16 + ×1）。Hex → denary: left digit ×16 + right digit (for three digits: ×256 + ×16 + ×1).
  • 十进制 → 十六进制：先转二进制最快，再 4 位一组切开。Denary → hex: convert to binary first, then split into fours — quickest route.

例题 · 三进制互转Example · all three ways

(a) 0111 1101 转成十六进制和十进制。　(b) 把十进制 200 转成十六进制。 (a) Convert 0111 1101 to hex and to denary. (b) Convert denary 200 to hex.
(a) 分组：0111 = 7，1101 = 13 = D → 7D；十进制 = 7 × 16 + 13 = 112 + 13 = 125（验算：64+32+16+8+4+1 = 125 ✓）(a) Groups: 0111 = 7, 1101 = 13 = D → 7D; denary = 7 × 16 + 13 = 125 (check: 64+32+16+8+4+1 = 125 ✓) (b) 200 → 二进制 1100 1000（上站算过）→ 分组 1100 = 12 = C，1000 = 8 → C8；验算 12 × 16 + 8 = 192 + 8 = 200 ✓(b) 200 → binary 1100 1000 → groups 1100 = C, 1000 = 8 → C8; check 12 × 16 + 8 = 200 ✓

练习Practice

先劈成 4 位一组，别先转十进制。Split into fours first — do not go via denary.

① 1010 0011 转成十六进制。1. Convert 1010 0011 to hex.

② 十六进制 4F 转成二进制，再转成十进制。2. Convert hex 4F to binary, then to denary.

点开看答案Show answer

① 1010 = 10 = A，0011 = 3 → A3（十进制 163）1. 1010 = A, 0011 = 3 → A3 (denary 163)

② 4 = 0100，F = 15 = 1111 → 0100 1111；十进制 = 4 × 16 + 15 = 792. 4 = 0100, F = 1111 → 0100 1111; denary = 4 × 16 + 15 = 79

— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 二进制加法与溢出Binary addition and overflow Overflow溢出
这一站要会的：做两个 8 位二进制数的加法；说出什么是溢出、为什么会发生。 Goal: add two 8-bit binary numbers; state what overflow is and why it happens.
说人话：二进制加法和十进制一样是「满位就进位」，只不过十进制满 10 进 1，二进制满 2 就进 1。所以 1+1 在二进制里不是 2（没有 2 这个符号），而是「本位写 0，往左进 1」= 10。溢出就像往一个只能装 8 格的盒子里塞 9 个东西：8 位最多表示 255，如果结果需要第 9 位，那一位存不下就丢了，算出来的数变得离谱。 In plain words: binary addition carries just like denary, except denary carries at 10 and binary carries at 2. So 1+1 is not "2" (there is no symbol 2) — you write 0 and carry 1, giving 10. Overflow is like pushing nine items into an eight-slot box: eight bits hold at most 255; if the answer needs a ninth bit, there is nowhere to put it, so it is lost and the result goes wrong.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 四条规则：0+0=0；0+1=1；1+1=0 进 1；1+1+1=1 进 1。Four rules: 0+0=0; 0+1=1; 1+1=0 carry 1; 1+1+1=1 carry 1.
  • 8 位能表示 0–255；溢出 = 结果超出寄存器能容纳的位数，最高位被丢掉。Eight bits hold 0–255; overflow means the result needs more bits than the register has, so the extra bit is lost.
  • 判断溢出：两个大数相加，结果看起来变小了（甚至变成 0），就是溢出了。Spotting overflow: adding two large numbers gives an answer that is smaller than expected — even zero.

例题 · 加法与溢出判断Example · addition and overflow

(a) 0101 1010 + 0011 0111　(b) 1100 0000 + 0100 0000（会不会溢出？） (a) 0101 1010 + 0011 0111　(b) 1100 0000 + 0100 0000 — does it overflow?
(a) 逐位相加得 1001 0001；验算：90 + 55 = 145，而 128+16+1 = 145 ✓ 没溢出(a) 1001 0001; check: 90 + 55 = 145 and 128+16+1 = 145 ✓ no overflow (b) 192 + 64 = 256，而 8 位最大 255 → 需要第 9 位 → 溢出。寄存器里留下的是 0000 0000（256 的二进制 1 0000 0000 中，最左边那个 1 丢了）(b) 192 + 64 = 256 but 8 bits stop at 255 → a ninth bit is needed → overflow. The register holds 0000 0000 (the leading 1 of 1 0000 0000 is lost)

练习Practice

先把两个数都转成十进制估算一下，再动笔加。Estimate in denary first, then add.

① 0111 1111 + 0000 0001 = ？有没有溢出？1. 0111 1111 + 0000 0001 = ? Any overflow?

② 1010 1010 + 0110 0110 = ？有没有溢出？2. 1010 1010 + 0110 0110 = ? Any overflow?

点开看答案Show answer

① 127 + 1 = 128 → 1000 0000，刚好装得下，没有溢出。1. 127 + 1 = 128 → 1000 0000; it just fits, so no overflow.

② 170 + 102 = 272 > 255 → 溢出；结果需要 9 位（1 0001 0000），8 位寄存器里留下 0001 0000（16），答案明显不对，这就是溢出的样子。2. 170 + 102 = 272 > 255 → overflow; the result needs 9 bits (1 0001 0000) and the 8-bit register keeps 0001 0000 (16) — visibly wrong, which is what overflow looks like.

— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —
6 逻辑移位：左移乘 2、右移除 2Logical shifts: ×2 and ÷2 Shift移位
这一站要会的：做左移、右移；说出移位等于乘/除以 2 的几次方；知道移出去的位会丢掉。 Goal: perform left and right shifts; state the multiply/divide rule; know that shifted-out bits are lost.
说人话：把一串 0/1 整体往左挪一格，原来在「2」位置上的那个 1 就跑到「4」的位置上了——每个人的身价都翻倍，所以整个数翻倍。右移就相反，整体除以 2。这也是计算机做乘除法特别快的原因：它不用真的做乘法，挪一下就行。 In plain words: shift every bit one place left and the bit that was in the "2" column lands in the "4" column — every bit doubles in value, so the whole number doubles. Shifting right divides by two. This is why multiplying and dividing by powers of two is so fast for a computer: it just slides the bits.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 左移 n 位 = 乘以 2ⁿ；右边空出来的位补 0。Left shift by n = multiply by 2ⁿ; zeros fill in from the right.
  • 右移 n 位 = 除以 2ⁿ（向下取整）；左边补 0，右边被挤出去的位直接丢弃。Right shift by n = divide by 2ⁿ (rounded down); zeros fill from the left and bits pushed off the right are lost.
  • 右移遇到除不尽就取整：5（0000 0101）右移 1 位 = 2（0000 0010），那个 1 被丢掉了。Right shifts round down: 5 (0000 0101) shifted right = 2 (0000 0010); the final 1 is discarded.
  • 左移也可能造成溢出（超出 8 位的部分丢失）。Left shifts can also overflow — anything past eight bits is lost.

例题 · 移位Example · shifting

(a) 0000 1101 左移 1 位是多少？　(b) 0011 0011 右移 2 位是多少？ (a) Shift 0000 1101 left by 1. (b) Shift 0011 0011 right by 2.
(a) 0000 1101 是 13，左移 1 位 → 0001 1010 = 26（= 13 × 2）✓(a) 0000 1101 is 13; shifting left gives 0001 1010 = 26 (= 13 × 2) ✓ (b) 0011 0011 是 51，右移 2 位 = 51 ÷ 4 = 12.75 → 取整 12；二进制 0000 1100（掉出去的两位是 11，直接丢了）(b) 0011 0011 is 51; right shift by 2 = 51 ÷ 4 = 12.75 → 12; binary 0000 1100 (the two shifted-out bits, 11, are lost)

练习Practice

先算十进制结果，再写成 8 位二进制。Work out the denary answer first, then write the 8 bits.

① 0000 0111 左移 2 位 = ？1. Shift 0000 0111 left by 2 places.

② 1000 0000 右移 1 位 = ？（提示：右边补 0，不是补 1）2. Shift 1000 0000 right by 1 place. (Hint: a zero fills in from the left.)

点开看答案Show answer

① 7 × 4 = 28 → 0001 11001. 7 × 4 = 28 → 0001 1100

② 128 ÷ 2 = 64 → 0100 00002. 128 ÷ 2 = 64 → 0100 0000

— 第 6 站完成 —— Stop 6 done —
7 补码：负数怎么存Two's complement: storing negative numbers Two's complement补码
这一站要会的：用补码表示负数（取反加一）；把补码读回十进制；知道 8 位补码的范围。 Goal: represent negative numbers (invert and add one); read a two's complement value back; know the 8-bit range.
说人话：计算机还是只有 0 和 1，没法在前面写个减号。于是约定：最高位当成「负的身价」——8 位时最高位的身价不是 128，而是 −128。这样 1111 1111 就等于 −128+64+32+16+8+4+2+1 = −1，很自然。求某个负数的写法有个口诀：先写正数 → 全部取反 → 最后加 1。 In plain words: a computer still only has 0s and 1s and cannot write a minus sign. So we agree that the top bit counts as a negative place value: in 8 bits it is worth −128, not +128. That makes 1111 1111 equal to −128+64+32+16+8+4+2+1 = −1, which fits neatly. To write a negative number: write the positive value → invert every bit → add 1.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 8 位补码的位权：−128 64 32 16 8 4 2 1。8-bit two's complement place values: −128 64 32 16 8 4 2 1.
  • 取负步骤：正数二进制 → 取反（0 变 1、1 变 0）→ 加 1。To negate: write the positive binary → invert every bit → add 1.
  • 8 位补码范围：−128 到 +127（不是 −127 到 +127，这是常考的坑）。8-bit range: −128 to +127 — not −127 to +127 (a favourite trap).
  • 补码的好处：加法和减法用同一套电路，减法就是「加上一个负数」。Why it works: addition and subtraction use the same circuitry — subtracting is just adding a negative.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：题目让你「写步骤」，按这四步写就不会漏分。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: when asked to show your working, use these four steps. Write +5 in binary: 00000101. Invert every bit: 11111010. Add 1: 11111011. So −5 = 11111011.

例题 · 补码Example · two's complement

(a) 用 8 位补码表示 −5。　(b) 补码 1001 0110 表示十进制的几？ (a) Write −5 in 8-bit two's complement. (b) What denary value is 1001 0110?
(a) +5 = 0000 0101 → 取反 1111 1010 → 加 1 → 1111 1011（验算：−128+64+32+16+8+2+1 = −5 ✓）(a) +5 = 0000 0101 → invert 1111 1010 → add 1 → 1111 1011 (check: −128+64+32+16+8+2+1 = −5 ✓) (b) 最高位是 1 → 是负数：−128 + 16 + 4 + 2 = −106(b) The top bit is 1, so it is negative: −128 + 16 + 4 + 2 = −106

练习Practice

写负数的口诀：写正数 → 取反 → 加 1。Mantra for negatives: write positive → invert → add 1.

① 用 8 位补码表示 −40。1. Write −40 in 8-bit two's complement.

② 补码 1000 0010 是十进制的几？2. What denary value is 1000 0010?

点开看答案Show answer

① +40 = 0010 1000 → 取反 1101 0111 → 加 1 → 1101 1000（验算：−128+64+16+8 = −40 ✓）1. +40 = 0010 1000 → invert 1101 0111 → add 1 → 1101 1000 (check: −128+64+16+8 = −40 ✓)

② −128 + 2 = −1262. −128 + 2 = −126

— 第 7 站完成 —— Stop 7 done —
8 文字、图片、声音怎么存How text, images and sound are stored File size文件大小
这一站要会的：说出 ASCII 与 Unicode 的区别；会算图片和声音的文件大小；知道提高画质/音质会让文件变大。 Goal: distinguish ASCII from Unicode; calculate image and sound file sizes; know that better quality means bigger files.
说人话：三句话记牢：文字 → 给每个字符编个号（ASCII 编号 0–127，只够英文；Unicode 才能放中文和 emoji）；图片 → 切成小方格（每个方格记一个颜色，方格越多越清晰，每个方格用的位数越多颜色越丰富）；声音 → 每秒钟量很多次「现在有多响」（量得越勤、每次量得越精确，声音越真，文件也越大）。 In plain words: three lines to remember: text → number every character (ASCII covers 0–127, English only; Unicode is needed for Chinese characters and emoji); images → cut into tiny squares (each square stores one colour; more squares means sharper, more bits per square means richer colour); sound → measure "how loud right now" many times a second (more often and more precisely means truer sound and a bigger file).
记住这几点（计算必背）：Key points (learn these for calculations):
  • 图片文件大小 = 宽 × 高 × 色深（单位是位，再除以 8 得字节）Image file size = width × height × colour depth (in bits; divide by 8 for bytes)
  • 声音文件大小 = 采样率 × 位深 × 声道数 × 秒数Sound file size = sample rate × sample resolution × channels × seconds
  • ASCII 用 7 位（可扩展到 8 位），只能表示英文和符号；Unicode（UTF-8/16/32）用更多位，能表示所有语言与 emoji。ASCII uses 7 bits (often stored as 8) and covers English and symbols only; Unicode (UTF-8/16/32) uses more bits and covers every language plus emoji.
  • 色深 n 位 → 最多 2ⁿ 种颜色（8 位 = 256 色，24 位 = 约 1670 万色）。Colour depth of n bits gives up to 2ⁿ colours (8 bits = 256, 24 bits ≈ 16.7 million).
  • 文件里除了数据还有 元数据 metadata（尺寸、创建时间、格式说明），题目一般忽略不计。Files also carry metadata (dimensions, date created, format) — usually ignored in exam calculations.

例题 · 文件大小计算Example · file size

(a) 一张 800 × 600、色深 16 位的图片，文件大小是多少字节？约多少 KiB？
(b) 一段 44 100 Hz、16 位、单声道、10 秒的录音，文件大小约多少 KiB？ (a) An 800 × 600 image with 16-bit colour depth — how many bytes? Roughly how many KiB?
(b) A 10-second mono recording at 44 100 Hz and 16 bits — roughly how many KiB?
(a) 800 × 600 = 480 000 个像素；480 000 × 16 = 7 680 000 位；÷8 = 960 000 字节；÷1024 ≈ 937.5 KiB(a) 800 × 600 = 480 000 pixels; × 16 = 7 680 000 bits; ÷ 8 = 960 000 bytes; ÷ 1024 ≈ 937.5 KiB (b) 44 100 × 16 × 1 × 10 = 7 056 000 位；÷8 = 882 000 字节；÷1024 ≈ 861.3 KiB(b) 44 100 × 16 × 1 × 10 = 7 056 000 bits; ÷ 8 = 882 000 bytes; ÷ 1024 ≈ 861.3 KiB

练习Practice

先算位数，再 ÷8 变字节，最后 ÷1024 变 KiB。Bits first, then ÷8 for bytes, then ÷1024 for KiB.

① 一张 1024 × 768、24 位色深的图片，约多少 MiB？1. A 1024 × 768 image at 24-bit colour depth — roughly how many MiB?

② 一段 8000 Hz、8 位、单声道、30 秒的录音，约多少 KiB？2. A 30-second mono recording at 8000 Hz and 8 bits — roughly how many KiB?

点开看答案Show answer

① 1024 × 768 × 24 = 18 874 368 位 → ÷8 = 2 359 296 字节 → ÷1024 = 2304 KiB → ÷1024 = 2.25 MiB1. 1024 × 768 × 24 = 18 874 368 bits → 2 359 296 bytes → 2304 KiB → 2.25 MiB

② 8000 × 8 × 1 × 30 = 1 920 000 位 → ÷8 = 240 000 字节 → ÷1024 ≈ 234.4 KiB2. 8000 × 8 × 1 × 30 = 1 920 000 bits → 240 000 bytes → ≈ 234.4 KiB

— 第 8 站完成 —— Stop 8 done —
9 压缩：把重复的省掉Compression: cutting out the repetition Lossy / Lossless有损 / 无损
这一站要会的：说出为什么要压缩；分清无损与有损并各举一例；会用游程编码 RLE 压缩一串数据。 Goal: say why we compress; distinguish lossless from lossy with an example of each; compress data using run-length encoding.
说人话：压缩就是「把重复的地方记成一句话」。AAAAA 不必真写五个 A，写「5 个 A」就行——这叫无损，解压后跟原来一模一样。另一种更狠：有损，直接把人眼不太注意的颜色细节、人耳不太注意的高频声音扔掉，文件能小十倍，但再也变不回原样。照片用 JPEG、音乐用 MP3 就是这么干的。 In plain words: compression means writing repetition once instead of many times. Instead of five A's you write "5 A" — that is lossless: uncompressing gives back exactly the original. The harsher option is lossy: throw away colour detail the eye barely notices, or high frequencies the ear barely hears. The file can shrink tenfold, but you can never get the original back — that is what JPEG and MP3 do.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 为什么要压缩：文件更小 → 传得更快、占更少存储空间、更省流量。Why compress: smaller files transfer faster, take less storage space and use less bandwidth.
  • 无损 lossless：能完全还原原始数据，用于文本、程序、电子表格（错一个字就毁了）。方法：游程编码 RLE、字典/霍夫曼编码。格式：ZIP、PNG。Lossless: the original is recovered exactly; used for text, programs, spreadsheets where one wrong character ruins it. Methods: RLE, dictionary/Huffman coding. Formats: ZIP, PNG.
  • 有损 lossy：永久丢掉一部分信息，用于图片、音频、视频。格式：JPEG、MP3、MPEG。Lossy: information is permanently discarded; used for images, audio, video. Formats: JPEG, MP3, MPEG.
  • RLE 的局限：数据里重复越少越没用，极端情况反而让文件变大（每个值都不重复时，还要多写「1」）。RLE's weakness: it only helps when there is repetition, and can even make a file bigger when nothing repeats.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：题目常问「这种文件该用哪种压缩，为什么？」——先说类型，再说理由。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: questions often ask which type of compression suits a file and why — state the type, then the reason. Lossless compression should be used for a text file because no data can be lost — losing a single character would change the meaning. Lossy compression is suitable for a photograph because small losses in detail cannot be seen by the human eye and the file is much smaller.

例题 · RLEExample · RLE

用游程编码压缩这一串：W W W W B B B B B B W W（共 12 个值） Compress this sequence using RLE: W W W W B B B B B B W W (12 values)
数连续段：4 个 W，再 6 个 B，再 2 个 W → 4W 6B 2WCount the runs: 4 W's, then 6 B's, then 2 W's → 4W 6B 2W 原来要存 12 个值，现在只要 6 个（3 个数字 + 3 个字母），省了一半。12 values become 6 (three counts plus three letters) — halved.

练习Practice

先判断类型，再给理由。Decide the type, then justify it.

① 医院存档的 X 光片，该用有损还是无损？为什么？1. An X-ray image stored by a hospital — lossy or lossless? Why?

② 视频通话的画面，该用有损还是无损？为什么？2. A video call picture — lossy or lossless? Why?

点开看答案Show answer

① 无损。X 光片要用于诊断，丢掉哪怕一点细节都可能影响医生判断，而且法律上要求存档保持原样。1. Lossless. The image is used for diagnosis — any lost detail could affect a doctor's judgement, and records must be kept exactly.

② 有损。视频通话要实时传输，画面小一点、糊一点用户能接受；卡住不能接受。流畅比保真更重要。2. Lossy. Video calls must be transmitted live; a slightly softer picture is acceptable, while stuttering is not. Smoothness matters more than perfect fidelity.

单元 1 完成 · 主线：一切都变成 0 和 1，再想办法变短、变回来看得懂Unit 1 done · the thread: everything becomes 0s and 1s, then we find ways to shorten it and read it back

回头看这一单元其实就三件事：① 怎么数（二进制、十六进制、补码）；② 怎么存（文本编号、图片切格、声音采样）；③ 怎么省（压缩）。转换题是送分题，练到不用想为止。In hindsight this unit is three things: 1. how to count (binary, hex, two's complement); 2. how to store (numbering text, gridding images, sampling sound); 3. how to save space (compression). Conversions are free marks — drill them until they are automatic.

单元 2 · 数据传输Unit 2 · Data transmissionPAPER 1

数据怎么从一台电脑跑到另一台电脑 · 这一单元几乎全是「说出 + 解释」的解释题，学会套答法就是送分How data travels from one computer to another · almost every question here is "state and explain", so learning the answer patterns earns easy marks

本单元包括什么What this unit covers 7 站 · 约 45 分钟7 stops · about 45 min

  1. 数据包与分组交换Packets and packet switching
  2. 串行传输与并行传输Serial vs parallel transmission
  3. 同步传输与异步传输Synchronous vs asynchronous transmission
  4. 单工 / 半双工 / 全双工Simplex, half-duplex and duplex
  5. 通用串行总线USB

建议顺序：先站 1 建立一个「数据是切成小包传的」画面，再顺着 2、3、4 看它是怎么走过去的，最后 5、6、7 讲接口、查错和补发。Suggested order: build the picture in stop 1 that data is chopped into small packets, follow stops 2–4 to see how it physically travels, then 5–7 cover the port, catching errors and resending.

1 数据包与分组交换Packets and packet switching Packet switching分组交换
这一站要会的：说出一个数据包每一部分装的是什么；说出为什么要把大文件切成小包；比较分组交换和电路交换。 Goal: state what each part of a packet contains; say why large files are split into packets; compare packet switching with circuit switching.
说人话：你想把一部电影发给同学，不可能把它整个塞进网线里一次推过去——太大了，而且中途一断就全白干。所以先切成几千个小包，每个小包都带上「发给谁、从哪来、这是第几块、一共几块」的小纸条；这些包各自出发，哪条路不堵就走哪条，到了对方那里再按编号拼回原来的样子。好处是这条网线可以被所有人轮流用，某一根光缆断了还能绕；麻烦是包可能迟到、乱序甚至丢失，所以接收方要重排，缺了还要让对方补发。 In plain words: to send a film to a friend you cannot push the whole thing down one cable in one go — it is far too big, and one break would ruin everything. So it is chopped into thousands of small packets, each carrying a little note saying who it is for, where it came from, its number and how many there are in total. Each packet sets off on its own, taking whichever route is least busy, and they are put back into order at the other end. The advantage is that the same cable is shared by everyone and a broken cable can be routed around; the drawback is that packets can arrive late, out of order, or not at all, so they must be reordered and missing ones requested again.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 头部（header）：发送方地址、接收方地址、包的编号、包的总数、用的是什么协议。The header holds the sender address, the destination address, the packet number, the total number of packets and the protocol used.
  • 数据部分（payload）：真正要送出去的内容，比如一段视频画面、一行聊天内容。The payload is the actual data: part of a video frame, a line of a message, and so on.
  • 尾部（trailer）：用来查错的一组数据（校验和或循环冗余校验）和「这个包到此结束」的标记。The trailer holds error-checking data (a checksum or cyclic redundancy check) plus an end-of-packet flag.
  • 为什么要分包：① 出错只要重发那一个包，不用整个文件重来；② 一条线路可以让很多人轮流用；③ 一个很大的文件不会长时间把线路占死。Why split into packets: 1. only the damaged packet has to be resent, not the whole file; 2. many users can share one line by taking turns; 3. one huge file cannot hog the line for a long time.
  • 分组交换（packet switching）：每个包自己选路，线路不独占，利用率高；缺点是可能乱序、丢失，需要排序和重传。Packet switching: each packet chooses its own route, no line is reserved, so the network is used efficiently; packets may arrive out of order or go missing, so they must be sorted and resent.
  • 电路交换（circuit switching）：通话前先建立一条专用通路，通信期间这条通路被独占，聊完才释放；数据顺序稳定、延迟固定，但你沉默时带宽也闲着，别人用不了。Circuit switching: a dedicated path is set up before communication and reserved for the whole session, then released. The order never changes and the delay is constant, but the bandwidth sits idle whenever you are silent and nobody else can use it.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：题目常问「为什么互联网用分组交换」，或是「说出数据包三部分分别是什么」，下面这句可以直接套。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: use this sentence for questions like "why does the internet use packet switching?" or "state what each part of a packet contains". Data is split into packets. The header contains the destination address and the packet number, the payload contains the actual data, and the trailer contains the error-checking data. In packet switching each packet takes the best available route, so many users can share the same link.

例题 · 算包数Example · counting packets

一个 3 MiB 的文件要通过分组交换发送。每个包的数据部分最多放 1500 字节，一共至少要多少个包？ A 3 MiB file is sent by packet switching. Each packet can carry up to 1500 bytes of data. How many packets are needed at minimum?
① 换成字节：3 × 1024 × 1024 = 3 145 728 字节1. Convert to bytes: 3 × 1024 × 1024 = 3 145 728 bytes ② 除以每包的容量：3 145 728 ÷ 1500 = 2097.1522. Divide by the payload size: 3 145 728 ÷ 1500 = 2097.152 ③ 剩下的零头也要单独装一包，所以要向上取整 → 2098 个包。别忘了每个包还有头部和尾部，实际传出去的位数比 3 MiB 更多。3. The remainder still needs its own packet, so round up → 2098 packets. Note that headers and trailers add extra, so more than 3 MiB actually travels down the wire.

练习Practice

先换算单位，再除，最后记得向上取整。Convert units first, then divide, then remember to round up.

① 说出数据包头部里必须有的两项内容，并说明它们的作用。1. State two items that must be in a packet header, and what each is for.

② 一个 2 MiB 的文件，每包的数据部分装 1024 字节，需要多少个包？2. A 2 MiB file is sent with a payload of 1024 bytes per packet. How many packets are needed?

点开看答案Show answer

① 接收方地址（知道该送给谁）和包编号（到终点后按编号重新拼装、也能发现哪一号包丢了）。1. The destination address (so the network knows where to send it) and the packet number (so packets can be reassembled in the right order and missing ones spotted).

② 2 MiB = 2 × 1024 × 1024 = 2 097 152 字节；2 097 152 ÷ 1024 = 2048 个包（正好整除，不用取整）。2. 2 MiB = 2 × 1024 × 1024 = 2 097 152 bytes; 2 097 152 ÷ 1024 = 2048 packets exactly, so no rounding needed.

— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 串行传输与并行传输Serial vs parallel transmission Serial / Parallel串行 / 并行
这一站要会的：说出串行、并行各是怎么传的；各给两个优点和两个缺点；说出为什么远距离要用串行。 Goal: describe how serial and parallel transmission work; give advantages and disadvantages of each; explain why serial is used over long distances.
说人话：传一个字节要送 8 位。并行就像八个人排成一排一起过桥——拉 8 条线同时各走 1 位，一个瞬间就到齐了。问题在于：距离一长，八条线的长短、粗细不可能完全一样，有的信号先跑到有的跑到晚（这叫歪斜），接收方在同一瞬间一读，就把「别人的位」读进来了；线挨得近还会互相干扰。串行只有一两条线，八个人排队依次走，慢慢送来。它一次只走一位，所以同样的时间内送得少，但没有人会和别人抢位置，跑多远都不会错位，线材也便宜细软。所以结论是：机器里那一小段用并行，出了机箱跑到外面的统统用串行——网线、电话线、通用串行总线都是。 In plain words: sending one byte means sending eight bits. Parallel transmission is eight people crossing a bridge side by side: eight wires each carry one bit and they all arrive together. The problem is that over long distances no two wires are identical, so some signals arrive before others (called skew); the receiver samples all lines at one instant and picks up the wrong combination. Wires packed together also interfere with each other. Serial transmission uses only one or two wires, so the bits go one after another. It moves fewer bits per second, but nothing competes for position, nothing goes out of step however far it travels, and the cable is cheap and thin. So: parallel for short hops inside the machine, serial for anything leaving the box — network cables, telephone lines and USB are all serial.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 串行（serial）：一条线，一次送一位。优点：线少、便宜、接插件小；不容易出现歪斜和线间串扰，长距离可靠。缺点：同样的节拍下每秒送的位数少。Serial: one wire, one bit at a time. Advantages: fewer wires, cheaper, smaller connectors, no skew or crosstalk, reliable over long distances. Disadvantage: fewer bits per second at the same clock rate.
  • 并行（parallel）：多条线同时各送一位，一次送一个字节甚至更多。优点：同样节拍下快。缺点：距离一大就歪斜、串扰；线多、粗、贵。Parallel: many wires each carry one bit at the same time, sending a whole byte at once. Advantage: faster at the same clock rate. Disadvantages: skew and crosstalk over distance; more wires mean thick, expensive cables.
  • 歪斜（skew）：同一批信号到达时间不一致，导致接收方读错。串扰（crosstalk）：相邻导线之间的电磁干扰。Skew: signals from the same group arrive at different times, so the receiver reads them wrongly. Crosstalk: electromagnetic interference between neighbouring wires.
  • 所以用在哪里：远距离、接外设 → 串行；机器内部很短的排线 → 并行。Where each is used: serial for long distances and external devices; parallel for very short internal connections.
最容易丢分的地方：题目问「为什么远距离用串行」，别只写「因为便宜」——最关键的一条是并行在长距离会产生歪斜（各条线的信号不再同时到达），这个是满分句。反过来问「为什么并行更快」就写「多条线同时各送一位，一次能送一个字节」。 Classic trap: for "why use serial over long distances?" do not write "because it is cheaper" alone — the key point is that parallel suffers from skew over distance because the signals no longer arrive together. For "why is parallel faster?" write that several wires each carry one bit, sending a whole byte at once.

例题 · 速度比较Example · comparing speeds

(a) 8 条数据线的并行口，每个时钟周期送几位？用同样的时钟，串行口要几个周期才能送完一个字节？
(b) 一根串行线每秒送 9600 位，若每个字节还要额外带上 1 位起始位和 1 位停止位，每秒最多送多少字节？ (a) An 8-wire parallel port sends how many bits per clock cycle? At the same clock rate, how many cycles does a serial port need for one byte?
(b) A serial line carries 9600 bits per second. If each byte also has 1 start bit and 1 stop bit, how many bytes per second at most?
(a) 并行 8 条线一次齐发 → 每个周期 8 位；串行一次 1 位 → 送完 8 位要 8 个周期。(a) Parallel sends all eight together → 8 bits per cycle. Serial sends one at a time → 8 cycles for one byte. (b) 每个字节实际要送 1 + 8 + 1 = 10 位；9600 ÷ 10 = 960 字节每秒。(b) Each byte actually needs 1 + 8 + 1 = 10 bits; 9600 ÷ 10 = 960 bytes per second.

练习Practice

「歪斜」这个词一定要会用。Make sure you can use the word "skew".

① 举两个理由说明为什么城市之间的通信用串行而不用并行。1. Give two reasons why communication between cities uses serial rather than parallel transmission.

② 一根并行线一次送 8 位，时钟是每秒 1000 个周期；它每秒送多少位？同样时钟的串行线每秒送多少位？2. A parallel cable sends 8 bits at a time with 1000 cycles per second. How many bits per second? What about a serial line with the same clock?

点开看答案Show answer

① 长距离时并行会产生歪斜（各条线的信号到达时间不同），还会串扰互相干扰，数据会读错；而且 8 条线的线材和接头又贵又笨重。串行只有一两条线，便宜且不受歪斜影响。1. Over long distances parallel suffers skew (signals arrive at different times) and crosstalk between the wires, so the data is misread; eight wires plus connectors are also expensive and bulky. Serial uses one or two wires: cheaper and immune to skew.

② 并行：8 × 1000 = 8000 位每秒；串行：1 × 1000 = 1000 位每秒。2. Parallel: 8 × 1000 = 8000 bits per second. Serial: 1 × 1000 = 1000 bits per second.

— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 同步传输与异步传输Synchronous vs asynchronous transmission Baud / Bit rate波特率 / 比特率
这一站要会的：说出两种方式的差别；说出起始位、停止位的作用；区分波特率与比特率；说出同步为什么更快。 Goal: describe the difference between the two methods; state the purpose of start and stop bits; distinguish baud rate from bit rate; say why synchronous is faster.
说人话：两台机器互传比特，最大的难题是「什么时候读下一位」。同步的做法是专门拉一根时钟线打拍子（或者把节拍藏在信号里），发送方每响一拍送一位，接收方照着拍子读，一串 1000 位可以连着发不用停。异步的做法是不共享节拍，改成每送一小段（通常一个字节）就在前面加一位「起始位」喊一声开始，接收方听到就把自己的表对准，读完再补一位「停止位」表示这段结束、顺便让线路回到空闲状态。所以异步每一小段都要重新对一次表，两边时钟差一点也没关系——代价是每 8 位要多花 2 到 3 位，平白少了一两成的速度，所以同步更快、批量大数据用同步；线路简单便宜的场合（鼠标、键盘、老串口）用异步。 In plain words: the hard part of sending bits is knowing when to read the next one. Synchronous transmission runs a dedicated clock line to beat time (or hides the timing inside the signal); the sender puts one bit out per beat and the receiver reads per beat, so a thousand bits can stream out without stopping. Asynchronous transmission shares no clock: before each small block (usually one byte) it sends a start bit saying "here I come", the receiver resets its own internal timer on hearing it, and after the block it sends a stop bit to mark the end and return the line to idle. Every block resynchronises, so slightly mismatched clocks do not matter — but it costs two or three extra bits for every eight, losing maybe 20–30% of the speed. So synchronous is faster and suits bulk data, while asynchronous suits cheap simple links such as a mouse, a keyboard or an old serial port.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 同步（synchronous）：双方共用时钟信号（clock），按固定节拍连续发送一大块数据，中间不用暂停。Synchronous: both sides share a clock signal and one large block streams out at a fixed rate without pausing.
  • 异步（asynchronous）：不共享时钟，每小段数据前面加起始位、后面加停止位，接收方靠起始位重新对表。Asynchronous: no shared clock; each small block is wrapped in a start bit and a stop bit, and the receiver resynchronises on the start bit.
  • 同步更快的原因：没有每字节的起止位开销，整块连续发送；异步的优点：线路简单、便宜，两端时钟不必严格一致。Synchronous is faster because there is no start/stop overhead per byte; asynchronous is simpler and cheaper and does not require the two clocks to match.
  • 波特率（baud rate）：每秒信号变化的次数。比特率（bit rate）：每秒传送的位数。一次变化只代表 1 位时，两者数值相等；一次变化能表示 2 位时，比特率就是波特率的 2 倍。Baud rate: the number of signal changes per second. Bit rate: the number of bits transferred per second. When one change carries one bit the two are equal; if one change encodes two bits, the bit rate is twice the baud rate.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：题目问「为什么异步比同步慢」时，一定要提到每字节额外的起止位。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: for "why is asynchronous slower than synchronous?" you must mention the extra bits around every byte. Asynchronous transmission sends a start bit and one or two stop bits with every byte, so extra bits are transmitted that carry no data. Synchronous transmission has no start and stop bits, so more of the bits sent are actual data, making it faster.

例题 · 开销与耗时Example · overhead and time

用 9600 位的速率传 1200 字节。同步方式（无额外开销）要多久？异步方式（每字节 1 位起始 + 1 位停止）要多久？ 1200 bytes are sent at 9600 bits per second. How long does synchronous take (no overhead)? How long does asynchronous take (1 start + 1 stop bit per byte)?
同步：1200 × 8 = 9600 位；9600 ÷ 9600 = 1 秒Synchronous: 1200 × 8 = 9600 bits; 9600 ÷ 9600 = 1 second 异步：每字节 10 位，1200 × 10 = 12 000 位；12 000 ÷ 9600 = 1.25 秒Asynchronous: 10 bits per byte, so 1200 × 10 = 12 000 bits; 12 000 ÷ 9600 = 1.25 seconds 多花 0.25 秒，正好是多出来的 2400 位 ÷ 9600 = 0.25 秒，对的。The extra 0.25 s matches the extra 2400 bits ÷ 9600 = 0.25 s, which checks out.

练习Practice

先算一个字节一共要传几位。Work out how many bits one byte actually needs first.

① 线路每秒传 9600 位，异步方式每字节 10 位，每秒能传多少字节？1. A line carries 9600 bits per second with 10 bits per byte in asynchronous mode. How many bytes per second?

② 一个信号变化能表示 2 位，波特率是 2400，比特率是多少？2. If one signal change represents 2 bits and the baud rate is 2400, what is the bit rate?

点开看答案Show answer

① 9600 ÷ 10 = 960 字节每秒。1. 9600 ÷ 10 = 960 bytes per second.

② 2400 × 2 = 4800 位每秒。2. 2400 × 2 = 4800 bits per second.

— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 单工 / 半双工 / 全双工Simplex, half-duplex and duplex Direction传输方向
这一站要会的：说出三种传输方向的定义，每种各举一个例子。 Goal: define the three transmission directions and give an example of each.
说人话：判断方法只有一个——看箭头。只有一个箭头，永远从甲到乙，乙从来不回答，就是单工（键盘只会把按键告诉电脑，电脑不会反过来用键盘「打字」）。两边都能传，但要排队，你说的时候我说不了，说完喊一句「完毕」才轮到对方，就是半双工（对讲机就是这样，所以才会抢话）。两边同时说也同时听，互不干扰，就是全双工（打电话就是这样，你这边哼一声对方也能听见）。全双工通常要两条通道（或两条线），所以最贵也最快。 In plain words: there is only one test — look at the arrows. One arrow, always from A to B, with B never replying, is simplex (a keyboard reports key presses to the computer; the computer never "types" back at it). Both directions possible but taking turns — you cannot talk while I am talking, so you say "over" to hand over — is half-duplex (a walkie-talkie, which is why people clash). Both sides at the same time, listening while talking, is duplex (a telephone call — your friend hears you hum). Duplex usually needs two channels or two pairs of wires, so it costs the most and performs best.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 单工（simplex）：数据只能朝一个方向传。例：键盘 → 主机、鼠标 → 主机、电台广播 → 收音机。Simplex: data flows in one direction only. Examples: keyboard → computer, mouse → computer, radio station → radio set.
  • 半双工（half-duplex）：两个方向都行，但同一时刻只能有一个方向在传。例：对讲机、老式的总线。Half-duplex: data flows both ways but only one way at a time. Examples: walkie-talkie, older shared bus networks.
  • 全双工（duplex）：两个方向同时进行。例：电话通话、现代以太网。通常需要两条独立通道（或两组线）。Duplex (full-duplex): data flows both ways simultaneously. Examples: a telephone call, modern Ethernet. It normally needs two separate channels or pairs of wires.
  • 从快到慢、从贵到便宜的排序一般是：全双工 > 半双工 > 单工。Performance and cost generally run duplex > half-duplex > simplex.
容易混淆的一点：打印机主要接收数据，所以常说是单工；但很多现代打印机还会把「缺纸」「没墨了」这种状态回报给电脑，这时它就是双向的。遇到这种题，按题目给的情形判断，别死记。 A point that confuses people: a printer mostly receives data, so it is often called simplex; but many modern printers report status such as "out of paper" back to the computer, which makes that link two-way. Judge from the situation given in the question rather than memorising.

例题 · 判断方向Example · deciding the direction

说出下面每种情形属于哪一种传输方向，并给一句理由。
(a) 老师在教室用广播系统放听力录音　(b) 两个人用对讲机对话　(c) 用手机打电话 State which transmission direction each situation uses, with a reason.
(a) a teacher plays a listening recording over the school sound system　(b) two people talking on walkie-talkies　(c) a mobile phone call
(a) 单工：声音只从广播主机传到各个喇叭，喇叭不会把数据传回去。(a) Simplex: sound travels from the player to the speakers only; the speakers send nothing back. (b) 半双工：双方都能说，但同一时刻只有一方在占用频道，得轮流。(b) Half-duplex: both can talk, but only one occupies the channel at a time, so they take turns. (c) 全双工：两边同时说同时听，你哼一声对方立刻能听见。(c) Duplex: both sides speak and listen at once — the other person hears you hum immediately.

练习Practice

先在草稿纸上画两个方块和箭头。Sketch two boxes and the arrows first.

① 电脑把画面送给显示器，属于哪一种？说明理由。1. A computer sends the picture to a monitor. Which type? Give a reason.

② 为什么全双工往往需要两条通道（两组线）？2. Why does duplex transmission usually need two channels (two pairs of wires)?

点开看答案Show answer

① 单工：画面数据只从主机流向显示器，没有反向数据（触控屏除外，触屏要把触摸位置传回来，那就变成双向了）。1. Simplex: picture data flows from the computer to the monitor only, with nothing coming back (unless it is a touch screen, which sends the touch position back and is then two-way).

② 因为两个方向要同时传，各走各的通道互不干扰；如果共用一条通道就会撞在一起，只能变成半双工。2. Because both directions transmit at the same time, so each needs its own path; sharing one path would cause collisions and turn it into half-duplex.

— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 通用串行总线USB Plug and play即插即用
这一站要会的：说出通用串行总线为什么「通用」、为什么用串行；说出即插即用是什么意思；说出它最多能接多少设备；列出优点和缺点。 Goal: say why USB is "universal" and why it is serial; explain plug and play; state the maximum number of devices; list advantages and disadvantages.
说人话：以前每个设备都有自己的接口：打印机一种、鼠标一种、扫描仪又一种，插口不够用还得加装扩展卡。通用串行总线把这些全统一了：一个标准口，插上集线器一层层往下开，最多可以扩展到 127 个设备。它还有三件贴心事：① 自动认人——插上去操作系统自己发现「来了个新家伙」，自动装好驱动程序、分好编号，不用你设置也不用重启；② 自带电源——口子里有 5 伏的电，键盘鼠标优盘直接取电，不用另外插电线；③ 用串行——线细、便宜、拉个几米都不怕歪斜。缺点也说清楚：线不能太长（单根一般几米），老版本速度不够快，而且同一个口上的设备要分这一条通道的带宽。 In plain words: every device used to have its own connector — one sort for the printer, another for the mouse, another for the scanner — and if you ran out of ports you fitted an expansion card. USB unified all of them: one standard port that, with hubs, can reach 127 devices. It also does three kind things: 1. it identifies devices by itself — the operating system notices something new has arrived, loads the driver and assigns it an address, with no setup and no restart; 2. it supplies power — the port provides 5 V so keyboards, mice and flash drives need no mains lead; 3. it is serial — thin, cheap cables that do not suffer skew even over several metres. The downsides are clear too: cables cannot be long (a few metres at most), older versions were slow, and devices on one hub share the bandwidth of a single link.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 通用：一种标准接口就能接几乎所有外设（键盘、鼠标、打印机、摄像头、优盘、手机），还能向下兼容老设备。Universal: one standard connector handles almost every peripheral (keyboard, mouse, printer, camera, flash drive, phone) and is backward compatible with older devices.
  • 串行：数据一位一位走，所以叫通用串行总线；好处是线细成本低、长一点也不歪斜。Serial: bits travel one at a time — hence the name. The cable is thin, cheap and immune to skew.
  • 即插即用（plug and play）：插上就被自动检测到，操作系统自动装入设备驱动程序并完成配置，不用手动设置，也支持热插拔（不用关机）。Plug and play: the device is detected automatically, the operating system loads the device driver and configures it; no manual setup and it supports hot swapping.
  • 可供电：接口自带电源，可以给低功率设备供电；打印机这种大功率的还是要插市电。Power: the port supplies power to low-power devices; hungry ones like printers still need the mains.
  • 数量上限：通过集线器扩展，最多可接 127 个设备。Limit: up to 127 devices can be connected by using hubs.
  • 缺点：单根线缆长度有限（通常约 5 米），旧版本速度慢，同一条通道上的设备要分带宽。Disadvantages: cable length is limited (around 5 m), older versions were slow, and devices share the bandwidth of one link.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：题目问「说出通用串行总线的三个优点」，挑最稳的三条写：通用、自动识别配置、自带电源。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: for "state three advantages of USB" pick the three safest: universal, automatic configuration, and power supply. USB is a universal standard so many different peripherals use the same connector. It is plug and play: the device is detected automatically and the driver is loaded without the user doing anything. The port also supplies power, so small devices do not need their own supply.

例题 · 用特点解释现象Example · using features to explain

小明把刚买的优盘插到电脑上，几秒钟后就能存文件了，整个过程他没有装任何东西。打印机插上同一个接口却还要另外插电源线。
(a) 用两个通用串行总线的特点解释前半句。　(b) 为什么打印机还要插电？ Ali plugs in a new flash drive and can save files seconds later without installing anything. The printer on the same port still needs a mains cable.
(a) Explain the first part using two features of USB. (b) Why does the printer still need mains power?
(a) 两个特点：即插即用（操作系统自动检测到新设备、自动装入驱动程序并完成配置）和接口供电（优盘靠接口取电，不用额外电源）。(a) The two features are plug and play (the operating system detects the device, loads the driver and configures it automatically) and bus power (the drive is powered from the port). (b) 接口只能提供比较小的功率，打印机要驱动加热部件和电机，功率需求超过接口能给的上限，所以必须插市电。(b) A port supplies only limited power; a printer drives motors and a heater, so its power requirement exceeds what the port can supply and it needs the mains.

练习Practice

优点要配一句解释，光写名词不给分。Always add one clause explaining each advantage — a bare name earns nothing.

① 说出通用串行总线的三个优点，每个都配一句说明。1. State three advantages of USB, each with a short explanation.

② 说出它的两个缺点。2. State two disadvantages of USB.

点开看答案Show answer

① 通用（一个口接各种外设，还能兼容老设备）；即插即用（自动检测、自动装驱动，热插拔不用重启）；可供电（接口自带电源，小设备不用另插电线）。1. Universal (one port for every kind of peripheral, still compatible with older ones); plug and play (automatic detection and driver loading, hot swapping with no restart); bus-powered (low-power devices take their supply from the port).

② 线缆长度有限（一根一般只能几米）；共享带宽，接多了会变慢；旧版本速度比较慢（写出任意两个即可）。2. Limited cable length (a few metres per cable); bandwidth is shared so adding devices slows things down; older versions were relatively slow. Any two of these.

— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —
6 查出路上出的错：四种办法Detecting errors: four methods Parity / Checksum奇偶校验 / 校验和
这一站要会的：会加奇偶校验位；说出校验和、回送检查、检查数字各自怎么工作；说出每种方法的局限。 Goal: add a parity bit; describe how checksum, echo check and check digit work; state the limitation of each method.
说人话：数据在路上会被干扰，0 可能被翻成 1。所以要想办法发现错误（注意：这些办法只能发现错，不能自动改错，发现之后是让它重发，下一站讲）。四种常用办法：
① 奇偶校验——约定「一段数据里 1 的个数必须是偶数」（偶校验）或「必须是奇数」（奇校验），然后补一位凑数；接收方数一数，不符合约定就是出错了。
② 校验和——把这段数据按固定长度分组，把各组的值加起来得到一个数，和数据一起发；接收方用同样的方法重算，结果不一样就说明坏了。
③ 回送检查——接收方把收到的东西原封不动发回去，发送方跟原件对比；不一样就重发。
④ 检查数字——一串编号的最后一位是按规则从前面算出来的（比如条形码），机器重新算一遍就能发现你输错了一位或者把相邻两位打反了。 In plain words: interference on the wire can flip a 0 into a 1, so we need ways to detect errors (note: these methods detect but do not correct them — detection is followed by retransmission, the topic of the next stop). Four common methods:
1. Parity check — agree that the number of 1s must always be even (even parity) or always odd (odd parity) and add one bit to make it so; the receiver counts the 1s and complains if the rule is broken.
2. Checksum — group the data into fixed-size blocks, add the values up and send the total along with the data; the receiver repeats the calculation and a different total means corruption.
3. Echo check — the receiver sends back exactly what it received and the sender compares it with the original, retransmitting if they differ.
4. Check digit — the last digit of a code number is calculated from the rest by a fixed rule (barcodes do this), so recalculating catches a mistyped digit or two digits transposed.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 奇偶校验（parity check）：在数据后面加一位校验位，使整段数据里 1 的个数为偶数（偶校验）或奇数（奇校验）。Parity check: one extra parity bit is added so that the total number of 1s is even (even parity) or odd (odd parity). Add 0 if the count is already correct, otherwise add 1.
  • 奇偶校验的致命缺点：如果两位同时翻（或任意偶数个位翻），1 的个数的奇偶性不变，检查不出来；它也不知道是哪一位错了。The fatal weakness of parity: if two bits flip (or any even number), the count of 1s keeps its parity and the error is not detected; nor does it say which bit is wrong.
  • 校验和（checksum）：把数据分组后求和（有时还包括每组的位置信息），把这个和随数据一起发送；接收方重算比对。比奇偶校验能发现更多种错误，但也可能碰巧漏掉某些组合。Checksum: data is divided into blocks which are added together (sometimes with positional weighting) and the total is sent with the data; the receiver recomputes and compares. It catches more kinds of error than parity, but some combinations can still slip through.
  • 回送检查（echo check）：接收方把收到的数据原样发回，发送方比对；不同就重发。缺点：数据要传两趟，慢；如果两趟在同一位置都错了，或者回送途中才出错，就无法判断是哪里出的错。Echo check: the receiver sends the data straight back and the sender compares; a difference triggers retransmission. Disadvantages: everything travels twice, and it cannot tell which copy is wrong if both journeys are damaged.
  • 检查数字（check digit）：一串编号的最后一位由前面各位按固定规则算出；录入时机器重算比对，能发现输错一位、两位打颠倒等常见错误。例：条形码、书号。Check digit: the final digit of a code is calculated from the others by a fixed rule; the machine recalculates it on entry to catch mistyped or transposed digits. Examples: barcodes, ISBN numbers.
最容易丢分的地方：题目问「奇偶校验有什么局限」，标准答案就两句话——只能发现奇数个位出错，偶数个位同时出错就发现不了；而且它只能告诉你有错，无法指出错在哪一位。千万别写成「它能自动改正错误」。 Classic trap: for "what is the limitation of parity checking?" the answer is two sentences — it only detects an odd number of bit changes, so an even number of flips goes unnoticed, and it cannot say which bit is wrong. Never write that it corrects the error.

例题 · 补校验位与算校验和Example · parity bits and a checksum

(a) 数据 1011001 用偶校验要补几位？用奇校验呢？
(b) 发送方要发 23、47、56、88 这四个值，请算出校验和，并说明接收方怎么用它。
(c) 为什么接收方发现奇偶校验不对时只能请求重发？ (a) What parity bit is added to 1011001 for even parity? For odd parity?
(b) The sender transmits the values 23, 47, 56 and 88. Calculate the checksum and explain how the receiver uses it.
(c) Why can the receiver only ask for retransmission when parity fails?
(a) 数一数 1 的个数：1+0+1+1+0+0+1 → 4 个，是偶数。偶校验补 0；奇校验要凑成奇数，补 1。(a) Count the 1s: 1+0+1+1+0+0+1 gives four, which is even. Even parity adds 0; odd parity needs an odd total, so it adds 1. (b) 23 + 47 + 56 + 88 = 214，发送方把 214 作为校验和一起发过去。接收方把收到的四个数再算一遍：如果得到 214，就认为没出错；如果比如得到 212，说明数据在路上变了样，于是请求重发。(b) 23 + 47 + 56 + 88 = 214, which the sender transmits as the checksum. The receiver adds the four values again: a total of 214 means all is well, whereas 212 means something changed in transit and retransmission is requested. (c) 奇偶校验只知道「1 的个数不对」，不知道是哪一位翻了，所以没法自己改，只能让对方再发一次。例如 1011001 里第 1 位和第 5 位同时翻转，1 的个数仍是偶数，连错都查不出来。(c) A parity check only knows that "the number of 1s is wrong" and not which bit changed, so it cannot repair the data — the sender must send it again. If bit 1 and bit 5 of 1011001 both flipped, the count of 1s would stay even and even the error itself would be missed.

练习Practice

第一步永远是：数清 1 的个数。Step one is always: count the 1s.

① 数据 1101011 用偶校验要补几位？用奇校验呢？1. What parity bit is added to 1101011 for even parity? For odd parity?

② 说出回送检查是怎么做的，并说一个它的缺点。2. Describe how an echo check works and give one disadvantage.

点开看答案Show answer

① 1 的个数是 1+1+0+1+0+1+1 = 5 个（奇数）。偶校验要凑成偶数 → 补 1（变成 6 个）；奇校验已经是奇数 → 补 0。1. The 1s number 1+1+0+1+0+1+1 = five, which is odd. Even parity needs an even count, so add 1 (making six); odd parity already matches, so add 0.

② 做法：接收方把刚收到的数据原封不动发回发送方，发送方把它和原件逐个比对，一样就没错，不一样就重发。缺点：所有数据都要传两遍，速度减半；而且如果两趟都在同一位置出错，或者回送途中才出错，就判断不出问题出在哪。2. Method: the receiver sends the received data straight back to the sender, which compares it with the original character by character; if they differ the data is sent again. Disadvantage: everything is transmitted twice so throughput halves, and if both journeys corrupt the same position it cannot be identified.

— 第 6 站完成 —— Stop 6 done —
7 自动重传请求与加密ARQ and encryption ARQ / Encryption重传 / 加密
这一站要会的：说出自动重传请求用到的三个机制；说出超时、确认、否定确认各自的含义；说出数据在传输途中为什么需要加密。 Goal: state the three mechanisms ARQ uses; explain timeout, acknowledgement and negative acknowledgement; say why data must be encrypted during transmission.
说人话：上一站说的办法只能发现错了，发现之后怎么办？答案是：让它再发一次。这就是自动重传请求的做法，双方先约好三条规矩：① 发出就掐表——发送方每发一个包就启动计时器；② 收到要回话——接收方检查通过就回一个确认（意思是收到了，继续），检查不通过就回一个否定确认（意思是这包坏了，重发）；③ 掐表到点没等到回话就是超时——说明包可能压根没到，立刻把这一包再发一遍。为了防止「补发来的包和原来那份撞在一起」认不出来，每个包都带着编号，接收方收到重复的就丢掉，缺哪号就让对方补哪号。
最后再解决一个问题：数据在网上要经过一大堆中间设备，任何一环都可能被人偷看。所以要先加密——用密钥和一套加密算法把原来的内容打成乱码（密文）再发出去；路上就算被截获，那人手里没有密钥，拿到的也只是一串没有意义的字符。 In plain words: the methods in the last stop only detect errors — so what happens next? Answer: ask for it to be sent again. That is Automatic Repeat reQuest (ARQ), and the two sides agree on three rules: 1. start a timer when sending — the sender times every packet; 2. always answer — if the checks pass the receiver sends an acknowledgement (got it, carry on), otherwise it sends a negative acknowledgement (damaged, send again); 3. if the timer runs out first, that is a timeout — the packet probably never arrived, so it is sent once more. To avoid confusing a resent packet with the original, every packet carries a number: duplicates are discarded and missing numbers are requested again.
One more problem remains: data crosses a long chain of intermediate devices, any of which could be listening in. So it is encrypted first — a key and an encryption algorithm turn the readable message into meaningless ciphertext. Anyone who intercepts it without the key ends up holding nonsense.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 自动重传请求（ARQ）：一种出错后自动要求重发的机制，必须用在这两台设备事先约定的规则之上。ARQ (Automatic Repeat reQuest): a method of automatically asking for retransmission after an error, using rules agreed in advance by both devices.
  • 确认（acknowledgement）：接收方检查通过时发出的信号，表示「收到了，请继续」。An acknowledgement signals "received correctly, please carry on".
  • 否定确认（negative acknowledgement）：检查出错误时发出的信号，表示「这包坏了，请重发」。A negative acknowledgement signals "this packet is corrupt, please send it again".
  • 超时（timeout）：发送方发出后开始计时，规定时间内没收到任何回应就当作失败并重发。A timeout happens when no reply arrives within the set time, and the sender resends.
  • 为什么要有编号：重传可能造成收到两份一样的包、也可能包的顺序乱了；编号让接收方能识别重复并按顺序拼回去。Why number the packets: retransmission can deliver duplicates and reorder them; numbering lets the receiver spot duplicates and rebuild the sequence.
  • 优点 / 代价：能保证数据可靠到达；但重传会拖慢整体速度、增加延迟。Benefit and cost: delivery is reliable, but retransmission slows throughput and adds delay.
  • 为什么要加密：传输途中的数据会经过公共线路和多台中间设备，可能被窃听截获；加密后用密钥把明文变成密文，没有密钥的人即使截获也读不懂内容。Why encrypt: data crosses public links and many intermediate devices where it can be intercepted; encryption turns plaintext into ciphertext with a key, so an interceptor without the key cannot read it.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：题目常问「说明数据包是怎么保证可靠到达的」或「为什么网上要加密」，下面两句可以直接套。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: ready-made answers for "explain how reliable delivery is achieved" and "why is data encrypted?" The sender starts a timer when it sends a packet. If the receiver finds no error it sends an acknowledgement; if it detects an error it sends a negative acknowledgement. If the timer expires before a reply arrives, the sender resends the packet. Data is encrypted because it may be intercepted while travelling across the network, and encrypted data cannot be understood without the key.

例题 · 三种情况怎么办Example · three situations

(a) 发送方发出第 3 包后一直没等到任何回应，接下来会发生什么？
(b) 接收方连着收到了两个「第 3 包」，它会怎么做？
(c) 有人从线路上悄悄拷走了一份加密的数据，他为什么读不懂？ (a) The sender gets no reply to packet 3. What happens next?
(b) The receiver is delivered two copies of "packet 3". What does it do?
(c) Someone copies off an encrypted copy of the data. Why can he not read it?
(a) 规定时间到 = 超时，发送方认为这一包丢了，自动把第 3 包再发一次。(a) The timer expires — a timeout — so the sender assumes the packet was lost and automatically resends packet 3. (b) 看包编号发现这一号已经有了，说明是重传来的重复包，丢掉第二份，只留一份参与拼装。(b) The packet number shows this one has already arrived, so the duplicate from the retransmission is discarded and only one copy is kept. (c) 他拿到的是密文：数据是用发送方和接收方约定的密钥打乱的，没有密钥就没有办法还原成原始数据，看到的只是一串没有意义的字符。(c) What he holds is ciphertext: the data was scrambled using a key known only to the sender and receiver, and without that key there is no way back to the original — just a meaningless string.

练习Practice

先分别说出三种信号，再解释。Name the three signals first, then explain each.

① 说出自动重传请求用到的三个东西（机制或信号），并说明各自的作用。1. State three things used by ARQ (mechanisms or signals) and say what each does.

② 为什么网上银行的页面地址要用加密的方式传输数据？2. Why does online banking send its data encrypted?

点开看答案Show answer

① 确认（收到且无错，通知继续）；否定确认（发现有错，要求重发）；超时计时器（发出后开始计时，规定时间内没有任何回应就自动重发）。三者配合，保证数据可靠送达。1. An acknowledgement (received correctly, carry on); a negative acknowledgement (error found, please resend); a timeout timer (counts down after sending and triggers automatic resending if nothing comes back). Together they guarantee reliable delivery.

② 因为数据要穿过公共网络和多台中间设备，可能被窃听截获；加密后即使被拿到，没有密钥也解不开，账号和密码不会被读出来。2. The data crosses a public network and many intermediate devices where it could be intercepted; once encrypted it cannot be unlocked without the key, so account numbers and passwords stay unreadable.

— 第 7 站完成 —— Stop 7 done —

单元 2 完成 · 主线：切成包 → 各自选路走过去 → 发现错了就重发Unit 2 done · the thread: chop into packets → send them on their way → detect damage and resend

回头看这一单元其实就四个问题：① 数据怎么打包（头部、数据、尾部）；② 怎么走（串行还是并行、同步还是异步、能不能双向）；③ 从哪个口出去（通用串行总线）；④ 路上出错怎么办（四种查错方法 + 自动重传 + 加密）。解释题的答案其实都很短，关键是每个名词后面跟一句解释。In hindsight this unit asks four things: 1. how is data packaged (header, payload, trailer); 2. how does it travel (serial or parallel, synchronous or asynchronous, which direction); 3. through which port (USB); 4. what happens when it goes wrong (four detection methods, plus ARQ and encryption). Exam answers here are short — the trick is to add one explanatory clause after every term.

Stop 3 matters most: it appears in the most exam questions.

1 计算机系统与冯·诺依曼结构Computer systems and Von Neumann architecture Architecture体系结构
这一站要会的：说出计算机系统的主要部件；说出冯·诺依曼结构的两个关键特点；说出三种总线各自送什么。 Goal: name the main parts of a computer system; state two key features of the Von Neumann architecture; say what each of the three buses carries.
说人话：一台完整的计算机系统就是四样东西加一根总线：处理器（干活的大脑）、主存（正在用的东西放这里）、输入设备（把外面的消息送进来）、输出设备（把结果送出去），全部挂在同一组总线上互相传东西。冯·诺依曼结构说的就是两件事：① 程序和数据住在同一个内存里——你写的程序和它要处理的数据，在内存里没有区别地排着队，都按地址存放；② 处理器按照地址一条一条把指令取出来执行，默认从上往下走，遇到跳转指令才换地方。这样的好处是结构简单灵活（想换个程序？把内存里的内容换掉就行，不用改硬件）；代价是数据和指令要抢同一条总线，一次只能拿一样。 In plain words: a complete computer system is four things plus a bus: the processor (the brain that works), main memory (holds what is being used right now), input devices (bring messages in) and output devices (send results out), all attached to one set of buses. The Von Neumann architecture makes two statements: 1. programs and data live in the same memory — your program and the data it handles sit in memory side by side, stored by address, with nothing to tell them apart except how they are used; 2. the processor fetches instructions one at a time by address, moving onwards unless an instruction tells it to jump. That is simple and flexible (want a different program? just change the contents of memory — no rewiring), but data and instructions must share one bus, so only one can cross at a time.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 主要部件：处理器、主存、输入设备、输出设备（还有后面要讲的二级存储）。Main parts: processor, main memory, input devices, output devices (plus secondary storage, later).
  • 冯·诺依曼的两个特点：① 存储程序——指令和数据存放在同一个主存里；② 处理器按顺序按地址取指令执行。Two features of Von Neumann: 1. the stored program concept — instructions and data share one main memory; 2. the processor fetches and executes in order, by address.
  • 地址总线：传送要访问的内存地址，单向（处理器 → 内存）。The address bus carries the address of the location to be accessed and is one-way (processor → memory).
  • 数据总线：传真正的数据，双向（可以读也可以写）。The data bus carries the actual data and is two-way (read and write).
  • 控制总线：传控制信号和时序信号，例如读 / 写、时钟、中断、复位。The control bus carries control and timing signals such as read/write, clock, interrupt and reset.
  • 好处 / 代价：换程序只要换内存内容，不用改硬件；代价是共享一条总线，一次只能取指令或数据。Benefit and cost: changing the program only means changing memory contents, not hardware; the cost is sharing one bus so only one item crosses at a time.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：题目问「说出冯·诺依曼结构的两个特点」，写两条就够了。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: for "state two features of the Von Neumann architecture" two lines are enough. Instructions and data are stored together in the same main memory, each with its own address. The processor fetches instructions from memory, one at a time and in sequence, decodes them and executes them.

例题 · 结构理解Example · understanding the structure

(a) 说出冯·诺依曼结构里「存储程序」是什么意思。
(b) 内存地址 500 里放的一串二进制，处理器怎么知道它是一条指令还是一个数字？
(c) 举例说明控制总线在这一过程中起什么作用。 (a) What does "stored program" mean in the Von Neumann architecture?
(b) How does the processor know whether the bits at address 500 are an instruction or a number?
(c) Give an example of what the control bus does during this.
(a) 意思是：要执行的程序自己就存放在主存里，和数据放在一起、都用地址编号；要换个任务只要把内存换个内容，不用改硬件。(a) It means the program to be run is itself stored in main memory, alongside data and addressed in the same way; changing the task means changing memory contents, not rewiring. (b) 光看内容分不出来——内存里只有 0 和 1。区别在于处理器怎么用它：被程序计数器指到、取到指令寄存器里的，就当成指令译码；被指令当成地址去取出来的，就当成数据处理。(b) You cannot tell from the bits alone — memory holds nothing but 0s and 1s. It depends on how the processor uses them: anything pointed at by the program counter and fetched into the instruction register is decoded as an instruction; anything fetched because an instruction asked for it is treated as data. (c) 处理器要在控制总线上发出读信号（告诉内存这一次我要读，不是写），顺便用时钟信号让所有部件按同一个节拍动作。(c) The processor sends a read signal on the control bus (saying "this is a read, not a write") together with clock signals that keep every part in step.

练习Practice

总线「单向 / 双向」是常考的送分点。Whether each bus is one-way or two-way is a classic easy mark.

① 说出三种总线的名字，各送什么。1. Name the three buses and say what each carries.

② 地址总线是单向的，数据总线是双向的，各说明一句原因。2. Explain why the address bus is one-way while the data bus is two-way.

点开看答案Show answer

① 地址总线：送要访问的内存地址。数据总线：送真正的数据（或指令）。控制总线：送读 / 写、时钟、中断、复位等控制信号。1. The address bus carries the address of the location to access. The data bus carries the actual data (or instruction). The control bus carries signals such as read/write, clock, interrupt and reset.

② 地址永远由处理器决定「去哪里」，内存不会反过来给处理器下地址，所以地址总线单向；数据既要从内存读到处理器，也要从处理器写回内存，所以数据总线必须双向。2. The processor alone decides which address to access and memory never sends an address back, so the address bus is one-way. Data must travel into the processor and back out to memory, so the data bus has to be two-way.

— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 处理器内部：运算单元、控制单元、寄存器、缓存Inside the CPU: ALU, control unit, registers and cache Registers寄存器
这一站要会的：说出运算单元和控制单元各干什么；说出程序计数器、地址寄存器、数据寄存器、指令寄存器、累加器各自存什么；说出缓存、时钟频率、多核的作用。 Goal: state what the ALU and control unit do; say what PC, MAR, MDR, CIR and ACC each hold; explain cache, clock speed and multiple cores.
说人话：处理器里面其实只有两拨人：控制单元是总管，它负责发命令、看指令、安排谁做什么（相当于指挥）；算术逻辑单元是干活的师傅，加减乘除、比较大小、与或非这些活都它包了。干活的时候总得有地方临时放东西，这些就在处理器芯片上一排极小的寄存器——速度比主存快 N 倍，每个都有明确分工：程序计数器记着「下一条指令放在内存哪个地址」；地址寄存器记着「这次要读写的内存地址」；数据寄存器暂存「刚取回来或正要写进去的数据」；指令寄存器放着「正在处理的那条指令」；累加器是运算结果的落脚点。再外面还有缓存：一小块又快又贵的存储器，把最近常用的指令和数据事先搬进来，处理器就不用每次都去等主存。时钟频率是节拍器：每秒响几下（单位赫兹），处理器每个节拍做一小步，所以频率越高、单位时间内做的步数越多。多核就是在同一个芯片里放好几个完整的处理中心，它们可以同时干不同的活——前提是软件能把任务拆开。 In plain words: inside a processor there are really only two groups. The control unit is the manager: it issues control signals, reads instructions and decides what each part should do. The arithmetic and logic unit (ALU) is the worker: addition, subtraction, comparisons, AND/OR/NOT — all its job. Work needs somewhere to keep things momentarily, and that job falls to the tiny registers on the chip itself — far faster than main memory, each with a clear role. The program counter keeps the address of the next instruction; the memory address register holds the address being read or written; the memory data register holds the value just fetched or about to be written; the current instruction register holds the instruction being decoded; the accumulator is where results land. Outside them sits cache: a small, expensive, very fast store holding whatever is likely to be needed next, so the processor rarely waits for slow memory. The clock provides the beat — pulses per second, measured in hertz — and each pulse lets the processor take one small step, so a higher frequency means more steps per second. Multiple cores put several complete processing units on one chip so different jobs run at once, provided the software can be split up.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 算术逻辑单元（ALU）：完成算术运算（加减乘除）和逻辑运算（与、或、非、比较大小）。The ALU performs arithmetic (add, subtract, multiply, divide) and logic operations (AND, OR, NOT, comparisons).
  • 控制单元（CU）：发送控制信号指挥各部件，负责译码并协调取指、译码、执行的整个过程。The control unit sends control signals to every part, decodes instructions and coordinates the whole fetch-decode-execute cycle.
  • 程序计数器（PC）：存放下一条要执行的指令在主存中的地址。The program counter holds the address in main memory of the next instruction to be executed.
  • 内存地址寄存器（MAR）：存放这次要读或写的内存单元的地址。The MAR holds the address of the memory location being read from or written to.
  • 内存数据寄存器（MDR）：暂存刚从内存读出、或正要写入内存的数据。The MDR temporarily holds data read from, or about to be written to, memory.
  • 当前指令寄存器（CIR）：存放刚取出来、正在被译码和执行的那条指令。The CIR holds the instruction currently being decoded and executed.
  • 累加器（ACC）：存放算术逻辑单元运算的结果（中间结果也放在这里）。The accumulator holds the results produced by the ALU, including intermediate results.
  • 缓存（cache）：处理器和主存之间又小又快的存储器，存放近期常用的指令和数据；命中缓存就不用去访问主存，速度明显加快。缓存越大命中率越高，但价格也越贵。Cache is small, fast storage between the processor and main memory holding recently used instructions and data; a cache hit avoids a slow memory access, so programs run faster. Larger caches mean more hits but cost more.
  • 时钟频率（clock speed）：每秒的时钟脉冲次数，单位赫兹；频率越高，每秒能执行的步骤越多，但不是唯一决定因素（缓存、架构、核心数都很要紧）。Clock speed is the number of clock pulses per second, measured in hertz; higher means more steps per second, but it is not the only factor (cache, architecture and core count matter too).
  • 多核（multi-core）：一个芯片上有多个独立核心，真正同时执行多条指令流；适合多任务，但软件要能被拆成并行任务才有效果。Multi-core means several independent cores on one chip executing instruction streams truly in parallel; good for multitasking, but only if software can be split into parallel tasks.
最容易丢分的地方：寄存器名字一定要连着它装的东西一起记，而且分清楚地址和内容：地址寄存器装的是「门牌号」，数据寄存器装的才是「屋里的人」。题目问「哪里放着下一条指令的地址」——答程序计数器，不是指令寄存器。 Classic trap: learn each register with its contents, and keep address apart from contents: the MAR holds the house number, the MDR holds whoever lives there. "Where is the address of the next instruction stored?" — the program counter, not the instruction register.

例题 · 谁负责什么Example · who does what

(a) 处理器要把内存地址 2000 里的值取回来。写出这一步用到的两个寄存器，各自装什么。
(b) 一条加法指令执行完，结果应该出现在哪里？
(c) 说明缓存为什么能加快速度。 (a) The processor must fetch the value at address 2000. Name the two registers involved and what each holds.
(b) Where does the result of an addition instruction end up?
(c) Explain why cache makes the system faster.
(a) 地址 2000 放进地址寄存器；从内存中读出来的那个值放进数据寄存器。注意：地址寄存器装的是地址，数据寄存器装的是内容。(a) The address 2000 goes into the MAR; the value read out goes into the MDR. Note the MAR holds the address, the MDR the contents. (b) 算术逻辑单元算出的结果先留在累加器里，需要时再写回主存。(b) The ALU result stays in the accumulator and is written back to main memory later if needed. (c) 缓存是处理器芯片附近一块又快又小的存储区，把马上要用的指令和数据先搬进来。处理器要取的东西已经在缓存里（命中）时，就不必等慢得多的主存，等待时间大幅减少。(c) Cache is a small, fast store next to the processor holding the instructions and data about to be used. When the wanted item is already in cache (a hit), the processor avoids waiting for much slower main memory, cutting the delay dramatically.

练习Practice

每个寄存器配一句「它装着什么」。Pair every register with one sentence about what it holds.

① 程序计数器和指令寄存器装的分别是什么？有什么不一样？1. What do the program counter and the current instruction register each hold? How do they differ?

② 为什么时钟频率更高的处理器不一定真的更快？说出两个别的影响因素。2. Why is a higher clock speed not a guarantee of a faster processor? Give two other factors.

点开看答案Show answer

① 程序计数器装的是下一条指令的地址（一个门牌号）；指令寄存器装的是已经被取出来、正在处理的那条指令本身（屋子里的东西）。一个是地址，一个是指令内容。1. The program counter holds the address of the next instruction (a house number); the current instruction register holds the instruction itself once fetched and being processed (what is inside the house). One is an address, the other is the instruction.

② 还有缓存容量（命中率高就少等主存）、核心数量（能同时干几个任务）、以及架构设计。说白了，频率只看「每秒多少拍」，还得看「每拍干多少活」。2. Other factors are cache size (more hits, less waiting), number of cores (how many tasks run side by side) and the architecture (how much work is done per clock cycle). Frequency only tells you the beat; what matters is how much gets done per beat.

— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 取指、译码、执行周期The fetch-decode-execute cycle F-D-E cycle取指译码执行
这一站要会的：按顺序说出周期的三个阶段；每一步说出涉及哪几个寄存器、里面装什么；说出每一步之后哪些寄存器发生了变化。 Goal: state the three stages in order; for each step name the registers involved and what they hold; say which registers change afterwards.
说人话：处理器做的事惊人地单调——取一条指令，看懂它，执行它，再取下一条，从开机转到关机。把它想象成照着菜谱做菜：取指就是伸手去拿食谱下一行（程序计数器告诉你去哪一行拿，看完这一行后自动把书签往下一行挪一格）；译码就是看懂这行写的是「把鸡蛋打进碗里」而不是先去数它有几个字；执行就是真的去做这件事。整个过程中，处理器每次干活都先问内存要地址，再拿回内容——所以地址寄存器和数据寄存器几乎每步都要出场。还有一个容易忘的细节：程序计数器是在取指时就加 1 的，所以它永远指着「下一条」，等你这条干完，它早就准备好了。 In plain words: what the processor does is astonishingly repetitive — fetch an instruction, understand it, carry it out, fetch the next, from power-on to power-off. Think of cooking from a recipe: fetch is reaching for the next line (the program counter tells you the line number, and once you have read it the bookmark moves down one line automatically); decode is understanding the line says "crack an egg into the bowl"; execute is actually doing it. Through all this the processor always asks memory for an address first, then receives the contents, which is why the MAR and MDR appear at nearly every step. One easy-to-forget detail: the program counter is incremented during the fetch itself, so it always points at "the next one" and is ready before the current instruction finishes.
记住这几点（逐步流程，建议背下来）：Key points (learn this sequence):
  • 取指 Fetch：① 程序计数器里的地址被复制到地址寄存器；② 程序计数器加 1；③ 控制单元在控制总线上发读信号，该地址单元的内容经数据总线送进数据寄存器；④ 数据寄存器的内容被复制到指令寄存器。Fetch: 1. the address in the PC is copied to the MAR; 2. the PC is incremented; 3. the control unit sends a read signal and the contents of that location arrive on the data bus into the MDR; 4. the MDR contents are copied to the CIR.
  • 译码 Decode：控制单元把指令寄存器里的指令拆成操作码（要做什么）和操作数（数据或者数据所在的地址），并据此发出控制信号。Decode: the control unit splits the instruction in the CIR into an opcode (what to do) and an operand (the data, or the address of it), then issues control signals accordingly.
  • 执行 Execute：如果还要取数据，把地址放进地址寄存器再读进数据寄存器；运算时把数据送进算术逻辑单元，结果留在累加器，需要时再写回主存。Execute: if data is needed its address goes into the MAR and the value is read into the MDR; during calculation the operands go to the ALU and the result stays in the accumulator, later written back to memory if required.
  • 然后循环：回到取指，重复执行。跳转指令会改写程序计数器，于是下一条就不按顺序来了。Then repeat: back to fetch. A jump instruction overwrites the program counter, so the next instruction is taken out of sequence.
  • 常考结论：程序计数器存的是地址；地址寄存器存地址；数据寄存器存数据；指令寄存器存指令；累加器存结果。The line examiners love: the PC holds an address; the MAR holds an address; the MDR holds data; the CIR holds the instruction; the accumulator holds the result.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：这道题基本是必考的六分题，把下面这段流程背熟，考试时按顺序写出来就行。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: this six-mark question几乎 appears every year — learn this passage and write it out in order. The address of the next instruction is copied from the program counter to the memory address register. The program counter is then incremented. The instruction at that address is sent along the data bus and stored in the memory data register, then copied to the current instruction register. The control unit decodes the instruction. Finally the instruction is executed: further data is fetched if needed, the ALU performs the operation and the result is stored in the accumulator.

例题 · 完整走一遍Example · walking the whole cycle

假设程序计数器里的值是 300。请描述接下来「取指」这一步的完整过程，写一个寄存器也不漏。
之后执行的是一条加法指令：把内存单元 800 里的值加到累加器上，说明还要用到哪些寄存器。 The program counter contains 300. Describe the complete fetch step without missing a register. The instruction turns out to be "add the value stored at memory location 800 to the accumulator" — which other registers are then used?
① 地址 300 从程序计数器复制到地址寄存器1. The address 300 is copied from the PC to the MAR ② 程序计数器加 1，变成 301，指向下一条指令2. The PC is incremented to 301, pointing at the next instruction ③ 控制单元发读信号；地址 300 里的内容经数据总线送进数据寄存器3. The control unit sends a read signal; the contents of location 300 travel on the data bus into the MDR ④ 这条指令从数据寄存器复制到指令寄存器，等待译码4. The instruction is copied from the MDR to the CIR ready for decoding ⑤ 执行加法：操作数地址 800 放进地址寄存器，读出来的值放进数据寄存器，然后送进算术逻辑单元与累加器里原来的值相加，结果回到累加器5. To add: the operand address 800 goes into the MAR, the value read arrives in the MDR, then goes to the ALU to be added to the existing accumulator contents, with the result returning to the accumulator

练习Practice

先在草稿纸上画四个小方块：程序计数器、地址寄存器、数据寄存器、指令寄存器。Sketch four little boxes first: PC, MAR, MDR, CIR.

① 说出「取指」阶段的四个动作，每个都要提到寄存器。1. State the four actions of the fetch stage, mentioning a register in each.

② 为什么程序计数器要在取指时就加 1，而不是等这条指令执行完再加？2. Why is the program counter incremented during the fetch rather than after the instruction finishes?

点开看答案Show answer

① ① 地址从程序计数器复制到地址寄存器；② 程序计数器加 1；③ 选中单元的内容经数据总线送到数据寄存器；④ 内容从数据寄存器复制到指令寄存器。1. 1. the address is copied from the PC to the MAR; 2. the PC is incremented; 3. the contents of the selected location travel along the data bus to the MDR; 4. the contents are copied from the MDR to the CIR.

② 因为这样它就能永远指着下一条：这条指令一执行完，处理器立刻知道去哪取下一条，中间不用停顿。如果一条跳转指令要改变流向，它只要在执行阶段把程序计数器改写成新的地址就行。2. Incremented early, it always points at the next instruction, so the moment the current one finishes the processor already knows where to go with no pause. If a jump instruction needs to change direction, it simply overwrites the PC with the new address during the execute stage.

— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 主存：随机存储器、只读存储器与虚拟内存Primary memory: RAM, ROM and virtual memory RAM / ROM随机存储器 / 只读存储器
这一站要会的：比较随机存储器和只读存储器；说出为什么需要只读存储器；说出什么是虚拟内存、它有什么代价。 Goal: compare RAM with ROM; say why ROM is needed; explain virtual memory and its cost.
说人话：主存分两种。随机存储器是我们的工作台：程序运行时把自己搬到这里，处理的数据也放这里，CPU 可以直接读写，速度飞快；缺点是一断电就像黑板被擦干净——所以它叫易失存储器。既然断电什么都留不下，那开机第一件事怎么办？总得有个东西告诉电脑「先检查内存、再找硬盘、最后把操作系统请进来」吧，这就是只读存储器里放的启动程序（也叫引导程序）：它被固化在芯片里，断电也丢不了，一上电就能最先运行。
还有个情况：主存不够用了怎么办？虚拟内存——把硬盘的一部分先假装成内存，把暂时用不到的数据搬过去存着，要用时再搬回来。代价很明显：硬盘（即便是固态硬盘）比主存慢得多，机械硬盘还要等盘片转到位，所以来回一搬，机器肉眼可见地卡。 In plain words: there are two kinds of primary memory. RAM is the workbench: a running program loads itself there together with its data, and the CPU can read and write it directly at great speed. Its weakness is that a power cut wipes it like a clean board — hence "volatile". If nothing survives power-off, how does a computer start? Something must tell it "check the memory, find the disk, then load the operating system", and that something is the bootstrap program held in ROM: burnt into the chip, non-volatile, and therefore running first the instant power arrives.
Then there is what happens when RAM runs out: virtual memory. Part of the disk pretends to be memory, temporarily unused data is moved out, and brought back when needed. The cost is obvious: a disk (even an SSD) is far slower than RAM, and a mechanical disk must also wait for the platter to rotate into position, so all that swapping makes the machine visibly sluggish.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 随机存储器（RAM）：可读可写；易失（断电内容丢失）；存放正在运行的程序和数据，处理器可以按地址直接读写，速度很快；容量越大能同时跑的程序越多。RAM: read/write; volatile (contents lost when power is removed); holds the programs and data currently in use; more RAM means more programs can run at once.
  • 只读存储器（ROM）：只能读出（一般情况下不能改写）；非易失（断电内容保留）；存放开机自检和启动（引导）程序，以及嵌入式设备的固件。ROM: read-only in normal use; non-volatile; holds the bootstrap/start-up program (and firmware in embedded devices).
  • 虚拟内存（virtual memory）：当主存不够时，把二级存储的一部分当作主存使用；数据在主存和磁盘之间换入换出（分页）。Virtual memory: when RAM is full, part of secondary storage is used as if it were main memory, with data swapped in and out (paging).
  • 为什么变慢：二级存储的读写速度远低于主存，而且搬运本身也要占用处理器和总线时间；频繁到几乎停住的现象叫「抖动」。Why it slows: secondary storage is much slower than RAM and the swapping itself consumes processor and bus time; extreme cases where the system almost stops are called thrashing.
  • 好处：能运行比实际主存更大的程序，多个程序同时开也不至于直接崩溃。Benefit: programs larger than physical RAM can still run, and many applications can be open without the system failing outright.

例题 · 三个场景各一句解释Example · three situations

(a) 为什么电脑一断电，刚写完没保存的文档就没了，而下次开机还能正常启动？
(b) 一台主存为 4 GiB 的电脑要打开总共需要 6 GiB 的程序，会发生什么？为什么明显变慢？
(c) 说出三点随机存储器和只读存储器的区别。 (a) Why does unsaved work vanish at a power cut, yet the computer still starts up next time?
(b) A computer with 4 GiB of RAM opens programs needing 6 GiB in total. What happens, and why is it much slower?
(c) State three differences between RAM and ROM.
(a) 文档在随机存储器里，一断电就清空；而启动流程写在只读存储器里，断电不丢，所以还能照常开机。(a) The document sat in RAM, which is wiped when power goes; the start-up routine lives in ROM, which survives, so booting still works. (b) 操作系统会启用虚拟内存：把暂时用不到的部分搬到硬盘上，用到时再换回来。由于硬盘比主存慢得多，加上搬运本身也占用时间，所以机器会明显变慢，甚至接近卡死。(b) The operating system turns to virtual memory: whatever is not needed for the moment is moved to disk and brought back on demand. Because the disk is far slower than RAM and the swapping itself costs time, everything slows down noticeably and can nearly stall. (c) ① 能否写入：随机存储器可读写，只读存储器只能读；② 易失性：随机存储器断电丢失，只读存储器断电保留；③ 装什么：随机存储器放正在运行的程序和数据，只读存储器放开机自检与启动程序。(c) 1. Writable? RAM can be written to, ROM generally cannot. 2. Volatility: RAM loses its contents at power-off, ROM keeps them. 3. Contents: RAM holds running programs and data, ROM holds the start-up and bootstrap routines.

练习Practice

三个维度：能不能写、断电丢不丢、里面装什么。Think along three axes: can it be written, does it survive power-off, what is inside.

① 为什么必须有只读存储器？如果只有随机存储器会怎么样？1. Why is ROM essential? What would happen with RAM alone?

② 说出使用虚拟内存的两个缺点。2. State two disadvantages of using virtual memory.

点开看答案Show answer

① 随机存储器断电就没有内容了，刚上电时里面空空的，处理器无处下手。只读存储器里固化着开机自检和引导程序，上电后第一条指令从这里来，才能把操作系统从硬盘请进主存——没有它电脑根本起不来。1. RAM is empty after power-off, so at start-up the processor has nowhere to begin. ROM holds the permanently written start-up and bootstrap program, supplying the first instruction that loads the operating system from disk — without it nothing would boot.

② ① 速度慢：硬盘读写比主存慢得多，来回搬运要花大量时间；② 频繁换页会让机器几乎停住（抖动），还要占用硬盘空间并增加读写损耗。2. 1. Slower: disk access is far slower than RAM and swapping costs a great deal of time. 2. Heavy paging can almost stop the machine (thrashing); it also consumes disk space and adds wear.

— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 二级存储六种方式大对比Comparing secondary storage Storage存储设备
这一站要会的：说出二级存储的作用；比较六种常见存储方式的容量、速度、便携性、耐用性和成本；会根据场景挑合适的方案。 Goal: state the purpose of secondary storage; compare six common media on capacity, speed, portability, durability and cost; choose the right one for a scenario.
说人话：主存又快又好用，可惜一断电就清空，而且容量有限还贵。所以还需要第二种存储——二级存储：断电内容照样在（非易失）、容量大、每 GB 便宜，专门用来长期保存文件。怎么挑？记住三个取舍：速度（固态硬盘最快，磁带因为只能顺序读取所以最慢）、便携（优盘、存储卡、光盘随手一装就是）、省钱（数据量越大，磁带和机械硬盘越划算；云存储看着便宜，但长期订阅要一直付钱，而且离不开网络）。 In plain words: main memory is fast and convenient, but it is wiped when the power goes, limited in size and expensive. So we need a second kind: secondary storage, which keeps its contents without power (non-volatile), offers lots of space and costs little per gigabyte — purpose-built for keeping files long term. How to choose? Remember three trade-offs: speed (SSDs are fastest, tape is slowest because it can only be read in sequence), portability (flash drives, memory cards and discs just slip into a pocket) and cost (for huge amounts of data, tape and hard disks win; cloud storage looks cheap but subscriptions add up and nothing works without the network).
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 二级存储的作用：长期保存程序和数据，非易失、容量大、便宜，断电不丢。Purpose of secondary storage: long-term storage of programs and data; non-volatile, large capacity, cheap, survives power-off.
  • 机械硬盘（HDD）：磁性盘片旋转 + 移动磁头；有机械延迟、怕震动、有噪音；容量大、每 GB 成本最低之一。HDD: spinning magnetic platters with a moving head; suffers mechanical delay, dislikes knocks, makes noise; large capacity, among the lowest cost per GB.
  • 固态硬盘（SSD）：纯闪存芯片，无活动部件；读写快、安静、抗震、省电；每 GB 更贵，且写入次数有限。SSD: pure flash chips with no moving parts; fast, silent, shock-resistant and low power; dearer per GB with a limited number of writes.
  • 光盘（CD/DVD/蓝光）：激光读取盘面上的凹坑和平地；便宜、轻薄好分发；读写慢、表面容易被划伤。Optical disc: a laser reads pits and lands on the surface; cheap, light and easy to hand out; slow to read and write, and easily scratched.
  • 优盘 / 存储卡：闪存 + 小巧 + 即插即用；抗震但不耐粗暴插拔，容易丢。Flash drive / memory card: flash memory, tiny, plug and play; shock-resistant but the connectors can snap and they are easy to lose.
  • 磁带：顺序访问（要找到中间一段必须一路绕过去），读得慢；容量极大、单位成本最低、适合长期归档备份。Magnetic tape: serial/sequential access (to reach the middle you wind through everything before it), so it is slow to read; enormous capacity, lowest cost per unit, ideal for long-term archival backup.
  • 云存储：数据放在远程数据中心，通过网络访问；容量几乎无限、随时取用、便于共享和异地备份；依赖网络，速度受带宽限制，还有隐私和安全方面的顾虑。Cloud storage: data lives in a remote data centre accessed over the network; capacity is effectively unlimited, always available, easy to share and good for off-site backup; depends on the network, limited by bandwidth, with privacy and security concerns.
