这张表建议抄一遍：横向记「类型」，纵向按「容量、速度、便携、耐用、成本」五列背关键词就够了。Copy this table once: learn the type down the side and the five comparison words along the top.

🗣 考场英文怎么说：「说明理由」的题一定要落到具体特点上，不能直接说「因为它好」。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: for "justify your choice" questions, always land on a concrete feature rather than saying "it is better". An SSD would be used because it has no moving parts, so it is shock-resistant and suitable for a portable device, and it has a much faster data access time than a hard disk, which helps when filming high-resolution video.

例题 · 挑合适的存储Example · choosing storage

(a) 摄影师要在高原野外拍大量高清视频，随身设备里的存储该选什么？给两个理由。
(b) 医院要把十年内几乎不再查阅的病历档案长期保存，数据量很大，该选什么？给两个理由。
(c) 按 1 GB = 1000 MB 估算，把 25 GB 的数据以平均 100 MB 每秒的速度写入，需要多久？ (a) A cameraman shoots a lot of high-definition video in remote mountains. Which storage for his portable equipment? Give two reasons.
(b) A hospital must keep ten years of records it rarely looks at, a very large amount of data. Which storage? Give two reasons.
(c) Using 1 GB = 1000 MB, how long does writing 25 GB take at an average of 100 MB per second?
(a) 选固态硬盘或高速存储卡：① 没有活动部件，抗震，经得起野外颠簸搬运；② 读写速度快，扛得住高清视频这种大数据流量。(a) An SSD or a fast memory card: 1. no moving parts, so it survives knocks and rough transport; 2. fast read/write, which suits the huge data rate of high-definition video. (b) 选磁带（或磁带 + 云备份）：① 数据量极大时单位成本最低；② 适合长期归档，保存寿命长，也不用天天通电。缺点是要找到某一段内容会很慢，但「几乎不查阅」正好可以接受。(b) Magnetic tape (perhaps with a cloud backup): 1. the lowest cost per unit at this scale; 2. built for long-term archiving with a long storage life and no need to keep powered. The slow search is acceptable precisely because the records are rarely consulted. (c) 25 GB = 25 000 MB；25 000 ÷ 100 = 250 秒，也就是 4 分 10 秒（这是理想平均值，实际还要再慢些）。(c) 25 GB = 25 000 MB; 25 000 ÷ 100 = 250 seconds, i.e. 4 minutes 10 seconds at this ideal average — reality is slower.

练习Practice

先说选什么，再给两个「因为」。Say what you would choose, then give two "because" clauses.

① 把一台老笔记本的机械硬盘换成固态硬盘，会带来哪些好处和不好的地方？1. What are the advantages and disadvantages of replacing an old laptop's hard disk with an SSD?

② 一家设计公司要给分散在各个城市的同事共享大文件，用哪一种存储最合适？说明理由。2. A design firm wants to share large files between staff in different cities. Which storage suits best? Justify it.

点开看答案Show answer

① 好处：开机和打开软件明显变快（存取时间短）；没有活动部件，更安静、更抗震、更省电、发热更低。不好：每 GB 价格更高，同样的钱买到的容量更小；每个存储单元的写入次数有限，寿命有限度。1. Advantages: booting and launching programs get much faster (short access time); no moving parts means quieter, shock-resistant, lower power and cooler running. Disadvantages: higher cost per GB so less space for the money, and each memory cell has a limited number of write cycles, giving a finite life.

② 云存储最合适：大家在不同城市，只要联网就能随时取用同一个文件；容量可以按需要伸缩，不用每个人各拷贝一份；服务商还负责备份和维护。（也可以答：配合版本管理，避免来回收附件。）2. Cloud storage fits best: staff in different cities reach the same file whenever they have a connection; capacity grows as needed and nobody keeps a separate copy; the provider handles backup and maintenance. (Bonus: version control beats emailing attachments around.)

6 输入设备Input devices Sensors传感器
这一站要会的：说出六种以上输入设备并给出应用场景；说出光学字符识别、模数转换各自是什么意思；说明为什么传感器也算输入设备。 Goal: name at least six input devices with uses; explain OCR and analogue-to-digital conversion; say why sensors count as input devices.
说人话：输入设备的共同点是：把外界的信息变成机器能处理的电信号，最后变成二进制。键盘按下就发出一个字符编号；鼠标和触控板报告「往哪个方向动了多少」；摄像头把光变成一个个像素的数值；麦克风量的是空气压力的变化——这些物理量本来是连续变化的（模拟信号），所以必须经过模数转换才能被计算机接受。扫描仪把纸面变成一张大图片，再用光学字符识别软件把图片里的字认出来变成能编辑的文本。条码 / 二维码扫描器读的是黑白条纹反射光的差异，解出里面那一串编号。触摸屏既是输入也是输出：它显示画面的同时，用手指的位置当输入。最后一类很容易被忽略——传感器（温度、光线、湿度、压力、距离、运动……）同样是「把现实世界的量送进来」，所以它也是输入设备。 In plain words: every input device does the same thing: turn outside information into electrical signals and finally into binary. A keyboard emits a character code when a key goes down; a mouse or trackpad reports "moved this far in that direction"; a camera converts light into pixel values; a microphone measures changing air pressure. Those physical quantities vary continuously (analogue), so they must pass through analogue-to-digital conversion before a computer can use them. A scanner turns paper into one big image, and then optical character recognition (OCR) software reads the characters out of that image as editable text. A barcode or QR scanner reads the light reflected from black and white stripes and decodes the number behind them. A touch screen is input and output at once: it shows a picture and takes the finger position as input. The last group is easy to overlook — sensors (temperature, light, humidity, pressure, distance, motion) also "bring measurements of the real world inside", so they count as input devices too.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 键盘／鼠标／触控板：人手敲进去；键盘适合输入文字，鼠标适合指点位置。Keyboard / mouse / trackpad: direct human input — a keyboard for text, a mouse for pointing.
  • 扫描仪+光学字符识别（OCR）：先把纸面扫成图像，再把图像里的字认出来变成可编辑文本（省得重新打字）。Scanner + OCR: the page becomes an image, then the characters in the image are recognised as editable text instead of retyping.
  • 麦克风、摄像头：采集连续的声音、图像，必须经过模数转换（ADC）变成数字数据。Microphone and camera: capture continuous sound or light, which must be converted from analogue to digital (ADC).
  • 触摸屏：既是输入也是输出设备；常见于手机、自助取票机。Touch screen: input and output in one; found in phones and ticket machines.
  • 条码扫描器 / 二维码扫描器：读出黑白图案代表的编号，再用这个编号去数据库查价格、库存等信息。Barcode / QR scanner: reads the pattern as a number, which is then looked up in a database for price, stock and so on.
  • 传感器：自动测量现实世界的物理量，也要经过模数转换；常用于自动控制系统（温室控温、路灯感光开关、手机自动转屏）。Sensors: measure real-world quantities automatically, again via ADC; heavily used in automatic control systems (greenhouses, street lights that react to darkness, screen rotation).
容易丢分的地方：题目问「触摸屏属于哪一类设备」，标准答案是「既是输入也是输出设备」——只看它显示画面就答「输出」会漏一半分。另外「麦克风需要模数转换」这句一定要会写。 Classic trap: asked "what kind of device is a touch screen?" the expected answer is "both input and output" — calling it an output device alone loses half the marks. Also learn to write that a microphone needs analogue-to-digital conversion.

例题 · 场景串起来Example · a full scenario

在超市结账时，收银员用条码扫描器扫商品。
(a) 说明从扫码到显示价格，中间涉及哪些环节。
(b) 为什么条码扫描器属于输入设备？
(c) 收银机还要用模数转换吗？说明原因。 At a supermarket checkout the cashier scans each item.
(a) Describe the chain from the scan to the price appearing.
(b) Why is the scanner an input device?
(c) Does the till need analogue-to-digital conversion here? Explain.
(a) 扫描器的光照射条码 → 黑条和白隙反射的光强弱不同 → 变成一串数字编号送给计算机 → 计算机用这个编号去数据库里查价格和商品名 → 显示在屏幕上并计入总价。(a) The scanner shines light on the barcode; black bars and white gaps reflect different amounts of light, giving a stream of digits sent to the computer; the computer looks that number up in a database for the price and name, shows them on screen and adds them to the total. (b) 因为它把外界的信息（条码图案）送进计算机，转换成计算机能处理的数据，方向是「外部 → 主机」，所以是输入设备。(b) It takes information from outside (the barcode pattern) into the computer as data — the direction is "outside → machine" — which makes it an input device. (c) 条码这一步不需要：反射光的明暗本身就是可以直接区分成 0 和 1 的数字式信号。需要的场景是麦克风、摄像头、各类传感器——它们产生的是连续变化的模拟信号。(c) Not for the barcode itself: light and dark resolve directly into 0s and 1s. It is needed for microphones, cameras and sensors, all of which produce continuously varying analogue signals.

练习Practice

每个设备最好配一个具体场景。Pair every device with a concrete situation.

① 说出三种输入设备，各给一个应用场景。1. Name three input devices and give a use case for each.

② 为什么传感器也算输入设备？举两个例子说明。2. Why is a sensor classified as an input device? Give two examples.

点开看答案Show answer

① 例：键盘（在文档里打字）；鼠标（在游戏里瞄准目标）；摄像头（视频通话）；条码扫描器（超市结账）；麦克风（语音输入）。写出任意三个加场景即可。1. For example: keyboard (typing a document); mouse (aiming in a game); camera (video call); barcode scanner (supermarket checkout); microphone (voice input). Any three with a scenario.

② 因为传感器也是把现实世界的量送进计算机：它的读数经过模数转换后变成计算机能用的数字。温度感测器：温室里测到温度过高，计算机就开风扇；光线感测器：天一黑就自动亮路灯；加速度感测器：手机横过来就自动转屏。2. Sensors also bring real-world measurements into the computer: their readings become numbers the machine can use after analogue-to-digital conversion. A temperature sensor tells the computer to start a greenhouse fan; a light sensor switches street lights on at dusk; an accelerometer rotates the phone screen.

— 第 6 站完成 —— Stop 6 done —
7 输出设备与执行器Output devices and actuators Actuator执行器
这一站要会的：说出常见输出设备及其用途；说出什么是执行器并举出四个例子；会比较激光打印机和喷墨打印机。 Goal: name common output devices and their uses; define an actuator with four examples; compare laser and inkjet printers.
说人话：输出设备做的是反向的事：把处理器算出来的二进制结果变回人能看懂的画面、文字和声音。显示器把一堆像素值变成画面（清晰度看分辨率，也就是有多少个像素）；打印机把文字印到纸上（激光打印机靠静电吸附碳粉，快、清晰、适合印很多页文字；喷墨打印机把极小墨点喷到纸上，机器便宜、彩照效果好，但慢而且墨盒贵）；扬声器和耳机需要数模转换——把一串数字变回连续变化的电流，推动里面的膜片振动发声。
还有一类特别重要：执行器。它不只是告诉你结果，而是真的动手改变物理世界：电机转起来、加热器热起来、蜂鸣器响起来、发光二极管亮起来。洗衣机、空调、机器人身上全是这类东西。 In plain words: output devices do the opposite job: turn binary results back into pictures, text and sound a person can use. A monitor turns pixel values into a picture (sharpness depends on resolution, the pixel count); a printer puts text onto paper (a laser printer uses static charge to attract toner — fast and crisp, good for many pages of text; an inkjet sprays tiny droplets — cheap to buy and good at colour photos, but slow with expensive cartridges); speakers and headphones need digital-to-analogue conversion to turn numbers back into a smoothly varying current that vibrates a membrane and makes sound.
One further group matters a lot: actuators. They do not merely report results, they act on the physical world: a motor turns, a heater heats, a buzzer sounds, an LED lights. Washing machines, air conditioners and robots are full of them.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 显示器：把计算结果变成画面；分辨率是水平像素数 × 垂直像素数，越高越清晰。Monitor: turns results into a picture; resolution is pixels across × pixels down — higher is sharper.
  • 激光打印机：快、文字边缘清晰，适合印量大的办公环境，机器本身较贵。喷墨打印机：买着便宜、彩色照片效果好，但速度慢、墨盒长期成本高。Laser printer: fast with crisp text, suited to heavy office use, dearer to buy. Inkjet printer: cheap to buy and good with colour photos, but slower with expensive cartridges.
  • 扬声器 / 耳机：需要数模转换（DAC）把数字音频变回连续的模拟信号才能发声。Speakers / headphones: need digital-to-analogue conversion before digital audio can move a membrane.
  • 执行器（actuator）：把电信号转换成物理动作的器件。例：电机（转动）、加热器（发热）、蜂鸣器（发声）、发光二极管（发光）、电磁铁 / 继电器（吸合开关）。An actuator converts electrical signals into physical action: a motor (rotation), a heater (heat), a buzzer (sound), an LED (light), a solenoid/relay (switching).
  • 一个完整的控制系统往往是「传感器进来 → 处理器判断 → 执行器动手」，比如温度太高就开风扇。A complete control system usually runs "sensor in → processor decides → actuator acts", such as starting a fan when it gets too hot.

例题 · 洗衣机里的输出Example · the outputs in a washing machine

(a) 一台自动洗衣机里可能会用到哪些执行器？各自把电信号变成什么动作？
(b) 洗完以后蜂鸣器提示「好了」，它属于输出设备还是执行器？
(c) 为什么扬声器前面必须有数模转换器？ (a) Which actuators might a washing machine contain, and what action does each perform?
(b) The buzzer that signals "finished" — output device or actuator?
(c) Why must a speaker be preceded by a digital-to-analogue converter?
(a) 电机（转动滚筒或带动排水泵）、进水阀 / 电磁铁（开合水路）、加热器（把水温升起来）、指示灯（告诉用户状态）、蜂鸣器（提示完成）。(a) A motor (turning the drum or driving the pump), an inlet valve / solenoid (opening the water path), a heater (raising the water temperature), an indicator lamp (showing status) and a buzzer (signalling the end). (b) 两者都对：它接收处理器发出的电信号再做出反应，所以是输出设备；它把电信号变成了物理声音，所以同时也是一类执行器。(b) Both: it responds to a signal from the processor, making it an output device, and it converts that signal into real sound, which also makes it an actuator. (c) 存放和传输的音频是一串数字（0 和 1），而让膜片振动需要连续变化的电流；数模转换器负责把数字序列还原成那种连续信号。(c) Stored audio is a stream of numbers, but vibrating a membrane needs a continuously varying current. The DAC turns the sequence back into that smooth signal.

练习Practice

区分的关键：结果给「人」看，还是作用于「物」。The key distinction: does the result go to a person, or act on things?

① 说出三种输出设备，各给一个使用场景。1. Name three output devices and give a use case for each.

② 举出两个执行器的例子，并说明它们把电信号变成了什么。2. Give two examples of actuators and say what each converts its signal into.

点开看答案Show answer

① 显示器（老师投屏讲题）；打印机（把作业稿印出来）；投影仪（会议室放映）；扬声器（下课铃广播）；耳机（英语听力练习）。写出任意三个加场景即可。1. Monitor (a teacher projecting solutions); printer (printing coursework); projector (a meeting-room slide show); speakers (school bell announcements); headphones (listening practice). Any three with a scenario.

② 例一：电机——把电信号变成转动（风扇、机器人轮子）；例二：加热器——把电信号变成热量（电水壶、洗衣机加热洗）；例三：发光二极管——变成光（指示灯）。写出任意两个即可。2. Example 1: a motor turns electricity into rotation (fans, robot wheels). Example 2: a heater turns it into heat (kettle, hot wash cycle). Example 3: an LED turns it into light (indicator lamps). Any two.

— 第 7 站完成 —— Stop 7 done —
8 嵌入式系统与网络硬件Embedded systems and network hardware Embedded / Router嵌入式 / 路由器
这一站要会的：说出嵌入式系统的定义和三个特点并举三个例子；说出网卡、硬件地址、集线器、交换机、路由器、无线接入点各自是干什么的。 Goal: define an embedded system with three features and three examples; explain NIC, MAC address, hub, switch, router and wireless access point.
说人话：先看嵌入式系统。你家洗衣机里面其实有一台完整的计算机——有处理器、内存、只读存储器，只是它没有键盘也没有大屏幕，一辈子只会一件事：按你选的程序转、进水、加热、排水。这就是嵌入式系统：装在一台更大的机器里面、为某个专门任务而设计的计算机系统。它跟普通电脑的差别是：只干一件事，所以处理器和内存都按「够用就行」来配（便宜、省电）；常常要求实时（按下按钮必须立刻有反应，汽车安全气囊更是慢一毫秒都不行）；几乎不关机，常年连续运行。
再看网络硬件。每台要联网的设备都有一块网卡，它负责把数据变成适合网线或无线电传输的信号，并且出厂时就带着一个全球唯一的硬件地址（也就是 MAC 地址），相当于设备的身份证号。收到的数据该往哪送？集线器最笨，收到就从所有口喊出去，谁都听到，因而拥挤又容易被旁人偷听；交换机聪明得多，它记住每个口接的是哪个地址，只把数据发给目标所在的那个口，于是又快又安全；路由器负责把不同的网络之间连起来，替数据包挑路送到互联网；无线接入点则让大家不用插线，用无线电就能接入网络。 In plain words: take embedded systems first. Your washing machine contains a complete computer — processor, memory, ROM — except it has no keyboard or big screen and will only ever do one job: spin, fill, heat and drain according to the cycle you chose. That is an embedded system: a computer system built into a larger machine to perform a specific task. It differs from a general-purpose computer because: it does one job, so the processor and memory are only as big as necessary (cheap, low power); it is often real-time (a button must respond at once and an airbag controller cannot be a millisecond late); and it rarely gets switched off, running for years.
Now network hardware. Every networked device has a network interface card (NIC), which turns data into signals suited to the cable or radio link and carries a globally unique hardware address (the MAC address) written in at manufacture — an identity card for the device. Where does incoming data go? A hub is the dumbest option: it shouts everything out of every port so all devices hear it, which is crowded and easy to listen in on. A switch is cleverer: it learns which address sits behind which port and forwards only to the intended one, making it both faster and more private. A router joins whole networks together and picks the route for packets heading to the internet. A wireless access point lets everyone join without plugging in.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 嵌入式系统（embedded system）：装在更大机器里的专用计算机系统，通常包含处理器、存储器和输入输出部件。An embedded system is a dedicated computer system built into a larger machine, usually with processor, memory and input/output parts.
  • 三个特点：① 专用：只完成一个（或很少几个）明确的任务；② 资源受限：处理器、内存按够用来配，成本低、功耗小；③ 常常要求实时：必须在规定时间内做出响应。Three features: 1. dedicated — one or very few well-defined tasks; 2. resource-limited — just enough processor and memory, keeping cost and power low; 3. often real-time — it must respond within a set time.
  • 常举的例子：洗衣机、微波炉、汽车电控单元、防抱死制动系统、智能手表、数码相机、交通信号灯、无人机、血糖仪。Standard examples: washing machine, microwave, car engine control unit, anti-lock braking, smart watch, digital camera, traffic lights, drone, blood glucose meter.
  • 网卡（NIC）：把设备的数据转换成适合所用传输介质的信号并负责收发；里面有唯一的硬件地址。A NIC converts device data into signals suited to the medium and handles transmission and reception; it carries a unique hardware address.
  • 硬件地址（MAC address）：网卡出厂时就写好的唯一标识，在局域网里辨认这台设备；交换机靠它决定从哪个口转发。The MAC address is a unique identifier written into the NIC at manufacture, used to identify the device on a LAN; switches use it to choose the output port.
  • 集线器与交换机：集线器把收到的数据转发给所有端口（浪费带宽、容易冲突）；交换机只发给目标所在的端口（效率高、冲突少、更不容易被窃听）。Hub vs switch: a hub forwards incoming data to every port (wasting bandwidth and causing collisions); a switch sends it only to the destination port (efficient, fewer collisions, harder to eavesdrop).
  • 路由器：把不同的网络连起来，根据地址选择路径转发数据包，通常也是局域网通向互联网的出口。无线接入点：用无线电让设备接入有线网络。A router joins separate networks, forwards packets along the chosen route and is usually the gateway from the LAN to the internet. A wireless access point lets devices join a wired network by radio.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：「说出并描述嵌入式系统」是高频题，先定义再补特点，最后给例子。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: "state what an embedded system is and describe one" is a frequent question — define, add features, then give an example. An embedded system is a computer system built into a larger device to carry out one specific task. It usually has limited processing power and memory, often has to respond in real time, and runs continuously — for example the controller in a washing machine or a car engine control unit. A switch sends data only to the device with the matching MAC address, whereas a hub sends it to every device.

例题 · 两个主题各一题Example · one question on each topic

(a) 说出嵌入式系统的定义，举一个例子并说明它为什么适合。
(b) 家里用无线网上网，从手机到互联网经过了哪些硬件？各起什么作用？
(c) 学校机房把集线器换成交换机后明显流畅了，为什么？ (a) Define an embedded system, then give an example and explain why it suits.
(b) Which hardware does data cross from a phone on home Wi-Fi to the internet, and what does each do?
(c) A school replaced its hubs with switches and everything got smoother. Why?
(a) 定义：装在更大机器里、为完成某个专门任务而设计的计算机系统。例子：汽车里的防抱死制动控制单元——它的任务只有一个：随时监视车轮转速并在抱死前调整制动力，必须实时反应、还要一直稳定运行，正好符合嵌入式系统的特点。(a) Definition: a computer system built into a larger machine to perform a specific task. Example: the anti-lock braking controller in a car, whose only job is to watch wheel speed and adjust braking before a wheel locks — it must react in real time and run continuously, which matches an embedded system exactly. (b) 手机的无线网卡把数据变成无线电信号 → 无线接入点（通常就在家里的路由器里）接收并交给有线网络 → 路由器挑好路径把数据包转发给互联网；回来的数据沿原路反向走一遍。(b) The phone's wireless NIC turns data into radio signals → the wireless access point (usually inside the home router) receives them and passes them onto the wired network → the router chooses the route and forwards packets to the internet. Replies travel back along the same path. (c) 集线器会把每一份数据发给所有端口，大家的网线都被无关数据占满，还经常撞车；交换机认得每个口接的是哪个硬件地址，只发给目标那一口，其它线路空出来可以同时传别的，冲突也少多了。(c) A hub sends every frame out of all ports, so everyone's cable carries traffic meant for someone else and collisions are common. A switch knows which hardware address sits behind each port and forwards only to that one, leaving the other links free for further traffic with far fewer collisions.

练习Practice

嵌入式系统题记得把「特点 + 例子」配成对。For embedded systems, always pair a feature with an example.

① 举出两个嵌入式系统的例子，说出各自的专门任务。1. Give two examples of embedded systems and state the dedicated task of each.

② 说出交换机比集线器好的两个理由。2. Give two reasons why a switch is better than a hub.

点开看答案Show answer

① 智能手表：计步、测心率、显示通知这一类固定任务；数码相机：控制镜头、感光成像、存取照片；微波炉：按设定火力与时间控制加热；交通信号灯：按时间表切换红绿灯。写出任意两个即可。1. A smart watch: fixed tasks such as counting steps, measuring heart rate and showing notifications. A digital camera: controlling the lens, capturing the image and saving photos. A microwave: controlling heating to the set power and time. Traffic lights: switching lights on a timetable. Any two.

② ① 只发给目标端口，其它线路可以同时在传别的数据，整体速度快得多；② 冲突少（不再大家一起喊），且别人不容易顺手监听到发给他人的数据，安全性更好。2. 1. It forwards only to the destination port, leaving other links free for simultaneous traffic, so overall throughput is much higher. 2. Fewer collisions, and it is harder for others to overhear traffic addressed to someone else, so it is more secure.

— 第 8 站完成 —— Stop 8 done —

单元 3 完成 · 主线：处理器顺着流水线跑，靠总线和内存换数据，再通过外设和外面的世界来往Unit 3 done · the thread: the processor runs its cycle, exchanges data through buses and memory, and reaches the outside world via peripherals

回头看这一单元其实是四句话：① 结构——指令和数据挤在同一块内存里，用总线连起来；② 处理器——控制单元指挥、算术逻辑单元干活、五个寄存器各管一格、缓存夹在中间提速；③ 存储层次——寄存器、缓存、主存、二级存储、云，越靠近处理器越快越贵越小；④ 外设——输入设备把现实世界送进来，输出设备和执行器把结果送出去。In hindsight this unit is four sentences: 1. structure — instructions and data share one memory, joined by buses; 2. the processor — control unit directs, ALU works, five registers each hold a specific item, cache speeds things up between; 3. the storage ladder — registers, cache, main memory, secondary storage, cloud — closer to the processor means faster, dearer and smaller; 4. peripherals — input devices bring the real world in, output devices and actuators send results back out.

下一步：单元 4 讲软件——只有硬件是动不起来的，操作系统怎么把这台机器管起来，程序又是怎么被翻译成机器能懂的话。Next: Unit 4 covers software — hardware alone never does anything, so we look at how the operating system manages the machine and how programs are translated into something it understands.

单元 4 · 软件Unit 4 · SoftwarePAPER 1

系统软件和应用软件怎么分工 · 操作系统怎么把机器管起来 · 程序又是怎么从「人话」变成机器能懂的话How system and application software divide the work · how the operating system runs the machine · how programs become something it understands

本单元包括什么What this unit covers 6 站 · 约 40 分钟6 stops · about 40 min

  1. 系统软件与应用软件System software vs application software
  2. 操作系统到底在忙什么What the operating system does
  3. 中断：为什么处理器能被随时叫停Interrupts
  4. 高级语言与低级语言High-level and low-level languages
  5. 编译器、解释器与汇编器Compilers, interpreters and assemblers
  6. 集成开发环境、调试与三类错误IDEs, debugging and the three types of error

建议顺序：站 1、2、3 讲的是「管机器的软件」，站 4、5、6 讲的是「写程序的软件」。站 5 的对比表几乎年年考，务必练熟。Suggested order: stops 1–3 are about software that manages the machine, stops 4–6 about software for writing programs. The comparison in stop 5 comes up almost every year — drill it.

1 系统软件与应用软件System software vs application software Utility工具软件
这一站要会的：说出系统软件和应用软件的定义；各举三个以上的例子；说出两者的分工差别。 Goal: define system software and application software; give at least three examples of each; explain how they divide the work.
说人话：软件分成两大类，用一句话区分就够了：系统软件是给机器自己用的，应用软件是给你办事的。系统软件负责把硬件管好、把环境搭好，让别的程序能安心跑：里面有操作系统（大管家，下一站专门讲）、设备驱动程序（教操作系统怎么跟某个型号的打印机对上话）、工具程序（杀毒、磁盘清理、碎片整理、备份、压缩解压这些打杂的活）、库程序（别人早就写好的常用功能，你的程序直接拿来调）、以及把程序翻译成机器码的翻译器。应用软件则是你主动打开来做某件事的：写文章的文字处理软件、算账的电子表格、做演示的幻灯片软件、上网的浏览器、听歌看片的播放器、画图的制图软件、各种游戏。关键关系：应用软件要用到硬件时，都得通过系统软件；反过来，你可以少装一个应用软件，但不能完全没有操作系统。 In plain words: one sentence separates the two: system software is for the machine itself, application software is for getting your work done. System software manages the hardware and sets up an environment other programs can rely on: the operating system (the housekeeper, next stop), device drivers (teaching the OS how to talk to a particular printer), utility programs (antivirus, disk cleanup, defragmentation, backup, compression — the odd jobs), library programs (ready-made routines you call from your own code) and translators that turn source code into machine code. Application software is what you deliberately open to do something: a word processor for writing, a spreadsheet for accounts, presentation software, a web browser, media players, drawing tools, games. The key relationship: whenever an application touches the hardware it goes through system software; you can drop an application, but going without an operating system is not an option.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 系统软件（system software）：用来运行和管理计算机硬件，并为应用软件提供运行平台的软件。System software runs and manages the computer hardware and provides a platform for application software.
  • 系统软件的例子：操作系统（视窗、苹果系统、Linux 之类）、设备驱动程序、工具程序（杀毒、备份、磁盘碎片整理、压缩解压）、库程序、翻译器（编译器、解释器、汇编器）。Examples: the operating system (Windows, macOS, Linux), device drivers, utilities (antivirus, backup, defragmentation, compression), libraries and translators (compiler, interpreter, assembler).
  • 应用软件（application software）：为用户完成某项具体任务的软件。Application software performs a particular task for the user.
  • 应用软件的例子：文字处理、电子表格、演示文稿、数据库软件（办公类）；浏览器、邮件、聊天（通讯类）；图片处理、视频剪辑、音乐播放器、游戏（多媒体类）；记账、绘图设计、日程表（专业类）。Examples: word processors, spreadsheets, presentation and database software (office); browsers, email and chat (communication); image editors, video editors, media players and games (multimedia); accounting, design tools and diaries (professional).
  • 分工差别：系统软件在后台全年无休地服务机器，应用软件在前台替人办事；没有系统软件，应用软件一行都跑不动。Division of labour: system software serves the machine in the background around the clock; applications work for people in the foreground. Without system software, no application runs at all.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：判断一个软件属于哪一类时，先一句话定性，再补一句它做什么。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: when classifying software, make one statement about what it is, then add what it does. A word processor is application software because it performs a specific task for the user (writing documents). Antivirus software is utility software, which is system software, because it maintains the computer rather than doing the user's own task.

例题 · 分类并说明理由Example · classify with a reason

判断下列各项属于系统软件还是应用软件，并各给一句理由：防病毒软件、网页浏览器、打印机驱动程序、电子表格、翻译器、视频剪辑软件。 Classify each as system or application software with a reason: antivirus software, a web browser, a printer driver, a spreadsheet, a translator, a video editor.
系统软件：防病毒软件（保护和维护计算机的工具程序）；打印机驱动程序（让操作系统能与该型号打印机通信）；翻译器（把程序翻译成机器码）。System software: antivirus (a utility that maintains the computer); the printer driver (lets the OS talk to that printer model); the translator (turns source code into machine code). 应用软件：网页浏览器（用户用它看网页）；电子表格（用户用它算账做表）；视频剪辑软件（用户用它剪片子）。Application software: the browser (the user views web pages), the spreadsheet (the user works with numbers) and the video editor (the user edits video).

练习Practice

记口诀：「给机器用」还是「给人用」。Mantra: "for the machine" or "for the person"?

① 举出三个系统软件的例子，各配一句说明。1. Give three examples of system software, each with a short explanation.

② 举出三个应用软件的例子，并说明为什么没有应用软件电脑照样能开机，而没有系统软件就不行。2. Give three examples of application software, and explain why a computer still starts without them but will not start without system software.

点开看答案Show answer

① 操作系统（管理硬件并提供平台）；设备驱动程序（让操作系统能控制某个具体的外设）；备份工具（把重要文件定期复制到别处）；库程序（提供别人写好的常用功能）。写出任意三个。1. The operating system (manages hardware and provides the platform); a device driver (lets the OS control one specific peripheral); a backup utility (copies important files elsewhere regularly); a library (ready-made routines). Any three.

② 例子：文字处理软件、浏览器、游戏。理由：应用软件是在操作系统之上运行的任务工具，缺了它只是少做一件事，开机过程照样进行；而系统软件负责启动机器、管理内存和硬件，连把操作系统从硬盘读进内存都得靠它，没有它开机流程根本走不完。2. Examples: a word processor, browser and game. Why: applications are just task tools running on top of the OS, so missing one only means missing one job — start-up proceeds regardless. System software boots the machine, manages memory and hardware, including loading the operating system itself from disk; without it the boot sequence never completes.

— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 操作系统到底在忙什么What the operating system does OS操作系统
这一站要会的：说出操作系统的六项主要功能，每项配一句说明或例子。 Goal: state six main functions of the operating system, each with an explanation or example.
说人话：操作系统就是大管家。机器里其实很难管的几件事，全被它悄悄包了：它给你一个界面（图形窗口也好，敲命令也好），让你不必对着芯片和内存打交道；每个程序启动时要占多少内存、放在哪一段由它分配，程序关掉时还得把内存收回来；你同时开着浏览器、音乐和文档，它负责让处理器在几个任务之间来回切换（多任务），快到你觉得三个都在同时跑；每个外设怎么打招呼靠设备驱动程序翻译，所以新买的打印机插上去要装驱动；你的照片、作业、下载的东西在硬盘上怎么摆，由它负责建文件夹、改名、移动、删除；它还管谁是什么账号、能看什么文件，顺便负责安全——登录验证、权限检查、防火墙这些。正因为有它，写应用软件的人再也不用自己关心「这块数据到底在磁盘的第几个扇区」。 In plain words: the operating system is the housekeeper. It quietly takes over the awkward jobs: it gives you an interface (graphical windows or a command line) so you never deal with chips and raw memory; it decides how much memory each program gets and where it goes, reclaiming memory when a program closes; with a browser, music player and document open at once it switches the processor between them (multitasking) so fast that everything seems to run together; it talks to each peripheral through its device driver, which is why a new printer needs one installed; it organises your photos, coursework and downloads on disk, creating folders and renaming, moving and deleting files; it keeps track of accounts and permissions — who may read what — and handles security: login checks, permission checks and firewalls. Thanks to all this, application programmers no longer care which disk sector holds their data.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 用户界面：提供图形界面（点图标开窗）或命令行界面（敲命令），让人不必直接操作硬件。User interface: a graphical interface (icons and windows) or a command-line interface, so users never operate hardware directly.
  • 内存管理与多任务：给每个运行的程序分配内存空间（互不越界），程序结束后回收；用调度让处理器在多个任务之间快速切换，看上去像是同时跑。Memory management and multitasking: allocates memory to each running program without overlap and reclaims it afterwards; scheduling switches the processor between tasks so fast that they appear simultaneous.
  • 外设管理与设备驱动：通过设备驱动程序把通用请求翻译成某个具体设备听得懂的信号；没装驱动，设备就在那儿杵着。Peripheral management and drivers: each device driver translates general requests into signals one specific device understands; without it the hardware just sits there.
  • 文件管理：建立、命名、重命名、移动、复制、删除文件和文件夹，并记录它们在磁盘上的存放位置与属性。File management: creating, naming, renaming, moving, copying and deleting files and folders, and recording where they sit on disk along with their attributes.
  • 用户账号与权限：管理账号、登录密码，规定谁能读、写、执行哪些文件（也可以按组管理）。Accounts and access rights: managing accounts and passwords, deciding who may read, write or execute which files (often by group).
  • 安全管理：登录验证、权限检查、防火墙与日志记录等，防止未经授权的访问和破坏。Security: authentication, permission checks, firewalls and logging to block unauthorised access and damage.
容易丢分的地方：题目问「为什么装新打印机要安装驱动程序」时，完整答案是两步：操作系统不认识这个具体型号 → 驱动程序负责把系统的通用打印命令翻译成这个型号听懂的信号。只写「因为要让它能用」不得分。 Classic trap: for "why must a driver be installed for a new printer?" a full answer has two parts: the OS does not know this specific model → the driver translates general print commands into the signals that model understands. "Because otherwise it will not work" earns nothing.

例题 · 六项功能给场景Example · matching situations to functions

下面每种情形，主要由操作系统的哪项功能负责？
(a) 同时听歌、下载文件、写文档，还互不干扰　(b) 安装一台新打印机后还要加装一个小程序　(c) 家里的弟弟只能读共享文件夹，不能改里面的文件 Which function mainly handles each case?
(a) Music playing, files downloading and a document being written at once, without interfering　(b) a small extra piece of software after fitting a new printer　(c) a younger sibling may read the shared folder but not change it
(a) 内存管理与多任务：操作系统给三个程序分别分配内存，并安排处理器在它们之间快速切换。(a) Memory management and multitasking: the OS gives each of the three programs its own memory and schedules the processor between them. (b) 外设管理与设备驱动：驱动把系统的通用打印命令翻译成这台具体型号能懂的信号。(b) Peripheral management and drivers: the driver translates general print commands into signals this exact model understands. (c) 用户账号与权限（属于安全管理的一部分）：给不同账号设置不同的访问权限。(c) Accounts and access rights (part of security): different accounts are given different access levels.

练习Practice

每项都配一句「具体管什么」。Add one clause about what exactly is managed.

① 说出操作系统的四项功能，各配一句说明。1. State four functions of the operating system, each with an explanation.

② 为什么有了操作系统，写应用软件的人就不用关心数据存在磁盘的哪个位置？2. Why does having an operating system free application programmers from worrying about where data sits on disk?

点开看答案Show answer

① 例如：用户界面（提供窗口和图标让操作变简单）、内存管理（给程序分配空间并回收）、外设管理（通过驱动控制设备）、文件管理（新建 / 删除 / 移动文件）、账号与权限、安全管理。写出任意四项加说明即可。1. For example: user interface (windows and icons make it usable); memory management (allocating and reclaiming space); peripheral management (controlling devices through drivers); file management (create/delete/move files); accounts and permissions; security. Any four with explanations.

② 因为文件管理由操作系统统一负责：应用软件只要说「保存到文件名某某」「读某某文件」，操作系统会自己去查这个文件在哪、实际写到磁盘哪些位置，还顺便防止两个程序互相覆盖。2. Because file management is centralised in the OS: an application merely says "save as this filename" or "read that file", and the OS works out where the file lives, which disk blocks to use, and prevents two programs overwriting each other.

— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 中断：为什么处理器能被随时叫停Interrupts ISR中断服务程序
这一站要会的：说出什么是中断、为什么需要它；说出处理中断的五个步骤；给两个中断的例子；说出中断优先级的作用。 Goal: explain what an interrupt is and why it is needed; give the five handling steps; give two examples; state the purpose of interrupt priority.
说人话：处理器正埋头干活，打印机突然没纸了。要是没人管，它要么继续打印到空气里，要么得每隔几毫秒就去问一次「打印机还好吗」（这叫轮询，白白占着处理器时间）。更聪明的办法是：让打印机主动喊一声——这就是中断，一个打过来的信号，告诉处理器「这儿有事，先处理我」。处理器收到后按五步走：① 停下手里的活；② 把当前寄存器的内容（现场）先存起来，就像游戏存档；③ 跑到对应的那段小程序去处理这件事，这段程序叫中断服务程序；④ 处理完把刚才存档的寄存器内容读回来；⑤ 从被打断的那一句接着往下跑，就像什么都没发生过。
如果好几件事同时喊呢？那就看优先级：电源快没电、硬件故障这类比鼠标点击急得多，先办急的；更狠的是，优先级高的中断还可以打断优先级低的中断服务程序。 In plain words: the processor is hard at work when the printer runs out of paper. Left unchecked it either prints into thin air or must ask "printer still OK?" every few milliseconds (polling, which wastes processor time). The smarter option is to let the printer shout: that shout is an interrupt, a signal saying "deal with me first". The processor then follows five steps: 1. stop the current task; 2. save the current register contents (its state), like saving a game; 3. run the little routine that handles this event, called the interrupt service routine (ISR); 4. restore the saved register contents; 5. resume from exactly the instruction that was interrupted, as if nothing happened.
If several things shout at once, priority decides: imminent power loss or a hardware fault beats a mouse click, and a high-priority interrupt may even interrupt the ISR of a lower-priority one.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 中断（interrupt）：由硬件或软件发出的信号，告诉处理器有事情需要立刻处理。An interrupt is a signal from hardware or software telling the processor something needs attention now.
  • 中断服务程序（ISR）：专门用来处理某一种中断的那段程序。The interrupt service routine (ISR) is the piece of code that deals with one particular interrupt.
  • 五步流程：中断到来 → 保存寄存器内容（存到堆栈）→ 跳转到中断服务程序并执行 → 恢复寄存器内容 → 回到被中断的地方继续执行。Five steps: interrupt arrives → save register contents (onto the stack) → jump to the ISR and run it → restore the registers → resume where it left off.
  • 为什么用中断而不用轮询：轮询要处理器不断去查每个设备，白白浪费时间；中断让设备主动报告，处理器平时专心干活，效率更高、响应更快。Why interrupts beat polling: polling makes the processor check every device constantly, wasting time; interrupts let devices report for themselves, so the processor works uninterrupted and responds faster.
  • 中断优先级（priority）：多个中断同时到来时先处理优先级高的；优先级高的还能打断优先级低的中断服务程序。Interrupt priority: when several arrive together, the highest priority is served first, and a higher-priority interrupt can interrupt the ISR of a lower-priority one.
  • 常见例子：打印机缺纸或没墨、鼠标点击、按下按键、定时器到点、磁盘读写完成、电量过低、硬件出错。Common examples: printer out of paper or ink, mouse click, key press, timer expiring, disk transfer complete, low battery, hardware failure.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：「描述中断的处理过程」几乎每年都考，把下面六句按顺序写。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: "describe how an interrupt is handled" appears most years — write these sentences in order. An interrupt is a signal that requires the processor's immediate attention. The processor stops the current task and saves the contents of its registers. It then runs the interrupt service routine for that interrupt. The priority decides which interrupt is handled first if several occur together. Finally the saved register contents are restored and the original task continues.

例题 · 逐步描述一次中断Example · describing an interrupt step by step

正在打印一份很长的作业时，打印机突然缺纸。
(a) 描述处理器接下来要做的事。
(b) 为什么不能让处理器每隔几毫秒就去查一次打印机？
(c) 恰好此时电量也降到极低，两个中断同时来了，谁先被处理？ A long print job stops because the printer has run out of paper.
(a) Describe what the processor does next.
(b) Why not just check the printer every few milliseconds?
(c) At the same moment the battery level becomes critical. Which interrupt is served first?
(a) 打印机发出中断信号 → 处理器停下打印任务 → 把寄存器内容存起来 → 执行「缺纸」的中断服务程序（通常暂停作业并在屏幕上提示「请加纸」）→ 恢复寄存器内容 → 加好纸后从被打断的地方继续打印。(a) The printer raises an interrupt → the processor stops the print job → saves the register contents → runs the ISR for "out of paper" (usually pausing the job and showing a message) → restores the registers → once paper is loaded printing resumes where it stopped. (b) 那叫轮询，处理器得不停地把注意力从正事挪开去查状态，白白浪费执行时间，还可能错过别的更急的事。(b) That is polling: the processor keeps turning away from real work to check status, wasting execution time and possibly missing something more urgent. (c) 看优先级：电量过低先处理——断电会让所有数据丢失，比暂停打印紧急得多，而且它可以打断「缺纸」的中断服务程序。(c) By priority, the low battery is served first: losing power would lose everything, which is far more urgent than pausing a print job, and it can even interrupt the new-paper ISR.

练习Practice

流程题最爱考「保存 / 恢复寄存器」这一句，别忘了写。Step questions always want the "save/restore registers" sentence — do not leave it out.

① 按顺序列出处理一次中断的五个步骤。1. List the five steps of handling an interrupt in order.

② 举出两个会产生中断的情形，并说明为什么它们的优先级会不同。2. Give two events that cause interrupts and explain why their priorities differ.

点开看答案Show answer

① ① 中断信号到来 → ② 保存寄存器内容（存进堆栈）→ ③ 执行对应的中断服务程序 → ④ 恢复寄存器内容 → ⑤ 回到被打断的指令继续执行。1. 1. an interrupt arrives → 2. the register contents are saved (onto the stack) → 3. the matching ISR runs → 4. the registers are restored → 5. execution continues from the interrupted instruction.

② 例：鼠标点击与电量即将耗尽。鼠标点击晚几毫秒处理，用户基本没感觉；电量耗尽却不处理就会整台机器掉电，正在做的活全没了，所以后者优先级更高。同理，硬件故障、过热报警都比键盘鼠标优先。2. Example: a mouse click versus a battery about to die. Delaying a click by a few milliseconds is unnoticeable, whereas ignoring empty battery kills the whole machine and loses everything unsaved — so the latter ranks higher. Hardware faults and over-heating alarms outrank mouse and keyboard for the same reason.

— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 高级语言与低级语言High-level and low-level languages Machine code机器码
这一站要会的：说出机器码、汇编语言、高级语言各是什么；比较它们的优缺点；说出汇编器、编译器、解释器分别翻译哪一种语言。 Goal: describe machine code, assembly language and high-level languages; compare their advantages and disadvantages; say which translator handles which language.
说人话：处理器其实只听得懂一种话：一串 0 和 1，这就是机器码。它完美——不用翻译，跑得最快，每个位都被用上，最省内存；但它难读写到什么程度呢？一行 01110101，你得查表才知道它让干嘛。
于是出现了汇编语言：给每种机器指令起个好记的短名字（叫助记符），比如用 ADD 表示加法、STA 表示存回内存。一条汇编指令基本对应一条机器指令，用汇编器翻译。它依然紧贴硬件——你可以指名用哪个寄存器、哪块内存，所以程序又小又快；代价是：难学难写、程序特别长、开发很慢，而且每种处理器的指令都不同，换一块处理器就得重写（不可移植）。
再往上就是高级语言：一行话对应好多条机器指令。它好写好读，句子接近自然语言和数学算式，还有一大堆现成的库可用；同一份代码换个编译器或解释器就能跑到别的机器上（可移植）。代价是必须翻译才能运行，而且程序员没法精确控制硬件细节，生成出来的程序通常更大、更慢一些。 In plain words: the processor understands exactly one language: a stream of 0s and 1s called machine code. It is perfect in one way — no translation needed, fastest possible execution, every bit doing useful work, minimum memory. But reading 01110101 means looking up what it commands.
So assembly language arrived: each machine instruction gets a memorable short name (a mnemonic) such as ADD for addition or STA for storing to memory. One assembly instruction maps onto roughly one machine instruction, translated by an assembler. It stays close to the hardware — you can name the exact register or address — so programs are small and fast. The cost: it is hard to learn, long to write and slow to develop, and every processor family has its own instructions, so the code must be rewritten elsewhere (it is not portable).
Above that sit high-level languages, where one line expands into many machine instructions. They read well, look like natural language and formulae, and come with huge libraries; the same source can run on another machine with a different compiler or interpreter (portability). The cost is that the code must be translated and the programmer cannot control hardware details precisely, so the result is usually larger and a little slower.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 机器码（machine code）：纯粹的二进制，处理器直接执行；优点：不用翻译、执行最快、占用内存最少；缺点：人几乎无法读写和调试。Machine code: pure binary executed directly by the processor. Advantages: no translation, fastest execution, least memory. Disadvantage: practically unreadable and unmaintainable for humans.
  • 汇编语言（assembly language）：用助记符代替二进制操作码，一条指令基本对应一条机器指令，由汇编器翻译。Assembly language: mnemonics replace binary opcodes; one instruction maps to roughly one machine instruction; translated by an assembler.
  • 低级语言的优点：直接控制硬件（可指定寄存器和地址），程序占用内存少、运行快。常用在嵌入式系统、设备驱动这类讲究速度和体积的地方。Advantages of low-level languages: direct hardware control (naming registers and addresses), small memory footprint and fast execution. Used where size and speed matter, e.g. embedded systems and device drivers.
  • 低级语言的缺点：难学难写难调试、开发慢、程序长，而且不可移植（绑死在一种处理器上）。Disadvantages: hard to learn, write and debug; slow development; long programs; and not portable (tied to one processor).
  • 高级语言的优点：易学易写易读、接近自然语言、可移植、库丰富、开发快。缺点：必须翻译、不能直接控制硬件细节、程序通常更大更慢。Advantages of high-level languages: easy to learn, write and read; close to natural language; portable; rich libraries; fast development. Disadvantages: translation required, no fine hardware control, usually larger and slower.

同一件事，用高级语言写只要几行 —— 下面这一段是 CAIE 0478 官方风格的伪代码，意思是「把 1 到 N 全加起来，再除以 N 求平均」：The same job takes only a few lines in a high-level language — this is pseudocode in the CAIE 0478 style, adding 1 to N and dividing by N:

INPUT N Total ← 0 FOR Count ← 1 TO N Total ← Total + Count NEXT Count OUTPUT Total / N // 一行就搞定求平均// one line gives the average

同样的活在汇编里得这么写（示意用，不同处理器的写法不一样）：每一步都要自己安排「把数搬到哪个寄存器」：The same job in assembly (illustrative — each processor differs): you decide yourself which register each value moves into:

LDA #0 // 把累加器清零// clear the accumulator STA Total // 清零的结果先存回内存// store the zeroed result to memory LDA Count // 取出一个加数// load the next number ADD Total // 加到总和上// add it to the total STA Total // 再把总和存回去// store the total again
🗣 考场英文怎么说：题目常问「为什么这种场合要用低级语言」，答案一定扣住速度快、省内存和直接控制硬件。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: for "why use a low-level language here?" keep to speed, small memory use and direct hardware access. A low-level language should be used for this device driver because the programmer needs to control specific registers and memory addresses. The resulting machine code uses less memory and executes faster than code produced from a high-level language.

例题 · 按场景选语言Example · choosing a language for the job

(a) 学校要做一个学生成绩管理系统，交给一位新手写，该用高级语言还是低级语言？给两个理由。
(b) 一段要嵌在无人机飞控里、每一毫秒都要精确处理传感器数据的代码呢？
(c) 说出高级语言的两个缺点。 (a) A school needs a student mark system written by a beginner. High-level or low-level? Give two reasons.
(b) What about code inside a drone controller that must handle sensor data every millisecond?
(c) State two disadvantages of high-level languages.
(a) 用高级语言：① 易学易写，新手能很快上手，开发速度快；② 可移植，换台机器也能跑，加上库多，报表、界面这些功能做起来省力。(a) Use a high-level language: 1. it is easier to learn and write, so a beginner is productive quickly; 2. it is portable with rich libraries, making reports and screens much simpler. (b) 选低级语言（汇编甚至直接机器码）：这类代码要直接读写传感器所在的寄存器和地址，而且对实时性要求在毫秒级，低级语言执行快、占用内存少最合适。(b) Choose a low-level language (assembly or even machine code): this code must read the sensor's own registers and addresses with millisecond real-time deadlines, and low-level code is fast and compact. (c) ① 必须先翻译（编译或解释）才能让机器执行；② 不能直接操纵具体寄存器等硬件细节，生成的目标代码通常更大、执行更慢。(c) 1. it must be translated (compiled or interpreted) before the machine can run it; 2. it gives no direct control of specific registers or hardware details, and the resulting code is usually larger and slower.

练习Practice

每条优缺点都配上「所以适合什么场景」。Attach each advantage to the situation it suits.

① 说出机器码、汇编语言、高级语言各一个优点和一个缺点。1. Give one advantage and one disadvantage of machine code, assembly language and high-level languages.

② 为什么用汇编语言写的程序通常不可移植？2. Why is an assembly-language program usually not portable?

点开看答案Show answer

① 机器码：执行最快（优点），人几乎读不懂（缺点）；汇编：能直接指定寄存器、省内存又快（优点），难写难读、程序长（缺点）；高级语言：好写好学、可移植（优点），必须翻译、不好精确控制硬件（缺点）。1. Machine code: fastest execution (advantage), practically unreadable (disadvantage). Assembly: names registers directly, compact and fast (advantage), hard and long to write (disadvantage). High-level: easy to learn and portable (advantage), needs translation and loses fine control (disadvantage).

② 因为汇编指令是跟着具体处理器设计的：不同种类的处理器，寄存器数量、指令名称、地址方式都不一样。换个系列就得按新的指令集重写一遍。2. Because assembly instructions are designed around one specific processor: another family has different registers, different instruction names and different addressing modes, so the program must be rewritten for the new instruction set.

— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 编译器、解释器与汇编器Compilers, interpreters and assemblers Translator翻译器
这一站要会的：说出汇编器、编译器、解释器各翻译什么语言；比较编译器与解释器的优缺点；按场景说出该用哪一个。 Goal: say which language each translator handles; compare compilers and interpreters; choose the right one for a given situation.
说人话：三种翻译器干的都是同一件事：把人写的源文件变成机器能执行的东西，只是方式不同。汇编器最死板：一条汇编指令翻一条机器指令，一对一。编译器像是一次性笔译：把整份源程序从头到尾翻完，生成一份目标代码（可以直接运行的程序文件）；翻完之后运行就不再需要编译器了，而且跑得飞快；它的缺点也很明显：所有错误要等到翻译结束才一次性列出来，学生写作业时不太方便。解释器像是同声传译：读一句、翻一句、立刻执行一句，下一句再来一遍；好处是一出错就停在那一行，特别好调试，边写边试非常顺手；代价是每次运行都要重新翻译一遍（运行慢），而且运行时这台机器上必须装着对应的解释器，别人的源码也等于公开展示。 In plain words: all three translators do the same job — turn human-written source into something the machine can execute — but they differ in style. The assembler is the most literal: one assembly instruction becomes one machine instruction, one to one. A compiler is like a written translation done in one sitting: it translates the whole source program and produces object code, a runnable program file. From then on the compiler is not needed and the program runs fast; the downside is that all errors appear together only at the end, which is awkward while learning. An interpreter is more like simultaneous interpreting: read one statement, translate it, execute it, then repeat for the next. The bonus is that it stops right at the offending line, making debugging easy; the price is that every run translates everything again (slow) and every machine must have that interpreter installed, with the source effectively on display.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 汇编器（assembler）：把汇编语言翻译成机器码（一条对一条）。An assembler translates assembly language into machine code, one instruction at a time.
  • 编译器（compiler）：把整份高级语言源程序一次性翻译成目标代码（机器码）。A compiler translates a whole high-level source program into object code (machine code) in one go.
  • 解释器的做法（interpreter）：逐行翻译并立即执行，翻译一行再翻译下一行。An interpreter translates and executes line by line, moving to the next statement only after running the previous one.
  • 编译器的优点：生成可执行的目标代码，运行时不需要编译器在场；执行速度快；目标代码不容易被别人看到源码。缺点：错误在翻译结束后才一次性报出，调试不便；目标代码依赖特定平台。Compiler advantages: object code is produced and the compiler is not needed at run time; execution is fast; the source is not exposed. Disadvantages: all errors appear only at the end, which slows debugging; the object code is tied to one platform.
  • 解释器的优点：出错立即停在当前这一行，容易定位和修改；便于边写边测试。缺点：每次运行都要重新翻译，执行速度慢；运行时必须有对应解释器；源程序要交给对方。Interpreter advantages: an error stops at the current line, easy to find and fix, ideal for writing and testing as you go. Disadvantages: everything is re-translated on every run, so it is slower; the interpreter must be present; the source has to be handed over.
翻译器Translator 翻译对象Translates 一次翻多少How much per go 运行时还要不要它Needed at run time 运行速度Execution speed 调试方便吗Easy to debug
汇编器Assembler 汇编语言 → 机器码assembly → machine code 一条对一条one instruction at a time 不要no 最快fastest 不方便hard
编译器Compiler 高级语言 → 目标代码high-level → object code 整份一起翻all at once 不要no 快fast 不太方便moderate
解释器Interpreter 高级语言 → 边翻边执行high-level, as it runs 一次一句one statement at a time 每次都要yes, every run 慢slow 很方便very easy

这张表一定要能默写：考试只要问到翻译器，照着这六列写就不会漏点。Learn this table by heart: any translator question can be answered column by column.

🗣 考场英文怎么说：比较两个翻译器时，两边都各写两点，句子越短越好。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: when comparing translators, give two points for each side and keep sentences short. A compiler translates the whole source program into object code at one time, so the finished program runs faster and the compiler is not needed at run time. An interpreter translates and executes one statement at a time, so it stops as soon as there is an error, which makes debugging easier, but it is slower because the program must be translated again every time it runs.

例题 · 三种场合选三种翻译Example · three occasions, three translators

(a) 学生第一次学编程，要不停地改错再试；
(b) 公司把写好的成品软件发给客户；
(c) 一段汇编语言的设备排放标准代码。
分别该用哪种翻译器？各给一个理由。 (a) A beginner amends errors and retries constantly; (b) a company ships finished software to customers; (c) a device routine written in assembly language. Which translator for each, and why?
(a) 解释器：出错就停在那一行，改完立刻重来，调试最方便。(a) An interpreter: it stops at the offending line, so the beginner can fix it and try again immediately. (b) 编译器：翻译一次生成目标代码，客户运行时既快也不需要额外的翻译软件，还不必把源码交出去。(b) A compiler: translate once and ship the object code — the customer's copy runs fast, needs no extra software, and the source stays private. (c) 汇编器：只有汇编器能把这种一对一映射到机器指令的语言翻译成机器码。(c) An assembler: only an assembler handles this one-instruction-to-one-machine-instruction language.

练习Practice

「目标代码 + 运行快 + 不用编译器在场」是编译器的三件套。"Object code + fast + no compiler needed" is the compiler triple.

① 说出编译器的两个优点和一个缺点。1. Give two advantages and one disadvantage of a compiler.

② 说出解释器的两个优点和一个缺点。2. Give two advantages and one disadvantage of an interpreter.

点开看答案Show answer

① 优点：生成可独立运行的目标代码，运行时不需要编译器；执行速度快，源码也不必公开。缺点：所有错误到最后才一起报出来，定位修改不太方便。1. Advantages: produces standalone object code and the compiler is not needed to run it; execution is fast and the source stays hidden. Disadvantage: all errors are reported at the end, making them harder to locate.

② 优点：出错立即停在当前行，很好定位；边写边试非常方便，改完就能再跑。缺点：每次运行都要重新翻译，所以慢；而且运行时必须有对应的解释器在场。2. Advantages: it stops at the line that failed, so faults are easy to find; ideal for trying things out as you write. Disadvantage: the program is re-translated every time it runs, which is slow, and the matching interpreter must be installed.

— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —
6 集成开发环境、调试与三类错误IDEs, debugging and the three types of error Debug调试
这一站要会的：说出集成开发环境包含哪些工具；说出断点、单步执行、变量监视各自的作用；分辨语法错误、逻辑错误和运行时错误并各举一例。 Goal: list the tools in an IDE; explain breakpoints, single stepping and variable watches; tell the three error types apart with an example of each.
说人话：写程序可以只用记事本加一个命令行编译器，但那样太难受了。于是有人把写程序要用的东西打包成一个软件，这就是集成开发环境（简称开发环境）：里面有编辑器（写代码，还会把关键字染成彩色、自动缩进、自动补全、自动提醒漏掉的括号）、翻译器（编译或解释，一键就跑起来）、调试器（找毛病用的）、再加上包管理、项目管理、版本管理等。
调试器三件套要记牢：断点——在某一行打个叉，程序运行到这里就暂停，你可以慢慢看；单步执行——按一下走一行，亲眼盯着每一步往哪儿走；变量监视——把某个变量挂在小窗口里，它的值怎么变看得清清楚楚。
最后是最常考的三类错误：语法错误是「写错字」——句子不合规范，翻译阶段就被拦下，程序压根没运行；逻辑错误是「想错了」——语法全对，机器照做，可惜做法不对，结果当然错，而且它不会报错，最阴险；运行时错误是「中途出事」——跑到一半撞上了不可能完成的动作，例如除以 0，程序当场崩溃。 In plain words: you could write programs in Notepad with a command-line compiler, but that is miserable, so someone bundled everything into one application: the integrated development environment (IDE). It contains an editor (colour-coded keywords, auto-indent, auto-complete, warnings about missing brackets), a translator (compile or interpret and run at one click), a debugger for hunting faults, plus project and version management.
Learn the three debugging tools: a breakpoint marks a line where the program pauses so you can look around; single stepping executes one statement per click so you watch where control goes; a variable watch pins a variable in a small window so you see how its value changes.
Then the three error types examiners love. A syntax error is "bad grammar" — the code breaks the language's rules and is caught during translation, so the program never runs. A logic error is "wrong thinking" — perfectly valid code that does the wrong thing, giving wrong answers with no error message at all, which makes it the sneakiest. A run-time error happens part-way through: the program attempts something impossible, like dividing by zero, and crashes there and then.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 集成开发环境（IDE）：把编辑器、翻译器（编译器或解释器）、调试器等工具集中在一个软件里，还可能带上项目管理、版本管理、包管理。An IDE bundles an editor, a translator (compiler or interpreter) and a debugger in one application, often with project, version and package management.
  • 编辑器的帮助：语法高亮、自动补全、自动缩进、括号配对检查、折叠代码块。Editor help: syntax highlighting, auto-complete, auto-indent, bracket matching, code folding.
  • 断点（breakpoint）：给某一行做标记，程序执行到这行暂停，方便查看此刻的变量值。A breakpoint marks a line where execution pauses so you can inspect variables at that moment.
  • 单步执行（single stepping）：一次执行一条语句，逐步观察程序走向和变量变化。Single stepping runs one statement at a time so you can follow control flow and see values change.
  • 变量监视（variable watch）：把某个变量放进监视窗口，随时看它的值怎么变。A variable watch pins a chosen variable in a window showing how its value changes.
  • 语法错误（syntax error）：违反语言的语法规则，例如漏写 ENDIF、关键字拼错、括号不配对；在翻译阶段就被发现，程序无法运行。A syntax error breaks the grammar of the language — a missing ENDIF, a misspelt keyword, unmatched brackets — and is caught at translation time, so nothing runs.
  • 逻辑错误（logic error）：语法完全正确，程序能跑出结果，但结果是错的，而且不会报错；例如把大于等于写成大于、循环少执行一次、公式抄错。A logic error leaves the grammar fine and the program running, but the answer is wrong and no error is reported: using > where >= was needed, looping one time too few, mistyping a formula.
  • 运行时错误（run-time error）：程序执行过程中出错停机，例如除以 0、数组下标越界、要打开的文件不存在。往往只有某些输入才触发。A run-time error halts the program while it is running: division by zero, an out-of-range index, a missing file. Often only some inputs trigger it.

三类错误各看一段伪代码，一眼就能认出来：One short piece of pseudocode for each error type makes them easy to recognise:

① 语法错误——漏了 ENDIF：1. Syntax error — the ENDIF is missing:

INPUT Score IF Score >= 50 THEN OUTPUT "通过" OUTPUT "结束" // 这里缺少 ENDIF，翻译时就会报错// ENDIF is missing here, so translation fails

② 逻辑错误——程序照跑，但除以 2 而不是除以个数，结果当然不对：2. Logic error — it runs fine but divides by 2 instead of by the count, so the answer is wrong:

Total ← 0 FOR i ← 1 TO 10 Total ← Total + i NEXT i OUTPUT Total / 2 // 语法没问题，可它并不报错，只是答案错了// perfectly valid syntax; no complaint, just the wrong answer

上面这一段的正确答案是总和 55 除以 10 等于 5.5，但它会算出 27.5。The correct answer here is 55 ÷ 10 = 5.5, but this version prints 27.5.

③ 运行时错误——输入 0 的那一刻才出的事：3. Run-time error — it only goes wrong once the input is zero:

INPUT Number OUTPUT 100 / Number // 一旦输入 0，就是除以 0，程序当场崩溃// typing 0 means division by zero and the program crashes

例题 · 判断错误类型Example · naming the error type

判断下面每种情况属于哪一类错误，并说明理由。
(a) 写完的程序一编译，屏幕上列出三处以红色标记的地方，程序没能运行。
(b) 程序顺利跑完，把全班的平均分算成了 27.5，而正确答案是 5.5。
(c) 输入人数为 0 时，程序突然停止并弹出一行提示。 Identify the error type in each case with a reason.
(a) Compiling fails with three red messages and nothing runs.
(b) The program finishes and reports an average of 27.5 when it should be 5.5.
(c) Entering 0 for the number of students makes the program stop with a message.
(a) 语法错误：违反了语言的语法规则，在翻译阶段就被发现，所以程序没能开始运行。(a) A syntax error: the grammar of the language was broken, so it was caught during translation and the program never started. (b) 逻辑错误：语法完全正确，程序也正常跑完，只是算法写错了（这里是把总和除以 2，而不是除以人数），所以结果不对却不报错。(b) A logic error: valid syntax, normal run, wrong method (dividing by 2 rather than by the count), so the answer is wrong with no error message. (c) 运行时错误：人数为 0 时做「总和 ÷ 人数」就是除以 0，程序在执行途中出错并停止。(c) A run-time error: with zero students the calculation divides by zero, so the program fails while running.

练习Practice

先判断错误类型，再写怎么改。Name the type first, then say how to fix it.

① 说出断点、单步执行、变量监视各自有什么用。1. Explain the purpose of breakpoints, single stepping and variable watches.

② 哪一种错误最不容易被发现？为什么？应该用什么办法找出来？2. Which type of error is hardest to spot? Why, and how do you track it down?

点开看答案Show answer

① 断点：让程序运行到指定那一行就暂停，方便看那一刻的状态；单步执行：一次只执行一条语句，逐步看清程序往哪儿走；变量监视：把变量的值显示在窗口里，跟着它的变化找问题。1. A breakpoint pauses execution at a chosen line so you can inspect that moment. Single stepping runs one statement at a time so you can follow the path taken. A variable watch displays a variable's value continuously so you can see where it goes wrong.

② 逻辑错误最不容易发现：语法完全正确，也不会有任何报错，只是结果不对。
找的办法：用多组数据（包括边界数据）测试并把手算结果和程序输出对比；再借助断点、单步执行和变量监视一步步看各个变量的实际取值，就能定位错在哪一句。2. A logic error is hardest: the syntax is perfect and no error is ever reported — only the answer is wrong. To find it: test with several sets of data including boundary cases, comparing the output with hand-calculated results; then use breakpoints, single stepping and watches to follow the values until the faulty statement appears.

— 第 6 站完成 —— Stop 6 done —

单元 4 完成 · 主线：先有系统软件把机器管起来，再靠翻译器把人的想法送到机器里Unit 4 done · the thread: system software runs the machine, then translators carry human ideas into it

回头看这一单元其实是三段：① 软件的分类——系统软件服务机器，应用软件服务人；② 操作系统——界面、内存与多任务、外设与驱动、文件管理、账号权限、安全，再加上随时能被中断叫停的能力；③ 语言与工具——低级语言快而难写、高级语言好写却要翻译，编译器一次翻完、解释器逐句翻译，最后在开发环境中用断点、单步、变量监视把三类错误揪出来。In hindsight this unit is three parts: 1. categories of software — system software serves the machine, applications serve people; 2. the operating system — interface, memory and multitasking, peripherals and drivers, file management, accounts, security, plus the ability to be stopped at any moment by an interrupt; 3. languages and tools — low-level is fast but painful, high-level is pleasant but needs translation, compilers translate once and interpreters line by line, and inside an IDE you hunt the three error types with breakpoints, stepping and watches.

下一步：单元 5 从一台机器走到互联网——地址、域名、协议，以及为什么这些东西要分层处理。Next: Unit 5 steps beyond one machine onto the internet — addresses, domain names, protocols and why it is all arranged in layers.

单元 5 · 互联网与它的用途Unit 5 · The internet and its usesPAPER 1

网址是怎么变成一台电脑的、网页是怎么跑到你屏幕上的、路上有哪些坏人、怎么防 · 这一单元几乎全是「解释题」，背下句式就是分How a web address becomes a computer, how pages reach your screen, what threats exist and how to stop them · this unit is mostly "explain" questions — learn the sentence patterns and the marks follow

本单元包括什么What this unit covers 7 站 · 约 55 分钟7 stops · about 55 min

  1. 互联网和万维网不是一回事The internet is not the world wide web
  2. 地址：IP、域名、URL 与 DNSAddresses: IP, domain names, URLs and DNS
  3. HTTP / HTTPS、浏览器、服务器、cookieHTTP/HTTPS, browsers, servers and cookies
  4. HTML 与网页是怎么搭起来的HTML and how web pages are built
  5. 网络安全威胁：六种坏人的招数Cyber security threats: six attacks
  6. 防护措施：一招对一招Protection: one measure per threat
  7. 数字货币与区块链 · 法律与伦理Digital currency, blockchain, law and ethics

建议顺序：站 1、2、3 是一条线（先有路再有地址再有车），站 5 和站 6 必须连着看——每种威胁都有对应的防护办法，考试最爱成对提问。Suggested order: stops 1–3 are one story (the road, then the address, then the traffic). Read stops 5 and 6 together — every threat has a matching defence and exams love asking for both.

1 互联网和万维网不是一回事The internet is not the same as the world wide web Internet · WWW互联网 · 万维网
这一站要会的：说出互联网和万维网的区别；说明 ISP、服务器、海底光缆各自干什么。 Goal: state the difference between the internet and the world wide web; describe the role of an ISP, a server and undersea cables.
说人话：这俩词天天被混着用，但真不是一回事。互联网 internet 是「一堆网络连起来」这件事本身——光纤、电缆、路由器、卫星、你家楼下的机柜，全是它。万维网 WWW 是「一堆网页用链接串起来」——它是跑在互联网上的一个节目。打个比方：互联网是高速公路网，万维网是路上跑的快递车。没有高速公路，快递车跑不了；路修好了，上面除了快递车，还能跑救护车（邮件）、客车（在线游戏）、货车（视频通话）。
顺便补个冷知识：互联网在 1970 年代就建起来了，那时候只能发邮件、传文件；万维网是 1989 年才出现的，网页和超链接是后加的装修。 In plain words: people use these two words as if they meant the same thing, but they do not. The internet is the network itself — fibre optics, copper cables, routers, satellites, the cabinet at the end of your street. The world wide web is a service that runs on top of it: pages joined together by hyperlinks. Think of a motorway network (the internet) and the delivery vans driving along it (the web). No motorway, no deliveries — but once the road exists you can also run ambulances (email), coaches (online games) and lorries (video calls).
Trivia for free: the internet dates back to the 1970s and could only carry email and file transfers; the web arrived in 1989, adding pages and hyperlinks on top.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 互联网：全球计算机网络互相连接而成的网络，本质是硬件基础设施（电缆、光纤、路由器、交换机）。The internet is a network of interconnected networks — essentially hardware infrastructure: cables, fibre optics, routers and switches.
  • 万维网：建立在互联网之上的信息服务，由网页、图片、视频以及连接它们的超链接组成。The world wide web is an information service running on the internet, made of pages, images, video and the hyperlinks between them.
  • ISP（互联网服务提供商）：把你家设备接进互联网的公司，给你分配 IP 地址、提供宽带或移动数据。An ISP (internet service provider) connects your devices to the internet, gives you an IP address and sells you broadband or mobile data.
  • 服务器 server：存放网站文件、几乎 24 小时开机的高性能计算机，收到请求就把网页发回去。A server is a powerful computer that stores the site's files, stays on almost all the time and sends pages back when asked.
  • 海底光缆：铺在海床上的光纤电缆，把各大洲连起来，绝大多数国际数据都靠它传输。Undersea fibre-optic cables lie on the sea bed, link the continents and carry almost all international data.
最容易丢分的地方：题目问「state the difference between the internet and the world wide web」，很多同学写「万维网是互联网的一部分」然后就没了——没说清一个是硬件一个是服务，通常只给一半分。 Classic trap: when asked for the difference, many candidates write "the web is part of the internet" and stop there. Without saying "hardware versus service" you usually get only half the marks.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：这一句直接背，两分题够用。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: memorise this sentence — it covers a two-mark question. The internet is the physical network of interconnected computers and cables. The world wide web is a service that runs on the internet, consisting of web pages linked together by hyperlinks.

例题 · 分清归属Example · which is which

下面四件事，属于互联网还是万维网？各用一句话说明理由。
(a) 家里装的光纤宽带　(b) 用浏览器打开一个新闻网页　(c) 连接两个大洲的海底光缆　(d) 点一下文字就跳到另一个页面 For each of the following, say whether it belongs to the internet or the web, and give a one-line reason.
(a) fibre broadband installed at home (b) opening a news page in a browser (c) an undersea cable linking two continents (d) clicking text to jump to another page
(a) 互联网——它是把你家接进网络的物理线路。(a) Internet — it is the physical link joining your home to the network. (b) 万维网——你消费的是网页这种信息服务。(b) Web — what you consume is the information service called a web page. (c) 互联网——光缆是实打实的硬件设施。(c) Internet — a cable is physical hardware. (d) 万维网——超链接正是万维网的发明核心。(d) Web — the hyperlink is exactly what the web was invented for.

练习Practice

先自己说一遍，再点开对答案。Say your answer out loud first, then open the answer.

① 用一句话说出互联网和万维网的区别。1. State the difference between the internet and the world wide web in one sentence.

② 说一说 ISP 和服务器分别做什么。2. Describe what an ISP does and what a server does.

③ 为什么跨洋访问网站要用海底光缆，而不是全靠卫星？3. Why are undersea cables used for intercontinental traffic instead of relying on satellites?

点开看答案Show answer

① 互联网是把全球网络连起来的硬件与线路；万维网是跑在它上面的网页与超链接构成的信息服务。1. The internet is the hardware and cabling that connects networks worldwide; the web is the information service of pages and hyperlinks running on top of it.

② ISP 负责把你接入互联网并分配 IP 地址；服务器负责存放网站文件，收到请求就把网页内容发回去。2. An ISP connects you to the internet and assigns your IP address; a server stores the site's files and returns page content when requested.

③ 光纤的带宽大、延迟低、成本更低也更稳定；卫星信号要上天下地跑很远，延迟明显，带宽有限，还受天气影响。（海底光缆坏了会修好几个月，这是它唯一的弱点。）3. Fibre gives far more bandwidth, lower latency and lower cost; satellite signals must travel up and back down, so latency is high, bandwidth limited and weather can interfere. (Cable repairs can take months — fibre's one weakness.)

— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 地址：IP、域名、网址与 DNSAddresses: IP, domain names, URLs and DNS URL · DNS网址 · 域名系统
这一站要会的：说出 IP 地址和域名各自是什么；把一个网址拆成协议、域名、路径三段；讲清 DNS 的作用。 Goal: state what an IP address and a domain name are; split a URL into protocol, domain and path; explain what DNS does.
说人话：网上的每台机器都得有个门牌号，这就是 IP 地址，长得像 192.168.1.20 这样四段数字。可惜人脑记不住数字，于是又给服务器起了名字，叫域名，比如 example.com。DNS 就是一本超级电话簿：你在浏览器里敲名字，它替你查出号码，然后电脑才按号码上门。
再往上，网址 URL 是「怎么去 + 去哪家 + 进哪个房间」三件事合起来：https:// 是用什么方式去（协议），www.example.com 是去哪家（域名），/about/team.html 是进哪个房间拿哪个文件（路径）。 In plain words: every machine on a network needs a house number — that is the IP address, four groups of digits like 192.168.1.20. Humans cannot remember digits, so servers also get a name, the domain name, such as example.com. DNS is the giant phone book: you type the name, it looks up the number, and only then does your computer knock on the right door.
One level up, a URL combines three things: how to travel (the protocol, https://), whose house (the domain, www.example.com) and which room and file (the path, /about/team.html).
记住这几点：Key points:
  • IP 地址：网络上设备的唯一编号，用来定位和路由数据。IPv4 是 4 段十进制数，每段 0–255，例如 192.168.1.20。An IP address uniquely identifies a device on a network so data can be routed to it. IPv4 is four decimal numbers, each 0–255, e.g. 192.168.1.20.
  • IP 地址会变（重新拨号、换 Wi-Fi 就可能换一个），所以不适合直接拿来打广告。IP addresses can change (reconnecting or switching Wi-Fi may give you a new one), which is why they are not advertised directly.
  • 域名：给人看的、好记的名字，全球唯一，要向注册机构付费注册；从右往左读：com / org / cn 是顶级域，中间是二级域，最左的 www 是主机名。A domain name is a memorable, globally unique name you register (and pay for); read it right to left: com/org/cn is the top-level domain, the middle part is the second-level domain, and www on the left is a host name.
  • URL（统一资源定位符）：协议://域名/路径，指向互联网上一个具体资源的完整地址。A URL (uniform resource locator) is protocol://domain/path — the full address of one specific resource.
  • DNS（域名系统）：分布式的数据库，把域名解析成 IP 地址；没有它，网址就打不开。DNS (domain name system) is the distributed database that resolves names into IP addresses; without it, no web address would open.
容易混的三个词：IP 地址是机器的号码，域名是机器的名字，URL 是某个文件的完整地址。example.com 只是域名，https://example.com/price.pdf 才是网址。 Three easily confused words: the IP address is the machine's number, the domain name is its name, and the URL is the full address of one file. example.com is only a domain; https://example.com/price.pdf is a URL.

例题 · 拆网址Example · taking a URL apart

把 https://www.school-ict.org/exam/paper2.pdf 拆成三段，并说明每一段的作用。 Split https://www.school-ict.org/exam/paper2.pdf into three parts and say what each does.
协议：https:// —— 用加密的方式传输（下一站细讲）。Protocol: https:// — transfer the data in encrypted form (more next stop). 域名：www.school-ict.org —— 从右往左读：org 是顶级域（非营利组织），school-ict 是二级域，www 是主机名。Domain: www.school-ict.org — read right to left: org is the top-level domain (non-profit), school-ict is the second-level domain, www is the host. 路径：/exam/paper2.pdf —— 服务器上的 exam 文件夹里那个叫 paper2.pdf 的文件。Path: /exam/paper2.pdf — the file paper2.pdf inside the exam folder on the server.

练习Practice

先找 :// 这两个符号，它前面是协议，后面到第一个 / 为止是域名。Find the :// first — everything before it is the protocol, everything up to the next / is the domain.

① 说出 IP 地址和域名的区别，并解释为什么需要 DNS。1. State the difference between an IP address and a domain name, and explain why DNS is needed.

② 拆开这个网址并说出顶级域是什么：http://news.bbc.co.uk/sport/index.html2. Split this URL and name the top-level domain: http://news.bbc.co.uk/sport/index.html

点开看答案Show answer

① IP 地址是设备在网络上的数字编号（机器用，可能变化）；域名是好记的名字（人用，全球唯一）。因为人记不住数字、机器只认数字，所以需要 DNS 在两者之间做翻译。1. An IP address is the device's numeric identifier (used by machines, may change); a domain name is a memorable name (used by people, globally unique). DNS is needed to translate between them.

② 协议 http://；域名 news.bbc.co.uk；路径 /sport/index.html。顶级域是 uk（国家顶级域），co 是二级域，bbc 是三级域，news 是主机名。2. Protocol http://; domain news.bbc.co.uk; path /sport/index.html. The top-level domain is uk (a country code); co is second level, bbc third, and news is the host name.

— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 HTTP、HTTPS、浏览器与 cookieHTTP, HTTPS, browsers and cookies Client-server客户端与服务器
这一站要会的：区分 HTTP 和 HTTPS；说明浏览器和 web 服务器各自做什么；解释客户端-服务器模型；说出 cookie 是什么、用来干什么。 Goal: distinguish HTTP from HTTPS; describe the role of a browser and a web server; explain the client-server model; say what a cookie is and what it is used for.
说人话：浏览器是「要东西的人」，web 服务器是「给东西的人」——这套分工就叫客户端-服务器模型：客户端开口要，服务器给，服务器从来不主动往你家塞东西。他们说话要守规矩，这套规矩叫 HTTP（超文本传输协议）。
问题是 HTTP 说的话是明文的，就像明信片，路上经手的路由器都能读。于是有了 HTTPS：先把内容加密再发（SSL/TLS），并且服务器要拿出一张数字证书证明「我真的是这家银行，不是冒牌的」。成了之后地址栏左边会出现一把小锁。
最后是 cookie：网站往你电脑里存的一小段文本。它有用的一面——你关掉浏览器明天再来，它还记得你登录过、购物车里有什么；也有让人不舒服的一面——广告公司用它跟踪你在哪些网站逛过。 In plain words: the browser is the one who asks; the web server is the one who gives. That division of labour is the client-server model: the client requests, the server responds, and the server never pushes things at you uninvited. The rules they speak by are called HTTP (hypertext transfer protocol).
The trouble is that HTTP is plain text — a postcard that every router on the route can read. So we have HTTPS: the content is encrypted first (using SSL/TLS), and the server must present a digital certificate proving "I really am this bank, not an impostor". When it works, a small padlock appears in the address bar.
Finally, a cookie is a short piece of text a site stores on your computer. The helpful side: tomorrow it still knows you are logged in and what is in your basket. The uncomfortable side: advertising companies use them to follow you from site to site.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • Web 浏览器：向服务器发请求、把收到的 HTML/CSS/图片渲染成人看得懂的页面。A web browser sends requests and renders the returned HTML, CSS and images into a page you can read.
  • Web 服务器：存着网站的文件，收到请求就把对应文件发回去，通常 24 小时在线。A web server holds the site's files and sends the requested one back; it is usually online around the clock.
  • HTTP 明文传输；HTTPS = HTTP + SSL/TLS 加密 + 数字证书，适合传密码、银行卡号。HTTP is unencrypted; HTTPS = HTTP plus SSL/TLS encryption plus a digital certificate — for passwords and card numbers.
  • SSL/TLS 负责把数据加密；数字证书由受信任的机构签发，用来证明网站身份。SSL/TLS does the encrypting; a digital certificate, issued by a trusted authority, proves the site's identity.
  • Cookie：网站存在用户电脑上的小文本文件，每次访问会带回服务器；用于记住登录状态、购物车、偏好设置、统计与广告追踪。A cookie is a small text file a site stores on your computer and gets back on every visit; used for logins, shopping baskets, preferences, analytics and ad tracking.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：「为什么要选 HTTPS 而不是 HTTP」是常考题，这两句都能直接抄。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: "why HTTPS rather than HTTP" comes up often — either sentence below earns the marks. HTTPS encrypts the data so that anyone intercepting it cannot read it. The digital certificate proves that the website belongs to the organisation it claims to be, so the user knows they are not on a fake site.

例题 · 解释题Example · explain questions

(a) 说明客户端和服务器在打开网页时分别做什么。
(b) 小明要在一个网站上输入银行卡号，说明为什么应该用 HTTPS 而不是 HTTP。 (a) Describe what the client and the server each do when a web page is opened.
(b) Ming is about to type a card number into a website. Explain why HTTPS should be used rather than HTTP.
(a) 客户端（浏览器）把网址交给 DNS 查到 IP，然后向服务器发请求；服务器找到对应的文件，把网页内容发回客户端；浏览器再把它渲染成页面。(a) The client (browser) uses DNS to find the IP address and sends a request; the server locates the file and returns the page content; the browser then renders it. (b) 因为 HTTPS 会把数据加密后再传输，被截获也读不出来；同时数字证书能验证网站身份，确认不是钓鱼网站；而 HTTP 是明文，中间人能直接看到卡号。(b) HTTPS encrypts the data so an interceptor cannot read it, and the certificate verifies the site's identity so it is not a phishing copy; HTTP is plain text and anyone in the middle can read the number.

练习Practice

答 cookie 题记得两面都写：对用户的好处 + 对隐私的隐患。For cookie questions, write both sides: benefit to the user and privacy risk.

① 说出 HTTP 和 HTTPS 的一个区别。1. Give one difference between HTTP and HTTPS.

② 说出 cookie 的两个用途，并说出它可能带来的一个问题。2. Give two uses of cookies and one problem they may cause.

点开看答案Show answer

① HTTPS 使用 SSL/TLS 对数据进行加密，HTTP 不加密。（另外 HTTPS 用数字证书验证网站身份，地址栏显示小锁。）1. HTTPS encrypts data using SSL/TLS; HTTP does not. (HTTPS also verifies identity with a certificate and shows a padlock.)

② 用途：记住登录状态、保存购物车内容、记住语言/主题偏好。问题：会被用来追踪用户浏览行为做广告，属于隐私隐患；如果 cookie 被偷，别人可能冒充你的登录状态。2. Uses: keeping you logged in, holding basket contents, remembering language or theme. Problem: they can track your browsing for advertising (a privacy risk), and a stolen cookie may let someone else use your session.

— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 HTML 与网页是怎么搭起来的HTML and how web pages are built Markup标记语言
这一站要会的：说出 HTML 代表什么；解释为什么它是标记语言而不是编程语言；说出标签和属性的作用；说明浏览器在其中的角色。 Goal: say what HTML stands for; explain why it is a markup language and not a programming language; state the role of tags and attributes; describe what the browser does.
说人话：写网页不是写程序，更像给文章做标记。你用标签把内容圈起来：这一行是标题、这一段是正文、这个位置放一张图、这几个字是个链接。这叫标记语言 markup language。为什么它不算编程语言？因为编程语言能定义变量、做判断、循环、算数；HTML 一样都不会，它只是「说明书」，照着画完就完事。
HyperText 是「超文本」的意思——文字里能藏链接，点一下跳到别处，这才是网页跟普通文档的根本区别。 In plain words: writing a web page is not programming; it is more like marking up a document. You wrap content in tags: this line is a heading, this block is a paragraph, put an image here, these words are a link. That is a markup language. Why is it not a programming language? Programming languages have variables, decisions, loops and arithmetic; HTML has none of them. It is a set of instructions for drawing, and nothing more.
HyperText means text that can carry links — click and you jump elsewhere. That is the essential difference between a web page and an ordinary document.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • HTML = HyperText Markup Language（超文本标记语言），用来创建网页。HTML = HyperText Markup Language, used to create web pages.
  • 标签 tag：用尖括号括起来的记号，绝大多数成对出现——开始标签 <p>、结束标签 </p>（多一个斜杠）。A tag is a marker in angle brackets; almost all come in pairs — an opening tag <p> and a closing tag </p> (with a slash).
  • 属性 attribute：写在开始标签里，给这个元素补充信息，例如 <img src="cat.png"> 里的 src 说明图片文件在哪。An attribute sits inside the opening tag and adds information, e.g. src in <img src="cat.png"> says where the image file is.
  • 常用标签：<h1> 大标题、<p> 段落、<a href="..."> 链接、<img> 图片、<br> 换行。Common tags: <h1> heading, <p> paragraph, <a href="..."> link, <img> image, <br> line break.
  • 浏览器的工作：读 HTML 文件 → 按标签的含义把内容渲染 render成页面（排版、显示图片、把链接做成可点的）。The browser reads the HTML file and renders it — laying out the text, showing images and making links clickable.
常考题：「Explain why HTML is not a programming language.」标准答案三条——没有变量、没有选择（IF）和循环（FOR/WHILE）这类结构、不能进行运算，它只是描述页面的结构和内容。 Common question: "Explain why HTML is not a programming language." The three expected points: no variables, no selection (IF) or iteration (FOR/WHILE), and no arithmetic — it only describes structure and content.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：把这三句连起来写，四分题稳了。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: put these three sentences together and a four-mark question is safe. HTML is a markup language, not a programming language. It does not contain variables, selection or iteration, and it cannot perform calculations. It only uses tags to describe the structure and content of a web page, which the browser then renders.

例题 · 认标签Example · reading tags

看这行代码，回答两个问题：<a href="home.html">回到首页</a>
(a) 哪个是标签名？哪个是属性？属性的值是什么？
(b) 浏览器会把「回到首页」显示成什么样？ Look at <a href="home.html">Back to home</a> and answer: (a) which part is the tag name, which is the attribute, and what is the attribute's value? (b) How will the browser display "Back to home"?
(a) 标签名是 a（链接）；属性是 href（目标地址）；属性的值是 home.html。(a) The tag name is a (an anchor); the attribute is href (the destination); its value is home.html. (b) 显示成一段可点击的链接文字（通常带下划线、换个颜色），点一下就跳到 home.html。(b) It appears as clickable link text (usually underlined and coloured); clicking takes you to home.html.

练习Practice

回答「为什么不是编程语言」时，说「它没有 XX」比说「它很简单」得分高。For "why not a programming language", saying "it has no …" scores better than "it is simple".

① 说出 HTML 的三个特点，说明它为什么是标记语言而不是编程语言。1. Give three reasons why HTML is a markup language and not a programming language.

② 写出一行 HTML，让浏览器显示一张叫 logo.png 的图片。2. Write one line of HTML that displays an image called logo.png.

点开看答案Show answer

① ① 没有变量，不能存数据；② 没有选择和循环，不能根据条件改变行为；③ 不能做算术和逻辑运算。它只用标签描述页面的结构和内容。1. 1. No variables, so no data is stored; 2. no selection or iteration, so behaviour cannot change with conditions; 3. no arithmetic or logic. It only describes structure and content with tags.

② <img src="logo.png">（写成 <img src="logo.png" /> 也对）2. <img src="logo.png"> (writing <img src="logo.png" /> is also fine)

— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 网络安全威胁：坏人们怎么下手Cyber security threats: how attackers strike Threats威胁
这一站要会的：说出六类威胁各自是什么、怎么起作用（每种一句话）；能对号入座判断某个场景属于哪种威胁。 Goal: name each of the six threats and say in one sentence how it works; identify which threat a scenario describes.
说人话：把网络安全想成保护三样东西：别让人偷看（机密性）、别让人乱改（完整性）、别让人用不了（可用性）。坏人的招数无非是从这三个方向下手。下面这六种是考试大纲点名要会的，每一种都配一句「它到底干了什么」：
恶意软件是总称，下面四个最常见：病毒要寄生在别的文件上，你不打开那个文件它就不发作；木马伪装成好软件骗你安装，进门后开后门；蠕虫最勤快，不用寄生、自己复制自己、顺着网络爬到别的机器；间谍软件最阴，安安静静记录你的按键和浏览记录再发出去。
钓鱼不做技术活，它做「像」——做个和银行一模一样的假网站，发邮件让你点进去输密码。暴力破解最笨最有效：把所有密码组合挨个试。DDoS是「人海战术」，控制一大堆机器同时访问一个网站，把它挤瘫。SQL 注入是在网页的输入框里塞进数据库命令，让网站执行本来不该执行的操作。社会工程则是直接攻人：打电话自称 IT 部门，让你把密码报出来。 In plain words: think of security as protecting three things: keep it private (confidentiality), keep it correct (integrity), keep it available (availability). Every attack pushes on one of these three. Here are the six the syllabus names, each with its "what it actually does" line:
Malware is the umbrella term; four types matter. A virus attaches itself to a file and only strikes when that file runs. A trojan pretends to be useful software so you install it, then opens a back door. A worm needs no host — it copies itself and crawls across the network on its own. Spyware is the quiet one: it records your keystrokes and browsing, then sends them out.
Phishing is not technical, it is about looking real — a copy of your bank's site, an email asking you to log in. Brute force is stupid and effective: try every password combination. DDoS is a crowd: thousands of machines visit one site at once until it collapses. SQL injection types database commands into a web form so the site does something it should not. Social engineering attacks the human instead — a caller claiming to be IT support asks for your password.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 病毒 virus：附加在文件上、需要宿主程序被运行才能传播，会破坏或篡改数据。A virus attaches to a file and needs the host program to run before it spreads; it corrupts or alters data.
  • 木马 trojan：伪装成正常软件，安装后在后台开后门、偷数据。A trojan disguises itself as legitimate software, then opens a back door and steals data.
  • 蠕虫 worm：不需要宿主文件，能自我复制并通过网络自动传播，吃带宽、拖慢系统。A worm needs no host file: it copies itself and spreads across the network, eating bandwidth and slowing systems.
  • 间谍软件 spyware：秘密监视用户（按键记录器 keylogger）并把信息发给第三方。Spyware secretly monitors the user (a keylogger records keystrokes) and sends the information to a third party.
  • 钓鱼 phishing：伪造邮件或网站冒充可信机构，骗用户交出密码、卡号。Phishing uses fake emails or websites impersonating a trusted organisation to trick users into giving passwords or card numbers.
  • 暴力破解 brute-force：程序反复尝试密码组合直到猜中；密码越短越简单，越快被猜出。A brute-force attack tries password combinations repeatedly until one works; short simple passwords fall fastest.
  • DDoS：大量被控制的设备同时向一台服务器发请求，让真正的用户访问不了。A DDoS attack floods one server with requests from many controlled devices so real users cannot get in.
  • SQL 注入：把恶意的数据库语句塞进输入框，让网站执行未授权的操作（查看、修改、删除数据）。SQL injection puts malicious database statements into an input box so the site performs unauthorised actions — viewing, changing or deleting data.
  • 社会工程：利用人的信任、恐惧或疏忽骗取信息，比如假装技术支持要密码。Social engineering exploits trust, fear or carelessness — pretending to be support staff and asking for a password.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：题目给你一个场景让你判断是哪类威胁，下面四句是最常见的四种。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: when a scenario is described and you must name the threat, these four sentences cover the common cases. Phishing: a fake email or website is used to trick the user into entering their password. Brute-force: every possible password is tried automatically until the correct one is found. DDoS: many computers send requests at the same time so the server cannot cope and genuine users are denied access. SQL injection: SQL commands are typed into an input box so that the database performs an unintended action.

例题 · 判断威胁类型Example · naming the threat

说出下面每种情况属于哪类威胁，并各写一句话解释。
(a) 小李收到一封「银行」邮件，点进去的页面和银行官网一模一样，他输入了账号密码。
(b) 某购物网站在 10 分钟内收到来自上万台电脑的访问请求，最后彻底打不开。
(c) 有人在搜索框里输入了一串奇怪的字符，结果整个会员表被显示出来。 Name the threat in each case and explain in one sentence.
(a) Li receives an email from his "bank"; the page looks identical to the real site and he types in his details.
(b) A shop's website receives requests from tens of thousands of computers within ten minutes and stops responding.
(c) Someone types strange characters into a search box and the whole customer table is displayed.
(a) 钓鱼——伪造的邮件和网站冒充可信机构，目的是骗取账号密码。(a) Phishing — a forged email and website impersonate a trusted organisation to steal credentials. (b) DDoS 攻击——海量设备同时发请求，把服务器挤瘫，让正常用户访问不了。(b) DDoS — a flood of requests from many devices overwhelms the server so genuine users are denied service. (c) SQL 注入——把数据库命令塞进输入框，让网站执行了原本不允许的查询。(c) SQL injection — database commands entered into an input box make the site run a query it should never allow.

练习Practice

判断的关键：看它攻的是「人」还是「机器」，是「偷」还是「堵」。The clue: does it attack the person or the machine, and is it stealing or blocking?

① 说出病毒和蠕虫的一个关键区别。1. Give one key difference between a virus and a worm.

② 公司前台接到电话，对方自称 IT 部，说要修电脑请她说出登录密码。这是哪类威胁？该怎么办？2. A receptionist gets a call from someone claiming to be IT, asking for her login password to fix a computer. Which threat is this, and what should she do?

③ 为什么短密码更容易被暴力破解攻破？3. Why are short passwords easier to break by brute force?

点开看答案Show answer
