① 病毒必须依附在宿主文件上、靠运行该文件才传播；蠕虫不需要宿主，能独立自我复制并通过网络自动传播。1. A virus needs a host file and only spreads when that file runs; a worm needs no host and replicates itself across the network independently.

② 这是社会工程。正确做法：不提供密码，挂断后用公司内部公布的官方号码回拨核实；正规 IT 部门永远不会主动索要密码。2. This is social engineering. She should not give the password; she should hang up and call back on the organisation's official published number. Genuine IT staff never ask for passwords.

③ 因为可能的组合数少得多。比如 6 位纯数字只有 10⁶ = 1 000 000 种组合，计算机几秒就能试完；12 位含大小写字母数字的组合数会大到无法穷举。3. There are far fewer combinations to try. Six digits give only 10⁶ = 1 000 000 possibilities, which a computer exhausts in seconds; twelve characters mixing cases and digits give far too many to try.

— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —
6 防护措施：一招对一招Protection: one measure for each threat Prevention防护
这一站要会的：说出防火墙、代理服务器、加密、生物识别、双因素认证、杀毒软件、打补丁、备份各自的作用；能把每种防护对上它防的威胁。 Goal: describe firewalls, proxy servers, encryption, biometrics, two-factor authentication, anti-virus, patching and backups; match each measure to the threat it stops.
说人话：防护的思路就三层：先别让他进来，再让他看不懂，最后留一份后路。
防火墙是大门保安，手里有一张规则表（哪些端口、哪些地址能过），不符合的通通拦下；代理服务器是中间的代办，外人只看到代理，看不到你内部电脑的真实地址，它还能缓存常用网页、顺手屏蔽违规网站。
加密让数据就算被截获也是一堆乱码，数字证书负责证明「这个网站是真的」，配上 HTTPS 就有了那把小锁。生物识别是「你是什么」——指纹、人脸、虹膜；单靠密码（「你知道什么」）不够牢，所以再加一层「你有什么」（手机验证码、令牌），这就叫双因素认证。
杀毒软件负责定期扫描、把可疑文件隔离或删除，但前提是病毒库要更新；打补丁是把软件上刚发现的漏洞补上（漏洞不补，防火墙再厚也没用）。最后备份是真正的后悔药：哪怕文件被勒索软件锁死，你还有另一份。 In plain words: defence has three layers: keep them out, make it unreadable, and keep a way back.
A firewall is the guard on the gate holding a rule table (which ports and addresses may pass); anything not on the list is refused. A proxy server is the middleman: outsiders see only the proxy, never your real internal addresses, and it can cache popular pages and block forbidden sites.
Encryption means intercepted data is unreadable rubbish, and a digital certificate proves "this site is genuine" — together with HTTPS that is what gives you the padlock. Biometrics is "something you are" — fingerprint, face, iris. A password alone ("something you know") is not enough, so add "something you have" (a phone code or token): that is two-factor authentication.
Anti-virus scans regularly and quarantines or deletes suspicious files, but only if its signature database is kept up to date; patching closes holes discovered in software (unpatched holes make even a thick firewall pointless). Finally, backups are the real undo button: even if ransomware locks your files, another copy exists.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 防火墙（硬件或软件）：按规则监控进出的网络流量，阻止未授权访问，并记录可疑活动。A firewall (hardware or software) monitors incoming and outgoing traffic against rules, blocking unauthorised access and logging suspicious activity.
  • 代理服务器：代替客户端去请求，隐藏内部 IP 地址、缓存常用页面加快访问、过滤/屏蔽网站。A proxy server makes requests on the client's behalf: it hides internal IP addresses, caches popular pages and can filter or block sites.
  • 加密 + 数字证书：SSL/TLS 把数据加密；证书由权威机构签发，用来验证网站身份；HTTPS 与小锁是这对组合的标志。Encryption plus a certificate: SSL/TLS encrypts the data; the certificate, issued by an authority, verifies identity; HTTPS and the padlock are the visible sign.
  • 生物识别：指纹、人脸、虹膜、声纹——属于「你是什么」，不容易被忘掉或转借。Biometrics — fingerprint, face, iris, voice — is "something you are", hard to forget or lend.
  • 双因素认证：从「你知道的（密码）、你拥有的（手机/令牌卡）、你是什么（生物特征）」里任选两种。Two-factor authentication uses two of: something you know (password), something you have (phone/token), something you are (biometric).
  • 杀毒软件：扫描文件、邮件和下载内容，与已知特征比对（或用行为判断），隔离或清除恶意软件，必须经常更新病毒库。Anti-virus scans files, email and downloads, compares them with known signatures (or watches behaviour) and quarantines or removes malware; the signature database must be kept updated.
  • 打补丁：安装厂商发布的更新，修补新发现的安全漏洞。Patching installs vendor updates that close newly discovered security holes.
  • 备份：把数据另存一份在不同地方（外接硬盘、异地、云端），定期做、并确认能恢复出来。Backups keep a second copy somewhere else (external drive, off-site, cloud), taken regularly and checked to be restorable.
最容易丢分的地方：题目问「how to prevent a virus」时，只写「装杀毒软件」不够，通常还要写定期更新病毒库和及时打补丁——因为杀毒软件只能识别它认识的病毒。 Classic trap: for "how to prevent a virus", "install anti-virus" alone is not enough — add keep the signature database updated and apply patches promptly, because anti-virus only recognises malware it already knows.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：「说出两种防护措施并解释」是高频题，选你最熟的两个，写成「做什么 + 为什么有用」两截。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: "describe two methods of protection and explain" is very common. Pick two you know well and write each as "what it does + why it helps". A firewall monitors all incoming and outgoing traffic and blocks anything that does not match its rules, so unauthorised users cannot reach the network. Taking regular backups means that if data is lost, corrupted or encrypted by ransomware, an earlier copy can be restored.

例题 · 配对Example · matching

为下面四种威胁各选一种最合适的防护措施，并说明理由。
(a) 蠕虫在公司网络里扩散　(b) 有人反复尝试登录管理员账号　(c) 员工收到伪造的银行邮件　(d) 服务器硬盘损坏 Choose one suitable protection for each threat and justify it.
(a) a worm spreading through a company network (b) repeated attempts to log in as administrator (c) staff receiving a forged bank email (d) a server hard disk fails
(a) 杀毒软件 + 打补丁：蠕虫靠系统漏洞传播，补丁把漏洞堵上，杀毒软件负责查杀已进来的。(a) Anti-virus plus patching: worms travel through software holes; patches close the holes and anti-virus removes what already got in. (b) 双因素认证 + 失败次数限制：就算密码被猜出来，没有第二个因素也进不去。(b) Two-factor authentication and a lockout after failed attempts: even a guessed password is not enough without the second factor. (c) 用户培训 + 邮件过滤：教员工核对网址与发件人，别点链接；过滤系统把可疑邮件挡在收件箱外。(c) Staff training plus email filtering: teach people to check the address and sender and not to click; filtering keeps suspicious mail out of the inbox. (d) 备份：硬盘坏了数据就没了，但存在别处的副本可以恢复。(d) Backups: a failed disk loses data, but a copy held elsewhere can be restored.

练习Practice

答「为什么有用」时，写「即使……也……」这个句式，很清楚。For "why it helps", the pattern "even if … still …" makes the point clearly.

① 说出防火墙的一个作用，并说明它挡住了什么。1. Give one function of a firewall and say what it blocks.

② 为什么双因素认证比只用密码更安全？2. Why is two-factor authentication more secure than a password alone?

③ 公司每周备份一次，但从来没有试过恢复。这样做有什么隐患？3. A company backs up weekly but has never tested a restore. What is the risk?

点开看答案Show answer

① 防火墙按规则检查进出的数据包，拦下未授权的访问和可疑连接，同时记录尝试入侵的行为。1. A firewall checks every packet against its rules and blocks unauthorised access and suspicious connections, logging the attempts.

② 因为攻击者即使拿到密码，也拿不到第二件东西（手机验证码或指纹），还是进不去；而单一密码一旦泄露就等于全开了门。2. Even if the attacker obtains the password, they still lack the second factor (phone code or fingerprint), so they cannot get in; with a single password, a leak hands over everything.

③ 备份可能早已失败或格式不对，从没验证过的备份等于没有备份。正确做法是定期做一次恢复演练，确认真的能还原。3. The backups may have been failing or saved in an unusable format: an untested backup is not really a backup. Restore drills should be run regularly to prove the data comes back.

— 第 6 站完成 —— Stop 6 done —
7 数字货币与区块链 · 法律与伦理Digital currency, blockchain, law and ethics Blockchain区块链
这一站要会的：说出什么是数字货币和区块链；解释挖矿；各列出数字货币的两个优点和两个缺点；说出数据保护法和版权法对企业的要求。 Goal: say what digital currency and a blockchain are; explain mining; give two advantages and two disadvantages; state what data protection and copyright law require.
说人话：数字货币（比如比特币）就是只存在于电脑里的钱，背后没有央行发行、没有政府担保，大家认它是因为它有一套公开可查的账本。
这套账本叫区块链：一段时间里的交易打包成一个「区块」，每个区块都记着上一个区块的指纹（哈希），于是一块咬一块串成链。你想改掉中间某一笔账？改完那个区块的指纹就变了，后面所有区块都对不上，而且全世界还有成千上万份副本，你得同时改掉一半以上才行——成本大到根本做不到。
挖矿就是「谁来记下一页账」的比赛：所有矿工用算力去猜一个数，猜中了就能把新区块挂上去，并获得新发行的币和手续费作为奖励。 In plain words: a digital currency such as Bitcoin exists only on computers. No central bank issues it and no government guarantees it; people accept it because there is a public, checkable ledger behind it.
That ledger is the blockchain: the transactions of a period are packed into a block, and every block records the fingerprint (hash) of the block before it, so the blocks chain together. Want to alter a transaction in the middle? The altered block's fingerprint changes, every later block stops matching, and there are thousands of copies worldwide — you would have to change most of them at once, which costs more than it is worth.
Mining is the contest to decide who writes the next page: miners use processing power to guess a number; the winner attaches the new block and is rewarded with newly issued coins plus transaction fees.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 数字货币 / 加密货币：只以电子形式存在的货币，用密码学保护交易，通常去中心化（不由中央银行控制）。A digital/crypto currency exists only electronically, is protected by cryptography and is usually decentralised (not controlled by a central bank).
  • 区块链：一份分布式的共享账本；交易打包成区块，区块之间用哈希首尾相连；每个参与者都保存一份完整副本。A blockchain is a distributed shared ledger: transactions are bundled into blocks, blocks are linked by hashes, and every participant keeps a full copy.
  • 挖矿：用计算机竞争求解一道困难的数学题，胜者把新区块加入链并获得数字货币奖励；需要大量算力和电力。Mining: computers race to solve a hard mathematical problem; the winner adds the block and earns currency. It needs huge processing power and electricity.
  • 优点：不需要中间机构，跨境转账快、手续费低；账本公开透明、极难被篡改；全天候可用。Advantages: no intermediary, so cross-border transfers are fast and cheap; the ledger is transparent and almost impossible to tamper with; available at all hours.
  • 缺点：价格波动极大；交易不可撤销，转错就没了；挖矿耗电巨大；可能被用于洗钱等非法活动；不受政府保护，密钥丢了钱就永远没了。Disadvantages: the price is extremely volatile; transactions are irreversible (a mistake is permanent); mining uses enormous electricity; it can be used for money laundering; there is no government protection and a lost key means the money is gone forever.
法律与伦理（同样会考）：Law and ethics (also examinable):
  • 版权法：保护原创作品（音乐、软件、文章、图片）；未经许可复制、传播属于侵权，也就是盗版。Copyright law protects original work (music, software, articles, images); copying or distributing it without permission is infringement — piracy.
  • 数据保护法：掌握个人数据的机构必须做到——安全保存、准确且及时更新、只用于收集的既定目的、不保存超过必要时间、不随意外传；本人有权查看和更正自己的数据。Data protection law requires organisations holding personal data to keep it secure, accurate and up to date, use it only for the purpose it was collected for, not keep it longer than necessary, and not pass it on improperly; individuals may view and correct their own data.
  • 隐私：个人有权决定自己的信息被如何使用；cookie 追踪、数据共享、社交平台过度收集都是隐私议题。Privacy: individuals control how their information is used; cookie tracking, data sharing and over-collection by social platforms are privacy issues.
  • 计算机滥用法：未经授权访问他人电脑、带有非法目的的入侵、擅自修改他人数据都属违法，哪怕没造成损失也算。Computer misuse law: unauthorised access to someone else's computer, intrusion with criminal intent, and unauthorised modification of data are offences — even if nothing was damaged.
  • 伦理：不共享账号密码、不抄袭他人作品、遵守软件许可协议、考虑自动化对就业的影响。Ethics: do not share accounts or passwords, do not plagiarise, respect software licences, and consider automation's effect on jobs.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：「为什么区块链上的数据很难被篡改」几乎每年都考，按这三步写。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: "why is data on a blockchain hard to change" appears almost every year — write it in these three steps. Each block contains the hash of the previous block. If the data in one block is changed, its hash changes, so every following block no longer matches. The attacker would have to change every copy of the ledger held by all the other nodes at the same time, which requires enormous computing power.

例题 · 区块链与法律Example · blockchain and the law

(a) 说明为什么区块链上的记录很难被偷偷修改。
(b) 一家网店收集了顾客的姓名、地址和银行卡号。依据数据保护法，说出它必须做到的三点。
(c) 小陈把付费买的软件发给同学用，这可能违反什么法律？ (a) Explain why records on a blockchain are hard to alter secretly.
(b) A shop collects customers' names, addresses and card numbers. Give three obligations under data protection law.
(c) Chen shares software he paid for with classmates. Which law might this break?
(a) 每个区块都含上一块的哈希，改动会让本块哈希变化、后续区块全部失配；而且账本在每个节点都有副本，攻击者必须同时改掉大部分副本，算力和成本高到不现实。(a) Each block holds the previous block's hash, so a change alters this block's hash and breaks every later block; every node also holds a copy, so the attacker must change most copies at once — impractical in cost and computing power. (b) ① 安全保存（如加密、访问控制）；② 只用于收集时说明的目的，不外传；③ 保持准确、最新，不长期留存，允许顾客查看和更正。(b) 1. keep it secure (encryption, access control); 2. use it only for the purpose it was collected for and do not pass it on; 3. keep it accurate and up to date, do not keep it too long, and let customers view and correct it. (c) 版权法。他买到的通常只是使用许可，把软件再复制分发给他人属于侵权（盗版）。(c) Copyright law. He bought a licence to use it, not the right to copy and distribute it — that is piracy.

练习Practice

优缺点题各写两条，每条都配一句理由，这样最稳。For advantages/disadvantages, write two of each with a reason attached.

① 说出使用数字货币的一个优点和一个缺点。1. Give one advantage and one disadvantage of using a digital currency.

② 用自己的话说一说什么是「挖矿」。2. In your own words, explain what mining is.

③ 学校网站放了一张从网上找来的照片，没有署名也没有付费。说出这涉及哪部法律，以及正确的做法。3. A school website uses a photo found online, with no credit and no payment. Which law is involved and what should they have done?

点开看答案Show answer

① 优点：跨境转账快、手续费低，因为不需要银行等中间机构。缺点：价格波动极大，或者交易不可撤销、转错无法追回。1. Advantage: fast, cheap international transfers because no intermediary such as a bank is involved. Disadvantage: the price is extremely volatile, or transactions are irreversible so a mistake cannot be undone.

② 挖矿是矿工用计算机比拼算力去解决一道很难的数学题；谁先算出符合要求的结果，谁就有权把新区块加到链上，并获得数字货币作为奖励。2. Mining is a race in which computers compete using processing power to solve a hard mathematical problem; the first to find a valid answer may add the next block and is rewarded with currency.

③ 涉及版权法。正确做法是取得作者许可（或使用明确允许免费使用的素材并署名），或改用自己拍摄/购买授权的图片。3. Copyright law. They should obtain the creator's permission (or use material explicitly licensed for free use, with credit), or use their own photo or a licensed stock image.

— 第 7 站完成 —— Stop 7 done —

单元 5 完成 · 主线：路 → 地址 → 车 → 装修 → 路上的坏人 → 怎么防 → 钱和规矩Unit 5 done · the thread: road → address → traffic → building → threats → defences → money and rules

把这一单元串成一句话就是：先有物理的互联网，再给它配上地址和名字，然后网页在上面跑；跑的路上有坏人想偷、想改、想堵，于是我们用防火墙、加密、认证、杀毒、补丁和备份一层层挡回去。最后两件事是延伸：钱也可以只存在于电脑上（区块链），而用这些东西要守法律和伦理。The whole unit in one sentence: there is a physical internet, we give it addresses and names, pages travel across it, attackers try to steal, alter or block that traffic, and we push back with firewalls, encryption, authentication, anti-virus, patches and backups. Two extensions close it off: money can exist purely digitally (blockchain), and all of this is governed by law and ethics.

最该练熟的两个句式：「互联网是硬件，万维网是服务」和「每种威胁 + 对应防护」。这两类题几乎年年出现。The two patterns to drill: "internet is hardware, the web is a service" and "threat plus its matching defence". Both come up nearly every year.

下一步：单元 6 讲机器怎么自己干活——传感器、机器人、人工智能，以及物联网、云、虚拟现实、量子计算这些新东西。Next: Unit 6 is about machines that work by themselves — sensors, robots, artificial intelligence, plus IoT, cloud, VR/AR and quantum computing.

单元 6 · 自动化与新兴技术Unit 6 · Automation and emerging technologiesPAPER 1

机器怎么自己干活 · 传感器、机器人、人工智能，再加物联网、云、虚拟现实和量子计算 · 这一单元以「说出优点缺点」为主，记住套路就很好拿分How machines work by themselves · sensors, robots, AI, plus IoT, cloud, VR/AR and quantum computing · this unit is mostly "give one advantage and one disadvantage" — learn the pattern and the marks come easily

本单元包括什么What this unit covers 6 站 · 约 50 分钟6 stops · about 50 min

  1. 自动系统：传感器 → 处理器 → 执行器Automatic systems: sensor → processor → actuator
  2. 八种传感器与模数转换Eight sensors and analogue-to-digital conversion
  3. 机器人：能自己干活的机器Robots: machines that work by themselves
  4. 人工智能：专家系统与机器学习AI: expert systems and machine learning
  5. 人工智能的利与弊 · 图灵测试Pros and cons of AI · the Turing test
  6. 物联网、云计算、VR/AR、量子计算IoT, cloud computing, VR/AR and quantum computing

建议顺序：站 1、2 是硬件基础，站 3、4 是往上搭的应用，站 5、6 是「评价题」——先弄懂再背优缺点，不然很容易背成两张皮。Suggested order: stops 1–2 are the hardware foundation, stops 3–4 build applications on top, stops 5–6 are evaluation questions. Understand first, then learn the advantages — otherwise they never stick.

1 自动系统：机器怎么自己干活Automatic systems: how a machine works alone Feedback loop反馈回路
这一站要会的：说出自动系统由哪三部分组成、各自干什么；用一个具体例子讲清反馈回路。 Goal: name the three parts of an automatic system and what each does; explain the feedback loop using a concrete example.
说人话：自动系统就像一个人：传感器是眼睛和皮肤（负责量），微处理器是脑袋（负责想：够不够？要不要动？），执行器是手和脚（负责干）。脑袋里存着一个设定值，比如「室温保持 20 度」。温度一低，脑袋就命令加热器打开；温度一上来，又命令关掉。
关键在最后那一步：加热器一工作，房间温度就变了，传感器会再量一次。这条「动作 → 改变环境 → 再检测」的回路叫反馈回路。有了它，机器才是「自动」的——不是做一次就完，而是一直盯着，一直调整。 In plain words: an automatic system is like a person: the sensor is the eyes and skin (it measures), the microprocessor is the brain (it decides: is it enough? should we act?), and the actuator is the hands and feet (it acts). The brain stores a set value, such as "keep the room at 20 °C". When the temperature drops it switches the heater on; when it rises, off again.
The last step is the point: once the heater works, the room changes and the sensor measures again. That loop of "act → change the environment → measure again" is the feedback loop. It is what makes the system automatic — not one action, but watch and adjust, forever.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 传感器 sensor：检测环境中的物理量（温度、光、压力等），把它变成电信号。A sensor detects a physical quantity in the environment (temperature, light, pressure…) and turns it into an electrical signal.
  • 微处理器 microprocessor：读取传感器的数值，与存储好的设定值 / 阈值比较，按程序决定发出什么指令。The microprocessor reads the sensor value, compares it with a stored set value / threshold and decides what command to send.
  • 执行器 actuator：把电信号变成实际动作——电机、加热器、水泵、阀门、继电器、蜂鸣器、指示灯都算。An actuator turns the electrical signal into real movement — motors, heaters, pumps, valves, relays, buzzers and lamps all count.
  • 反馈回路：执行器改变环境后，传感器再次测量，形成闭环，系统持续自动调节。The feedback loop: after the actuator changes the environment, the sensor measures again, forming a closed loop that keeps adjusting.

例题 · 两个例子Example · two systems

(a) 家用恒温器：设定 20 ℃，说明系统里每个部分做什么。
(b) 自动浇花装置：土壤湿度低于 30% 就浇水。说出三部分分别是什么，并说明回路怎么闭合。 (a) A home thermostat set to 20 °C — describe what each part does.
(b) An automatic plant watering system waters when soil moisture falls below 30%. Name the three parts and explain how the loop closes.
(a) 传感器：温度传感器不断测室温；微处理器：把测得值与 20 ℃ 比较，低于就开、高于或达到就关；执行器：加热器（或空调）。加热器改变室温 → 传感器再测 → 决策 → 循环。(a) Sensor: a temperature sensor keeps measuring the room. Processor: compares the reading with 20 °C, switching the heater on when it is lower and off when it is not. Actuator: the heater (or air conditioner). The heater changes the room, the sensor reads again, the decision repeats. (b) 传感器：土壤湿度传感器；微处理器：低于 30% 就输出开阀信号；执行器：电磁阀 / 水泵。浇水后土壤变湿，湿度传感器再次读数，达到阈值就停止——回路闭合，不用人管。(b) Sensor: a soil moisture sensor. Processor: outputs an open-valve signal when the value is below 30%. Actuator: a solenoid valve or pump. After watering, the soil is wetter, the sensor reads again and the valve closes at the threshold — the loop closes with no human involved.

练习Practice

答「三部分」的题时，每个部分都写成「它做什么」，别只写名字。For "three parts" questions, write what each part does, not just its name.

① 说出自动系统的三个组成部分，并各用一句话说明作用。1. Name the three parts of an automatic system and say in one sentence what each does.

② 自动门在有人靠近时打开。指出传感器、微处理器、执行器分别是什么。2. An automatic door opens when someone approaches. Identify the sensor, processor and actuator.

③ 恒温器如果没有反馈回路（加热器打开后就再也不测温度），会发生什么？3. What happens if a thermostat has no feedback loop (the heater switches on and the temperature is never measured again)?

点开看答案Show answer

① 传感器检测环境并输出电信号；微处理器把测得值与设定值比较并做出决策；执行器根据指令产生实际动作。1. The sensor detects the environment and outputs an electrical signal; the microprocessor compares the reading with the set value and decides; the actuator carries out the physical action.

② 传感器：红外或超声波传感器（检测有人靠近）；微处理器：判断是否有人并发出开门信号；执行器：电机（带动门开合）。2. Sensor: an infrared or ultrasonic sensor detecting an approaching person. Processor: decides whether someone is there and sends the open signal. Actuator: the motor that slides the door.

③ 系统就变成了「开环」：房间会一直被加热，越来越热，永远停不下来（或者反过来一直不加热）。反馈回路的作用正是让系统知道自己做的动作有没有效果。3. The system becomes "open loop": the room keeps heating and never stops (or never starts). The feedback loop exists precisely so the system knows whether its own action had the intended effect.

— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 八种传感器与模数转换Eight sensors and analogue-to-digital conversion ADC模数转换
这一站要会的：说出至少六种传感器分别测什么；解释模拟信号和数字信号的区别；说明为什么必须用模数转换。 Goal: state what at least six sensors measure; explain the difference between analogue and digital signals; say why ADC is necessary.
说人话：现实世界里几乎所有量都是连续的——温度可以从 20.1 慢慢爬到 20.2，中间经过无数个值。传感器把这个量变成电压，电压也是连续变化的，这就是模拟信号。
可微处理器只认 0 和 1。于是需要一个翻译官：模数转换器 ADC。它干两件事——采样（每隔一小会儿量一次，比如每秒 10 次）和量化（把量到的电压四舍五入成最接近的整数，再写成二进制）。
这里有个取舍：采得越密、每个值用越多位表示，还原得越像，但数据量也越大。就像把照片切成更多更小的格子——清楚，但文件变大。反过来，采样太稀疏的话，快速变化的信号会被「跳过」，还原出来就失真了。 In plain words: almost everything in the real world is continuous — the temperature creeps from 20.1 to 20.2, passing through countless values in between. A sensor turns that quantity into a voltage that also varies continuously: an analogue signal.
But a microprocessor only understands 0 and 1. So we need a translator, the analogue-to-digital converter (ADC), which does two jobs: sampling (measuring at fixed intervals, say ten times a second) and quantisation (rounding each measured voltage to the nearest whole number and writing it in binary).
There is a trade-off: the more often you sample and the more bits you use per value, the truer the result — and the bigger the data. Like cutting a photo into more, smaller squares: sharper, but heavier. Sample too rarely and a fast-changing signal is skipped altogether, so the reconstruction is wrong.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 模拟信号：连续变化，可以取范围内的任意值（真实世界的温度、声音、光都是）。An analogue signal varies continuously and can take any value in a range (real-world temperature, sound and light are all analogue).
  • 数字信号：只取离散的若干值，计算机里就是 0 和 1。A digital signal takes only discrete values — in a computer, just 0 and 1.
  • ADC：按固定时间间隔对模拟信号采样，把每个采样值量化成最接近的整数，再编码成二进制。为什么必须转？因为微处理器只能处理数字信号。An ADC samples the analogue signal at fixed intervals, quantises each sample to the nearest whole number and encodes it in binary. Why is it needed? Because the microprocessor can only handle digital signals.
  • 分辨率与采样率越高，还原越精确，但产生的数据量越大，需要的存储和带宽也更多。Higher resolution and sample rate mean a more faithful result but much more data to store and transmit.
  • 八种传感器：温度（冷热）、光（明暗）、压力（受力、重量）、湿度（水分）、红外（热源、人体、遥控）、超声波（距离）、加速度（晃动、倾斜、下落）、磁场（方向、方位）。Eight sensors: temperature, light, pressure (force/weight), humidity/moisture, infrared (heat sources, people, remotes), ultrasonic (distance), acceleration (tilt, shake, fall) and magnetic field (direction/heading).
常考的坑：题目问「为什么传感器不能直接连到微处理器上」，标准答案是传感器输出模拟信号，而微处理器只能处理数字信号，因此需要模数转换器。只答「需要转换」不给分，要说清是模拟转数字。 Classic trap: for "why can't a sensor be connected directly to a microprocessor", the expected answer is the sensor outputs an analogue signal while the microprocessor can only process digital signals, so an ADC is needed. Saying merely "a conversion is needed" scores nothing — say analogue to digital.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：把模拟、数字、ADC 这三层写清楚，四分题就到手。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: cover the three layers — analogue, digital, ADC — and a four-mark question is yours. Sensors produce an analogue signal which varies continuously. A microprocessor can only process digital signals made of 0s and 1s. An analogue-to-digital converter samples the signal at regular intervals and converts each sample into a binary value.

例题 · 选传感器Example · choosing sensors

为下面四个场景各选一种最合适的传感器，并说明它测的是什么。
(a) 晚上路上有人经过时自动亮灯　(b) 洗衣机检测桶里水位　(c) 手机屏幕横竖自动切换　(d) 无人机知道自己离地面多高 Choose one suitable sensor for each scenario and say what it measures.
(a) a light that switches on when someone passes at night (b) a washing machine checking the water level (c) a phone switching between portrait and landscape (d) a drone knowing its height above the ground
(a) 红外传感器（或超声波）：检测人体发出的红外热辐射 / 有人靠近。(a) An infrared sensor (or ultrasonic): detects the infrared heat given off by a person, or that someone is near. (b) 压力传感器（水位越高底部压力越大）或湿度传感器。(b) A pressure sensor (more water means more pressure at the bottom) or a moisture/humidity sensor. (c) 加速度传感器：感知重力方向，判断手机是横着还是竖着。(c) An accelerometer: senses the direction of gravity and so whether the phone is portrait or landscape. (d) 超声波传感器：发出声波并接收回波，用时间算出距离。(d) An ultrasonic sensor: sends out a sound pulse, receives the echo and works out the distance from the time taken.

练习Practice

选传感器时先问自己：这个场景里「变了的东西」是什么？When choosing a sensor, ask first: what is the thing that changes in this scenario?

① 说出模拟信号和数字信号的一个区别。1. Give one difference between an analogue and a digital signal.

② 为什么自动系统里传感器和微处理器之间通常需要模数转换器？2. Why is an ADC usually needed between the sensor and the microprocessor in an automatic system?

③ 采样频率提高一倍会带来什么好处和什么代价？3. What is gained and what is lost if the sampling rate is doubled?

点开看答案Show answer

① 模拟信号连续变化，可取范围内的任意值；数字信号只能取离散的若干个值（计算机里是 0 和 1）。1. An analogue signal varies continuously and can take any value in the range; a digital signal can only take discrete values (0 and 1 in a computer).

② 因为传感器输出的是模拟信号，而微处理器只能处理数字信号，ADC 负责把模拟信号采样、量化成二进制。2. Because the sensor produces an analogue signal while the microprocessor handles only digital signals; the ADC samples and quantises the analogue signal into binary.

③ 好处：还原更接近原始信号，快速变化的细节不容易漏掉。代价：数据量翻倍，需要更多存储空间和更快的传输。3. Gain: the reconstruction is closer to the original and fast-changing detail is less likely to be missed. Cost: the data doubles, needing more storage and faster transmission.

— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 机器人：能自己干活的机器Robots: machines that work by themselves Robot机器人
这一站要会的：说出机器人的定义与三个组成部分；举出三个应用场景；各说出两个优点和两个缺点。 Goal: define a robot and name its three parts; give three application areas; give two advantages and two disadvantages.
说人话：机器人其实就是上一站那个自动系统加了腿和胳膊。它同样靠传感器看世界，用处理器做判断，但执行器变成了电机和机械臂，能真正动起来。三个词概括它的工作：感知 sense → 规划 plan → 行动 act，然后回到感知，一圈一圈转。
它跟普通机器最大的不同是「可编程」——同一台机械臂，换个程序就能从焊汽车变成搬箱子，不用改造硬件。
优点很直观：稳、准、不累、不怕死。汽车厂里的焊接机器人一天干 24 小时，每一道焊缝都一样；火星车能去人根本活不下来的地方。缺点也很实在：贵、难修、不会随机应变。它擅长重复和精确，遇到没见过的场面就容易卡住；而且一旦大规模上岗，原来做这些工作的人就没了岗位。 In plain words: a robot is last stop's automatic system with legs and arms attached. It still senses with sensors and decides with a processor, but the actuators are motors and mechanical arms that actually move. Three words cover the cycle: sense → plan → act, then back to sense, round and round.
The big difference from an ordinary machine is that a robot is programmable: the same arm can weld car bodies today and move boxes tomorrow just by changing the program — no hardware change needed.
The advantages are obvious: steady, precise, tireless and expendable. A welding robot in a car plant works 24 hours and every weld is identical; a Mars rover goes where no human could survive. The disadvantages are equally real: expensive, hard to maintain and poor at improvising. Robots excel at repetition and precision but stall when faced with something unfamiliar; and when they arrive in numbers, the people who did that work lose their jobs.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 定义：机器人是能感知环境、做出判断并自动完成一连串动作的可编程机器。Definition: a robot is a programmable machine that can sense its environment, make decisions and carry out a series of actions automatically.
  • 三部分：① 传感器（看、听、量）② 处理器/控制器（比较、规划、发指令）③ 执行器（电机、机械臂、轮子）。Three parts: 1. sensors (see, hear, measure); 2. a processor/controller (compare, plan, command); 3. actuators (motors, arms, wheels).
  • 应用：制造（焊接、喷漆、装配、搬运）、医疗手术、太空探索、家务（扫地、割草）、危险环境（拆弹、消防、核电站、深海）、农业与仓储物流。Uses: manufacturing (welding, painting, assembly, moving), surgery, space exploration, domestic work (vacuuming, mowing), hazardous environments (bomb disposal, firefighting, nuclear plants, deep sea), farming and warehousing.
  • 优点：效率高、精度高且稳定一致、可 24 小时连续工作、能进入人类无法生存的环境、不会疲劳。Advantages: high efficiency, precision and consistency, 24-hour operation, able to enter places humans cannot survive, no fatigue.
  • 缺点：初期成本高、需要专业人员维护与编程、对未预料的情况应变能力差、会取代人工造成失业、出事故时责任难划分。Disadvantages: high initial cost, needs skilled maintenance and programming, poor at handling the unexpected, replaces workers, and responsibility for accidents is hard to assign.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：优缺点题写成「一点 + 一句解释」最容易拿满分。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: for advantages and disadvantages, write a point plus one sentence of explanation — the easiest way to full marks. One advantage is that robots can work continuously without getting tired, so production never stops. One disadvantage is that they are very expensive to buy and need specialist staff to maintain and program them.

例题 · 优缺点Example · pros and cons

一家工厂打算用机器人代替人工装配手机。
(a) 说出这种做法的两个优点，并各写一句解释。
(b) 说出两个缺点，并各写一句解释。
(c) 指出这套机器人系统里的传感器和执行器分别可能是什么。 A factory plans to replace human workers with robots that assemble phones.
(a) Give two advantages with an explanation each. (b) Give two disadvantages with an explanation each. (c) Name a likely sensor and actuator in this robot system.
(a) ① 精度高且一致：每个动作都完全一样，次品率下降。② 可以 24 小时工作：不用休息和换班，产量提高。(a) 1. Precision and consistency: every movement is identical, so defects fall. 2. 24-hour operation: no breaks or shift changes, so output rises. (b) ① 初期投入大：买机器人、改造产线、培训人员都要钱。② 灵活性差：换一款手机就要重新编程调试，遇到意外情况不会随机应变。(b) 1. Large initial cost: buying robots, rebuilding the line and training staff all cost money. 2. Inflexibility: a new phone model means reprogramming and recalibration, and unexpected situations are not handled well. (c) 传感器：摄像头、压力传感器（确认零件放好）、红外传感器；执行器：机械臂的电机与夹爪、传送带电机。(c) Sensors: a camera, pressure sensors (to check a part is seated), infrared sensors. Actuators: the motors and grippers of the arm, and the conveyor motor.

练习Practice

说机器人的组成时，直接套用「传感器 / 处理器 / 执行器」这三格，不会漏。Use the three slots "sensor / processor / actuator" and you will never miss a part.

① 说出一个机器人的定义。1. Give a definition of a robot.

② 说出机器人的两个优点和两个缺点。2. Give two advantages and two disadvantages of using robots.

③ 为什么手术机器人比人手更适合做某些精细操作？3. Why can surgical robots suit some delicate procedures better than a human hand?

点开看答案Show answer

① 机器人是一种可编程的机器，能够感知环境、做出判断，并自动执行一连串动作。1. A robot is a programmable machine able to sense its environment, make decisions and automatically carry out a series of actions.

② 优点：精度高且稳定、可全天候工作、能进入危险环境。缺点：成本高、维护需要专业人员、应变能力差、可能取代人工。2. Advantages: precise and consistent, works around the clock, can enter dangerous environments. Disadvantages: high cost, needs specialist maintenance, poor at coping with the unexpected, may replace workers.

③ 因为机械臂可以消除人手的抖动、把动作缩小到很细微的幅度，还能长时间保持稳定；配合摄像头能放大视野，创口也可以开得更小。3. The arm removes hand tremor, can scale movements down to tiny ones and holds steady for hours; with a camera the view is magnified, so incisions can be much smaller.

— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 人工智能：专家系统与机器学习AI: expert systems and machine learning Expert system专家系统
这一站要会的：说出人工智能的定义；说出专家系统的三个组成部分及各自作用；说明机器学习和专家系统的区别。 Goal: define artificial intelligence; name the three parts of an expert system and their roles; explain how machine learning differs from an expert system.
说人话：人工智能 AI 就是「让机器去做那些本来只有人才能做的事」——看懂图片、听懂说话、下棋、做诊断。
最早的一派做法叫专家系统：把某个领域专家的经验写成一条条「如果……就……」的规则存起来。比如医生看病：如果发烧 + 咳嗽 + 嗓子疼，就判断可能是感冒。系统里三样东西：知识库放事实（病人的症状），规则库放规则（如果……就……），推理机负责把事实往规则上一套，推出结论。
后来出现另一派做法叫机器学习：干脆不写规则，改成喂大量数据。你给它看成千上万张标好「猫 / 狗」的照片，它自己琢磨出区分的规律，形成一个「模型」；以后再给它一张新照片，它就能猜出是猫还是狗。
一句话对比：专家系统是人教它规则，机器学习是它自己从数据里总结规律。 In plain words: artificial intelligence (AI) means getting a machine to do things that used to need a human — understand pictures, follow speech, play chess, make a diagnosis.
The older approach is the expert system: write an expert's experience down as "if … then …" rules. A medical example: if fever and cough and sore throat, then probably a cold. It has three parts: the knowledge base holds the facts (the patient's symptoms), the rule base holds the rules (if … then …), and the inference engine matches facts against rules to reach a conclusion.
The newer approach is machine learning: no hand-written rules at all — instead, feed in a huge amount of data. Show it thousands of photos labelled "cat" or "dog" and it works out the difference for itself, producing a model; show it a new photo afterwards and it can tell you which it is.
One-line contrast: an expert system is taught rules; machine learning discovers patterns in data.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • AI 定义：让机器系统表现出需要人类智能才能完成的能力，例如学习、推理、识别图像、理解语言、解决问题。Definition of AI: getting a machine system to show abilities that would need human intelligence — learning, reasoning, recognising images, understanding language, solving problems.
  • 专家系统：模拟人类专家的决策能力。由知识库（已知事实）、规则库（如果……就……的规则）、推理机（把事实与规则匹配、推导结论）和用户界面组成。An expert system mimics an expert's decision making: a knowledge base (known facts), a rule base (if…then rules), an inference engine (matches facts to rules and derives conclusions) and a user interface.
  • 机器学习：用大量数据训练出模型，让系统自己找出规律，再用模型对新数据做出预测或判断。Machine learning trains a model on large amounts of data so the system finds the patterns itself, then uses the model to predict or classify new data.
  • 核心区别：专家系统的规则由人编写，机器学习没有显式规则，规律来自数据。Key difference: in an expert system humans write the rules; in machine learning there are no explicit rules — the patterns come from the data.
  • 常见应用：垃圾邮件过滤、商品与视频推荐、语音助手、人脸识别、医学影像辅助诊断、自动驾驶。Typical uses: spam filtering, product and video recommendations, voice assistants, face recognition, medical image support, self-driving cars.
容易丢分的地方：题目问「说明专家系统和机器学习的区别」，只写「一个用规则一个不用」不够，最好补上「机器学习需要大量训练数据」这一句。 Classic trap: for "explain the difference", "one uses rules and the other does not" is not enough — add "machine learning needs a large amount of training data".
🗣 考场英文怎么说：专家系统的四件事按这个顺序说：存事实、存规则、匹配推理、给出结论。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: describe an expert system in this order: store facts, store rules, match and reason, give a conclusion. An expert system has a knowledge base containing facts and a rule base containing IF…THEN rules. The inference engine matches the facts supplied by the user against the rules and draws a conclusion, which is displayed through the user interface.

例题 · 判断类型Example · which kind of AI

(a) 一个植物诊断系统里有 200 条规则和一批已知植物特征，用户症状输进去就能得到结论。说出它的两个组成部分及其作用。
(b) 某邮箱系统读了上百万封被用户标记为「垃圾邮件」的信件后，开始能自动拦截新的垃圾邮件。这属于哪种人工智能？为什么？ (a) A plant diagnosis system holds 200 rules and a set of known plant features; entering symptoms gives a conclusion. Name two of its components and their roles.
(b) A mail system has read millions of messages users marked as spam and now blocks new spam automatically. Which kind of AI is this, and why?
(a) 知识库存放已知事实（植物特征、用户输入的情况）；规则库存放「如果……就……」的诊断规则；推理机把两者匹配，推出可能的病害。用户界面负责输入问题、显示结论。(a) The knowledge base stores known facts (plant features and the user's inputs); the rule base stores the if…then diagnostic rules; the inference engine matches them and derives the likely disease. The user interface takes questions in and shows the answer. (b) 属于机器学习。因为没有人手写「什么算垃圾邮件」的规则，而是用大量已标记的数据训练出模型，让它自己总结出规律，再对新邮件做判断。(b) Machine learning. Nobody wrote rules saying what counts as spam; a model was trained on large amounts of labelled data and found the patterns itself, then applied them to new mail.

练习Practice

先判断「规则是谁写的」，答案就出来了。Ask "who wrote the rules?" and the answer follows.

① 说出人工智能的一个定义。1. Give a definition of artificial intelligence.

② 说出专家系统的三个组成部分。2. Name three components of an expert system.

③ 说出机器学习与专家系统的一个区别。3. Give one difference between machine learning and an expert system.

点开看答案Show answer

① 人工智能是让机器表现出需要人类智能才能完成的能力，如学习、推理、识别图像、理解语言、解决问题。1. AI is getting machines to show abilities that need human intelligence: learning, reasoning, image recognition, language understanding, problem solving.

② 知识库（事实）、规则库（如果……就……的规则）、推理机（匹配事实与规则并推出结论），还可以加上用户界面。2. The knowledge base (facts), the rule base (if…then rules) and the inference engine (matches facts to rules and concludes); the user interface may be added.

③ 专家系统的规则由人编写；机器学习没有显式规则，而是从大量训练数据中自己学出规律。3. An expert system's rules are written by people; machine learning has no explicit rules and learns patterns from large amounts of training data.

— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 人工智能的利与弊 · 图灵测试Pros and cons of AI · the Turing test Turing test图灵测试
这一站要会的：各说出人工智能的两个好处和两个坏处；描述图灵测试是怎么做的。 Goal: give two benefits and two drawbacks of AI; describe how the Turing test works.
说人话：人工智能好在哪？它不困、不累、不抱怨，还能一次看完全人类看不完的数据。医院里它先扫一遍影像，把可疑的地方圈出来提醒医生；工厂里它盯着几万个传感器预测哪台机器要坏。
坏处也得说清楚。第一是饭碗——重复性的岗位被替代得最快。第二是偏见——模型是从人产生的数据里学的，如果历史数据里本来就带歧视，学出来的结果也会带，而且看起来还挺「客观」。第三是隐私——要学就得收数据，收得越多隐私风险越大。第四是责任——自动驾驶出了事故，是车主、厂商还是写算法的人负责？目前还没有一致答案。
图灵测试是 1950 年图灵提出的一个思想实验：让提问者隔着屏幕同时跟一个人和一台机器打字聊天，如果他分不清哪个是机器，就认为这台机器表现出了智能。它很聪明的一点是绕开了「什么叫思考」这种吵不清的问题，只看结果。但它也有局限：只测「像不像」，不测「懂不懂」——现在的聊天机器人能骗过不少人，可它并不真的理解自己在说什么。 In plain words: what is good about AI? It never sleeps, never tires, never complains, and can read more data than all of humanity combined. In hospitals it pre-reads scans and flags suspicious areas for the doctor; in factories it watches thousands of sensors and predicts which machine will fail.
The downsides matter too. First, jobs — repetitive roles are replaced fastest. Second, bias — a model learns from data produced by people, so if the historical data is discriminatory, the output will be too, and it will look reassuringly "objective". Third, privacy — learning requires collecting data, and the more you collect the greater the risk. Fourth, responsibility — when a self-driving car crashes, is it the owner, the manufacturer or the programmer? Nobody agrees yet.
The Turing test is a thought experiment from 1950. An interrogator types to a human and a machine at the same time, through a screen; if they cannot tell which is the machine, the machine is said to show intelligence. Its cleverness is that it steps around the unanswerable question "what is thinking?" and looks only at results. But it has limits: it tests whether a machine seems human, not whether it understands — today's chatbots fool plenty of people without comprehending a word they say.
好处（记住四条）：Benefits (learn four):
  • 自动化重复工作：把人从枯燥流程里解放出来，速度快、错误少。Automating repetitive work: frees people from dull processes, faster and with fewer errors.
  • 24 小时不间断：不需要休息、轮班和假期。Works around the clock: no breaks, shifts or holidays.
  • 处理海量数据：能在极短时间内分析人类几辈子都看不完的数据。Handles huge data: analyses in moments what a human could not read in a lifetime.
  • 辅助诊断与危险作业：帮医生发现早期病变；代替人进入辐射、火灾、深海等环境。Supports diagnosis and dangerous work: helps doctors spot early disease; replaces people in radioactive, fire or deep-sea environments.
坏处（记住四条）：Drawbacks (learn four):
  • 失业：重复性岗位被取代，相关从业者需要重新培训。Unemployment: repetitive jobs disappear and workers must retrain.
  • 偏见：训练数据里的人类偏见会被学进去，做出不公平的判断。Bias: prejudice in the training data is learned and produces unfair decisions.
  • 隐私：需要收集大量个人数据，泄露或滥用的风险上升。Privacy: large amounts of personal data must be collected, raising the risk of leaks or misuse.
  • 责任归属不清：出错时难以确定由开发者、使用者还是厂商负责；另外缺少常识与同理心。Unclear responsibility: when things go wrong it is hard to say whether the developer, user or manufacturer is at fault; AI also lacks common sense and empathy.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：图灵测试按「谁 + 怎么聊 + 什么算通过」三步写。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: write the Turing test in three steps: who, how they communicate, and what counts as passing. In the Turing test a human interrogator has a text-only conversation with both a human and a computer, without being able to see or hear either. If the interrogator cannot reliably tell which one is the computer, the computer is said to be intelligent.

例题 · 利弊与图灵测试Example · pros, cons and the Turing test

(a) 某公司用人工智能筛选简历。说出这种做法的两个好处和两个风险。
(b) 描述图灵测试是怎么进行的。
(c) 有人工智能通过了图灵测试，这能说明它真的「理解」语言吗？ (a) A company uses AI to screen CVs. Give two benefits and two risks.
(b) Describe how the Turing test is carried out.
(c) If an AI passes the Turing test, does that prove it really understands language?
(a) 好处：① 几秒钟就能处理上千份简历，速度快；② 按同一标准打分，一致性好。风险：① 若历史录用数据存在偏见，模型会学会歧视；② 涉及大量个人信息，存在隐私与数据保护问题。(a) Benefits: 1. thousands of CVs processed in seconds — speed; 2. the same criteria applied to all — consistency. Risks: 1. if past hiring data is biased the model learns the discrimination; 2. large amounts of personal data create privacy and data protection problems. (b) 提问者通过键盘与一个人和一台机器同时对话，看不到也听不到对方；如果他分辨不出哪个是机器，机器就算通过测试。(b) An interrogator types to a human and a machine at the same time, unable to see or hear either; if they cannot tell which is the machine, the machine passes. (c) 不能。图灵测试只判断表现得像不像人，不检验是否真正理解含义或具备意识；系统完全可能靠模仿给出正确答案。(c) No. The test only judges whether the machine behaves like a human, not whether it understands meaning or has consciousness; a system can produce convincing answers purely by imitation.

练习Practice

利弊题一定要「一点 + 一句解释」，光列名词往往只有一半分。For pros and cons, always give a point plus one sentence of explanation — a bare list usually scores half.

① 说出使用人工智能的两个好处，并各配一句解释。1. Give two benefits of AI, each with an explanation.

② 说出使用人工智能的两个坏处，并各配一句解释。2. Give two drawbacks of AI, each with an explanation.

③ 为什么图灵测试被认为「不够严格」？3. Why is the Turing test considered limited?

点开看答案Show answer

① ① 可 24 小时工作，服务不中断；② 能在极短时间内分析海量数据，找出人类难以发现的规律。1. 1. It works 24 hours a day so the service never stops; 2. it analyses huge amounts of data quickly and finds patterns humans would miss.

② ① 会取代重复性岗位，造成失业；② 训练数据中的偏见会被学进模型，导致不公平的结果。2. 1. It replaces repetitive jobs, causing unemployment; 2. bias in training data is learned by the model, producing unfair results.

③ 因为它只衡量「像不像人」这个外在表现，不检验机器是否真正理解内容、具备意识和常识；靠模仿和大量语料也可能通过。3. Because it measures only the outward appearance of seeming human, not real understanding, consciousness or common sense; imitation backed by huge amounts of text can be enough to pass.

— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —
6 物联网、云计算、VR/AR、量子计算IoT, cloud computing, VR/AR and quantum computing Emerging新兴技术
这一站要会的：说出物联网、云计算、虚拟现实、增强现实、量子计算各是什么；每项都能给一个好处和一个隐患。 Goal: say what IoT, cloud computing, virtual reality, augmented reality and quantum computing are; give one benefit and one risk for each.
说人话：这四项没什么共同原理，就一个一个记，每项三句话：是什么、好处、隐患。
物联网：把电饭煲、空调、手表、汽车这些日常物件都连上网，让它们互相说话。好处是生活自动化——快到家时空调自己开了；隐患是设备太多且很多根本不设防，一旦被控制，成千上万台设备一起发请求就能搞垮网站。
云计算：把存储、算力和软件都放在远方的数据中心，你通过网络按需使用。好处是不用自己买服务器、随时扩容、异地协作；隐患是网一断就全歇，而且数据放在别人机房里，合规和隐私都要操心。
虚拟现实是戴上头显，完全进入计算机造出来的世界，把现实挡在外面；增强现实相反，是把虚拟图像叠在真实世界上（导航箭头、试戴眼镜）。好处是训练和展示很直观、没有真实风险；隐患是眩晕疲劳，还有走路看手机式的安全问题。
量子计算：传统位只能是 0 或 1，量子位可以同时处于 0 和 1 的叠加，再加纠缠效应，某些问题（分解大数、模拟分子）能快到离谱。好处是新药和材料研发提速；隐患是它有能力破解现在保护网银和聊天记录的加密。 In plain words: these four share no single principle, so learn them one at a time, three sentences each: what it is, one benefit, one risk.
IoT: everyday objects — rice cookers, air conditioners, watches, cars — are connected so they can talk to each other. The benefit is automation: the air conditioner switches on before you get home. The risk is that there are huge numbers of devices and many are barely protected; hijack them and thousands can flood a site at once.
Cloud computing: storage, processing power and software live in a remote data centre and you use them over the network as needed. Benefit: no servers to buy, instant scaling, easy collaboration. Risk: if the network goes down everything stops, and your data sits in someone else's building, raising compliance and privacy worries.
Virtual reality puts you entirely inside a computer-generated world, shutting out reality; augmented reality does the opposite, overlaying computer images on the real world (navigation arrows, virtual glasses). Benefit: training and demonstration with no real-world risk. Risk: nausea and eye strain, plus the safety issues of walking around looking at a screen.
Quantum computing: a classical bit is 0 or 1, but a qubit can be both at once, and with entanglement some problems — factoring large numbers, simulating molecules — become vastly faster. Benefit: faster drug and materials discovery. Risk: it could break the encryption that currently protects online banking and private messages.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 物联网 IoT：日常设备嵌入传感器与网络模块，能互相交换数据、被远程监控和控制。好处：自动化与实时告警，例如设备快坏时提前报修。隐患：设备数量庞大、许多安全防护薄弱，容易被批量控制发起攻击；隐私数据大量被采集。IoT: everyday devices with sensors and network connections exchanging data and being monitored or controlled remotely. Benefit: automation and real-time alerts, e.g. warning that a machine is about to fail. Risk: enormous numbers of weakly protected devices can be hijacked in bulk for attacks, and vast amounts of private data are collected.
  • 云计算：通过互联网按需使用远程数据中心里的存储、处理能力与软件。好处：不必自购硬件、按需扩容、随时异地访问与协作、由供应商负责维护备份。隐患：依赖网络（断网即不可用）、数据在第三方（合规与隐私）、长期订阅费用、服务中断。Cloud computing: using storage, processing and software in a remote data centre over the internet on demand. Benefit: no hardware to buy, scale as needed, access and collaborate from anywhere, provider handles maintenance and backups. Risk: depends on the network, data sits with a third party (compliance and privacy), ongoing subscription cost, outages.
  • 虚拟现实 VR：用头戴设备把用户完全带入计算机生成的三维环境，屏蔽现实。好处：零风险的模拟训练（飞行、手术）。隐患：眩晕与眼疲劳、与现实脱节。Virtual reality: a headset takes the user entirely into a computer-generated 3D environment, blocking out reality. Benefit: risk-free simulated training (flying, surgery). Risk: nausea, eye strain and disconnection from the real world.
  • 增强现实 AR：把计算机生成的图像、文字叠加在真实场景上（手机或眼镜）。好处：信息直接出现在眼前，导航、维修、试戴都更直观。隐患：分散注意力，走路或驾车时尤其危险；摄像头持续采集带来隐私顾虑。Augmented reality: computer-generated images and text overlaid on the real scene (phone or glasses). Benefit: information right where you need it — navigation, repair, virtual try-on. Risk: distraction, especially while walking or driving; continuous camera capture raises privacy concerns.
  • 量子计算：利用量子位可同时处于 0 和 1 的叠加以及纠缠进行计算，对特定问题（大数分解、分子模拟、复杂优化）远快于传统计算机。好处：加速新药与材料研发、优化调度。隐患：可能破解现行的公钥加密，威胁网络安全；设备昂贵且需要极低温，尚未普及。Quantum computing: uses qubits that can be 0 and 1 at once, plus entanglement, to solve certain problems (factoring, molecular simulation, complex optimisation) far faster than classical machines. Benefit: faster drug and materials development, better scheduling. Risk: it could break today's public-key encryption and threaten cyber security; hardware is costly, needs extreme cooling and is not yet mainstream.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：四项技术挑你最熟的一项，按「定义 + 好处 + 隐患」三句写完，六分题稳拿。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: pick the technology you know best and write three sentences — definition, benefit, risk — and a six-mark question is safe. Cloud computing is the use of remote servers, accessed over the internet, to store data and run software instead of doing it on the user's own computer. One benefit is that the user does not have to buy expensive hardware and can increase capacity when needed. One risk is that if the internet connection fails, the service cannot be used at all.

例题 · 新技术解释题Example · explaining new technologies

(a) 智能家居里灯、空调、门锁都连上了网。说明这属于什么技术，并给出一个好处和一个隐患。
(b) 学校把学生的作业文件全部存到云端。说出一个好处和一个隐患。
(c) 为什么量子计算被认为会威胁网络安全？ (a) Lights, air conditioning and door locks in a smart home are all networked. Name the technology and give one benefit and one risk.
(b) A school stores all students' coursework in the cloud. Give one benefit and one risk.
(c) Why is quantum computing seen as a threat to cyber security?
(a) 属于物联网。好处：设备互联后可远程控制和自动联动（回家前开空调）。隐患：设备多、很多安全性差，被控制后可能被用来发起大规模攻击，也存在隐私泄露。(a) IoT. Benefit: devices can be controlled remotely and act together (air conditioning on before you arrive). Risk: many devices with weak security can be hijacked and used for large attacks, and private data may leak. (b) 好处：不用买和维护本地服务器，学生在任何地方都能打开自己的文件，还能多人同时协作。隐患：断网就用不了；文件存在第三方服务器上，一旦服务商出事或账号被盗就有风险。(b) Benefit: no local server to buy or maintain, students can open files anywhere and collaborate. Risk: no network means no access; files sit on a third party's servers, so a provider failure or stolen account puts them at risk. (c) 因为量子计算机在分解大数这类问题上极快，而目前广泛使用的公钥加密正是靠「大数难以分解」来保证安全；一旦能快速分解，现有的加密就会被破解。(c) Quantum computers are extremely fast at factoring large numbers, and today's public-key encryption relies precisely on that being hard. Once it becomes easy, existing encryption can be broken.

练习Practice

每项都按「是什么 → 好处 → 隐患」三句答，别只答一句。Answer each in three sentences: what it is → benefit → risk. One sentence is never enough.

① 说出虚拟现实和增强现实的一个关键区别。1. Give one key difference between virtual reality and augmented reality.

② 说出使用云计算的一个好处和一个隐患。2. Give one benefit and one risk of cloud computing.

③ 说出量子计算的一个好处和一个隐患。3. Give one benefit and one risk of quantum computing.

点开看答案Show answer

① 虚拟现实把用户完全代入计算机生成的世界，现实被屏蔽；增强现实是把虚拟内容叠加在真实世界上，现实仍然可见。1. VR puts the user fully inside a computer-generated world with reality shut out; AR overlays virtual content on the real world, which stays visible.

② 好处：不用自己买服务器，按需扩容，异地随时访问。隐患：依赖网络，断网就用不了；数据存在第三方，存在隐私与合规风险。2. Benefit: no servers to buy, capacity on demand, access from anywhere. Risk: it depends on the network — offline means unusable — and data held by a third party raises privacy and compliance issues.

③ 好处：在药物研发、分子模拟等特定问题上比传统计算机快得多。隐患：可能破解现行的公钥加密，威胁网络安全；设备昂贵、需要极低温，尚未普及。3. Benefit: vastly faster than classical computers on specific problems such as drug discovery and molecular simulation. Risk: it may break today's public-key encryption and threaten security; hardware is expensive, needs extreme cooling and is not yet common.

— 第 6 站完成 —— Stop 6 done —

单元 6 完成 · 主线：先能感知，才会自动，最后才有智能Unit 6 done · the thread: sense first, then automate, then intelligence

把这一单元倒过来看会更清楚：一切智能都建立在「能感知」之上——传感器把现实世界的量变成电信号，模数转换把它变成 0 和 1，微处理器才有东西可算。加上执行器就成了自动系统；加上能动的机械结构就成了机器人；再加上学习和推理就成了人工智能。物联网、云、虚拟现实、量子计算则是往外延伸的新工具。Read the unit backwards and it makes sense: all of this intelligence rests on being able to sense — sensors turn physical quantities into electrical signals, the ADC turns those into 0s and 1s, and only then does the processor have something to work on. Add actuators and you have an automatic system; add moving parts and you have a robot; add learning and reasoning and you have AI. IoT, cloud, VR and quantum computing are the new tools extending outwards.

这一单元最高频的题型是「说出一个优点和一个缺点」。练法很简单：每个技术准备两条好处、两条坏处，每条后面跟一句「因为……」，考场上直接套。The most frequent question type here is "give one advantage and one disadvantage". Prepare two of each per technology, each followed by a "because …", and you can drop them straight into the exam.

下一步：单元 7 开始进入 Paper 2——把大问题拆成小问题、画流程图、写伪代码，正式开始学编程思维。Next: Unit 7 moves into Paper 2 — breaking big problems into small ones, drawing flowcharts, writing pseudocode. This is where programming thinking really begins.

单元 7 · 算法设计与问题解决Unit 7 · Algorithm design and problem solvingPAPER 2

从「拿到一个大问题」到「写出能跑的伪代码」· 结构图、流程图、选择、循环、查找、排序、验证与追踪表 · 这是 Paper 2 的地基，后面三个单元全都建在它上面From "here is a big problem" to "pseudocode that works" · structure diagrams, flowcharts, selection, iteration, searching, sorting, validation and trace tables · this is the foundation of Paper 2; the last three units all stand on it

  1. 程序开发生命周期 · 分解与抽象Program development life cycle · decomposition and abstraction
  2. 结构图：自顶向下把大问题拆开Structure diagrams: top-down design
  3. 流程图符号与一个完整流程图Flowchart symbols and a complete flowchart
  4. 伪代码基础：变量、赋值、输入输出Pseudocode basics: variables, assignment, input/output
  5. 选择结构：IF 与 CASESelection: IF and CASE
  6. 三种循环：FOR、WHILE、REPEATThree loops: FOR, WHILE and REPEAT
  7. 线性查找与冒泡排序Linear search and bubble sort

Work through the hand simulation in stop 7 on paper yourself — reading it is not enough.

1 程序开发生命周期 · 分解与抽象Program development life cycle · decomposition and abstraction Analysis需求分析与设计
这一站要会的：按顺序说出程序开发生命周期的五个阶段；解释每个阶段做什么；说明分解和抽象分别是什么并各举一例。 Goal: state the five stages of the program development life cycle in order; explain what happens at each; define decomposition and abstraction with an example of each.
说人话：写程序不是上来就敲代码，得按五步走。
① 分析——弄清楚到底要解决什么问题：需要什么输入、要算出什么输出、屏幕上给谁看、有哪些限制。这一步的产物叫需求规格。
② 设计——想清楚怎么做：用什么算法（画流程图、写伪代码、画结构图）、数据怎么存、界面长什么样、准备哪些测试数据。
③ 编码——把设计翻译成某种编程语言。
④ 测试——用各种数据跑一遍，把错误找出来改掉。
⑤ 维护——交付之后继续修新发现的毛病、按新要求改功能、更新说明书。
整个过程里有两个法宝。分解就是「拆」：别想一口吃下「做一个校园管理系统」，先拆成登录、成绩录入、查询、统计，每块再继续往下拆，直到小到能直接写。抽象就是「删」：把跟当前问题无关的细节先划掉。看地铁图时你只关心「哪站换哪条线」，不关心这条隧道离地面多深——这就是抽象。 In plain words: writing a program does not start with typing code. There are five steps.
1. Analysis — work out what problem is actually being solved: what inputs are needed, what outputs must be produced, who sees it on screen, what the constraints are. The output is a requirements specification.
2. Design — decide how: which algorithm (flowchart, pseudocode, structure diagram), how data is stored, what the interface looks like, which test data to prepare.
3. Coding — translate the design into a programming language.
4. Testing — run it with all kinds of data and fix the errors found.
5. Maintenance — after delivery, keep fixing newly discovered faults, changing features to meet new needs and updating the documentation.
Two tools run through all of it. Decomposition means splitting: do not try to swallow "build a school management system" in one go — split it into login, grade entry, search and statistics, then split each again until every piece is small enough to write. Abstraction means deleting: drop the details irrelevant to the question at hand. Looking at a metro map you care only about "which line to change to at which station", not how deep the tunnel is — that is abstraction.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • ① 分析 analysis：弄清问题，确定输入、处理、输出、界面与性能要求，写出需求规格。1. Analysis: understand the problem and fix the inputs, processing, outputs, interface and constraints, producing a requirements specification.
  • ② 设计 design：设计算法（结构图、流程图、伪代码）、数据结构、用户界面，并制定测试计划。2. Design: design the algorithm (structure diagram, flowchart, pseudocode), the data structures and the interface, and plan the tests.
  • ③ 编码 coding：用选定的编程语言把设计实现出来。3. Coding: implement the design in the chosen programming language.
  • ④ 测试 testing：用正常、异常、极端、边界四类数据运行程序，发现并改正错误。4. Testing: run the program with normal, abnormal, extreme and boundary data and correct the faults found.
  • ⑤ 维护 maintenance：交付后继续修缺陷、按新需求改进、更新文档。5. Maintenance: after release, keep fixing faults, adapting to new requirements and updating documentation.
  • 分解：把一个大问题拆成若干更小、更容易解决的子问题。抽象：忽略无关细节，只保留解决问题必需的信息。Decomposition: break a large problem into smaller sub-problems that are easier to solve. Abstraction: ignore irrelevant detail and keep only what is needed.
常考的坑：题目问「设计阶段要做什么」，答「写代码」是错的——写代码是编码阶段。设计阶段的正确回答是画流程图 / 写伪代码 / 画结构图 / 确定测试数据。 Classic trap: asked what happens in the design stage, answering "write the code" is wrong — that is the coding stage. The right answers are draw a flowchart / write pseudocode / draw a structure diagram / decide test data.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：分解和抽象的定义各一句，背下来直接套。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: one sentence each for decomposition and abstraction — learn them and reuse them. Decomposition means breaking a large problem down into smaller sub-problems that are easier to solve. Abstraction means removing unnecessary detail so that only the information needed to solve the problem is kept.

例题 · 分解与抽象Example · decomposition and abstraction

要做一个「校园图书借阅系统」。
(a) 把它分解成四个子问题。
(b) 设计「借书记录」时，哪些信息是必要的，哪些可以抽象掉？
(c) 测试和编码分别属于哪个阶段，各做什么？ You are building a school library loan system.
(a) Decompose it into four sub-problems. (b) When designing a "loan record", which information is necessary and which can be abstracted away? (c) Which stage do testing and coding belong to, and what happens in each?
(a) ① 用户登录与权限；② 图书检索（按书名、作者、编号）；③ 借书与还书处理（改状态、记日期）；④ 逾期提醒与统计报表。(a) 1. login and permissions; 2. searching for books (title, author, ID); 3. borrowing and returning (change status, record dates); 4. overdue reminders and reports. (b) 必要：图书编号、借书人编号、借出日期、应还日期、是否已还。可以抽象掉：书的封面颜色、重量、放在第几层书架、借书人当天穿什么衣服——这些跟「管理借阅」无关。(b) Needed: book ID, borrower ID, date borrowed, date due, whether returned. Abstracted away: cover colour, weight, which shelf it sits on, what the borrower was wearing — none of it matters for managing loans. (c) 编码是用编程语言把设计写出来；测试是用各类数据运行程序，找出并改正错误。(c) Coding writes the design out in a programming language; testing runs the program with various data to find and fix errors.

练习Practice

五个阶段的顺序一定要能一口气背出来：分析、设计、编码、测试、维护。Learn the five stages in one breath: analysis, design, coding, testing, maintenance.

① 按顺序说出一个程序开发生命周期的五个阶段。1. State the five stages of the program development life cycle in order.

② 说明「分解」和「抽象」的区别，各举一个例子。2. Explain the difference between decomposition and abstraction, with an example of each.

③ 设计阶段会产出哪三种东西？3. Name three things produced during the design stage.

点开看答案Show answer

① 分析 → 设计 → 编码 → 测试 → 维护。1. Analysis → design → coding → testing → maintenance.

② 分解是「拆」：把大问题拆成小问题，例如把「做图书系统」拆成登录、检索、借还、统计。抽象是「删」：丢掉无关细节，例如借书记录只保留编号和日期，不记录书的重量和颜色。2. Decomposition splits: a large problem becomes small ones — "library system" becomes login, search, borrow/return, statistics. Abstraction deletes: irrelevant detail goes — a loan record keeps IDs and dates but not the book's weight or colour.

③ 结构图、流程图、伪代码（还可以加上界面设计和测试计划）。3. A structure diagram, a flowchart and pseudocode (plus the interface design and test plan).

— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 结构图：自顶向下把大问题拆开Structure diagrams: top-down design Top-down自顶向下
这一站要会的：看懂并画出结构图；说明自顶向下设计是什么意思；能把一个小问题拆成 3–4 个模块。 Goal: read and draw a structure diagram; explain what top-down design means; break a small problem into three or four modules.
说人话：结构图就是一张家谱：最顶上是整个任务，往下一层层分出「谁负责哪一块」。它跟流程图的差别很重要——流程图讲顺序（先做什么、后做什么、哪里判断），结构图讲分工（分成哪几块、谁属于谁），结构图里没有箭头表示先后顺序，只有「从属」关系。
拆的时候有个好用的检查标准：拆到每个框都能用几行伪代码写出来，就不用再拆了。比如「求总分」还能拆成「总分先清零」和「逐项累加」；而「输出平均分」一句话就写完了，就不用再往下分。 In plain words: a structure diagram is a family tree: the whole task sits at the top and below it each level shows "who is responsible for which part". The difference from a flowchart matters: a flowchart shows order (what happens first, what next, where the decision is), while a structure diagram shows division of labour (which parts there are and which belongs to which). There are no arrows meaning sequence — only "belongs to".
A useful test while splitting: stop when every box can be written in a few lines of pseudocode. "Find the total" can still split into "set the total to zero" and "add each item"; "output the average" is one line, so it needs no further splitting.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 结构图：用方框和连线表示系统的模块划分，最上面是总任务，下面是它分出的模块，模块还能继续往下分。A structure diagram uses boxes and lines to show how a system is divided into modules: the whole task at the top, its modules below, and those modules may be split further.
  • 自顶向下 top-down：先想整体，再一层层细化，这就是分解的可视化。Top-down means starting with the whole and refining level by level — decomposition made visible.
  • 与流程图的区别：结构图只表示包含关系，不表示执行顺序；流程图才表示顺序和判断。Difference from a flowchart: a structure diagram shows only what belongs to what, never execution order; a flowchart shows sequence and decisions.
  • 什么时候停：拆到每个模块能直接用几行伪代码写出来为止。When to stop: when each module can be written in a few lines of pseudocode.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：题目让你「draw a structure diagram」时，框里写动词短语（输入成绩、求总分），不要写成一个完整的句子。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: when asked to draw a structure diagram, put a short verb phrase in each box ("enter scores", "find the total"), not a full sentence. A structure diagram shows how a problem is broken down into smaller modules. The top box is the whole task, and each lower level splits a module into the sub-tasks it is made of.

例题 · 画结构图Example · drawing a structure diagram

为「统计一次考试的成绩」画一个结构图：需要输入全班每个人的分数，算出总分和平均分，找出最高分，最后把结果打印出来。 Draw a structure diagram for "process the results of one exam": enter every pupil's mark, work out the total and the average, find the highest mark, then print the results.
顶层：统计考试成绩。Top level: process exam results. 第二层四个模块：① 输入成绩、② 求总分、③ 求平均分与最高分、④ 输出结果。Second level, four modules: 1. enter marks, 2. find the total, 3. find the average and the highest, 4. output results. 再往下拆：② 求总分 拆成「总分清零」和「逐项累加」；③ 拆成「总分 ÷ 人数」和「逐个比较取最大」。Breaking down further: 2. find the total splits into "set total to zero" and "add each mark"; 3 splits into "total ÷ number of pupils" and "compare each mark to find the largest". ④ 输出结果 一句话就能写完，不再往下拆。4. output results is one line of code, so it is not split further.

练习Practice

先想「一共要干几件不同的事」，那就是第二层的模块数。Ask "how many different jobs are there?" — that is the number of modules on level two.

① 说出结构图和流程图的一个区别。1. Give one difference between a structure diagram and a flowchart.

② 为「自动售货机卖出一瓶饮料」画出结构图的第二层模块（至少三个）。2. Give at least three level-two modules for "a vending machine selling a drink".

点开看答案Show answer

① 结构图表示模块的包含关系、不表示顺序；流程图表示执行的先后顺序和判断分支。1. A structure diagram shows which modules belong to which and never a sequence; a flowchart shows the order of execution and the decision branches.

② 例如：① 接收投币、② 检查金额是否足够、③ 出货、④ 找零、⑤ 更新库存。（② 还能再拆成「比较金额」和「金额不足则退币」。）2. For example: 1. accept coins, 2. check that enough has been paid, 3. release the drink, 4. give change, 5. update stock. (2 can split further into "compare the amount" and "refund if not enough".)

— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 流程图符号与一个完整流程图Flowchart symbols and a complete flowchart Flowchart流程图
这一站要会的：认出并画出六种流程图符号；读懂一张流程图；能把一句中文需求画成流程图。 Goal: recognise and draw the six flowchart symbols; read a flowchart; turn a short requirement into a flowchart.
说人话：流程图就是给算法拍的一张「路线图」。记符号有个小窍门：圆角是头尾，方角是干活，斜边是进出，菱形是要选。
画的时候记住三条铁律：① 每个流程图只有一个开始、至少一个结束；② 判断框必须有两条出口，而且要把「是 / 否」标在对应的线上；③ 箭头不能凭空出现或消失，每一步都要连上。
判断框里只能放一个能回答「是或否」的问题（A > B？成绩 ≥ 60？），不能写成「判断成绩」这种看不出答案的话。 In plain words: a flowchart is a route map of an algorithm. A quick way to remember the shapes: rounded for start and end, square for doing work, slanted for in/out, diamond for choosing.
Three iron rules when drawing: 1. one start and at least one end; 2. a decision must have two exits, labelled yes and no on the correct lines; 3. arrows must not appear from nowhere or vanish — every step connects.
A decision box holds only a yes/no question (A > B? mark ≥ 60?) — never a vague phrase like "check the mark".
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 起止框 terminator（圆角矩形）：表示开始或结束，一个流程图只有一个开始。A terminator (rounded rectangle) marks the start or end; a flowchart has only one start.
  • 处理框 process（矩形）：计算或赋值，例如 Total ← Total + Score。A process box (rectangle) is a calculation or assignment, e.g. Total ← Total + Score.
  • 输入 / 输出框 input/output（平行四边形）：INPUT 读入数据、OUTPUT 显示结果。An input/output box (parallelogram): INPUT reads data in, OUTPUT shows results.
  • 判断框 decision（菱形）：里面是是/否问题，必须有两条出口，分别标注是 / 否。A decision box (diamond) holds a yes/no question and must have two exits, labelled yes and no.
  • 流程线：带箭头的线，表示下一步去哪，方向不能省。Flow lines are arrowed lines showing where to go next; the direction must always be shown.
  • 连接符 connector（圆圈，里面写字母）：图太长要分页时，两个相同字母的连接符表示同一条线继续。A connector (a circle with a letter) continues a line across a page break; two connectors with the same letter are the same line.
最容易丢分的地方：① 判断框只画一条出口线；② 忘了在分支上写「是 / 否」；③ 箭头忘记画方向；④ 处理框里写「判断成绩」（应该写进菱形框，并且写成能回答是或否的问题）。 Classic traps: 1. a decision with only one exit; 2. no yes/no label on the branches; 3. arrows without direction; 4. putting "check the mark" in a process box (it belongs in a diamond, phrased as a yes/no question).

例题 · 读流程图Example · reading a flowchart

看上面那张「输出较大数」的流程图，回答：
(a) 输入 8 和 3 时，走哪条支路？输出什么？
(b) 输入 5 和 5 时呢？
(c) 如果把判断改成 A >= B，输入 5 和 5 会输出什么？ For the flowchart above: (a) with inputs 8 and 3, which branch is taken and what is output? (b) with 5 and 5? (c) if the decision became A >= B, what is output for 5 and 5?
(a) 8 > 3 成立 → 走「是」支路 → 输出 8。(a) 8 > 3 is true → the yes branch → outputs 8. (b) 5 > 5 不成立 → 走「否」支路 → 输出 5（输出的是 B）。(b) 5 > 5 is false → the no branch → outputs 5 (it outputs B). (c) 5 >= 5 成立 → 走「是」支路 → 输出 A，也就是 5。两种情况结果一样，但走的支路不同——这就是边界值要专门测的原因。(c) 5 >= 5 is true → the yes branch → outputs A, which is 5. Both decisions give 5, but via different branches — exactly why boundary values need testing.

练习Practice

动笔画一遍比看十遍有用，画完检查「开始几个、出口标了没」。Drawing once beats reading ten times; afterwards check how many starts there are and whether exits are labelled.

① 说出流程图中判断框的作用，以及它必须有几条出口。1. What is a decision box for, and how many exits must it have?

② 画一张流程图：输入一个成绩，60 分及以上输出「及格」，否则输出「不及格」。2. Draw a flowchart: input a mark; if it is 60 or above output "pass", otherwise output "fail".

点开看答案Show answer

① 判断框提出一个能用「是 / 否」回答的问题，根据答案选择不同的路径。它必须有两条出口，并在两条线上标出是 / 否。1. A decision box asks a question that can be answered yes or no and chooses a path from the answer. It must have two exits, labelled yes and no on the lines.

② 顺序：开始（圆角）→ 输入 Score（平行四边形）→ 判断 Score >= 60（菱形）→ 是：输出「及格」；否：输出「不及格」→ 两条支路汇合到 结束（圆角）。2. Start (rounded) → input Score (parallelogram) → decision Score ≥ 60 (diamond) → yes: output "pass"; no: output "fail" → both branches rejoin at end (rounded).

— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 伪代码基础：变量、赋值、输入输出Pseudocode basics: variables, assignment, input/output Pseudocode伪代码
这一站要会的：会声明变量与常量；会用 ← 赋值；会写 INPUT / OUTPUT；知道标识符命名规则；会写注释。 Goal: declare variables and constants; assign with ←; use INPUT and OUTPUT; know the identifier naming rules; write comments.
说人话：伪代码不是能运行的程序，它是「用接近英语的方式把算法写下来」——不管你考的是哪门语言，写法都一样，考官看的也是它。
最容易搞反的一件事就是 ←。它叫赋值，意思是「把右边算好的结果，放进左边的变量里」。所以 Total ← Total + 5 在数学上是不成立的等式，在编程里却是最常见的操作：取出 Total 原来的值，加 5，再放回 Total。
变量是一个带名字的盒子，里面的值可以变；常量是一个贴上就撕不掉的标签，程序运行中不能改（比如圆周率）。用之前要先 DECLARE 声明，告诉计算机这个盒子叫什么、装什么类型。 In plain words: pseudocode is not a program you can run. It is writing an algorithm down in an English-like way — the same notation whatever language you study, and the examiner reads exactly that.
The one thing almost everyone gets backwards is ←. It means assignment: work out the right-hand side, then store the result in the variable on the left. So Total ← Total + 5 is nonsense as an equation but completely normal in programming: take the old value, add 5, put it back.
A variable is a named box whose contents can change; a constant is a label fixed once and never altered while the program runs (like pi). Before use you DECLARE it, telling the computer the name and the type of data it holds.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 声明变量：DECLARE Total : INTEGER（还有 STRING、REAL、BOOLEAN、CHAR、DATE）。Declaring: DECLARE Total : INTEGER (also STRING, REAL, BOOLEAN, CHAR, DATE).
  • 赋值：Total ← 0；常量：CONSTANT VAT ← 0.2。Assignment: Total ← 0; constant: CONSTANT VAT ← 0.2.
  • 输入 INPUT Score；输出 OUTPUT "总分是 ", Total（用逗号分隔多项）。Input: INPUT Score; output: OUTPUT "The total is ", Total (a comma separates items).
  • 标识符命名规则：只能用字母、数字、下划线；必须以字母开头；不能有空格；不能和关键字（IF、WHILE 等）重名；名字要有意义。Identifier rules: only letters, digits and underscores; must start with a letter; no spaces; must not be a keyword (IF, WHILE…); the name should be meaningful.
  • 注释用 // 开头，写给人看，计算机不执行。A comment starts with //; it is for humans and is never executed.
  • 运算：+ - * /，整数除法 DIV 和取余 MOD，字符串连接用 &，比较用 = < > <= >= <>（不等于）。Operators: + − * /, integer division DIV and remainder MOD, string join with &, comparisons = < > <= >= <> (not equal).

例题 · 写伪代码Example · writing pseudocode

写出一段伪代码：输入一个学生的姓名和两次考试分数，算出平均分并输出一句完整的话。 Write pseudocode that inputs a student's name and two exam marks, works out the average and outputs a complete sentence.
先声明要用到的变量：姓名是字符串，分数和平均分用实数。Declare what is needed: the name is a string, the marks and average are real numbers.
DECLARE Name : STRING DECLARE Score1, Score2, Average : REAL // 读入三项数据// read in the three items INPUT Name INPUT Score1 INPUT Score2 // 算出平均值// work out the average Average ← (Score1 + Score2) / 2 OUTPUT Name & " 的平均分是 ", Average
注意三点：① 括号保证先加再除；② / 得到实数结果，若写成 DIV 会丢掉小数；③ 输出里用 & 把文字和变量拼在一起。Three things to note: 1. brackets make the addition happen before the division; 2. / gives a real result, whereas DIV would throw the decimals away; 3. & joins the text and the variable together.
最容易丢分的地方：① 把 ← 写成 =；② 忘了先 DECLARE；③ 名字里带空格（total score 不合法，要写 TotalScore）；④ 字符串忘记加引号（OUTPUT Hello 会被当成一个变量）。 Classic traps: 1. writing = instead of ←; 2. forgetting to DECLARE first; 3. a space in a name (total score is illegal — write TotalScore); 4. missing quotes round a string (OUTPUT Hello looks like a variable).

练习Practice

先想清楚「用到几个盒子」，再一行行写。Decide how many "boxes" you need first, then write line by line.

① 说出三条标识符命名规则。1. Give three rules for naming identifiers.

② 下面这行执行后，X 的值是多少？X ← 5　X ← X * 2　X ← X - 32. What is the value of X after these lines? X ← 5　X ← X * 2　X ← X - 3

③ 写出伪代码：输入摄氏度，用公式「华氏度 = 摄氏度 × 9 / 5 + 32」换算后输出。3. Write pseudocode that inputs a Celsius temperature and outputs it converted using "Fahrenheit = Celsius × 9 / 5 + 32".

点开看答案Show answer

① 只能用字母、数字、下划线；必须以字母开头；不能有空格，也不能和关键字重名。1. Only letters, digits and underscores; must start with a letter; no spaces and no keywords.

② 5 → 10 → 7。2. 5 → 10 → 7.

③3.

DECLARE Celsius, Fahrenheit : REAL INPUT Celsius Fahrenheit ← Celsius * 9 / 5 + 32 OUTPUT "换算后是 ", Fahrenheit
— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 选择结构：IF 与 CASESelection: IF and CASE IF / CASE选择与分支
这一站要会的：会写 IF（含 ELSE、嵌套 IF）和 CASE OF；知道什么时候该用 CASE；会缩进对齐。 Goal: write IF (with ELSE and nesting) and CASE OF; know when CASE is the better choice; indent correctly.
说人话：选择结构就是「看情况办事」。IF 是最常用的：条件成立就做 A，不成立就做 B；如果「不成立」时什么都不用做，那 ELSE 整段可以省掉。
IF 里面还能再套 IF，这叫嵌套。但如果你发现自己在拿同一个变量跟一堆值比（成绩是 A 怎样、B 怎样、C 怎样），用嵌套 IF 会写得又长又乱，这时候换成 CASE OF 就清爽了：一个变量挂一横排，每个取值对应一句。
写的时候有个硬性要求：IF 必须配 ENDIF，CASE 必须配 ENDCASE；中间的语句要缩进两到四个空格。缩进不是给计算机看的，是给人看的——考试时阅卷老师一眼就能看出你的结构对不对。 In plain words: selection means acting according to the situation. IF is the workhorse: if the condition is true do A, otherwise do B. If nothing needs doing when it is false, the whole ELSE part can simply be left out.
An IF can sit inside another IF — that is nesting. But when you find yourself comparing one variable against a list of values (grade A does this, B does that, C does the other), nested IFs become long and messy. That is when CASE OF shines: one variable with a row of values beneath it.
One hard rule: every IF needs its ENDIF and every CASE needs its ENDCASE, and the statements in between must be indented by two to four spaces. Indentation is not for the computer, it is for people — in an exam it lets the marker see your structure at a glance.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • IF…THEN…ENDIF：条件成立才执行里面的语句，不成立就跳过。IF…THEN…ENDIF: the statements run only when the condition is true, otherwise they are skipped.
  • IF…THEN…ELSE…ENDIF：成立走 THEN 分支，不成立走 ELSE 分支，两条路必走且只走一条。IF…THEN…ELSE…ENDIF: true takes the THEN branch, false takes ELSE — exactly one of the two always runs.
  • 嵌套 IF：IF 里再放 IF，用来处理「分层判断」；要一层层缩进，每层配一个 ENDIF。Nested IF: an IF inside an IF for layered decisions; indent each level and give each its own ENDIF.
  • CASE OF…OTHERWISE…ENDCASE：拿同一个变量跟若干取值比较，每个取值对应一组语句；OTHERWISE 兜住所有没列出的情况。CASE OF…OTHERWISE…ENDCASE: compares one variable against several values, each with its own statements; OTHERWISE catches anything not listed.
  • 什么时候用 CASE：判断依据是同一个变量（或表达式）的多个离散取值时用 CASE；判断依据是不同的条件或范围时用 IF。When to use CASE: use CASE when the decision is based on several discrete values of one variable; use IF when it depends on different conditions or ranges.

例题 · 三种写法Example · three ways

输入一个成绩（0–100），输出「及格」或「不及格」，其中 80 分及以上还要输出「优秀」。 Input a mark (0–100) and output "pass" or "fail", plus "excellent" if the mark is 80 or above.
写法一：普通 IF + ELSE。Version 1: plain IF with ELSE.
IF Score >= 50 THEN OUTPUT "及格" ELSE OUTPUT "不及格" ENDIF
写法二：嵌套 IF（先分及格与否，再看是否优秀）。Version 2: nested IF (pass or fail first, then excellent or not).
IF Score >= 50 THEN IF Score >= 80 THEN OUTPUT "优秀" ELSE OUTPUT "及格" ENDIF ELSE OUTPUT "不及格" ENDIF
写法三：如果这个成绩用字母等级给出，就轮到 CASE 上场。Version 3: if the mark arrives as a letter grade, CASE takes over.
CASE OF Grade "A" : OUTPUT "优秀" "B" : OUTPUT "良好" "C" : OUTPUT "及格" OTHERWISE : OUTPUT "需要努力" ENDCASE
常考的坑：① 漏写 ENDIF / ENDCASE；② 把 = 写成赋值符号 ←（条件里应该用 = 比较）；③ 字符串取值要加引号，"A" 而不是 A。 Classic traps: 1. missing ENDIF or ENDCASE; 2. using the assignment arrow ← instead of = for comparison; 3. string values need quotes — "A", not A.

练习Practice

先判断「比的是同一个变量的一堆值」还是「几个不同的条件」，再选 IF 还是 CASE。Ask first whether you are comparing one variable's values or several different conditions, then choose CASE or IF.

① 什么时候应该用 CASE OF 而不是嵌套 IF？1. When should CASE OF be used instead of nested IF?

② 写出伪代码：输入一个整数，如果是偶数输出「偶数」，否则输出「奇数」。（提示：用 MOD 判断余数）2. Write pseudocode: input an integer; output "even" if it is even, otherwise "odd". (Hint: use MOD to test the remainder.)

③ 下面这段缺了什么？IF X > 10 THEN OUTPUT "大" ELSE OUTPUT "小"3. What is missing from IF X > 10 THEN OUTPUT "big" ELSE OUTPUT "small"?

点开看答案Show answer

① 当判断依据是同一个变量（或表达式）的多个离散取值时，用 CASE 更清楚；如果是不同的条件或区间（比如既要看年龄又要看分数），就用 IF。1. Use CASE when the decision rests on several discrete values of one variable; use IF for different conditions or ranges (for example checking both age and score).

②2.

DECLARE Number : INTEGER INPUT Number IF Number MOD 2 = 0 THEN OUTPUT "偶数" ELSE OUTPUT "奇数" ENDIF

③ 缺少 ENDIF。完整写法要在最后补上 ENDIF。3. ENDIF is missing — add it at the end.

6 三种循环：FOR、WHILE、REPEATThree loops: FOR, WHILE and REPEAT Iteration循环
这一站要会的：会写三种循环；说出它们各自什么时候用；知道 WHILE 和 REPEAT 最少执行几次的区别。 Goal: write all three loops; say when each is used; know how many times WHILE and REPEAT are guaranteed to run.
说人话：三种循环干的是同一件事——把一段代码重复执行——区别只在「怎么决定停下来」。
FOR 是计数循环：一开始就知道要转几圈（第 1 个到第 10 个），循环变量自己会加，不用你管。
WHILE 是先问再做：门口站着个保安，条件成立才让你进去干活。所以它可能一次都不做（第一次问就不成立）。
REPEAT 是先做再问：先闷头干一轮，干完再问「够了吗」。所以它至少做一次。
挑哪一个？问自己一句：「次数在开始前就确定了吗？」确定 → FOR；不确定，但可能一次都不用做 → WHILE；不确定，但一定要先做一次（比如必须让用户输入一次密码）→ REPEAT。 In plain words: all three loops do the same job — repeat a block of code — and differ only in how they decide to stop.
FOR is a count-controlled loop: you know how many times round before you start (first to tenth), and the loop variable increments itself.
WHILE tests first: a guard at the door lets you in only while the condition holds. So it may run zero times if the condition fails at once.
REPEAT does first, tests after: one round of work, then "is that enough?". So it runs at least once.
Which to pick? Ask: "is the number of repeats known before we start?" Yes → FOR. No, but it might be zero → WHILE. No, but it must happen at least once (you have to ask for the password once) → REPEAT.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • FOR：FOR i ← 1 TO 10 … NEXT i；循环次数事先已知，循环变量自动递增（可用 STEP 改变步长）。FOR: FOR i ← 1 TO 10 … NEXT i; the count is known in advance and the variable increments automatically (STEP changes the step size).
  • WHILE：WHILE 条件 DO … ENDWHILE；先判断，条件成立才做，可能一次都不执行。WHILE: WHILE condition DO … ENDWHILE; it tests first and may run zero times.
  • REPEAT：REPEAT … UNTIL 条件；先执行再判断，至少执行一次，条件成立时才停止。REPEAT: REPEAT … UNTIL condition; it runs then tests, so at least one pass, stopping when the condition becomes true.
  • 循环体里的语句必须缩进；循环变量要在循环里被改变，否则条件永远不变就死循环了。Indent the body; the loop variable must change inside the loop, otherwise the condition never changes and you get an infinite loop.

例题 · 输入 10 个数求和Example · total of ten numbers

输入 10 个数，求它们的和并输出。分别用 FOR 和 REPEAT 写一遍。 Input ten numbers, find their total and output it. Write it with FOR and then with REPEAT.
FOR 版（次数已知，最自然）：The FOR version (count known, the natural choice):
DECLARE Total, Score : INTEGER DECLARE Count : INTEGER // 总分必须先清零，否则会接着内存里的旧值加// the total must start at zero, otherwise it adds on to whatever was already there Total ← 0 FOR Count ← 1 TO 10 INPUT Score Total ← Total + Score NEXT Count OUTPUT "总分是 ", Total
REPEAT 版（自己数次数，至少输入一次）：The REPEAT version (count it yourself, at least one input):
DECLARE Total, Score, Count : INTEGER Total ← 0 Count ← 0 REPEAT INPUT Score Total ← Total + Score Count ← Count + 1 UNTIL Count = 10 OUTPUT "总分是 ", Total
追踪一下：如果输入的十个数是 1 到 10，那么 Total 依次变成 1、3、6、10、15、21、28、36、45、55。Trace it: with the numbers 1 to 10 the total becomes 1, 3, 6, 10, 15, 21, 28, 36, 45, 55.
最容易丢分的地方：① Total ← 0 这行忘写（累加前必须先清零）；② 累加写成 Score ← Score + Total（方向反了）；③ REPEAT 里忘了改循环变量，导致条件永远不成立 → 死循环。 Classic traps: 1. forgetting Total ← 0 before the loop; 2. writing Score ← Score + Total (the wrong way round); 3. not changing the loop variable in a REPEAT, so the condition never becomes true — an infinite loop.

练习Practice

先问「至少做几次」，答案就出来了。Ask "how many times must it run at minimum?" and the answer follows.

① 说出 WHILE 和 REPEAT 的一个关键区别。1. Give one key difference between WHILE and REPEAT.

② 让用户输入密码，输错就一直重试。该用哪种循环？为什么？2. A program asks for a password and keeps asking while it is wrong. Which loop, and why?

③ 写出伪代码：用 FOR 循环输出 1 到 5 的乘法表里 ×3 的那一行（3、6、9、12、15）。3. Write pseudocode using a FOR loop to output the ×3 row of the times table: 3, 6, 9, 12, 15.

点开看答案Show answer

① WHILE 先判断后执行，可能一次都不做；REPEAT 先执行后判断，至少做一次。1. WHILE tests before running and may run zero times; REPEAT runs then tests, so at least once.

② 用 REPEAT。因为至少要让用户输入一次密码才知道对不对，REPEAT 正好保证「先做一次再判断」。2. REPEAT. The user must be asked at least once before you can know whether the password is right — exactly "do once, then test".

③3.

DECLARE i : INTEGER FOR i ← 1 TO 5 OUTPUT i * 3 NEXT i
