7 线性查找与冒泡排序Linear search and bubble sort Search / Sort查找与排序
这一站要会的：会写线性查找和冒泡排序的伪代码；能手动模拟一遍；说出它们各自的比较次数和优缺点。 Goal: write pseudocode for a linear search and a bubble sort; hand-simulate both; state their comparison counts and their pros and cons.
说人话：线性查找是最笨也最实在的办法：从第一个开始，一个一个问「是你吗？」，问到就停。它的好处是数据不用先排序，坏处是运气差的时候要把整个数组问一遍。
冒泡排序的思路更像排队：从头开始，只比较相邻的两个，左边比右边大就交换。走完一轮，最大的那个一定被推到了最右边（它一路「冒」上去了）。然后不管它，对剩下的部分再来一轮，直到全部排好。
为什么叫冒泡？因为大的元素像水里的气泡，每一轮都往上（往末尾）浮一格，最后浮到自己该在的位置。
比较多少次？n 个元素第一轮比较 n−1 次，第二轮 n−2 次……最后 1 次，一共 n(n−1)/2 次。5 个数就是 5×4/2 = 10 次。这就是冒泡排序的短板——数据量一大，慢得让人受不了；它的优点只有一个：写起来特别简单。 In plain words: linear search is the dumbest and most dependable method: start at the first item and ask "is it you?" one at a time, stopping when found. Its advantage is that the data does not need sorting first; the disadvantage is that on a bad day you ask every single item.
Bubble sort is more like lining up: start at the beginning, compare only neighbouring pairs, and swap if the left one is larger. After one pass the largest value is guaranteed to have been pushed to the far right (it "bubbles" all the way up). Ignore it, then do another pass over what is left, until everything is ordered.
Why "bubble"? Large values rise like bubbles in water, floating one place further along each pass until they reach their position.
How many comparisons? For n items: n−1 in the first pass, n−2 in the second, down to 1 — n(n−1)/2 in total. For five items that is 5×4/2 = 10. That is bubble sort's weakness: it is painfully slow on large data. Its only merit is that it is extremely simple to write.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 线性查找：从第一个元素开始逐个比较，找到就停止；数据不必先排序。Linear search: compares each item in turn from the first, stopping when found; the data need not be sorted first.
  • n 个数据：最少比较 1 次（第一个就是），最多比较 n 次（找不到或最后一个才是）。For n items: fewest 1 comparison, most n comparisons (not found, or last one).
  • 冒泡排序：反复比较相邻两个元素，顺序不对就交换；每一轮把未排序部分最大的元素推到末尾。Bubble sort: repeatedly compares neighbouring items and swaps them if they are out of order; each pass pushes the largest remaining value to the end.
  • 比较次数：第一轮 n−1 次，总共 n(n−1)/2 次（最坏情况）；n = 5 时为 10 次。Comparisons: n−1 in the first pass, n(n−1)/2 in total (worst case); for n = 5 that is 10.
  • 优缺点：冒泡排序代码简单、占内存少，但比较次数多、效率低，只适合小规模数据。Pros and cons: bubble sort is simple to code and uses almost no extra memory, but it makes many comparisons and is inefficient — fine only for small data sets.

例题 · 伪代码与手动模拟Example · pseudocode and hand simulation

(a) 写出在一维数组里做线性查找的伪代码。
(b) 写出冒泡排序的伪代码。
(c) 用冒泡排序把 [5, 2, 9, 1, 6] 排好，写出每一轮结束后的数组，并说出总比较次数。 (a) Write pseudocode for a linear search in a 1D array. (b) Write pseudocode for a bubble sort. (c) Sort [5, 2, 9, 1, 6] with bubble sort, giving the array after each pass and the total number of comparisons.
(a) 线性查找：(a) Linear search:
DECLARE Found : BOOLEAN DECLARE Index : INTEGER Found ← FALSE Index ← 0 REPEAT Index ← Index + 1 IF Array[Index] = Target THEN Found ← TRUE ENDIF UNTIL Found = TRUE OR Index = Length IF Found = TRUE THEN OUTPUT "找到了，位置是 ", Index ELSE OUTPUT "没找到" ENDIF
(b) 冒泡排序（两层 FOR，外层管轮数，内层管相邻比较）：(b) Bubble sort (two FOR loops: the outer one for passes, the inner one for neighbouring comparisons):
DECLARE Temp : INTEGER DECLARE i, j : INTEGER FOR i ← 1 TO Length - 1 FOR j ← 1 TO Length - i IF Array[j] > Array[j + 1] THEN Temp ← Array[j] Array[j] ← Array[j + 1] Array[j + 1] ← Temp ENDIF NEXT j NEXT i
(c) 5 个数共需 4 轮。第 1 轮：5>2 交换 → [2,5,9,1,6]；5<9 不动 → [2,5,9,1,6]；9>1 交换 → [2,5,1,9,6]；9>6 交换 → [2,5,1,6,9]，9 到位。(c) Five numbers need four passes. Pass 1: 5>2 swap → [2,5,9,1,6]; 5<9 no swap → [2,5,9,1,6]; 9>1 swap → [2,5,1,9,6]; 9>6 swap → [2,5,1,6,9], with 9 in place. 第 2 轮（比较前 4 个）：5>1 交换 → [2,1,5,6,9]；5<6 不动 → [2,1,5,6,9]。Pass 2 (compare the first four): 5>1 swap → [2,1,5,6,9]; 5<6 no swap → [2,1,5,6,9]. 第 3 轮（比较前 3 个）：2>1 交换 → [1,2,5,6,9]。Pass 3 (compare the first three): 2>1 swap → [1,2,5,6,9]. 第 4 轮（比较前 2 个）：1<2 不动 → [1,2,5,6,9] 完成。Pass 4 (compare the first two): 1<2 no swap → [1,2,5,6,9] done. 比较次数 = 4 + 3 + 2 + 1 = 10 次。Comparisons = 4 + 3 + 2 + 1 = 10.
最容易丢分的地方：① 交换时必须先用一个临时变量存住，否则数据会被覆盖丢失；② 内层循环的终点写成 Length 会导致 Array[j+1] 越界，应该是 Length - i；③ 问「比较次数」时不要漏掉最后的 1 次。 Classic traps: 1. a swap needs a temporary variable, otherwise the value is overwritten and lost; 2. ending the inner loop at Length makes Array[j+1] go out of range — it should be Length - i; 3. when counting comparisons, do not forget the final one.

练习Practice

拿纸和笔真的走一遍，比盯着看有用十倍。Actually walk through it on paper — ten times better than just reading.

① 在 [8, 3, 12, 5, 9, 7] 里查找 12，需要比较几次？查找 4 呢？1. Searching for 12 in [8, 3, 12, 5, 9, 7] — how many comparisons? And for 4?

② 说出线性查找的一个优点和一个缺点。2. Give one advantage and one disadvantage of linear search.

③ 用冒泡排序处理 8 个数，最多需要比较多少次？3. Sorting eight numbers with bubble sort — what is the maximum number of comparisons?

点开看答案Show answer

① 查 12：8≠12，3≠12，12=12 → 3 次。查 4：六个数全比完都不等 → 6 次，并报告没找到。1. For 12: 8≠12, 3≠12, 12=12 → 3 comparisons. For 4: all six are compared without a match → 6 comparisons, then report not found.

② 优点：数据不需要先排序，写法简单；缺点：数据量大时慢，最坏要比较 n 次。2. Advantage: the data does not have to be sorted first and it is simple to write. Disadvantage: it is slow on large data, needing up to n comparisons.

③ 7 + 6 + 5 + 4 + 3 + 2 + 1 = 28 次（也就是 8 × 7 ÷ 2）。3. 7 + 6 + 5 + 4 + 3 + 2 + 1 = 28 (that is 8 × 7 ÷ 2).

— 第 7 站完成 —— Stop 7 done —
8 验证、检验、测试数据与追踪表Validation, verification, test data and trace tables Testing测试
这一站要会的：分清验证和检验；说出五种验证检查；说出四类测试数据并各举一例；会填追踪表。 Goal: distinguish validation from verification; name five validation checks; give the four types of test data with an example each; complete a trace table.
说人话：这两个词中文里很像，考试最爱考它们的区别。验证 validation 关心的是「这个数据合理吗」——程序自动按规则检查。检验 verification 关心的是「这个数据跟原件一致吗」——检查录入过程有没有抄错。
举个例子：报名表上年龄填 300，验证会发现「超出合理范围」并拒绝；年龄其实填的是 18 但手滑打成 81，验证看不出来（81 也是合理的），只有检验（让本人再输一遍，或者把输进去的内容再显示给他看）才能发现。
测试数据要准备四类：正常（应该被接受）、异常（应该被拒绝）、极端（有效范围的两端）、边界（刚好在分界线上和刚好越界的值，用来检查 > 和 >= 有没有写错）。
追踪表则是调试利器：把每个变量在每一步的值列成表，程序跑得对不对一眼就看出来。表里一般每一列一个变量，每一次循环或每一个输出一行。 In plain words: these two words are easily confused and the difference is a favourite exam question. Validation asks "is this data reasonable?" — the program checks it automatically against rules. Verification asks "does this match the original?" — it checks whether the entering process introduced a mistake.
An example: a form with age 300 fails validation as out of range. But if the real age is 18 and a slip types 81, validation sees nothing wrong (81 is perfectly reasonable) — only verification (asking for it a second time, or showing it back for confirmation) catches that.
Prepare four kinds of test data: normal (should be accepted), abnormal (should be rejected), extreme (the two ends of the valid range) and boundary (values exactly on the edge — just valid and just invalid — which is how you discover whether > should have been >=).
A trace table is the debugging weapon: list every variable's value at every step and whether the program is working becomes obvious. Usually one column per variable and one row per loop pass or output.
验证 validation 的五种检查：Five validation checks:
  • 类型检查 type check：数据类型对不对，例如年龄字段里输入了字母就拒绝。Type check: is the data type right — letters in an age field are rejected.
  • 范围检查 range check：是否落在允许区间内，例如分数必须在 0 到 100 之间。Range check: does it fall inside the allowed interval, e.g. a mark between 0 and 100.
  • 长度检查 length check：字符个数够不够、会不会太长，例如密码至少 8 位。Length check: the right number of characters, e.g. a password of at least 8.
  • 存在性检查 presence check：必填项有没有被跳过，不能留空。Presence check: a required field must not be left empty.
  • 格式检查 format check：是否符合规定的样式，例如日期写成 年-月-日、邮箱里必须有 @。Format check: does it follow the required pattern, e.g. a date as year-month-day or an email containing @.
检验 verification 的两种方法：Two verification methods:
  • 二次录入 double entry：同一份数据输两遍，计算机比较两次的结果，不一致就提示重输。Double entry: type the same data twice and let the computer compare; if they differ, ask again.
  • 屏幕确认 visual check：把刚输入的内容再显示一遍，让用户本人确认对不对。Visual (screen) check: show what was just entered and ask the user to confirm it is right.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：「验证和检验有什么不同」年年考，背这两句。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: "what is the difference between validation and verification" comes up every year — learn these two sentences. Validation checks that the data entered is reasonable or sensible, for example checking that a mark is between 0 and 100. Verification checks that the data entered matches the original source, for example typing it in twice and comparing the two copies.

例题 · 填追踪表Example · completing a trace table

有下面这段伪代码，输入依次是 5、8、3、4。填出完整追踪表，并说出最后输出什么。 For the pseudocode below with inputs 5, 8, 3, 4, complete the trace table and state the final output.
DECLARE Total, Score, i : INTEGER Total ← 0 FOR i ← 1 TO 4 INPUT Score Total ← Total + Score NEXT i OUTPUT Total
初始行：i = —，Score = —，Total = 0，OUTPUT = —。Initial row: i = —, Score = —, Total = 0, OUTPUT = —. 第 1 轮：i = 1，Score = 5，Total = 0 + 5 = 5。Pass 1: i = 1, Score = 5, Total = 0 + 5 = 5. 第 2 轮：i = 2，Score = 8，Total = 5 + 8 = 13。Pass 2: i = 2, Score = 8, Total = 5 + 8 = 13. 第 3 轮：i = 3，Score = 3，Total = 13 + 3 = 16。Pass 3: i = 3, Score = 3, Total = 13 + 3 = 16. 第 4 轮：i = 4，Score = 4，Total = 16 + 4 = 20；循环结束后 输出 20。Pass 4: i = 4, Score = 4, Total = 16 + 4 = 20; after the loop it outputs 20.

练习Practice

答验证题时写清「检查数据是否合理」，答检验题时写清「检查是否与原件一致」。For validation write "checks the data is sensible"; for verification write "checks it matches the original".

① 说出验证和检验的一个区别。1. Give one difference between validation and verification.

② 一个年龄字段允许输入 1 到 120。请各举一个正常、异常、极端、边界的例子。2. An age field accepts 1 to 120. Give one example each of normal, abnormal, extreme and boundary data.

③ 说出三种验证检查，并各配一个例子。3. Name three validation checks and give an example of each.

点开看答案Show answer

① 验证检查输入的数据是否合理（在范围内、格式对）；检验检查输入的数据是否与原始来源一致（有没有抄错）。1. Validation checks that the data entered is sensible (in range, right format); verification checks that it matches the original source (nothing mistyped).

② 正常：25；异常："abc"（类型不对，或 −5）；极端：1 和 120；边界：0、1、120、121（刚好在分界线两侧）。2. Normal: 25. Abnormal: "abc" (wrong type) or −5. Extreme: 1 and 120. Boundary: 0, 1, 120, 121 (either side of the limit).

③ 例如：类型检查（年龄里不能是字母）、范围检查（分数 0–100）、长度检查（密码至少 8 位）。（还可以答存在性检查和格式检查。）3. For example: type check (no letters in an age), range check (mark 0–100), length check (password at least 8 characters). Presence and format checks also count.

单元 7 完成 · 主线：先想清楚，再画出来，最后写成能一步步走通的代码Unit 7 done · the thread: think it through, draw it out, then write code that can be walked through step by step

这一单元是一条完整的流水线：分解与抽象帮你想清楚 → 结构图帮你分工 → 流程图帮你排顺序 → 伪代码帮你写出来 → 选择和循环让程序会判断、会重复 → 查找与排序是两个必须手写的经典算法 → 验证、测试数据和追踪表帮你确认它真的对。This unit is one complete pipeline: decomposition and abstraction clarify the problem → a structure diagram divides the work → a flowchart fixes the order → pseudocode writes it down → selection and iteration let the program decide and repeat → search and sort are the two classic algorithms you must be able to write by hand → validation, test data and trace tables prove it actually works.

最该练熟的三件事：① 三种循环的写法和区别；② 线性查找和冒泡排序的伪代码；③ 追踪表每一步都不能跳。这三样在 Paper 2 里年年出现，而且都是「写对就得分」的硬功夫。Three things to drill hardest: 1. the three loops and how they differ; 2. the pseudocode for linear search and bubble sort; 3. trace tables with no skipped steps. All three appear every year in Paper 2 and are pure marks for correct work.

1 数据类型、变量与常量Data types, variables and constants INTEGER · REAL · CHAR · STRING · BOOLEAN整数 · 实数 · 字符 · 字符串 · 布尔
这一站要会的：说出五种数据类型分别装什么；会写 DECLARE 声明变量、CONSTANT 声明常量；说出标识符命名的三条规则。 Goal: state what each of the five data types holds; declare variables with DECLARE and constants with CONSTANT; give three rules for naming identifiers.
说人话：程序要处理的东西五花八门：年龄是整数，身高是带小数的实数，性别只填一个字母就是字符，姓名是一串字就是字符串，而「考试及格了吗」只有两种答案——布尔（TRUE 或 FALSE）。计算机很死板，你得提前告诉它这个盒子里要装哪一类东西，它才知道该留多大空间、能对它做哪些操作。这就是「数据类型」。 In plain words: programs deal with all sorts of things: age is a whole number, height is a number with a decimal point, gender might be a single letter, a name is a string of letters, and "did you pass?" has only two answers — TRUE or FALSE. A computer is very literal: you must tell it in advance what kind of thing goes in the box, so it knows how much space to reserve and what it is allowed to do with it. That is a data type.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • INTEGER（整数）：没有小数部分的数，可以是负数，例如 −12、0、42。INTEGER: a whole number, may be negative, e.g. −12, 0, 42.
  • REAL（实数）：带小数部分的数，例如 3.14、−0.5、2.0。REAL: a number with a fractional part, e.g. 3.14, −0.5, 2.0.
  • CHAR（字符）：只放一个字符，用单引号，例如 'A'、'7'、'?'。CHAR: exactly one character, in single quotes, e.g. 'A', '7', '?'.
  • STRING（字符串）：一串字符，用双引号，例如 "Hello"、"2026"。STRING: a sequence of characters, in double quotes, e.g. "Hello", "2026".
  • BOOLEAN（布尔）：只有两个值，TRUE 和 FALSE。BOOLEAN: only two values are possible — TRUE and FALSE.
  • 变量 variable 是程序运行时值可以变化的存储单元；常量 constant 一旦设定就全程不变（比如圆周率、税率）。A variable is a named storage location whose value can change as the program runs; a constant is fixed for the whole run (pi, tax rate).
  • 标识符 identifier 命名规则：① 见名知意（用 TotalScore 而不是 x）；② 中间不能有空格；③ 不能用数字开头。Identifier rules: 1. make it meaningful (TotalScore, not x); 2. no spaces; 3. it cannot start with a digit.
最容易丢分的地方：① 把 "7" 当成数字——它是 STRING，不能做加法；② 忘记 CHAR 只能放一个字符，"AB" 是 STRING 不是 CHAR；③ 声明了却不赋值就直接用，值是不确定的。 Classic traps: 1. treating "7" as a number — it is a STRING and cannot be added; 2. forgetting a CHAR holds one character only, so "AB" is a STRING; 3. using a variable before giving it a value.

例题 · 声明与赋值Example · declaring and assigning

写一个程序片段：声明一个整数变量 Score、一个字符串变量 Name、一个常量 VAT（值 0.2），然后给 Score 赋 85，给 Name 赋 "Lin"。 Write a program fragment that declares an integer variable Score, a string variable Name, and a constant VAT with the value 0.2, then assigns 85 to Score and "Lin" to Name.
DECLARE Score : INTEGER DECLARE Name : STRING CONSTANT VAT = 0.2 Score ← 85 Name ← "Lin" OUTPUT Name, " scored ", Score
① 用 DECLARE 名字 : 类型 声明变量，冒号后面写类型。1. Declare with DECLARE name : TYPE — the type goes after the colon. ② 常量用 CONSTANT 名字 = 值，注意这里用的是 等号，不是 ←。2. Constants use CONSTANT name = value — note the equals sign, not ←. ③ 赋值用 ←（箭头指向被赋值的那个变量），字符串要加双引号。3. Assignment uses ← (the arrow points at the variable receiving the value); strings need double quotes. ④ VAT 是常量，后面再写 VAT ← 0.3 就是错的，程序不允许改常量。4. VAT is a constant, so writing VAT ← 0.3 later would be wrong — constants cannot be changed.

练习Practice

先自己判断类型，再看答案。Decide the types yourself first, then open the answer.

① 下面四个值分别该用什么数据类型？　(a) 3.14159　(b) 'Z'　(c) 100　(d) TRUE1. Which data type suits each value? (a) 3.14159 (b) 'Z' (c) 100 (d) TRUE

② 下面三个变量名哪些不合法，为什么？　2Name　My Score　TotalMarks2. Which of these identifiers are invalid, and why? 2Name　My Score　TotalMarks

点开看答案Show answer

① (a) REAL（有小数部分）；(b) CHAR（只有一个字符，而且是单引号）；(c) INTEGER；(d) BOOLEAN。1. (a) REAL; (b) CHAR (one character, single quotes); (c) INTEGER; (d) BOOLEAN.

② 2Name 不合法——用数字开头；My Score 不合法——中间有空格；TotalMarks 合法而且见名知意，是好名字。2. 2Name is invalid (starts with a digit); My Score is invalid (contains a space); TotalMarks is valid and meaningful.

— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 运算符与运算优先级Operators and precedence DIV · MOD · AND · OR · NOT整除 · 取余 · 与 · 或 · 非
这一站要会的：会算 DIV 和 MOD；会用六个比较运算符；会算 AND / OR / NOT；知道运算的优先顺序。 Goal: evaluate DIV and MOD; use the six comparison operators; evaluate AND, OR and NOT; know the order of precedence.
说人话：运算符就是「对数据做动作的符号」。分成四类记：算术（+ − * / 再加两个特殊的 DIV、MOD）、比较（比大小，结果一定是 TRUE 或 FALSE）、逻辑（把两个 TRUE/FALSE 合成一个）、连接（用 & 把两段字符串粘起来）。DIV 和 MOD 是考试必考：17 除以 5 商 3 余 2，所以 17 DIV 5 = 3、17 MOD 5 = 2。判断奇偶就用 MOD：一个数 MOD 2 等于 0 就是偶数。 In plain words: an operator is a symbol that does something to data. Learn them in four groups: arithmetic (+ − * / plus the two special ones, DIV and MOD), comparison (answers are always TRUE or FALSE), logical (combining two TRUE/FALSE values into one), and concatenation (joining two strings with &). DIV and MOD always come up: 17 ÷ 5 is 3 remainder 2, so 17 DIV 5 = 3 and 17 MOD 5 = 2. Use MOD to test odd/even — a number MOD 2 that gives 0 is even.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 算术运算：+ 加、− 减、* 乘、/ 除、DIV 整除（只留整数商）、MOD 取余（只留余数）。Arithmetic: + − * / plus DIV (whole-number quotient) and MOD (remainder).
  • 比较运算：= 等于、<> 不等于、< 小于、<= 小于等于、> 大于、>= 大于等于。结果一定是布尔值。Comparison: =, <> (not equal), <, <=, >, >= — the result is always Boolean.
  • 逻辑运算：AND（两边都为真才真）、OR（有一边为真就真）、NOT（取反）。Logical: AND (true only if both are true), OR (true if either is true), NOT (inverts).
  • 字符串连接用 &："Hello" & " Lin" 得到 "Hello Lin"。Concatenation uses &: "Hello" & " Lin" gives "Hello Lin".
  • 优先级：括号 () 最优先 → 乘除（含 DIV、MOD）→ 加减 → 比较 → NOT → AND → OR。拿不准就加括号，永远不会错。Precedence: brackets () first → multiply/divide (with DIV, MOD) → add/subtract → comparison → NOT → AND → OR. When in doubt, add brackets — it is never wrong.

例题 · 算一算Example · evaluate these

求下列表达式的值：(a) 20 DIV 6　(b) 20 MOD 6　(c) 2 + 3 * 4 − 6 / 3　(d) (7 > 5) AND (3 < 2)　(e) NOT (5 = 5) Evaluate: (a) 20 DIV 6 (b) 20 MOD 6 (c) 2 + 3 * 4 − 6 / 3 (d) (7 > 5) AND (3 < 2) (e) NOT (5 = 5)
(a) 20 ÷ 6 = 3 余 2 → 3(a) 20 ÷ 6 = 3 remainder 2 → 3 (b) 同上，余数 → 2(b) the remainder → 2 (c) 先乘除：3 * 4 = 12，6 / 3 = 2 → 2 + 12 − 2 = 12(c) multiply and divide first: 3 * 4 = 12, 6 / 3 = 2 → 2 + 12 − 2 = 12 (d) 7 > 5 是 TRUE，3 < 2 是 FALSE → TRUE AND FALSE = FALSE(d) 7 > 5 is TRUE, 3 < 2 is FALSE → TRUE AND FALSE = FALSE (e) 5 = 5 是 TRUE → NOT TRUE = FALSE(e) 5 = 5 is TRUE → NOT TRUE = FALSE

练习Practice

DIV 是商、MOD 是余数，别搞反。DIV is the quotient, MOD the remainder — do not swap them.

① 25 DIV 4 和 25 MOD 4 各是多少？两个结果有什么关系？1. What are 25 DIV 4 and 25 MOD 4? How are the two answers related?

② 写一个条件，判断整数变量 N 是不是 3 的倍数。2. Write a condition that tests whether the integer variable N is a multiple of 3.

点开看答案Show answer

① 25 ÷ 4 = 6 余 1 → DIV = 6，MOD = 1。关系：N = (N DIV 4) * 4 + (N MOD 4)，也就是 25 = 6 × 4 + 1。1. 25 ÷ 4 = 6 remainder 1 → DIV = 6, MOD = 1. They are linked by N = (N DIV 4) * 4 + (N MOD 4), i.e. 25 = 6 × 4 + 1.

② N MOD 3 = 0（能被 3 整除 → 余数是 0 → 就是 3 的倍数）。2. N MOD 3 = 0 — dividing by 3 leaves no remainder, so N is a multiple of 3.

— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 选择结构：IF 与 CASESelection: IF and CASE IF · THEN · ELSE · ENDIF如果 · 那么 · 否则 · 结束如果
这一站要会的：会写 IF…THEN…ELSE…ENDIF；会写嵌套 IF；会写 CASE OF…OTHERWISE…ENDCASE；知道什么时候该用 CASE 而不是 IF。 Goal: write IF…THEN…ELSE…ENDIF; nest IF statements; write CASE OF…OTHERWISE…ENDCASE; know when CASE beats IF.
说人话：程序不可能一直从上往下直线执行，它得会「看情况」：如果分数 ≥ 60 就输出「及格」，否则输出「不及格」——这就是选择结构。IF 是二选一（最多两条路）；CASE 是多选一，比如根据星期几做不同的事，用 CASE 写出来干净得多。记住每个 IF 都要有一个 ENDIF 关门，就像每个左括号都要有右括号，漏了程序就不成立。 In plain words: a program cannot always run straight down the page; it must depend on the situation: if the mark is at least 60 print "Pass", otherwise print "Fail" — that is selection. IF is a choice between two; CASE is a choice among many, such as doing something different for each day of the week, and CASE reads far more cleanly. Every IF needs an ENDIF to close it, just as every open bracket needs a closing one.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • IF … THEN … ENDIF：条件为真才执行 THEN 后面的语句；条件为假就整个跳过去。IF … THEN … ENDIF: the statements run only when the condition is true; otherwise they are skipped entirely.
  • IF … THEN … ELSE … ENDIF：二选一，一定且只会执行其中一支。IF … THEN … ELSE … ENDIF: one branch or the other runs, never both, never neither.
  • 嵌套 IF 就是在一个分支里再放一个 IF，写了几个 IF 就要写几个 ENDIF。A nested IF sits inside a branch — count your IFs and write the same number of ENDIFs.
  • CASE OF 变量：把变量的值和下面列出的每个取值逐一比对，命中哪个就执行哪个；都不命中就执行 OTHERWISE；最后 ENDCASE 收尾。CASE OF variable: the value is compared with each listed option; the matching one runs. If none matches, OTHERWISE runs. Close with ENDCASE.
  • CASE 适合「同一个变量等于各种固定值」的情况；如果条件是各种范围比较（比如 ≥ 80），还是用 IF。Use CASE when one variable is compared against fixed values; use IF when the conditions are ranges (such as ≥ 80).
最常犯的错：写 IF 忘了写 ENDIF；或者条件里用 = 判断字符串却忘了加引号（应该写 Day = "Mon"，不是 Day = Mon）。另外 等于号是 = 一个，不是 ==。 Most common mistakes: an IF with no ENDIF; comparing a string without quotes (Day = "Mon", not Day = Mon); and remember equals is a single =, not ==.

例题 · 成绩转等级（IF 版 + CASE 版）Example · marks to grades (IF and CASE)

输入一个 0–100 的分数 Score，输出等级：≥ 80 输出 "A"，≥ 60 输出 "B"，否则输出 "C"。再写一个 CASE 版本：输入 1–7 表示星期几，输出对应的英文星期名。 Input a mark Score from 0–100 and output a grade: at least 80 gives "A", at least 60 gives "B", otherwise "C". Then write a CASE version: input 1–7 for the day of the week and output the day name.
// 版本一：嵌套 IF INPUT Score IF Score >= 80 THEN OUTPUT "A" ELSE IF Score >= 60 THEN OUTPUT "B" ELSE OUTPUT "C" ENDIF ENDIF // 版本二：CASE OF INPUT Day CASE OF Day 1 : OUTPUT "Monday" 2 : OUTPUT "Tuesday" 3 : OUTPUT "Wednesday" 4 : OUTPUT "Thursday" 5 : OUTPUT "Friday" 6 : OUTPUT "Saturday" 7 : OUTPUT "Sunday" OTHERWISE : OUTPUT "Invalid" ENDCASE
① IF 版的判断顺序很关键：先问「够 80 吗」，不够再问「够 60 吗」。顺序反了就全错（先问 ≥ 60 的话，95 分也会被判成 B）。1. The order matters: ask "at least 80?" first, then "at least 60?". Reverse the order and it breaks — 95 would be graded B. ② 两个 IF 对应两个 ENDIF，缩进让自己一眼看清谁关谁。2. Two IFs, two ENDIFs — indentation shows which closes which. ③ CASE 版里每个分支写成 取值 : 语句，冒号前面是值、后面是动作。3. Each CASE branch is value : statement — the value before the colon, the action after. ④ OTHERWISE 是「兜底」，输入 8 或 0 时会执行它，程序不会莫名什么都不做。4. OTHERWISE is the safety net — an input of 8 or 0 lands there instead of doing nothing at all.

练习Practice

先想清楚「先问什么、后问什么」，再动笔。Decide the order of the questions before you start writing.

① 输入一个整数，如果是偶数就输出 "Even"，否则输出 "Odd"。（提示：用 MOD）1. Input an integer; output "Even" if it is even, otherwise "Odd". (Hint: use MOD.)

② 下面这段伪代码有错，找出来：
IF Score > 50
THEN OUTPUT "Pass"
ELSE OUTPUT "Fail"2. Find the error in this pseudocode:
IF Score > 50
THEN OUTPUT "Pass"
ELSE OUTPUT "Fail"

点开看答案Show answer

①
INPUT N
IF N MOD 2 = 0
THEN OUTPUT "Even"
ELSE OUTPUT "Odd"
ENDIF1.
INPUT N
IF N MOD 2 = 0
THEN OUTPUT "Even"
ELSE OUTPUT "Odd"
ENDIF

② 缺了 ENDIF。IF 一定要用 ENDIF 结束，否则结构不完整。2. The ENDIF is missing. Every IF must be closed with ENDIF.

— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 循环：FOR / WHILE / REPEATIteration: FOR / WHILE / REPEAT FOR · WHILE · REPEAT计数 · 当型 · 直到型
这一站要会的：会写三种循环；说清 WHILE 与 REPEAT 的区别；会写嵌套循环；会用 Total ← Total + x 求和、Count ← Count + 1 计数。 Goal: write all three loop types; explain WHILE versus REPEAT; nest loops; total with Total ← Total + x and count with Count ← Count + 1.
说人话：循环就是「同一段话重复做好几遍」，但三兄弟性格不同。FOR 是「做 10 遍」——次数你早就知道；WHILE 是「只要还饿就继续吃」——先看条件，条件一开始就不成立，那就一口都不吃；REPEAT 是「先吃一口，再问还饿吗」——至少吃一口。求和与计数是循环的招牌用法：准备一个初始为 0 的盒子，每轮往里累加（Total ← Total + x）或者让它加一（Count ← Count + 1）。 In plain words: a loop repeats a block of code, and the three types behave differently. FOR means "do this ten times" — you know the count in advance. WHILE means "keep eating while you are still hungry" — it checks first, so if the condition is false at the start the body never runs. REPEAT means "take a bite, then ask if you are still hungry" — the body always runs at least once. Totalling and counting are the classic uses: start a box at zero and either add into it each round (Total ← Total + x) or add one to it (Count ← Count + 1).
记住这几点：Key points:
  • FOR 计数循环：FOR i ← 1 TO 10 … NEXT i，次数事先确定，i 自己会加 1。FOR (count-controlled): FOR i ← 1 TO 10 … NEXT i — the number of repeats is known in advance and i increments automatically.
  • WHILE 前置条件循环：WHILE 条件 DO … ENDWHILE，先判断，条件不成立时一次都不执行。WHILE (pre-condition): WHILE condition DO … ENDWHILE — tested before the body, so it may run zero times.
  • REPEAT 后置条件循环：REPEAT … UNTIL 条件，先执行再判断，至少执行一次；条件是「到此为止」，条件为真就退出。REPEAT (post-condition): REPEAT … UNTIL condition — the body runs before the test, so it runs at least once; the condition means "stop now", so the loop ends when it becomes true.
  • 嵌套循环：外层每走一步，内层完整地走一圈。乘法表、二维数组的遍历都靠它。Nested loops: for every one step of the outer loop, the inner loop runs a full round. Use them for times tables and for walking through 2D arrays.
  • 求和：Total ← 0 开头，循环体内 Total ← Total + x；计数：Count ← 0 开头，循环体内 Count ← Count + 1。初值 0 别忘写。Totalling: start Total ← 0, then Total ← Total + x inside; counting: start Count ← 0, then Count ← Count + 1. Do not forget the initial zero.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：题目常问「WHILE 和 REPEAT 有什么不同」，答「测试条件的位置」和「最少执行次数」这两点。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: for "how does WHILE differ from REPEAT?" answer with two points: where the condition is tested, and the minimum number of times the body runs. A WHILE loop tests the condition before the statements, so the statements may never execute. A REPEAT loop executes the statements first and tests the condition after, so the statements always execute at least once.

例题 · 求和、计数与乘法表Example · totalling, counting and a times table

(a) 输入 10 个数，求它们的总和与平均值。　(b) 用嵌套循环输出 3 行乘法表（每行 3 个结果）。 (a) Input ten numbers and output their total and mean. (b) Use nested loops to print three rows of a times table (three results per row).
// (a) 求和与平均值 Total ← 0 FOR i ← 1 TO 10 INPUT Number Total ← Total + Number NEXT i OUTPUT "Total = ", Total OUTPUT "Mean = ", Total / 10 // (b) 嵌套循环做乘法表 FOR i ← 1 TO 3 FOR j ← 1 TO 3 OUTPUT i, " x ", j, " = ", i * j NEXT j NEXT i
① Total ← 0 必须在循环之前，写在循环里面就会每次清零，永远得不到总和。1. Total ← 0 must come before the loop — inside the loop it would reset every time and the total would never build up. ② Total ← Total + Number 读作「把原来的 Total 加上新数，再放回 Total」——不是数学等式，是覆盖。2. Total ← Total + Number means "take the old Total, add the new number, put the result back into Total" — it is an overwrite, not an equation. ③ 计数同理：想统计有多少人及格，就在 IF 里写 Count ← Count + 1。3. Counting works the same way: to count passes, put Count ← Count + 1 inside the IF. ④ 嵌套时 NEXT j 在内、NEXT i 在外，谁后开始谁先结束，顺序反了就不成立。4. In nesting, NEXT j (inner) comes before NEXT i (outer) — the loop started last finishes first.

练习Practice

判断该用哪种循环，先问自己：次数是已知的吗？To choose a loop, ask yourself: do I know the number of repeats in advance?

① 让用户输入密码，直到输入正确为止——用 WHILE 还是 REPEAT？为什么？1. The user enters a password until it is correct — WHILE or REPEAT? Why?

② 下面这段循环执行完后 Count 是多少？
Count ← 0
FOR i ← 2 TO 10
IF i MOD 3 = 0 THEN Count ← Count + 1
NEXT i
（提示：2 到 10 之间能被 3 整除的数有几个）2. What is Count after this loop?
Count ← 0
FOR i ← 2 TO 10
IF i MOD 3 = 0 THEN Count ← Count + 1
NEXT i
(Hint: how many numbers from 2 to 10 divide exactly by 3?)

点开看答案Show answer

① 用 REPEAT 更合适。密码至少要输入一次才能判断对不对，所以必须用 REPEAT…UNTIL（WHILE 得先有一个密码值才能判断，写起来要绕一圈）。写法：REPEAT INPUT P UNTIL P = "abc123"。1. REPEAT fits better: the password must be entered at least once before it can be checked, so use REPEAT … UNTIL P = "abc123".

② 2 到 10 之间能被 3 整除的是 3、6、9，共 3 个，所以 Count = 3。2. The multiples of 3 between 2 and 10 are 3, 6 and 9 — three of them — so Count = 3.

5 字符串处理String handling LENGTH · SUBSTRING · UCASE · ASC长度 · 截取 · 转大写 · 字符码
这一站要会的：会用 LENGTH、SUBSTRING、UCASE、LCASE、ASC、CHR 六个函数，并且知道每个返回什么类型。 Goal: use LENGTH, SUBSTRING, UCASE, LCASE, ASC and CHR, and know what type each one returns.
说人话：字符串就是「一串字符排成一排」，计算机给每个位置编了号，从 1 开始（这一点跟很多别的语言不一样，考试就爱考这个）。LENGTH 告诉你这一排有多长；SUBSTRING 是从第几位开始、切几个字符下来；UCASE / LCASE 就是全体变大写、全体变小写；ASC 和 CHR 是一对反操作——ASC 把字符变成它的编码数字，CHR 把编码数字变回字符。最后别忘了 &，它是把两段字符串粘起来的胶水。 In plain words: a string is a row of characters, and the computer numbers the positions starting at 1 (unlike many other languages, and a favourite exam detail). LENGTH says how long the row is; SUBSTRING cuts out a piece — you say where to start and how many characters to take; UCASE / LCASE convert the whole thing to upper or lower case; ASC and CHR are opposites — ASC turns a character into its code number, CHR turns a code number back into a character. And & is the glue that joins two strings together.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • LENGTH(x) → 返回 INTEGER，字符串里有几个字符（空格也算）。LENGTH(x) returns an INTEGER — how many characters, spaces included.
  • SUBSTRING(x, 开始位置, 长度) → 返回 STRING。第二个参数是起始下标，第三个是取几个字符。SUBSTRING(x, start, length) returns a STRING. The second argument is the starting position, the third is how many characters.
  • UCASE(x) / LCASE(x) → 返回 STRING，全部转大写 / 全部转小写。UCASE(x) / LCASE(x) return a STRING in upper / lower case.
  • ASC(ch) → 返回 INTEGER，这个字符的编码（例如 'A' 是 65）。ASC(ch) returns an INTEGER — the character code (for example 'A' is 65).
  • CHR(n) → 返回 CHAR，编码对应的字符（CHR(65) 是 'A'）。CHR(n) returns a CHAR — the character with that code (CHR(65) is 'A').
  • & 把两段字符串连接起来，例如 "Hi" & " !" 得到 "Hi !"。& concatenates two strings, e.g. "Hi" & " !" gives "Hi !".
  • 位置从 1 开始，最后一个字符的位置就是 LENGTH(x)。Positions start at 1, so the last character is at position LENGTH(x).

例题 · 把输入的名字转成大写并取首字母Example · upper-case a name and take the initial

输入一个名字，输出它的大写形式，再输出它的首字母，最后输出它有多少个字符。 Input a name; output it in upper case, then output its first letter, then output how many characters it has.
INPUT Name Big ← UCASE(Name) First ← SUBSTRING(Big, 1, 1) OUTPUT "大写：", Big OUTPUT "首字母：", First OUTPUT "长度：", LENGTH(Name)
① 输入 "lin" → UCASE 得到 "LIN"。1. Input "lin" → UCASE gives "LIN". ② SUBSTRING(Big, 1, 1) 从第 1 位开始取 1 个 → "L"。2. SUBSTRING(Big, 1, 1) starts at position 1 and takes one character → "L". ③ LENGTH("lin") = 3，注意是数字 3，不是字符串 "3"。3. LENGTH("lin") = 3 — a number, not the string "3". ④ 想取最后一个字符就写 SUBSTRING(Name, LENGTH(Name), 1)——这是考试的常见组合技。4. To take the last character write SUBSTRING(Name, LENGTH(Name), 1) — a classic exam combination.

练习Practice

SUBSTRING 的三个参数：字符串、从哪开始、取几个。SUBSTRING takes three arguments: the string, where to start, how many characters.

① LENGTH("IGCSE CS") 等于几？SUBSTRING("IGCSE CS", 7, 2) 得到什么？1. What is LENGTH("IGCSE CS")? What does SUBSTRING("IGCSE CS", 7, 2) give?

② 已知 ASC('A') = 65，那么 ASC('D') 是多少？CHR(70) 是什么字符？2. Given ASC('A') = 65, what is ASC('D')? What character is CHR(70)?

点开看答案Show answer

① "IGCSE CS" 一共 8 个字符（中间的空格也算一个）→ LENGTH = 8；第 7 位开始取 2 个，第 7 位是 'C'、第 8 位是 'S' → "CS"。1. "IGCSE CS" has 8 characters (the space counts) → LENGTH = 8; starting at position 7 and taking 2 gives 'C' then 'S' → "CS".

② A、B、C、D 依次是 65、66、67、68 → ASC('D') = 68；70 是往后数第 5 个字母 → CHR(70) = 'F'。2. A, B, C, D are 65, 66, 67, 68 → ASC('D') = 68; 70 is the fifth letter along → CHR(70) = 'F'.

— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —
6 过程与函数Procedures and functions PROCEDURE · FUNCTION · BYREF过程 · 函数 · 按引用
这一站要会的：会写 PROCEDURE 和 FUNCTION；说清两者的区别；说清 BYVALUE 与 BYREF 的区别；知道局部变量与全局变量。 Goal: write a PROCEDURE and a FUNCTION; state the difference between them; explain BYVALUE versus BYREF; know local and global variables.
说人话：写好一大段程序之后会发现「这段输入校验」要用到三次，总不能抄三遍。于是把它打包成一个带名字的小程序，用的时候喊一声名字就行——这就是子程序。它分两种：过程 PROCEDURE 是「去把这件事做了」，做完就完事，不给你任何结果；函数 FUNCTION 是「去把这个算出来」，算完必须把结果交回来，所以函数可以用在赋值右边（y ← Sq(5)），过程不行。至于参数，BYVALUE（按值）是把值复印一份递过去，子程序怎么折腾都不影响原件；BYREF（按引用）是把原变量的地址递过去，子程序改了，原件就真的变了。 In plain words: once a program grows you notice the same input check is needed three times, and copying it three times is horrible. So you wrap it up as a named mini-program and call it by name — that is a subroutine. There are two kinds: a PROCEDURE means "go and do this", and it hands nothing back; a FUNCTION means "go and work this out", and it must return the answer, which is why a function can sit on the right of an assignment (y ← Sq(5)) while a procedure cannot. For parameters, BYVALUE hands over a photocopy, so whatever the subroutine does leaves the original untouched; BYREF hands over the address of the original variable, so changes really do stick.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 过程：PROCEDURE 名字(参数 : 类型) … ENDPROCEDURE；调用时用 CALL 名字(实参)。Procedure: PROCEDURE name(param : TYPE) … ENDPROCEDURE; call it with CALL name(arguments).
  • 函数：FUNCTION 名字(参数 : 类型) RETURNS 类型 … ENDFUNCTION，函数体里必须有 RETURN 值。Function: FUNCTION name(param : TYPE) RETURNS TYPE … ENDFUNCTION, and the body must contain RETURN value.
  • BYVALUE（按值传递）：传进去的是副本，子程序里的修改不会影响主程序的变量。BYVALUE: a copy is passed, so changes inside the subroutine do not affect the original variable.
  • BYREF（按引用传递）：传进去的是变量本身，子程序里的修改会影响主程序的变量。BYREF: the variable itself is passed, so changes inside the subroutine do affect the original.
  • 局部变量 local：只在子程序内部存在，出去就消失，别的子程序看不到它；全局变量 global：整个程序都能用。Local variables exist only inside the subroutine and vanish on exit; global variables are visible everywhere in the program.
  • 好处：程序更短、更好读、改一次全都生效，还能多人分工各写各的模块。Why bother: shorter code, easier to read, one fix applies everywhere, and different people can write different modules.
考场上最容易混的三件事：① 函数忘了写 RETURN；② 过程当成函数用，写成 y ← Greet("Lin")（这是错的，过程没有值）；③ 忘了写 RETURNS 后面返回的类型。看到题目说「返回一个值」就用 FUNCTION，只说「输出 / 显示」就用 PROCEDURE。 Three things candidates muddle: 1. a function with no RETURN; 2. using a procedure as if it had a value, e.g. y ← Greet("Lin"); 3. omitting the type after RETURNS. If the question says "return a value", use FUNCTION; if it says "output / display", use PROCEDURE.

例题 · 一个过程 + 一个函数Example · one procedure and one function

写一个过程 ShowResult，接收一个字符串参数并输出它；再写一个函数 Average，接收两个实数，返回它们的平均值；然后在主程序里调用它们。 Write a procedure ShowResult that takes a string parameter and outputs it; then a function Average that takes two reals and returns their mean; then call both from the main program.
PROCEDURE ShowResult(Message : STRING) OUTPUT "结果是：", Message ENDPROCEDURE FUNCTION Average(a : REAL, b : REAL) RETURNS REAL RETURN (a + b) / 2 ENDFUNCTION // 主程序 DECLARE M : REAL M ← Average(70, 86) CALL ShowResult("平均分已算出") OUTPUT "平均分：", M
① 过程没有 RETURNS 那一行，函数必须有 RETURNS REAL。1. The procedure has no RETURNS line; the function must have RETURNS REAL. ② 调用过程用 CALL；函数直接写在赋值右边或表达式里。2. Call a procedure with CALL; a function goes on the right of an assignment or inside an expression. ③ Average(70, 86) 返回 (70+86)/2 = 78，存进 M。3. Average(70, 86) returns (70+86)/2 = 78, which is stored in M. ④ 参数默认按值传递（BYVALUE）。如果想让子程序改得动主程序的变量，要显式写成 PROCEDURE Change(BYREF n : INTEGER)。4. Parameters are passed by value by default. To let the subroutine change the caller's variable, write PROCEDURE Change(BYREF n : INTEGER).

练习Practice

先判断该用过程还是函数，再想参数怎么传。Decide procedure or function first, then how to pass the parameters.

① 主程序里 x ← 5，调用 CALL Add1(BYVALUE x)，子程序里写 x ← x + 1。调用结束后 x 是几？如果改成 BYREF 呢？1. The main program sets x ← 5 and calls CALL Add1(BYVALUE x), where the procedure does x ← x + 1. What is x afterwards? What if it were BYREF?

② 下面哪里写错了？
FUNCTION Total(a : INTEGER, b : INTEGER)
OUTPUT a + b
ENDFUNCTION2. What is wrong here?
FUNCTION Total(a : INTEGER, b : INTEGER)
OUTPUT a + b
ENDFUNCTION

点开看答案Show answer

① BYVALUE：传进去的是复印件，子程序里改成 6，主程序的 x 仍然是 5。BYREF：传进去的是原件，主程序的 x 变成 6。1. BYVALUE: a copy was passed, so the caller's x stays 5. BYREF: the original was passed, so x becomes 6.

② 两个错：函数没有 RETURNS INTEGER 这一行，而且函数体里没有 RETURN。函数必须交回一个值，只 OUTPUT 是不够的。正确写法：FUNCTION Total(a : INTEGER, b : INTEGER) RETURNS INTEGER + RETURN a + b。2. Two errors: there is no RETURNS INTEGER line and no RETURN in the body. A function must hand back a value; OUTPUT alone is not enough. It should read FUNCTION Total(a : INTEGER, b : INTEGER) RETURNS INTEGER with RETURN a + b.

— 第 6 站完成 —— Stop 6 done —
7 数组：一维与二维Arrays: 1D and 2D ARRAY · index · 2D数组 · 下标 · 二维
这一站要会的：会声明一维与二维数组；会用 FOR 遍历数组；会求和、找最大值；知道下标从 1 开始。 Goal: declare 1D and 2D arrays; traverse them with FOR; total them and find the maximum; remember indices start at 1.
说人话：存一个分数用一个变量就够了，存全班 40 个分数难道要建 40 个变量？当然不。数组就是「一组同类型、连号的小盒子」，共用一个名字，靠下标区分：Mark[1] 是第 1 个、Mark[2] 是第 2 个……注意从 1 开始数，不是从 0 开始（这是 IGCSE 的规定，也是最容易写错的地方）。二维数组就是「表格」：棋盘、座位表、成绩单都是它，定位要两个数——先说第几行，再说第几列。 In plain words: one variable is fine for one mark, but would you really create forty variables for a class of forty? No. An array is a group of same-type boxes sharing one name, told apart by an index: Mark[1] is the first, Mark[2] the second, and so on. Note that counting starts at 1, not 0 — that is the IGCSE convention and the easiest thing to get wrong. A 2D array is simply a table: a chess board, a seating plan, a mark sheet. You need two indices — the row first, then the column.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 声明：DECLARE Name : ARRAY[下界:上界] OF 类型，例如 DECLARE Mark : ARRAY[1:6] OF INTEGER。Declaration: DECLARE Name : ARRAY[lower:upper] OF TYPE, e.g. DECLARE Mark : ARRAY[1:6] OF INTEGER.
  • 二维声明：DECLARE Board : ARRAY[1:3,1:3] OF STRING，两组下标用逗号隔开。2D declaration: DECLARE Board : ARRAY[1:3,1:3] OF STRING — the two index ranges are separated by a comma.
  • 下标从 1 开始：ARRAY[1:6] 有 6 个元素，下标是 1 到 6，没有 Mark[0]，写 Mark[7] 就越界了。Indices start at 1: ARRAY[1:6] has six elements numbered 1 to 6; there is no Mark[0], and Mark[7] is out of range.
  • 遍历用 FOR：FOR i ← 1 TO 6 里面对 Mark[i] 操作，i 换成几就等于访问第几个。Traverse with FOR: FOR i ← 1 TO 6 and work on Mark[i] — changing i moves along the array.
  • 找最大值的套路：先把 Max 设成第一个元素（不要设成 0，万一全是负数就错了），然后逐个比较。Finding the maximum: start Max at the first element (not at 0 — that breaks when all values are negative), then compare one by one.
  • 二维数组遍历要两层循环：外层走行、内层走列。Walking a 2D array needs two nested loops: the outer one for rows, the inner one for columns.

例题 · 求和、找最大值、遍历二维座位表Example · total, maximum and walking a 2D seating plan

(a) 数组 Mark[1:6] 已存好 6 个分数，求总和与最高分。　(b) 有一个 3×3 的座位表 Board，把每个位置的名字逐个输出。 (a) The array Mark[1:6] already holds six marks; find the total and the highest. (b) A 3×3 seating plan Board holds names — output every position.
// (a) 求和与找最大值 DECLARE Mark : ARRAY[1:6] OF INTEGER Total ← 0 Max ← Mark[1] FOR i ← 1 TO 6 Total ← Total + Mark[i] IF Mark[i] > Max THEN Max ← Mark[i] ENDIF NEXT i OUTPUT "总和 = ", Total OUTPUT "最高分 = ", Max // (b) 二维数组遍历 DECLARE Board : ARRAY[1:3,1:3] OF STRING FOR Row ← 1 TO 3 FOR Col ← 1 TO 3 OUTPUT Board[Row,Col] NEXT Col NEXT Row
① Max ← Mark[1] 拿第一个元素当起点最保险；如果写成 Max ← 0，遇到全是负分就会输出 0，那是个不存在的成绩。1. Max ← Mark[1] is the safe starting point; Max ← 0 would report 0 for an all-negative set — a mark nobody actually got. ② 循环里 Mark[i] 的 i 会自己从 1 变到 6，所以 6 个元素都会被访问到。2. Inside the loop i runs from 1 to 6 by itself, so every element is visited. ③ 二维数组用 Board[Row,Col]，行在前、列在后，写成 Board[Col,Row] 就取错人了。3. A 2D element is Board[Row,Col] — row first, column second; swapping them fetches the wrong seat. ④ 内层循环必须先结束（NEXT Col 在 NEXT Row 前面），这样才是「一行走完再走下一行」。4. The inner loop must close first (NEXT Col before NEXT Row) so that each row is finished before moving to the next.

练习Practice

记住下标从 1 开始，别写出 [0]。Indices start at 1 — never write [0].

① DECLARE Temp : ARRAY[1:5] OF REAL 里有几个元素？合法的下标是哪些？1. How many elements does DECLARE Temp : ARRAY[1:5] OF REAL have? Which indices are valid?

② 数组 N[1:4] 依次装着 7、2、9、4。用上面的「找最大值」算法跑一遍，每一步 Max 是多少？最后 Max 是几？2. The array N[1:4] holds 7, 2, 9, 4. Trace the maximum algorithm: what is Max after each step, and at the end?

点开看答案Show answer

① 5 个元素，合法下标是 1、2、3、4、5。1. Five elements, with valid indices 1, 2, 3, 4, 5.

② 起点 Max = N[1] = 7；i=2 时 2 < 7，Max 仍是 7；i=3 时 9 > 7，Max 变成 9；i=4 时 4 < 9，Max 仍是 9。最后 Max = 9。2. Start Max = N[1] = 7; at i=2, 2 < 7 so Max stays 7; at i=3, 9 > 7 so Max becomes 9; at i=4, 4 < 9 so Max stays 9. Final Max = 9.

— 第 7 站完成 —— Stop 7 done —
8 文件处理与库函数File handling and library routines OPENFILE · EOF · RANDOM打开文件 · 判结尾 · 随机数
这一站要会的：背下 OPENFILE / WRITEFILE / READFILE / CLOSEFILE / EOF 五句；会写「写入全班成绩再读出来求和」的程序；会用 RANDOM()、ROUND()、INT()。 Goal: memorise OPENFILE, WRITEFILE, READFILE, CLOSEFILE and EOF; write a program that saves a class's marks then reads them back and totals them; use RANDOM(), ROUND() and INT().
说人话：变量和数组都住在内存里，一关机就没了。想让数据留下来，就得写到文件里。操作文件跟借书一个道理：先打开（OPENFILE，并说明是读还是写）、读或写（READFILE / WRITEFILE）、用完一定还（CLOSEFILE）。读文件有个特殊问题——你怎么知道读完了？答案是 EOF(文件名)，一到文件末尾它就变成 TRUE，所以循环条件写成 WHILE NOT EOF(...)。另外三个库函数是现成的小工具：RANDOM() 给一个 0 到 1 之间的随机小数，ROUND(x, n) 把 x 四舍五入到 n 位小数，INT(x) 直接砍掉小数部分。 In plain words: variables and arrays live in memory and vanish when the power goes off. To keep data, write it to a file. Working with a file is like borrowing a book: open it first (OPENFILE, saying whether you are reading or writing), read or write (READFILE / WRITEFILE), then always give it back (CLOSEFILE). Reading raises one extra question — how do you know when you have finished? The answer is EOF(filename), which turns TRUE at the end of the file, hence the loop condition WHILE NOT EOF(...). Three library routines come free: RANDOM() gives a random value between 0 and 1, ROUND(x, n) rounds x to n decimal places, and INT(x) chops off the fractional part.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • OPENFILE 文件名 FOR 模式，模式有三种：READ（读）、WRITE（写，会覆盖原来的内容）、APPEND（在末尾追加）。OPENFILE filename FOR mode, where mode is READ, WRITE (overwrites existing content) or APPEND (adds to the end).
  • WRITEFILE 文件名, 数据 把数据写进去；READFILE 文件名, 变量 读一行存进变量。WRITEFILE filename, data writes data; READFILE filename, variable reads one item into the variable.
  • CLOSEFILE 文件名：用完必须关闭，否则数据可能没真正存到盘上。CLOSEFILE filename — you must close it, otherwise the data may never actually reach the disk.
  • EOF(文件名) 返回一个布尔值：到末尾是 TRUE，还没到是 FALSE。所以读文件的循环写 WHILE NOT EOF(...)。EOF(filename) returns a Boolean: TRUE at the end of the file, FALSE otherwise — hence WHILE NOT EOF(...).
  • RANDOM() 返回 0 到 1 之间的随机小数；想摇一个 1 到 6 的骰子就写 INT(RANDOM() * 6) + 1。RANDOM() returns a random value between 0 and 1; to roll a die from 1 to 6 write INT(RANDOM() * 6) + 1.
  • ROUND(x, n) 四舍五入保留 n 位小数；INT(x) 只取整数部分（直接砍掉小数，不四舍五入）。ROUND(x, n) rounds to n decimal places; INT(x) keeps the integer part (it truncates, it does not round).

例题 · 写入全班成绩，再读出来求和Example · write a class's marks to a file, then read them back and total them

先让用户输入 5 个分数，逐个写进文件 Scores.txt；然后重新打开这个文件，把每个分数读出来求和并输出。 Input five marks and write each one to the file Scores.txt; then reopen the file, read every mark back and output the total.
// 第一步：写入文件 OPENFILE "Scores.txt" FOR WRITE FOR i ← 1 TO 5 INPUT Score WRITEFILE "Scores.txt", Score NEXT i CLOSEFILE "Scores.txt" // 第二步：读出来求和 Total ← 0 OPENFILE "Scores.txt" FOR READ WHILE NOT EOF("Scores.txt") DO READFILE "Scores.txt", Score Total ← Total + Score ENDWHILE CLOSEFILE "Scores.txt" OUTPUT "Total = ", Total
① 写文件用 FOR WRITE，读文件用 FOR READ——模式写反了就什么都读不到。1. Writing uses FOR WRITE, reading uses FOR READ — swap them and you read nothing at all. ② WRITEFILE 后面先写文件名、再写要写的数据，中间是逗号。2. After WRITEFILE comes the filename, then the data, separated by a comma. ③ 读写都结束后各写一次 CLOSEFILE，写完不关等于没保存。3. Call CLOSEFILE once after each phase — not closing after writing means nothing is saved. ④ WHILE NOT EOF(...) 的意思是「只要还没到文件末尾就继续读」，读到末尾自动停，不用事先知道有多少条。4. WHILE NOT EOF(...) means "keep reading while the end has not been reached" — it stops by itself, so you do not need to know the number of records in advance.

练习Practice

INT 是砍掉小数，ROUND 是四舍五入，别搞混。INT truncates, ROUND rounds — do not mix them up.

① 写出 INT(7.9)、ROUND(7.9, 0)、ROUND(3.14159, 2) 的结果。1. Give the results of INT(7.9), ROUND(7.9, 0) and ROUND(3.14159, 2).

② 想随机生成一个 1 到 100 之间的整数，怎么写？2. How would you generate a random integer between 1 and 100?

点开看答案Show answer

① INT(7.9) = 7（直接砍掉小数）；ROUND(7.9, 0) = 8（四舍五入到整数）；ROUND(3.14159, 2) = 3.14。1. INT(7.9) = 7 (truncated); ROUND(7.9, 0) = 8 (rounded to whole numbers); ROUND(3.14159, 2) = 3.14.

② N ← INT(RANDOM() * 100) + 1。RANDOM() 给 0 到 1 之间的小数，乘 100 变成 0 到 100 之间，INT 砍成 0 到 99，再加 1 就正好是 1 到 100。2. N ← INT(RANDOM() * 100) + 1. RANDOM() gives 0 to 1, × 100 gives 0 to 100, INT leaves 0 to 99, and + 1 shifts it to exactly 1 to 100.

— 第 8 站完成 —— Stop 8 done —

单元 8 完成 · 主线：数据存进盒子 → 用运算符加工 → 选择和循环控制流程 → 打包成模块Unit 8 done · the thread: put data in boxes → process it with operators → control the flow with selection and iteration → wrap it up in modules

回头看就四句话：① 先声明再使用（DECLARE 变量、数组，CONSTANT 常量）；② 三种结构搭一切（顺序、选择、循环）；③ 重复的代码就打包（过程不返回值、函数返回值）；④ 要留下来的数据写进文件。写伪代码时缩进要对齐、每个 IF 配一个 ENDIF、每个 FOR 配一个 NEXT——阅卷老师就是照这几条挑错的。In hindsight it is four lines: 1. declare before use (DECLARE for variables and arrays, CONSTANT for constants); 2. three structures build everything (sequence, selection, iteration); 3. wrap up repeated code (procedures return nothing, functions do); 4. write anything that must survive into a file. When writing pseudocode, keep the indentation tidy, pair every IF with an ENDIF and every FOR with a NEXT — that is exactly what the examiner scans for.

单元 9 · 数据库Unit 9 · DatabasesPAPER 2
