1 文件存数据的四个毛病Four problems with file-based storage Redundancy · Inconsistency冗余 · 不一致
这一站要会的：说出用普通文件存数据的四个问题；说出数据库对应的四个好处；考试时能结合场景举例说明。 Goal: state four problems with storing data in ordinary files; state four matching advantages of a database; illustrate each with an example.
说人话：把数据塞进一个普通文本文件，小打小闹还行，人一多就翻车。第一，同一个人的信息在十个订单里抄十遍（数据冗余）；第二，他搬家后你只改了其中三处，剩下七处还是老地址——同一件事出现了两个版本（数据不一致）；第三，老师想问「上海的学生有几个」，没有现成工具，只能写个新程序把文件从头翻到尾（查询困难）；第四，两个老师同时改同一个学生的成绩，后保存的那个直接把前一个覆盖掉（并发冲突）。数据库就是把这些活儿全接过去：数据只存一份、有统一的查询语言、还能给记录加锁、给不同人不同权限。 In plain words: stuffing data into a plain text file is fine on a small scale and falls apart as soon as it grows. First, the same person's details get copied into ten orders (redundancy). Second, when they move house you update three of those copies and seven still show the old address — one fact, two versions (inconsistency). Third, asking "how many students are from Shanghai?" has no ready tool, so someone must write a new program that scans the whole file (hard to query). Fourth, if two teachers edit the same mark at the same time, whoever saves last silently overwrites the other (concurrent access conflict). A database takes over all of that: one copy of the data, a standard query language, record locking, and different access rights for different users.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 数据重复 / 冗余 redundancy：同一份数据在多个文件里各存一份，浪费空间。Redundancy: the same data is duplicated across files, wasting space.
  • 数据不一致 inconsistency：改了其中一份、没改另一份，同一个事实出现两个版本，你不知道该信哪个。Inconsistency: one copy is updated and another is not, so the same fact has two versions and you cannot tell which is right.
  • 查询困难：没有通用的查询语言，每问一个新问题就得写一段新程序。Hard to query: there is no general query language, so every new question needs a new program.
  • 并发访问冲突：多人同时修改同一条数据，后写入的覆盖先写入的（丢失更新）。Concurrent access problems: several users edit the same record at once and the later save overwrites the earlier one (a lost update).
  • 数据库的好处：减少冗余、保证一致、用 SQL 快速查询、支持并发与加锁、可设置访问权限与备份。Database benefits: less redundancy, consistency, fast queries with SQL, concurrency with locking, and access rights plus backups.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：题目常问「说明用文件存数据的问题」，四选二作答，每条都要「问题 + 举例」。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: "describe problems with a file-based system" — give two, and each one needs a problem plus an example. Data is duplicated in several files, which wastes storage space. If a customer changes address, some copies may be updated and others not, so the data becomes inconsistent — for example one file shows the new address while another still shows the old one.

例题 · 场景分析Example · a scenario

一家小店用文本文件记录订单，每个订单一行，里面都写着顾客的姓名、电话和地址。说出这种做法的两个问题，并说明数据库怎么解决。 A small shop records orders in a text file, one line per order, each line containing the customer's name, phone number and address. Describe two problems with this and explain how a database solves them.
问题 1：老顾客下了 20 单，他的姓名、电话、地址就被抄了 20 遍，白白占空间（冗余）。解决：数据库里顾客单独一张表，每人只存一条，订单里只记他的编号。Problem 1: a regular customer with 20 orders has their name, phone and address copied 20 times, wasting space (redundancy). Fix: a database keeps customers in their own table, one row each, and the order just stores their ID. 问题 2：顾客换了手机号，你只改了最近几单，之前的单子还是旧号码，同一件事两个版本（不一致）。解决：数据库里改一次编号对应的那条记录，所有地方读到的都是新号码。Problem 2: when the customer changes phone number you update only recent orders, so older ones still show the old number — one fact, two versions (inconsistency). Fix: in a database you change that one record and every query then reads the new number. 加分项：还可以提「想统计本月销售额要写新程序」和「两个人同时改会互相覆盖」这两条。Extra credit: you can also mention "working out this month's sales needs a new program" and "two people editing at once overwrite each other".

练习Practice

答这类题的套路是「先说问题名称，再举个具体的例子」。The pattern is: name the problem, then give a concrete example.

① 「数据冗余」和「数据不一致」是一回事吗？请分别用一句话说明。1. Are "data redundancy" and "data inconsistency" the same thing? Explain each in one sentence.

② 学校用电子表格存全校成绩，两个老师同时打开同一份文件改分。说出会发生什么问题，以及数据库怎么避免它。2. A school keeps all marks in a spreadsheet, and two teachers open the same file to edit it at the same time. What problem occurs, and how does a database avoid it?

点开看答案Show answer

① 不是一回事。冗余是「同一份数据存了很多份」，不一致是「这些拷贝之间内容对不上」。冗余是不一致的原因——正因为抄了很多份，才会改漏。1. No. Redundancy is "the same data stored many times"; inconsistency is "those copies no longer agree". Redundancy is the cause of inconsistency — you cannot miss an update if there is only one copy.

② 会发生丢失更新：两人各自改完保存，后保存的那份把先保存的覆盖，其中一位的修改白做了。数据库用记录加锁解决——一个人正在改这条记录时，系统会锁住它，另一个人只能等，改完解锁。2. A lost update: each teacher saves their own version and the later save overwrites the earlier one, so one set of edits is silently lost. A database prevents it by locking the record — while one user is editing it, others must wait until it is released.

— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 表、记录、字段与主键Tables, records, fields and primary keys Table · Record · Field · Primary key表 · 记录 · 字段 · 主键
这一站要会的：说清 table / record / field 三个词；说出六种字段数据类型；说清主键是什么、有什么要求，并能为一张表挑出合适的主键。 Goal: define table, record and field; name six field data types; state what a primary key is and what it requires, and choose one for a given table.
说人话：数据库里的表跟电子表格长得一模一样。表 table 就是整张表；横着看一行 = 一条记录 record，是关于「一个东西」的完整信息（比如一个学生的全部资料）；竖着看一列 = 一个字段 field，是大家都有的一项属性（比如所有人的年龄）。每个字段要定数据类型，跟编程里一样：文字用 TEXT，整数用 INTEGER……最后是主键 primary key：它是每条记录的「身份证号」，不能重复、不能为空，凭它就能精准找到唯一的那条记录。 In plain words: a database table looks exactly like a spreadsheet. The table is the whole thing; read across, one row is one record — everything about one thing (one student's complete details); read down, one column is one field — one attribute that everybody has (everyone's age). Each field needs a data type, just as in programming: TEXT for words, INTEGER for whole numbers, and so on. Finally there is the primary key: each record's identity number. It must be unique and never empty, and it lets you pick out exactly one record with confidence.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 表 table：存放同一类数据的地方，由行和列组成。Table: where one kind of data is stored, made of rows and columns.
  • 记录 record：表里的一行，是关于一个对象（一个学生、一本书）的完整信息。Record: one row — the complete information about a single object (one student, one book).
  • 字段 field：表里的一列，是所有记录共有的一项属性，有自己的字段名和数据类型。Field: one column — one attribute shared by every record, with its own name and data type.
  • 数据类型：TEXT（文字）、CHARACTER（单个字符）、INTEGER（整数）、REAL（带小数）、BOOLEAN（是/否）、DATE/TIME（日期时间）。Data types: TEXT, CHARACTER, INTEGER, REAL, BOOLEAN, DATE/TIME.
  • 主键 primary key：唯一标识每条记录的字段。要求 ① 每条记录的值都不同（唯一）；② 不能为空。Primary key: the field that uniquely identifies each record. It must be 1. unique for every record; 2. never empty.
  • 选主键的思路：找一个肯定不会重、肯定都有的字段——学号、身份证号、商品编号都很合适。Choosing a key: pick a field that is guaranteed unique and always present — a student ID, passport number or product code.
为什么姓名不能当主键：① 会重名——年级里有两个 "Wang"，主键就重复了，数据库会拒绝；② 有人可能暂时没填名字，主键就空了。反过来学号是学校发的、每人一个、一定填得上，天然就是主键。 Why a name is a bad primary key: 1. names repeat — two students called Wang would break the uniqueness rule and the database would reject it; 2. a name might be missing for a new record, and a primary key cannot be empty. A student ID is issued once per student and always present, so it is a natural key.

例题 · 挑主键、定类型Example · choosing a key and types

有一张 Book 表，字段是：BookID、Title、Author、Price、InStock。
(a) 哪个字段适合当主键？为什么 Title 不合适？
(b) 给这五个字段各选一个合适的数据类型。 A Book table has the fields BookID, Title, Author, Price and InStock.
(a) Which field should be the primary key? Why is Title unsuitable?
(b) Suggest a data type for each of the five fields.
(a) 主键选 BookID——每本书一个编号，唯一且一定有值。Title 不行：同一个书名可能有好几本（重名），而且书名较长、容易打错。(a) Use BookID — one code per book, unique and always present. Title fails: several copies can share a title, and long titles are easy to mistype. (b) BookID → INTEGER（或 TEXT，如果是 "B-1001" 这种带字母的编号）；Title → TEXT；Author → TEXT；Price → REAL（有小数）；InStock → BOOLEAN（只有「有货 / 没货」两种）。(b) BookID → INTEGER (or TEXT for codes like "B-1001"); Title → TEXT; Author → TEXT; Price → REAL; InStock → BOOLEAN (in stock or not).

练习Practice

先问「会不会重复」「会不会为空」。Ask "could it repeat?" and "could it be empty?".

① 一张 Student 表有这些字段：Name、Email、DateOfBirth、Class、StudentID。哪个最适合作主键？Email 行不行？1. A Student table has Name, Email, DateOfBirth, Class and StudentID. Which makes the best primary key? Would Email do?

② 说明字段 PhoneNumber 该用 TEXT 还是 INTEGER。（提示：电话号码会不会拿来做加减乘除？会不会以 0 开头？）2. Should PhoneNumber be TEXT or INTEGER? (Hint: will anyone do arithmetic on a phone number? Can it start with 0?)

点开看答案Show answer

① 选 StudentID。Email 通常也唯一，但它可能为空（有的学生没填），而且学生会换邮箱，会变的值不适合当主键；另外 Name 会重名、DateOfBirth 会重复、Class 更是一堆人共用，都不行。1. StudentID. An email is usually unique too, but it may be empty and it can change — a value that changes makes a poor key. Names repeat, dates of birth repeat, and Class is shared by many.

② 用 TEXT。电话号码不会拿来做算术，而且常常以 0 开头（写成 INTEGER 会把前导的 0 丢掉），还可能有 +、空格、短横线。2. TEXT. Nobody does arithmetic on phone numbers, they often start with 0 (which an INTEGER would drop), and they may contain +, spaces or dashes.

— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 SQL 基础：SELECT … FROM … WHERESQL basics: SELECT … FROM … WHERE SELECT · WHERE · AND · OR查询 · 条件 · 与 · 或
这一站要会的：会写 SELECT … FROM … WHERE；会用 =、<、>、<=、>=、<> 六个比较条件；会用 AND、OR；知道文字要加引号、数字不加。 Goal: write SELECT … FROM … WHERE; use =, <, >, <=, >= and <>; combine conditions with AND and OR; know that text needs quotes and numbers do not.
说人话：SQL 就是跟数据库说话的语言，最常用的一句永远是这个套路：「我要哪几列（SELECT），从哪张表拿（FROM），只要满足什么条件的行（WHERE）」。把它想成挑人：SELECT 是「我要看他们的姓名和分数」，FROM 是「从学生表里挑」，WHERE 是「只要北京的、分数 80 以上的」。两个最容易扣分的地方：文字（字符串）要加单引号，数字不要加引号；AND 是「两个条件都要满足」，OR 是「满足任意一个就行」。 In plain words: SQL is the language you speak to a database, and one pattern covers almost everything: "which columns do I want (SELECT), from which table (FROM), and only rows meeting which condition (WHERE)". Think of picking people out of a crowd: SELECT is "I want their names and marks", FROM is "out of the student table", WHERE is "only those from Beijing with 80 or more". Two things cost marks constantly: text values need single quotes, numbers must not have them; AND means both conditions must hold, OR means either one.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 基本句型：SELECT 字段 FROM 表名 WHERE 条件;，关键字惯例用大写，结尾写一个分号。Basic form: SELECT fields FROM table WHERE condition; — keywords are conventionally upper case, with a semicolon at the end.
  • 比较条件：= 等于、<> 不等于、< 小于、<= 小于等于、> 大于、>= 大于等于。Comparisons: =, <> (not equal), <, <=, >, >=.
  • AND：两个条件同时成立才选中；OR：任意一个成立就选中。AND: both conditions must be true; OR: either one being true is enough.
  • 引号规则：City = 'Beijing' 文字加单引号；Mark >= 80 数字不加引号。写反了就一分没有。Quoting: City = 'Beijing' — text in single quotes; Mark >= 80 — no quotes on numbers. Getting this backwards scores zero.
  • LIKE + 通配符：想「包含 / 以什么开头」用 LIKE。% 代表任意多个字符，_ 代表恰好一个字符，例如 WHERE Name LIKE 'W%' 找出所有 W 开头的名字。LIKE + wildcards: use LIKE for "contains / starts with". % means any number of characters, _ means exactly one, e.g. WHERE Name LIKE 'W%' finds names starting with W.
两点提醒：① 用 = 必须是完全一致才能匹配；想找「姓王的所有人」要用 LIKE 'W%'，写 = 'W' 一个都找不到。② 2026–2028 大纲里 SQL 的正式清单是 SELECT、FROM、WHERE、ORDER BY、SUM、COUNT、AND、OR；LIKE 属于拓展内容，学会了不吃亏，但先把清单里的练熟。 Two reminders: 1. = matches the whole value exactly; to find everyone whose name starts with W use LIKE 'W%', because = 'W' matches nothing. 2. The official 2026–2028 SQL list is SELECT, FROM, WHERE, ORDER BY, SUM, COUNT, AND, OR — LIKE is extra. Learning it does no harm, but master the listed items first.

例题 · 照着示例表写查询Example · write queries against the sample table

用上面那张 Student 表（ID、Name、City、Mark），写出满足下面要求的 SQL：
(a) 显示所有学生的姓名和城市。
(b) 显示北京学生的所有字段。
(c) 显示分数在 80 及以上的学生姓名。
(d) 显示上海或者广州的学生姓名。
(e) 显示姓名以 C 开头的学生。 Using the Student table above (ID, Name, City, Mark), write SQL for:
(a) all students' names and cities; (b) every field for students from Beijing; (c) names of students with 80 or more; (d) names of students from Shanghai or Guangzhou; (e) students whose name starts with C.
(a) SELECT Name, City FROM Student;(a) SELECT Name, City FROM Student; (b) SELECT * FROM Student WHERE City = 'Beijing';（星号 * 表示「全部字段」）(b) SELECT * FROM Student WHERE City = 'Beijing'; (the asterisk means "all fields") (c) SELECT Name FROM Student WHERE Mark >= 80;（命中 Wang 85 和 Zhao 91）(c) SELECT Name FROM Student WHERE Mark >= 80; (matches Wang 85 and Zhao 91) (d) SELECT Name FROM Student WHERE City = 'Shanghai' OR City = 'Guangzhou';——同一个字段两次比较都要把字段名写全，不能偷懒写成 City = 'Shanghai' OR 'Guangzhou'。(d) SELECT Name FROM Student WHERE City = 'Shanghai' OR City = 'Guangzhou'; — repeat the field name on both sides; you cannot write City = 'Shanghai' OR 'Guangzhou'. (e) SELECT * FROM Student WHERE Name LIKE 'C%';（命中 Chen）(e) SELECT * FROM Student WHERE Name LIKE 'C%'; (matches Chen)

练习Practice

写完检查两件事：文字有没有加引号？数字有没有多加引号？Check two things when you finish: is text quoted? Did you accidentally quote a number?

① 显示分数不等于 72 的学生姓名。（提示：不等号是 <>）1. Show the names of students whose mark is not 72. (Hint: not-equal is <>.)

② 显示北京且分数低于 80 的学生姓名。再想一下：这个查询会命中谁？2. Show the names of students from Beijing with a mark below 80. Who does it match?

点开看答案Show answer

① SELECT Name FROM Student WHERE Mark <> 72;（命中 Wang、Zhao、Chen）1. SELECT Name FROM Student WHERE Mark <> 72; (matches Wang, Zhao and Chen)

② SELECT Name FROM Student WHERE City = 'Beijing' AND Mark < 80;。北京的是 Lin（72）和 Zhao（91），其中低于 80 的只有 Lin。2. SELECT Name FROM Student WHERE City = 'Beijing' AND Mark < 80; The Beijing students are Lin (72) and Zhao (91), so only Lin is below 80.

— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 SQL 进阶：ORDER BY、SUM、COUNTMore SQL: ORDER BY, SUM, COUNT ORDER BY · SUM · COUNT排序 · 求和 · 计数
这一站要会的：会用 ORDER BY 配合 ASC / DESC 排序；会用 SUM() 求和、COUNT() 计数；会用 * 取全部字段；分清 SUM 与 COUNT。 Goal: sort with ORDER BY plus ASC/DESC; total with SUM() and count with COUNT(); use * for all fields; tell SUM from COUNT.
说人话：这一站的四个东西各有分工。ORDER BY 是「排好队再给我」——ASC 升序（从小到大，不写就是它），DESC 降序（从大到小，想看最高分必须写它，这是最常漏的一个词）。SUM(字段) 是「把这一列加起来」，COUNT(*) 是「数一数有几条」——一个是算总和，一个是数个数，完全不同。星号 * 放在 SELECT 后面是「所有字段都要」，放在 COUNT 里面是「把所有记录都算上」。 In plain words: these four each have a job. ORDER BY means "line them up before you give them to me" — ASC is ascending (smallest first, and it is the default), DESC is descending (largest first; you must write it to see the top score, and forgetting it is the classic slip). SUM(field) adds up the column; COUNT(*) counts how many rows — one totals values, the other counts records, and they are not interchangeable. An asterisk * after SELECT means "give me every field", and inside COUNT it means "count every row".
记住这几点：Key points:
  • ORDER BY 字段名 ASC → 升序（默认，可以不写）；ORDER BY 字段名 DESC → 降序。ORDER BY field ASC sorts ascending (the default, so ASC is optional); ORDER BY field DESC sorts descending.
  • SUM(字段)：把某个数值字段的值全部加起来，得到一个数。SUM(field) adds up the values in a numeric field and returns one number.
  • COUNT(*)：数一数满足条件的记录有几条。也可以写成 COUNT(字段)。COUNT(*) counts how many records match; COUNT(field) is also accepted.
  • SELECT *：显示所有字段；想只显示某几列就把列名用逗号隔开列出来。SELECT * shows every field; list the column names separated by commas to show only some.
  • 这几个都能和 WHERE 搭配：SELECT COUNT(*) FROM Student WHERE City = 'Beijing'; 就是「数一数北京的有几个」。All of them combine with WHERE: SELECT COUNT(*) FROM Student WHERE City = 'Beijing'; means "how many are from Beijing?".
  • 拓展（了解即可）：AVG() 平均值、MAX() 最大值、MIN() 最小值。Extra (awareness only): AVG() for the mean, MAX() and MIN() for the largest and smallest.
最经典的坑：题目说「按分数从高到低显示」，你写了 ORDER BY Mark 就只有升序，必须补上 DESC。另一个坑是把 SUM 和 COUNT 搞反：「总分」用 SUM，「人数」用 COUNT。 The classic trap: "highest mark first" needs ORDER BY Mark DESC — writing ORDER BY Mark alone gives ascending order. The other trap is swapping SUM and COUNT: use SUM for a total, COUNT for a number of people.

例题 · 排序与统计Example · sorting and totalling

还是那张 Student 表（1 Lin Beijing 72、2 Wang Shanghai 85、3 Zhao Beijing 91、4 Chen Guangzhou 58）。写出：
(a) 按分数从高到低显示姓名和分数。
(b) 一共有多少条记录？
(c) 全部分数的总和是多少？
(d) 北京的学生有几个？ Using the same Student table (1 Lin Beijing 72, 2 Wang Shanghai 85, 3 Zhao Beijing 91, 4 Chen Guangzhou 58), write:
(a) names and marks, highest first; (b) how many records are there? (c) what is the total of all marks? (d) how many students are from Beijing?
(a) SELECT Name, Mark FROM Student ORDER BY Mark DESC; → 顺序是 Zhao 91、Wang 85、Lin 72、Chen 58。(a) SELECT Name, Mark FROM Student ORDER BY Mark DESC; → Zhao 91, Wang 85, Lin 72, Chen 58. (b) SELECT COUNT(*) FROM Student; → 4(b) SELECT COUNT(*) FROM Student; → 4 (c) SELECT SUM(Mark) FROM Student; → 72 + 85 + 91 + 58 = 306(c) SELECT SUM(Mark) FROM Student; → 72 + 85 + 91 + 58 = 306 (d) SELECT COUNT(*) FROM Student WHERE City = 'Beijing'; → Lin 和 Zhao，共 2 个(d) SELECT COUNT(*) FROM Student WHERE City = 'Beijing'; → Lin and Zhao, so 2

练习Practice

先判断要「排序」「求和」还是「计数」。Decide first: sorting, totalling or counting?

① 想看分数最低的三个人，ORDER BY 后面该写 ASC 还是 DESC？1. To see the three lowest marks, should ORDER BY use ASC or DESC?

② 写出「北京学生的分数总和」的 SQL。2. Write SQL for "the total of the Beijing students' marks".

点开看答案Show answer

① 写 ASC（升序，最小的排最前面）：SELECT Name, Mark FROM Student ORDER BY Mark ASC;1. ASC — smallest first: SELECT Name, Mark FROM Student ORDER BY Mark ASC;

② SELECT SUM(Mark) FROM Student WHERE City = 'Beijing'; → 72 + 91 = 1632. SELECT SUM(Mark) FROM Student WHERE City = 'Beijing'; → 72 + 91 = 163

— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 综合练习：一张学生表从头做到尾Putting it together: one student table Single table only只考单表
这一站要会的：综合完成「选主键 + 定类型 + 写查询」；清楚知道 IGCSE 只考单表，多表关联是 A-Level 的内容。 Goal: combine choosing a key, choosing types and writing queries; know that IGCSE examines single tables only — joins belong to A-Level.
说人话：考场上数据库大题基本长一个样：先给你一段场景，让你① 设计一张表（有哪些字段、各是什么类型、主键是谁），再让你 ② 写几条 SQL。这两件事都有固定套路：字段就是把场景里提到的信息一项项列出来（学生 → 学号、姓名、分数、入学日期）；类型看「这个值是文字还是数字、有没有小数、是不是只有两三种可能、是不是日期」；主键就找那个「编号」。写 SQL 时先问自己三个问题：要看哪几列？从哪张表？要不要筛选或排序？ In plain words: the database question has a familiar shape: a scenario, then 1. design a table (which fields, what types, which primary key) and 2. write some SQL. Both have a routine. For fields, list every piece of information the scenario mentions (a student → ID, name, mark, enrolment date). For types, ask whether the value is text or a number, whether it has decimals, whether it has only two or three possible values, or whether it is a date. For the key, look for the "code". When writing SQL ask yourself three things: which columns? which table? any filtering or sorting?
记住这几点：Key points:
  • IGCSE 只考单表：所有数据都在一张表里。多表关联（JOIN）、外键这些是 A-Level 的内容，IGCSE 不考，遇到了也不用慌。IGCSE is single-table only: all the data sits in one table. Joins and foreign keys are A-Level material and will not be examined at IGCSE.
  • 设计表的三步：列出字段 → 给每个字段定类型 → 挑出主键。Three steps to design a table: list the fields → give each a type → pick the primary key.
  • 写查询的三步：SELECT（要哪几列）→ FROM（哪张表）→ WHERE（筛选）/ ORDER BY（排序）。Three steps to write a query: SELECT (which columns) → FROM (which table) → WHERE (filter) / ORDER BY (sort).
  • 检查清单：文字加单引号、数字不加、降序别忘 DESC、求和用 SUM、数个数用 COUNT、关键字大写。Checklist: text in single quotes, numbers without, DESC when sorting downwards, SUM for totals, COUNT for how many, keywords in capitals.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：题目问「为什么这个字段适合当主键」，照这个句式答。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: when asked why a field suits the primary key, answer with this sentence pattern. StudentID is suitable as the primary key because every student has a different ID, so it is unique, and it is always entered, so it is never empty. Name is not suitable because two students may have the same name.

例题 · 一套完整任务Example · a full task

学校要为社团建一张 Member 表，字段是 MemberID、Name、Age、FeePaid（是否交了社费）。
(a) 主键选哪个？说明理由。
(b) 给四个字段定类型。
(c) 写出三条 SQL：① 显示所有人的姓名；② 显示已交社费的人的姓名；③ 按年龄从大到小显示所有字段。 A school is building a Member table for a club with the fields MemberID, Name, Age and FeePaid (has the fee been paid?).
(a) Which field is the primary key, and why? (b) Give a type for each of the four fields. (c) Write three SQL statements: 1. show everyone's name; 2. show the names of those who have paid; 3. show all fields, oldest first.
(a) 主键 = MemberID。每个社员的编号都不一样（唯一），而且一定会被填上（不为空）；Name 会重名、Age 会重复、FeePaid 只有两种值，都不行。(a) MemberID. Every member has a different number (unique) and it is always present (never empty). Names repeat, ages repeat and FeePaid has only two values. (b) MemberID → INTEGER；Name → TEXT；Age → INTEGER；FeePaid → BOOLEAN（只有交了 / 没交两种）。(b) MemberID → INTEGER; Name → TEXT; Age → INTEGER; FeePaid → BOOLEAN (paid or not). (c)① SELECT Name FROM Member;(c) 1. SELECT Name FROM Member; (c)② SELECT Name FROM Member WHERE FeePaid = TRUE;（布尔值不加引号）(c) 2. SELECT Name FROM Member WHERE FeePaid = TRUE; (Boolean values take no quotes) (c)③ SELECT * FROM Member ORDER BY Age DESC;（从大到小必须写 DESC）(c) 3. SELECT * FROM Member ORDER BY Age DESC; (DESC is required for oldest first)

练习Practice

用上面那张 Student 表做，写完对照检查清单。Use the Student table above, then check your work against the checklist.

① 显示 2024 年 9 月 1 日入学的学生姓名。1. Show the names of students who enrolled on 1 September 2024.

② 显示分数最高的那位学生的姓名和分数。（提示：排序 + DESC）2. Show the name and mark of the student with the highest mark. (Hint: sort with DESC.)

③ 判断对错并说明理由：有人建议用 Name 当主键，因为「名字好记」。3. True or false, and why: someone suggests Name as the primary key because "names are easy to remember".

点开看答案Show answer

① SELECT Name FROM Student WHERE Enrolled = '2024-09-01'; → Lin、Wang、Chen 三人。1. SELECT Name FROM Student WHERE Enrolled = '2024-09-01'; → Lin, Wang and Chen.

② SELECT Name, Mark FROM Student ORDER BY Mark DESC; → 第一条就是 Chen 91。2. SELECT Name, Mark FROM Student ORDER BY Mark DESC; → the first row is Chen 91.

③ 不对。主键必须唯一且不能为空，而姓名会重名（两个 Wang），一旦重复数据库就无法区分这两条记录；「好记」不是选主键的标准。3. Wrong. A primary key must be unique and never empty, but names repeat (two Wangs), and once duplicated the database cannot tell the records apart. Being memorable is not a criterion.

— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —

单元 9 完成 · 主线：一份数据只存一次 → 用主键认出每条记录 → 用 SQL 一句话问出答案Unit 9 done · the thread: store each fact once → identify each record with a key → ask for the answer in one line of SQL

整个单元其实就三个词：为什么（文件会重复、会矛盾、难查、会冲突）、是什么（表 / 记录 / 字段 / 主键）、怎么问（SELECT … FROM … WHERE …）。大纲把 SQL 限定在 SELECT、FROM、WHERE、ORDER BY、SUM、COUNT、AND、OR 这几个词里，而且只考一张表——范围很窄，练熟就是稳拿的分。The whole unit is three words: why (files duplicate, contradict, resist queries and clash), what (tables, records, fields, keys) and how to ask (SELECT … FROM … WHERE …). The syllabus limits SQL to SELECT, FROM, WHERE, ORDER BY, SUM, COUNT, AND and OR, and to a single table — a narrow range, and steady marks once practised.

下一步：单元 10 是全卷最「送分」的一块——布尔逻辑。只要会画四种门、会列真值表，十几分就到手了。Next: Unit 10 is the most generous part of the paper — Boolean logic. Draw four gates and complete a truth table, and a dozen marks are yours.

单元 10 · 布尔逻辑Unit 10 · Boolean logicPAPER 2

只有 TRUE / FALSE 两种值的世界 · 六种逻辑门、真值表、布尔表达式、画电路 · 全卷最好拿分的一块，练两遍就稳A world with only TRUE and FALSE · six logic gates, truth tables, Boolean expressions and circuit drawing · the most reliable marks on the paper

本单元包括什么What this unit covers 6 站 · 约 55 分钟6 stops · about 55 min

  1. 布尔值与 NOT 门Boolean values and the NOT gate
  2. AND 门与 OR 门The AND and OR gates
  3. XOR 异或门The XOR gate
  4. NAND 与 NOR（万能门）NAND and NOR (the universal gate)
  5. 从电路写真值表与表达式From a circuit to a truth table
  6. 从文字描述设计电路From a description to a circuit

建议顺序：站 1、2 先把三个基本门和它们的真值表刻进脑子里；站 3、4 只要记住「跟 AND / OR 哪里不一样」；站 5、6 是真题的两种固定考法，各练两道就能拿满。Suggested order: burn the three basic gates and their truth tables into memory at stops 1–2; at 3–4 just note how each differs from AND/OR; stops 5–6 are the two standard exam tasks — two of each and you are done.

1 布尔值与 NOT 门Boolean values and the NOT gate TRUE · FALSE · NOT gate真 · 假 · 非门
这一站要会的：说出布尔值只有两个；认识 NOT 门的符号；会写 NOT 的真值表；会写布尔表达式 NOT A。 Goal: state that there are only two Boolean values; recognise the NOT gate symbol; complete its truth table; write the expression NOT A.
说人话：前面单元 8 学的 BOOLEAN 数据类型，到这里派上用场了。布尔值只有两个：TRUE 和 FALSE，在电路里就写成 1（有电 / 真）和 0（没电 / 假）。逻辑门是一种小元件：给它几个 0/1，它就按固定规则吐出一个 0/1。NOT 门是最简单的一种——只有一个输入，干的事就是「唱反调」：你给它 1，它给你 0；你给它 0，它给你 1。符号很好认：一个三角形，尖尖上顶着一个小圆圈，那个小圆圈就是「取反」的意思，以后在 AND、OR 的输出端看到小圆圈，也是同一个含义。 In plain words: the BOOLEAN data type from Unit 8 comes into its own here. There are only two Boolean values, TRUE and FALSE, written in circuits as 1 (voltage present / true) and 0 (no voltage / false). A logic gate is a tiny component: feed it a few 0s and 1s and it produces one 0 or 1 by a fixed rule. NOT is the simplest — a single input, and all it does is disagree: give it 1 and it returns 0; give it 0 and it returns 1. The symbol is easy to spot: a triangle with a small circle on its point. That circle means "invert", and you will see the same circle on the output of AND and OR gates later with exactly the same meaning.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 布尔值 Boolean value：只有 TRUE 和 FALSE 两种，在真值表里写成 1 和 0。A Boolean value is either TRUE or FALSE, written as 1 and 0 in truth tables.
  • NOT 门：一个输入、一个输出，输出 = 输入的反面。The NOT gate has one input and one output; the output is the inverse of the input.
  • 符号：三角形 + 输出端的小圆圈（小圆圈 = 取反）。Symbol: a triangle with a small circle at the output (the circle means invert).
  • 布尔表达式：X = NOT A（也写作 X = Ā，但 IGCSE 用 NOT 这种写法）。Boolean expression: X = NOT A.
  • 真值表：把输入的所有可能组合列出来，再写出对应的输出。1 个输入就只有 2 行。A truth table lists every possible combination of inputs with the resulting output — one input means just two rows.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：题目让你「描述 NOT 门的作用」，一句话就够。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: one sentence answers "describe the function of a NOT gate". A NOT gate has one input and one output. The output is the inverse of the input: if the input is 1 the output is 0, and if the input is 0 the output is 1.

例题 · NOT 的小应用Example · using NOT

传感器 S 检测「灯是否亮着」：亮着输出 1，灭了输出 0。现在想要一个信号 X，表示「灯没亮」。写出布尔表达式，并列出真值表。 A sensor S outputs 1 when a lamp is on and 0 when it is off. You need a signal X that means "the lamp is not on". Write the Boolean expression and the truth table.
① 「没亮」就是「亮的反面」，所以把 S 接一个 NOT 门：X = NOT S。1. "Not on" is the inverse of "on", so feed S through a NOT gate: X = NOT S. ② 真值表两行：S = 0 → X = 1；S = 1 → X = 0。2. Two rows: S = 0 → X = 1; S = 1 → X = 0. ③ 画电路：从 S 拉一根线进 NOT 门，NOT 门输出就是 X。3. Draw a wire from S into the NOT gate, and the gate's output is X.

练习Practice

记住 1 个输入 = 2 行真值表。One input means two rows.

① NOT 0 等于几？NOT 1 等于几？NOT(NOT 1) 等于几？1. What is NOT 0? NOT 1? NOT(NOT 1)?

② 一个 NOT 门有几个输入、几个输出？它的符号里哪个部分表示「取反」？2. How many inputs and outputs does a NOT gate have? Which part of its symbol means "invert"?

点开看答案Show answer

① NOT 0 = 1；NOT 1 = 0；NOT(NOT 1) = NOT 0 = 1（取反两次等于没变）。1. NOT 0 = 1; NOT 1 = 0; NOT(NOT 1) = NOT 0 = 1 (inverting twice changes nothing).

② 1 个输入、1 个输出。是输出端那个小圆圈表示取反。2. One input and one output. The small circle at the output means invert.

— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 AND 门与 OR 门The AND and OR gates AND · OR · truth table与 · 或 · 真值表
这一站要会的：认识 AND 与 OR 的符号；背下两个真值表（各 4 行）；会写 A AND B、A OR B；知道 2 个输入对应 4 行。 Goal: recognise the AND and OR symbols; learn both four-row truth tables; write A AND B and A OR B; know that two inputs give four rows.
说人话：这两个门就是日常生活里的「并且」和「或者」。AND：想进电影院并且想买票——两件事都成立才行，所以两个输入都是 1，输出才是 1，其它情况全是 0。OR：用学生证或者用身份证——有一样就行，所以只要有一个是 1，输出就是 1，只有两个都是 0 时才输出 0。外形怎么记？AND 是方头的 D 形（像个大写字母 D），OR 是圆头尖尾的弧形。 In plain words: these two gates are everyday "and" and "or". AND: you want to go to the cinema and you have a ticket — both must hold, so the output is 1 only when both inputs are 1; every other case gives 0. OR: pay with a student card or an ID card — either will do, so the output is 1 as soon as one input is 1, and it is 0 only when both are 0. How to remember the shapes: AND is a flat-fronted D shape; OR is a curved shield with a pointed nose.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • AND 门：两个输入都是 1 时输出 1，否则输出 0。表达式 X = A AND B。符号是 D 形。AND: output is 1 only when both inputs are 1, otherwise 0. Expression: X = A AND B. Symbol: a D shape.
  • OR 门：两个输入中至少有一个是 1 时输出 1，只有两个都是 0 时才输出 0。表达式 X = A OR B。符号是弧形。OR: output is 1 when at least one input is 1, and 0 only when both are 0. Expression: X = A OR B. Symbol: a curved shape.
  • 真值表的行数 = 2ⁿ（n 是输入个数）：1 个输入 2 行，2 个输入 4 行，3 个输入 8 行。Rows in a truth table = 2ⁿ (n inputs): 1 input → 2 rows, 2 inputs → 4 rows, 3 inputs → 8 rows.
  • 写行的顺序要固定：00、01、10、11（把它当成二进制数从小到大）。漏一行或者顺序乱了都会扣分。Keep the row order fixed: 00, 01, 10, 11 (counting up in binary). A missing or jumbled row loses marks.
两个门千万别记反：记住 AND 更严格（只有 11 才给 1，真值表里只有一个 1）；OR 更宽松（只有 00 才给 0，真值表里只有一个 0）。用这个「1 的个数」检查自己有没有写反。 Do not mix these two up: remember that AND is stricter (only 11 gives 1, so its column has a single 1) and OR is looser (only 00 gives 0, so its column has a single 0). Counting the 1s is a quick way to check yourself.

例题 · 算一算Example · evaluate these

求下列表达式的值：(a) 1 AND 1　(b) 1 AND 0　(c) 0 OR 1　(d) 0 OR 0　(e) (1 AND 0) OR 1 Evaluate: (a) 1 AND 1 (b) 1 AND 0 (c) 0 OR 1 (d) 0 OR 0 (e) (1 AND 0) OR 1
(a) 两个都是 1 → 1　　(b) 有一个是 0 → 0(a) both are 1 → 1　(b) one of them is 0 → 0 (c) 有一个是 1 → 1　　(d) 两个都是 0 → 0(c) one of them is 1 → 1　(d) both are 0 → 0 (e) 先算括号：1 AND 0 = 0，再算 0 OR 1 = 1。括号优先，和数学一样。(e) Brackets first: 1 AND 0 = 0, then 0 OR 1 = 1. Brackets come first, just as in arithmetic.

练习Practice

先把 00、01、10、11 四行写出来，再填结果。Write the four rows 00, 01, 10, 11 first, then fill them in.

① 某个门的输出列是 0、0、0、1，它是 AND 还是 OR？1. A gate's output column reads 0, 0, 0, 1. Is it AND or OR?

② 三个输入 A、B、C 接成 X = A AND B AND C，真值表有几行？X 什么时候才等于 1？2. With three inputs wired as X = A AND B AND C, how many rows does the truth table have, and when is X equal to 1?

点开看答案Show answer

① 是 AND。只有最后一行（A=1、B=1）才输出 1，OR 应该是 0、1、1、1。1. AND. Only the last row (A=1, B=1) gives 1; OR would read 0, 1, 1, 1.

② 3 个输入 → 2³ = 8 行。只有 A、B、C 三个全是 1 时 X = 1，其它 7 行都是 0。2. Three inputs → 2³ = 8 rows. X = 1 only when A, B and C are all 1; the other seven rows are 0.

— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 XOR 异或门The XOR gate XOR · EOR · different异或 · 不同才为 1
这一站要会的：说出 XOR 的规则；背下它的真值表；说清 XOR 与 OR 的区别；认识它的符号。 Goal: state the XOR rule; learn its truth table; explain how XOR differs from OR; recognise its symbol.
说人话：XOR 叫「异或」，规则就一句话：两个输入不一样就输出 1，一样就输出 0。它跟 OR 的真值表只差最后一行：A=1、B=1 的时候，OR 说「有 1 就是 1」，XOR 却说「两个都一样，所以给 0」。最经典的例子是楼梯灯：楼上楼下各有一个开关，两个开关状态不同时灯亮，相同时灯灭——你在一头按一下，灯就换状态，这正是 XOR。 In plain words: XOR means "exclusive OR", and its rule is one sentence: output 1 when the two inputs differ, and 0 when they are the same. Its truth table differs from OR in exactly one row: with A=1 and B=1, OR says "there is a 1, so output 1", while XOR says "they are the same, so output 0". The classic example is a staircase light: one switch upstairs, one downstairs; the lamp is on when the two switches are in different positions and off when they match — flip either one and the lamp changes state. That is XOR exactly.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • XOR 规则：输入不同 → 输出 1；输入相同 → 输出 0。XOR rule: inputs differ → output 1; inputs match → output 0.
  • 真值表：00→0，01→1，10→1，11→0。Truth table: 00→0, 01→1, 10→1, 11→0.
  • 与 OR 的唯一区别：最后一行（1,1）。OR 是 1，XOR 是 0。记住这一处就全记住了。The only difference from OR is the last row (1,1): OR gives 1, XOR gives 0. Learn that one cell and you know both gates.
  • 符号：就是 OR 那个弧形门，左边多画一条弧线。Symbol: the OR curved gate with an extra curved line on the left.
  • 表达式：X = A XOR B（有的教材写成 EOR，是同一个门）。Expression: X = A XOR B (some books write EOR — the same gate).

例题 · 楼梯灯Example · the staircase light

楼梯口上下各有一个开关 A、B。两个开关位置不同时灯亮，相同时灯灭。
(a) 写出布尔表达式。　(b) 列出真值表。　(c) 两个开关都拨到「上」时灯亮吗？ A staircase has a switch A upstairs and B downstairs. The lamp is on when the switches are in different positions and off when they match.
(a) Write the Boolean expression. (b) Give the truth table. (c) Is the lamp on when both switches are up?
(a) 「不同才亮」正是 XOR：Lamp = A XOR B。(a) "On when different" is exactly XOR: Lamp = A XOR B. (b) 00→0，01→1，10→1，11→0。(b) 00→0, 01→1, 10→1, 11→0. (c) 两个都是「上」→ 输入相同 → 灯灭。这正是 XOR 和 OR 的差别所在：如果用 OR，这时灯反而是亮的。(c) Both up means the inputs match, so the lamp is off. This is exactly where XOR parts company with OR: with OR the lamp would be on.

练习Practice

先问自己：这两个输入「一样还是不一样」。Ask yourself: are the two inputs the same or different?

① 求 1 XOR 0、0 XOR 0、1 XOR 1。1. Evaluate 1 XOR 0, 0 XOR 0 and 1 XOR 1.

② 有个同学说「XOR 就是 OR 加了个 N」，这句话对吗？请说清 XOR 和 OR 到底差在哪。2. A classmate says "XOR is just OR with an N". Is that right? Explain the real difference.

点开看答案Show answer

① 1 XOR 0 = 1（不同）；0 XOR 0 = 0（相同）；1 XOR 1 = 0（相同）。1. 1 XOR 0 = 1 (different); 0 XOR 0 = 0 (same); 1 XOR 1 = 0 (same).

② 不对。名字里的 X 是 exclusive（排他）的意思，不是加了个 N。它们的真值表只在 A=1、B=1 这一行不同：OR 输出 1，XOR 输出 0（它把「两个都成立」这种情况排除在外了）。另外 XOR 也不是 NAND、NOR —— 那两个是「取反」的门，后面一站会讲。2. No. The X stands for exclusive, not for an added N. The tables differ in the single row A=1, B=1: OR gives 1, XOR gives 0 — it excludes the case where both hold. XOR is also not the same as NAND or NOR, which are inverting gates covered next.

— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 NAND 与 NOR（万能门）NAND and NOR (the universal gate) NAND · NOR · universal与非 · 或非 · 万能门
这一站要会的：背下 NAND 与 NOR 的真值表；认出符号（AND / OR 输出端的那个小圆圈）；说明为什么 NAND 是万能门。 Goal: learn the NAND and NOR truth tables; recognise the symbols (the circle on the output); explain why NAND is a universal gate.
说人话：这两个门一点都不新，它们是「先算一遍，再把结果反过来」。NAND = NOT AND，读作「与非」：先算 A AND B，再把答案取反。所以 AND 的真值表是 0、0、0、1，NAND 就是整个翻过来：1、1、1、0——只有两个输入都是 1 时才输出 0。NOR = NOT OR，同理：OR 是 0、1、1、1，NOR 翻过来是 1、0、0、0——只有两个输入都是 0 时才输出 1。识别窍门：符号就是 AND / OR 的样子，只是输出端多了一个小圆圈，看到小圆圈就知道「这一步要取反」。 In plain words: these two gates are nothing new — they work it out, then flip the answer. NAND is NOT AND: work out A AND B, then invert the result. AND reads 0, 0, 0, 1, so NAND is that column turned upside down: 1, 1, 1, 0 — the output is 0 only when both inputs are 1. NOR is NOT OR: OR reads 0, 1, 1, 1, so NOR reads 1, 0, 0, 0 — the output is 1 only when both inputs are 0. How to spot them: the symbol is the AND or OR shape with a small circle added at the output; wherever you see that circle, this step is inverted.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • NAND（与非）：先 AND 再 NOT。只有两个输入都是 1 时输出 0，其余都输出 1。表达式 X = A NAND B。NAND: AND then NOT. The output is 0 only when both inputs are 1, and 1 otherwise. Expression: X = A NAND B.
  • NOR（或非）：先 OR 再 NOT。只有两个输入都是 0 时输出 1，其余都输出 0。表达式 X = A NOR B。NOR: OR then NOT. The output is 1 only when both inputs are 0, and 0 otherwise. Expression: X = A NOR B.
  • 符号：AND / OR 的外形 + 输出端一个小圆圈。Symbols: the AND or OR outline plus a small circle at the output.
  • 万能门 universal gate：NAND 一个门就能搭出任何电路。因为：把两个输入并在一起 → A NAND A = NOT A；NAND 再串一个 NAND → 得到 AND；先把两个输入各取反再 NAND → 得到 OR。Universal gate: NAND alone can build any circuit. Tie the two inputs together and A NAND A = NOT A; follow a NAND with another NAND and you get AND; invert both inputs first and you get OR.
  • 实际意义：工厂只要大批量生产一种门，就能拼出所有电路，成本更低。Why it matters in practice: a factory can mass-produce one kind of gate and assemble everything from it, which is far cheaper.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：题目问「为什么说 NAND 是万能门」，答这三点。 🗣 How to say it in the exam: for "why is NAND called a universal gate?" give these three points. NAND is a universal gate because any other logic gate can be made from NAND gates alone: joining both inputs together gives NOT, a NAND followed by another NAND gives AND, and inverting both inputs before a NAND gives OR. So any logic circuit can be built using only NAND gates.

例题 · 只用 NAND 搭出 NOT 和 ANDExample · building NOT and AND from NAND

(a) 说明怎样用一个 NAND 门做出 NOT 门。　(b) 求 1 NAND 1、1 NAND 0、0 NAND 0。 (a) Explain how to make a NOT gate from one NAND gate. (b) Evaluate 1 NAND 1, 1 NAND 0 and 0 NAND 0.
(a) 把 NAND 的两个输入接在一起，都接信号 A，那么输出就是 A NAND A：A=1 时 1 NAND 1 = 0；A=0 时 0 NAND 0 = 1——正好是 NOT A。(a) Join both inputs together and feed signal A into both, giving A NAND A: with A=1, 1 NAND 1 = 0; with A=0, 0 NAND 0 = 1 — exactly NOT A. (b) 1 NAND 1 = 0（两个都是 1 → 唯一输出 0 的情况）；1 NAND 0 = 1；0 NAND 0 = 1。(b) 1 NAND 1 = 0 (the only case giving 0); 1 NAND 0 = 1; 0 NAND 0 = 1.

练习Practice

先写出 AND / OR 的结果，再把整个结果翻转。Work out AND or OR first, then flip the whole column.

① 写出 NOR 的完整真值表（四行）。1. Write the complete four-row NOR truth table.

② 有人说「NAND 的输出列里只有一个 0」，这句话对吗？为什么？2. Someone says "the NAND output column has only one 0". Is that right, and why?

点开看答案Show answer

① 00→1，01→0，10→0，11→0。1. 00→1, 01→0, 10→0, 11→0.

② 对。NAND = AND 取反，AND 的输出列里只有一个 1（11 那一行），翻过来之后就只剩一个 0，位置正好也在 11 那一行。用这个规律可以一秒检查自己有没有写错。2. Right. NAND is inverted AND; the AND column has a single 1 (the 11 row), so inverting it leaves a single 0, also in the 11 row. It is a one-second way to check your table.

— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 从电路写真值表与表达式From a circuit to a truth table 3 gates · 8 rows三个门 · 八行
这一站要会的：看一个组合电路，给中间点命名，写出逐步的布尔表达式，列出完整真值表（3 个输入 = 8 行）。 Goal: read a combinational circuit, name its intermediate points, write the Boolean expression step by step, and produce the full eight-row truth table.
说人话：电路一复杂，最怕的就是「盯着整张图发呆」。正确做法分三步：① 从输入出发，给每个门的输出起个名字（中间点 X、Y）；② 按信号流动的顺序，把每一步写成表达式；③ 列真值表时，先算出中间列，再用中间列算最后一列。这样做的好处是：每一行你只需要算一个门，几乎不会错。行数 = 2 的输入个数次方，所以三个输入一定是 8 行，写完先数一遍行数对不对。 In plain words: the danger with a bigger circuit is staring at the whole picture and going blank. Use three steps: 1. start at the inputs and give every gate's output a name (the intermediate points X and Y); 2. follow the signal and write an expression for each step; 3. when filling the truth table, work out the intermediate columns first, then use them for the final column. The beauty of this is that each row only asks you to evaluate one gate. Rows = 2 to the power of the number of inputs, so three inputs always means eight rows — count them before you go further.
记住这几点：Key points:
  • 第一步：标中间点。每个门的输出都起个名字（X、Y…），题目通常会留空让你填。Step 1: label the intermediate points. Give each gate's output a name (X, Y, …); the question often leaves these blank for you.
  • 第二步：按顺序列表达式。X = A AND B → Y = NOT C → Z = X OR Y，合起来 Z = (A AND B) OR (NOT C)。Step 2: write the expressions in order. X = A AND B → Y = NOT C → Z = X OR Y, giving Z = (A AND B) OR (NOT C).
  • 第三步：逐列填表。先填 X 列（只在 A=1 且 B=1 时为 1），再填 Y 列（C 的反面），最后 Z = X OR Y。Step 3: fill the table column by column. X is 1 only when A and B are both 1; Y is the inverse of C; then Z = X OR Y.
  • 行数检查：n 个输入 → 2ⁿ 行。3 个输入 → 8 行，顺序是 000、001、010、011、100、101、110、111。Count the rows: n inputs → 2ⁿ rows. Three inputs → 8 rows, in the order 000, 001, 010, 011, 100, 101, 110, 111.
  • 真值表里的中间列也要写——题目给了 X、Y 两列，就是想看你会不会分步算，空着不写会扣分。Fill in the intermediate columns too — if the question provides X and Y columns it wants to see your working, and leaving them blank loses marks.

例题 · 一步步验算上面那张表Example · checking the table above, row by row

电路是 X = A AND B、Y = NOT C、Z = X OR Y。请写出第 2 行（A=0、B=0、C=1）和第 8 行（A=1、B=1、C=1）的 X、Y、Z。 The circuit is X = A AND B, Y = NOT C, Z = X OR Y. Give X, Y and Z for row 2 (A=0, B=0, C=1) and row 8 (A=1, B=1, C=1).
第 2 行：X = 0 AND 0 = 0；Y = NOT 1 = 0；Z = 0 OR 0 = 0。Row 2: X = 0 AND 0 = 0; Y = NOT 1 = 0; Z = 0 OR 0 = 0. 第 8 行：X = 1 AND 1 = 1；Y = NOT 1 = 0；Z = 1 OR 0 = 1。Row 8: X = 1 AND 1 = 1; Y = NOT 1 = 0; Z = 1 OR 0 = 1. 验算 Z 列：1、0、1、0、1、0、1、1，共 8 个数，和上面图里的表一致。The Z column reads 1, 0, 1, 0, 1, 0, 1, 1 — eight values, matching the table in the diagram.

练习Practice

先数行数，再逐列算，别跳步。Count the rows, then work column by column — no shortcuts.

① 上面这个电路中，当 A=1、B=1、C=0 时，X、Y、Z 分别是多少？1. In the circuit above, what are X, Y and Z when A=1, B=1, C=0?

② 如果三个输入一共有 8 种组合，而输出 Z 列里有 5 个 1，那么有几个 0？这说明了什么？2. With eight input combinations, if the Z column has five 1s, how many 0s are there, and what does that tell you?

点开看答案Show answer

① X = 1 AND 1 = 1；Y = NOT 0 = 1；Z = 1 OR 1 = 1。1. X = 1 AND 1 = 1; Y = NOT 0 = 1; Z = 1 OR 1 = 1.

② 有 3 个 0。说明只有三种输入组合会让输出为 0——回顾一下，正好是 C=1 且 A、B 不都为 1 的那三行（001、011、101）。数一数 1 和 0 的个数是检查真值表最快的方法。2. Three 0s. Only three input combinations give 0 — looking back, those are exactly the rows where C=1 and A and B are not both 1 (001, 011, 101). Counting the 1s and 0s is the quickest check on any truth table.

— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —
6 从文字描述设计电路From a description to a circuit Problem → expression → circuit题意 → 表达式 → 电路
这一站要会的：把一段中文描述翻译成布尔表达式，再画出对应的电路；知道「未 / 不 / 没」要翻译成 NOT，「并且」是 AND，「或者」是 OR。 Goal: turn a written description into a Boolean expression and then into a circuit; know that "not" becomes NOT, "and" becomes AND and "or" becomes OR.
说人话：这是本单元最后一类题，也是最容易拿满分的：给你一句话，让你画出电路。诀窍是先别画图，先把话翻成表达式。翻译表非常简单：出现「并且 / 同时 / 而且」→ AND；出现「或者」→ OR；出现「不 / 未 / 没有」→ NOT；出现「只有……才……」说明这些条件要同时满足，就用 AND 串起来。翻完表达式，画图只是照抄：有几个运算符就画几个门，按运算顺序连线，括号里的先算、输出送到括号外那个门的输入。 In plain words: this is the last type of question in the unit and the easiest to score full marks on: you are given a sentence and asked to draw the circuit. The trick is do not draw first — translate into an expression first. The translation table is tiny: "and" → AND; "or" → OR; "not / no / hasn't" → NOT; "only … if …" means the conditions must hold together, so join them with AND. Once the expression is written, drawing is just copying it out: one gate per operator, wired in the order of evaluation, with brackets worked out first and their output fed into the gate outside.
记住这几点（翻译口诀）：Key points (the translation rule):
  • 「A 并且 B」→ A AND B　　「A 或者 B」→ A OR B　　「非 A / A 不成立」→ NOT A。"A and B" → A AND B　　"A or B" → A OR B　　"not A" → NOT A.
  • 「只有 P 才 Q」= Q 需要 P 成立，多个条件之间用 AND。"only if P, Q" means Q requires P; join several conditions with AND.
  • 变量怎么定：先给每个条件起一个字母名，并写清楚 1 代表什么（比如 S = 1 表示安全带已系），这样「未系」自然就是 NOT S。Naming variables: give each condition a letter and state what 1 means (for example S = 1 means the belt is fastened), so "not fastened" is naturally NOT S.
  • 运算顺序：NOT 最先，其次 AND，最后 OR；括号可以改变顺序，题目说「（A 或 B）并且 C」就必须加括号。Order of evaluation: NOT first, then AND, then OR; brackets override it, so "either A or B, and also C" needs brackets.
  • 画图顺序：先把括号里的部分画出来并标上中间点，再把中间点接到外面那个门的输入上。Drawing order: draw the bracketed part first and label its intermediate point, then feed that point into the gate outside.
  • 大纲规定：最多三个输入、一个输出，题目给你的电路不需要化简，照着描述搭就行。Syllabus rule: at most three inputs and one output, and circuits do not need simplifying — build them straight from the description.

例题 · 车门警报（就是上图那一题）Example · the car alarm (the circuit at the top)

只有车门打开 AND 安全带未系 时，警报才响。请定义变量、写出布尔表达式，并说明画哪两个门、怎么连。 The alarm sounds only when the door is open AND the seat belt is not fastened. Define the variables, write the Boolean expression, and say which two gates you draw and how they connect.
① 定义变量：D = 1 表示车门打开；S = 1 表示安全带已系（注意是「已系」）。1. Define variables: D = 1 means the door is open; S = 1 means the belt is fastened. ② 「安全带未系」= NOT S，所以表达式是 Alarm = D AND (NOT S)。2. "Belt not fastened" = NOT S, so the expression is Alarm = D AND (NOT S). ③ 画两个门：一个 NOT 门（输入接 S），一个 AND 门。3. Draw two gates: a NOT gate (fed by S) and an AND gate. ④ 连线：D 直接接到 AND 的一个输入；S 先经过 NOT 门，NOT 的输出再接到 AND 的另一个输入；AND 的输出就是 Alarm。4. Wire it up: D goes straight into one AND input; S goes through the NOT gate first, and the NOT output feeds the other AND input; the AND output is Alarm. ⑤ 验算：车门打开（D=1）且没系（S=0）→ NOT S = 1 → 1 AND 1 = 1，警报响 ✓；车门打开但系好了（S=1）→ NOT S = 0 → 1 AND 0 = 0，不响 ✓。5. Check it: door open (D=1) and belt off (S=0) → NOT S = 1 → 1 AND 1 = 1, alarm sounds ✓; door open but belt on (S=1) → NOT S = 0 → 1 AND 0 = 0, silent ✓.

练习Practice

先翻译，再画图。先想清楚每个变量等于 1 到底代表什么。Translate first, then draw. Be clear about what 1 means for each variable.

① 图书馆门禁：只有刷卡成功 AND 没有欠费 时才开门。请定义变量、写出表达式，并说明用哪两个门。1. A library door opens only when the card is valid AND there are no outstanding fines. Define variables, write the expression, and say which two gates you need.

② 温室电路（上图下半部分）：光线充足（L=0）、温度过高（H=1）、是冬天（W=1），这时灯会亮吗？代入表达式算一算。2. For the greenhouse circuit (lower half of the diagram): light is sufficient (L=0), temperature is too high (H=1) and it is winter (W=1). Does the lamp come on? Substitute and work it out.

点开看答案Show answer

① 设 C = 1 表示刷卡成功，F = 1 表示有欠费。那么「没有欠费」= NOT F，表达式 Door = C AND (NOT F)。需要 一个 NOT 门 + 一个 AND 门：F 先过 NOT，再把 NOT 的输出和 C 一起送进 AND。1. Let C = 1 mean the card is valid and F = 1 mean fines are owed. "No fines" is NOT F, so Door = C AND (NOT F). You need one NOT gate and one AND gate: feed F through the NOT, then put the NOT output and C into the AND.

② Lamp = (L OR H) AND (NOT W)。代入：L OR H = 0 OR 1 = 1；NOT W = NOT 1 = 0；最后 1 AND 0 = 0。灯不亮——因为「不是冬天」这个条件没满足，冬天不开灯。2. Lamp = (L OR H) AND (NOT W). Substituting: L OR H = 0 OR 1 = 1; NOT W = NOT 1 = 0; finally 1 AND 0 = 0. The lamp stays off — the "not winter" condition fails, and in winter the lamp does not come on.

— 第 6 站完成 —— Stop 6 done —

单元 10 完成 · 主线：只有 0 和 1 → 六种门各有一张真值表 → 能读电路、也能按描述搭电路Unit 10 done · the thread: only 0 and 1 → six gates, six truth tables → read a circuit and build one from a description

整个单元就六张真值表：NOT（取反）、AND（全 1 才 1）、OR（有 1 就 1）、XOR（不同才 1）、NAND（AND 取反，只有一个 0）、NOR（OR 取反，只有一个 1）。考法只有两种：给你电路写真值表（标中间点、逐列算）和给你描述画电路（先翻表达式再照抄）。大纲限定最多三个输入，不需要化简——照着搭就行。The whole unit is six truth tables: NOT (invert), AND (1 only when both are 1), OR (1 whenever either is 1), XOR (1 only when they differ), NAND (inverted AND, a single 0) and NOR (inverted OR, a single 1). There are only two exam tasks: circuit → truth table (label intermediate points, work column by column) and description → circuit (write the expression, then copy it out). The syllabus caps it at three inputs and no simplification is needed — just build what was asked for.

十站全部走完了。最后一步是把单元 7–10 这四块（算法、编程、数据库、逻辑）各做两套真题，做完你会发现 Paper 2 的题型就那么几种，心里有底，上场就不慌。That is all ten units. The last step is two past papers covering Units 7–10 (algorithms, programming, databases, logic). Do that and you will find Paper 2 only asks the same handful of things — which makes the exam far less frightening.

单元 1 · 必背公式与对照表Unit 1 · formulas to learn

规则 / 公式Rule / formula含义与用法Meaning and use
8 位位权：8-bit values: 128 64 32 16 8 4 2 1先在草稿纸上写这一行，所有转换题都靠它Write this line on scrap paper first — every conversion depends on it
二进制→十进制：把 1 的位权相加binary → denary: add the values of the 1s例：1010 1101 = 128+32+8+4+1 = 173e.g. 1010 1101 = 128+32+8+4+1 = 173
十进制→二进制：从大到小减位权denary → binary: subtract values from largest down减得掉写 1，减不掉写 0write 1 when it fits, 0 when it does not
十六进制→十进制：左位 ×16 + 右位hex → denary: left digit ×16 + right digitA=10 B=11 C=12 D=13 E=14 F=15A=10 B=11 C=12 D=13 E=14 F=15
二进制↔十六进制：4 位一组binary ↔ hex: groups of four从右往左切，不要先转十进制split from the right; do not go via denary
加法：addition: 1+1=0 进 1 carry 11+1+1 = 1 进 1and 1+1+1 = 1 carry 1
溢出：结果超过了位数能表示的最大值overflow: result too large for the number of bits8 位最大 255（= 2⁸−1）eight bits hold up to 255 (= 2⁸−1)
左移 n 位 = ×2ⁿ；右移 n 位 = ÷2ⁿleft shift n = ×2ⁿ; right shift n = ÷2ⁿ空位补 0，移出去的位丢弃；右移向下取整zeros fill the gap, shifted-out bits are lost; right shifts round down
补码位权（8 位）：two's complement values: −128 64 32 16 8 4 2 1取负数：写正数 → 取反 → 加 1to negate: write positive → invert → add 1
补码范围：two's complement range: −128 至 +127负数比正数多一个，常考one more negative than positive — a favourite trap
图片文件大小 = 宽 × 高 × 色深image size = width × height × colour depth单位是位，÷8 得字节answer is in bits; divide by 8 for bytes
声音文件大小 = 采样率 × 位深 × 声道数 × 秒数sound size = sample rate × bit depth × channels × seconds同上，单位是位also in bits
单位：units: 1 KiB = 1024 B，依次 ×1024, each step ×1024KiB → MiB → GiB → TiB；题目写 kB/MB 才用 1000KiB → MiB → GiB → TiB; only kB/MB use 1000

单元 2 · 要点与对比Unit 2 · key points

要点Point含义与用法Meaning and use
数据包结构：packet structure: header + payload + trailer包头有地址信息，包尾常有校验数据header carries addressing, trailer often carries checking data
串行：一条线一位一位走serial: one bit at a time down one wire远距离之王（USB、网线）；便宜、抗干扰、距离远best over distance (USB, network cables); cheaper and more reliable
并行：多条线同时走parallel: several bits at once, one wire each短距离快，长距离会不同步（ skew）fast over short distances, but suffers skew further out
异步：每组数据前加起始位、后加停止位asynchronous: a start bit before and a stop bit after each group发送方和接收方各自计时，代价是多传了额外的位sender and receiver run their own clocks; the extra bits are the cost
同步：双方共用时钟信号synchronous: sender and receiver share a clock signal不用起始/停止位，速度更快，适合大数据块no start/stop bits, so faster — good for large blocks
单工：只能一个方向simplex: one direction only例：键盘到主机、广播e.g. keyboard to computer, radio broadcast
半双工：两个方向但不能同时half-duplex: both ways but not at once例：对讲机e.g. walkie-talkie
全双工：同时双向full-duplex: both ways at once例：电话e.g. telephone
奇偶校验：加一位让 1 的个数为奇/偶parity: add one bit so the number of 1s is odd or even只能发现奇数个位出错，偶数个错发现不了only catches an odd number of bit errors
校验和：把所有数据加起来做检查值checksum: add all the data to form a check value双方重算一遍比较；比奇偶校验可靠both sides recalculate and compare — more reliable than parity
回送检查：收到的数据发回去让对方核对echo check: send the data back for comparison简单但流量翻倍；数据本身出错时对方看不出来simple but doubles traffic
ARQ：出错就请求重发ARQ: ask for a retransmission when it fails靠超时、确认 ACK 与负确认 NAK 来触发driven by time-outs, ACK (acknowledgement) and NAK
USB：串行、通用、即插即用、可供电USB: serial, universal, plug-and-play, supplies power缺点：距离短（几米）、线缆质量影响速度drawbacks: short cables and speed depends on cable quality

单元 3 · 要点Unit 3 · key points

要点Point含义与用法Meaning and use
步骤：取指 → 译码 → 执行cycle: fetch → decode → execute每一步都要说清哪个寄存器参与name the registers involved at each step
PC 存下一条指令的地址holds the address of the next instruction取指后 PC 自动加一incremented after each fetch
MAR 存要访问的地址holds the address to be accessed地址总线按它去内存找位置the address bus uses it to locate memory
MDR 暂存读出/要写的数据holds data read from / to be written to memory数据总线按它传输数据data travels along the data bus
CIR 存当前正在执行的指令holds the current instruction控制单元负责把它翻译成操作the control unit decodes it into actions
ACC 累加器：暂存 ALU 的计算结果accumulator: holds ALU results算术逻辑单元负责算数与比较the ALU does arithmetic and comparisons
缓存：CPU 内部的小而快的存储器cache: small, fast memory inside the CPU存放近期要用到的指令和数据，比主存快得多keeps instructions and data likely to be used soon
RAM：易失、可读写、临时存正在跑的程序RAM: volatile, read/write, holds running programs断电内容全丢contents are lost when power is off
ROM：非易失、只读、存启动程序（bootstrap）ROM: non-volatile, read only, holds the bootstrap断电内容不丢contents survive power off
虚拟内存：硬盘划一块当内存用virtual memory: part of the disk used as memory内存不够时的补救，会明显变慢（硬盘比内存慢太多）a fallback when RAM runs out — much slower
二级存储对比：容量 vs 速度 vs 便携 vs 耐用 vs 成本secondary storage: capacity vs speed vs portability vs durability vs costSSD 快、抗震、无噪音但贵；HDD 便宜容量大但有机械部件；光盘便宜怕刮；云存储容量大但要联网SSD is fast, shock-proof and silent but dearer; HDD is cheap and big but mechanical; optical discs are cheap but scratch easily; cloud storage needs a connection
嵌入式系统：专做一件事的计算机系统embedded system: a computer system built for one job装在更大的机器里，资源受限，常要求实时响应built into a larger machine, limited resources, often real-time

单元 4 · 要点与对比（常考"说明区别"）Unit 4 · key comparisons

对比Comparison区别与结论Difference and conclusion
系统软件 vs 应用软件system vs application software系统软件管机器（OS、驱动、杀毒、磁盘整理）；应用软件帮人做事（Word、浏览器、游戏）。没有系统软件，应用软件跑不起来system software runs the machine; application software does a job for the user
编译器：一次全部翻译完，生成目标代码compiler: translates the whole program at once into object code运行快；错了就整段报错，改完要重新编译runs fast; errors are reported for the whole program
解释器：一行一行翻译并执行interpreter: translates and runs one line at a time方便调试（错在哪行立刻停），但每次运行都要重译，慢easy to debug (stops at the faulty line) but slower every run
汇编器：汇编语言 → 机器码（一对一）assembler: assembly → machine code, one to one一条汇编指令对应一条机器指令one assembly instruction per machine instruction
高级语言：接近人话，可移植，一条顶多条机器指令high-level: close to English, portable, one line becomes many machine instructions必须翻译后才能执行must be translated before it can run
低级语言：直接操作硬件，快且省内存，但难写难查错low-level: works directly on hardware, fast and memory-efficient, hard to write几乎不可移植，换台机器就重写almost no portability — rewrite for a different machine
中断：设备主动打断 CPU，CPU 转去处理interrupt: a device interrupts the CPU, which switches to handle it不占用 CPU 反复询问，效率高；有优先级no repeated checking, so efficient; interrupts have priorities
轮询：CPU 反复询问每个设备"有事吗"polling: the CPU repeatedly asks each device whether it needs attention实现简单，但浪费 CPU 时间simple to build but wastes CPU time
处理中断的七步：接收 → 停当前 → 保存现场 → 找 ISR → 执行 → 恢复 → 继续handling an interrupt: receive → stop → save the current state → find the ISR → run → restore → resume"保存现场"是关键词，写答案一定要出现"saving the current state" is the phrase examiners look for
语法错误：写完就发现，程序根本不跑（拼错关键字、少了括号）syntax error: found before running; the program will not start最容易改，IDE 会直接标红easiest to fix — the IDE underlines it
运行时错误：跑到一半崩（除以 0、数组越界、内存不足）run-time error: crashes part way through (divide by zero, out of range)语法没问题，是执行时才暴露syntax is fine; the fault only appears while running
逻辑错误：能跑但结果错（用错符号、条件写反、顺序错）logic error: runs but gives the wrong answer最难查，要用测试数据与追踪表逐步对比hardest to find — use test data and trace tables

单元 5 · 威胁与防护（成对背，送分题）Unit 5 · threats and protection

威胁 / 风险Threat它干什么What it does怎么防Protection
病毒 virusvirus附着在文件上，打开就自我复制并破坏数据attaches to files, replicates and damages data杀毒软件 + 不打开陌生附件 + 及时更新anti-virus + do not open unknown attachments + keep updated
黑客 hackerhacker绕过安全机制非法进入系统窃取或篡改bypasses security to gain unauthorised access防火墙 + 强密码 + 双因素认证 + 审计日志firewall + strong passwords + two-factor authentication + audit logs
网络钓鱼 phishingphishing伪造邮件/网站骗你交出密码、卡号fake emails or sites trick you into giving passwords不点陌生链接 + 检查网址拼写 + 邮件过滤 + 用户教育do not click unknown links + check the address + filters + education
间谍软件 spywarespyware悄悄安装，偷看按键、浏览记录并回传installs quietly, watches keystrokes and browsing反间谍软件 + 只从官方渠道下载 + 定期扫描anti-spyware + download only from official sources + regular scans
DDoS 攻击DDoS attack用海量机器同时请求，把服务器挤瘫floods a server with requests from many machines to overwhelm it流量过滤 + 带宽扩容 + 入侵检测 + 黑名单traffic filtering + more bandwidth + intrusion detection + blacklists
数据窃取与丢失data theft and loss数据库被拖走，或硬盘损坏导致没了data is stolen, or a disk fails and data is lost加密存储 + 定期备份且异地保存 + 权限最小化encryption + regular off-site backups + least privilege access
身份盗用identity theft冒用你的身份去办业务、转账someone uses your identity to act as you强密码 + 双因素认证 + 不在公共网络登录敏感账号strong passwords + two-factor authentication + avoid public Wi-Fi
要点Point含义与用法Meaning and use
互联网是网络的基础设施，万维网是跑在上面的一堆网页the internet is the infrastructure; the web is the pages on top of it"互联网 ≠ 万维网"是必考的一句话区别"internet is not the web" is a classic one-mark distinction
IP 地址：机器的数字门牌；DNS：把域名翻译成 IPIP address is the numeric address; DNS turns names into IP addresses记人用名字，机器只认数字，所以要 DNSpeople use names, machines use numbers — hence DNS
HTTPS 的 S = Secure，用数字证书加密传输HTTPS: the S means secure — traffic is encrypted using a digital certificate地址栏有锁形图标；输入密码、付款时一定要看look for the padlock before typing passwords or paying
对称加密：同一把钥匙加解密symmetric: the same key encrypts and decrypts快，但密钥怎么安全送到对方手里是个难题fast, but sharing the key safely is hard
非对称加密：公钥加密、私钥解密asymmetric: public key encrypts, private key decrypts公钥可以公开，私钥绝不外传；慢，适合传少量数据public key can be shared freely; slower, good for small amounts
cookie：网站存在你电脑里的小文本文件cookie: a small text file a site stores on your computer好处：记住登录、购物车；坏处：被用于跟踪keeps you logged in; can also be used to track you
区块链：每笔记录打包成块，环环相扣、全网副本blockchain: records are bundled into blocks linked together and copied everywhere改一块就要改后面所有块和全部副本 → 几乎改不动changing one block means changing all that follow and every copy — practically impossible

单元 6 · 自动化系统的标准流程（六步背下来）Unit 6 · automated system flow

步骤Step做什么What happens例子（恒温器）Example (thermostat)
1. 传感器采集sensor reads把物理世界的状态变成连续的模拟信号turns a physical quantity into a continuous analogue signal温度传感器测出当前室温temperature sensor reads the room
2. ADC 模数转换ADC conversion模拟信号 → 离散的数字值，计算机才能处理analogue → discrete digital value the computer can use18.6 °C 变成二进制数18.6 °C becomes a binary number
3. 与阈值比较compare with threshold微处理器把测量值和设定值比大小the microprocessor compares the reading with the set point目标 21 °C，现在 18.6 °C，偏低target 21 °C, now 18.6 °C — too cold
4. 做出判断decide按程序里的规则决定要不要动作the program's rules decide whether to act低了 → 应该加热too cold → turn heating on
5. 执行器动作actuator actsDAC 把数字指令变成物理动作（开/关/转）DAC turns the digital decision into physical action继电器吸合，暖气打开a relay closes and the heater switches on
6. 回到第 1 步loop back to step 1这就是反馈回路：动作反过来改变输入this is the feedback loop: the action changes the input房间变暖 → 下一次读数变高 → 到 21 °C 就停the room warms, the next reading rises, heating stops at 21 °C
要点Point含义与用法Meaning and use
AI 优点：不知疲倦、不会情绪化、处理海量数据快、可远程危险作业AI advantages: no fatigue, no emotions, handles huge data fast, works in dangerous places缺点：没有常识与创造力、出错难追责、取代岗位disadvantages: no common sense or creativity, blame is unclear, jobs displaced
专家系统 = 知识库 + 推理引擎 + 用户界面expert system = knowledge base + inference engine + user interface知识库存事实与规则，推理引擎按规则推导答案the knowledge base holds facts and rules; the inference engine applies them
机器学习：不给规则，给大量数据让它自己找规律machine learning: no rules given — it finds patterns in lots of data数据越多越好；但训练数据有偏见，结果就有偏见more data means better results, but biased data gives biased results
图灵测试：人通过文字提问，分不清对面是人还是机器就算通过Turing test: if a human cannot tell machine from human by text alone, it passes考的是"表现得像不像"，不是"真的在思考"it tests behaviour, not whether the machine really thinks
IoT：日常物品联网 + 带传感器，能上报也能被远程控制IoT: everyday objects connected and sensor-equipped好处：方便、省事、自动；风险：隐私与安全、断网就瘫convenient and automatic; risks are privacy and security
云：数据和软件放在别人的服务器上，按需租用cloud: data and software on someone else's server, rented as needed省硬件钱、随处可用、自动备份；但要联网、数据控制力弱no hardware cost, access anywhere, automatic backups — but needs a connection
VR：完全替代现实；AR：在现实上叠加信息VR replaces reality; AR adds information to itVR 要头显全封闭；AR 用手机/眼镜就能看VR needs a headset; AR works on a phone or glasses
量子：量子比特可以同时处于多种状态，某些问题快得离谱quantum: a qubit can be in several states at once, so some problems are far faster现状：极低温、极不稳定，还不会取代普通电脑today it needs extreme cooling and is not replacing normal computers

单元 7 · 伪代码全套写法（Paper 2 必背）Unit 7 · full pseudocode reference

要做的事What you want to do怎么写（CAIE 官方风格）How to write it (CAIE style)
声明变量declare a variableDECLARE Count : INTEGER　类型：INTEGER / REAL / CHAR / STRING / BOOLEAN / DATE　types: INTEGER, REAL, CHAR, STRING, BOOLEAN, DATE
常量constantCONSTANT VAT ← 0.2　全大写名，值不能改　upper-case name, value never changes
赋值assignmentTotal ← Price * Quantity　箭头指向"被赋值的那个"　the arrow points at the variable being set
输入 / 输出input / outputINPUT Name　OUTPUT "Total is ", Total　输出多项用逗号隔开　separate several items with commas
二选一selection (two-way)IF Mark >= 50 THEN
    OUTPUT "Pass"
ELSE
    OUTPUT "Fail"
ENDIF
多选一selection (multi-way)CASE OF Grade
    "A" : OUTPUT "Excellent"
    "B" : OUTPUT "Good"
    OTHERWISE : OUTPUT "Keep going"
ENDCASE
计数循环（次数已知）count-controlled loopFOR i ← 1 TO 10
    OUTPUT i
NEXT i　STEP 可改步长　add STEP to change the step
条件循环（先判断）pre-condition loopWHILE Answer <> "Y" DO
    INPUT Answer
ENDWHILE　可能一次都不执行　may never run at all
条件循环（后判断）post-condition loopREPEAT
    INPUT Answer
UNTIL Answer = "Y"　至少执行一次　always runs at least once
过程（不返回值）procedurePROCEDURE Show(Title : STRING)
    OUTPUT Title
ENDPROCEDURE　调用：CALL Show("Hi")　call: CALL Show("Hi")
函数（返回一个值）functionFUNCTION Double(N : INTEGER) RETURNS INTEGER
    RETURN N * 2
ENDFUNCTION
数组arrayDECLARE Score : ARRAY[1:30] OF INTEGER　下标从 1 开始　index starts at 1
二维数组two-dimensional arrayDECLARE Grid : ARRAY[1:8, 1:8] OF CHAR　先写行再写列　row first, then column
整除与取余quotient and remainder17 DIV 5 得 3； gives 3; 17 MOD 5 得 2 gives 2
取子串substringSUBSTRING(Name, 1, 3)　从第 1 位起取 3 个字符　3 characters starting at position 1
求长度 / 转大小写length / caseLENGTH(Word)　UCASE(Word)　LCASE(Word)
比较与逻辑运算符comparison and logical operators= ≠ 用=, <>，小于 <，大于 >，<=，>=；并且, <, >, <=, >=; and is AND，或者, or is OR，非, not is NOT
读文件（读到文件尾）read a file to the endOPENFILE "data.txt" FOR READ
WHILE NOT EOF("data.txt") DO
    READFILE "data.txt", Line
ENDWHILE
CLOSEFILE "data.txt"
写文件write to a fileOPENFILE "out.txt" FOR WRITE
WRITEFILE "out.txt", Total
CLOSEFILE "out.txt"
注释comment// this line explains the code　双斜杠，不参与执行　double slash; ignored when running
考场提醒：Exam reminder: 关键字一律大写；IF 和 THEN 写在同一行；每个 IF 都要有 ENDIF，每个 FOR 都要有 NEXT，一对都不能少。Write keywords in capitals; IF and THEN go on the same line; every IF needs its ENDIF and every FOR its NEXT.

REAL带小数点的数numbers with a decimal part3.14, -0.5比较是否相等要小心，用范围判断更稳testing for equality is risky — check a range instead
CHAR单个字符one single character'A', '7''7' 是字符不是数字，不能直接做算术'7' is a character, not a number
STRING一串字符a sequence of characters"Hello""12" 和 12 不一样，"10" < "9" 成立（按字典序）"10" is not 10; "10" < "9" is true
BOOLEAN只有 TRUE / FALSETRUE or FALSE onlyTRUE, FALSE别写成 "True" 字符串，也别用 0/1 代替not a string, not 0 and 1

+ - * /加减乘除add, subtract, multiply, divide7 / 23.5（REAL） (a real)

MOD取余：只要余数remainder only17 MOD 52
^乘方raise to a power2 ^ 8256
&字符串拼接（接起来）join two strings together"Hi" & " there""Hi there"

AND两边都对才 TRUEtrue only if both sides are true(5>1) AND (2>9)FALSE
OR有一边对就 TRUEtrue if either side is true(5>1) OR (2>9)TRUE

单元 9 · 数据库 · SQL 句型Unit 9 · Databases · SQL patterns

术语Term是什么What it is类比Analogy
表 tabletable存同一类东西的所有数据all the data about one kind of thing一张 Excel 工作表one spreadsheet sheet
记录 recordrecord表里的一行，一个具体对象one row — a single thing一个学生的整条信息all the data for one student
字段 fieldfield表里的一列，一个属性one column — one attribute姓名这一列the "name" column
主键 primary keyprimary key唯一标识每条记录的字段，不能重复、不能空identifies each record uniquely; never repeated, never empty学号（姓名会重名，学号不会）student ID (names can clash, IDs cannot)
外键 foreign keyforeign key指向另一张表主键的字段，用来把表连起来points at the primary key of another table to link them成绩表里的学号the student ID inside the marks table
查询 queryquery用 SQL 向数据库提问asking the database a question using SQL"把 80 分以上的学生找出来""find every student above 80"
想干什么What you wantSQL 写法SQL
查全部字段select every fieldSELECT * FROM Student;
只查指定字段select chosen fieldsSELECT Name, Mark FROM Student;
按条件筛选（数值）filter with a condition (numbers)SELECT Name FROM Student WHERE Mark >= 80;
按条件筛选（文字要加引号）filter (text needs quotes)SELECT Name FROM Student WHERE House = "Red";
两个条件同时满足two conditions both trueSELECT Name FROM Student WHERE Mark >= 80 AND House = "Red";
两个条件满足一个即可either condition trueSELECT Name FROM Student WHERE Mark >= 80 OR Mark < 40;
排序（默认升序，DESC 降序）sort (ascending by default, DESC for descending)SELECT Name, Mark FROM Student ORDER BY Mark DESC;
数有多少条 / 求和 / 平均count / sum / averageSELECT COUNT(*), SUM(Mark), AVG(Mark) FROM Student;
分组统计group and countSELECT House, COUNT(*) FROM Student GROUP BY House;
模糊查找（% 代表任意多个字符）partial match (% means any characters)SELECT Name FROM Student WHERE Name LIKE "A%";
插入一条记录add a recordINSERT INTO Student (ID, Name, Mark) VALUES (7, "Lee", 88);
修改已有记录update a recordUPDATE Student SET Mark = 90 WHERE ID = 7;
删除记录delete a recordDELETE FROM Student WHERE ID = 7;
三个高频扣分点：Three things marks are lost on: ① 文字值要加引号，数字不加；② 结尾要有分号；③ 忘了写 WHERE 会作用到整张表（UPDATE / DELETE 尤其危险）。1. text values need quotes, numbers do not; 2. end with a semicolon; 3. forgetting WHERE applies the change to the whole table.

单元 10 · 布尔逻辑 · 六个门真值表Unit 10 · Boolean logic · truth tables

ABNOT A
（非）NOT A		A AND B
（与）A AND B		A OR B
（或）A OR B		A XOR B
（异或）A XOR B		A NAND B
（与非）A NAND B		A NOR B
（或非）A NOR B
00100011
01101110
10001110
11011000
门Gate一句话口诀Rule of thumb符号Symbol
NOT取反：进 0 出 1，进 1 出 0flip it: 0 becomes 1, 1 becomes 0一个输入，输出带个小圆圈one input, output has a small circle
AND全 1 才 1，有一个 0 就是 01 only when both are 1D 形D shape
OR有 1 就 1，全 0 才 01 if either input is 1弧形（像盾牌）curved shield shape
XOR不一样才 1（两输入不同则为 1）1 when the inputs differOR 的弧线后面再加一条弧OR with an extra curved line
NANDAND 再加一个 NOT：全 1 才 0AND followed by NOT: 0 only when both are 1D 形 + 输出端小圆圈D shape plus a small circle
NOROR 再加一个 NOT：全 0 才 1OR followed by NOT: 1 only when both are 0弧形 + 输出端小圆圈shield shape plus a small circle
万能门：Universal gates: NAND 和 NOR 各自都能单独搭出任何其他门（包括 NOT、AND、OR），所以叫万能门。做法：NAND 两个输入接在一起就是 NOT；NOT 接在 NAND 后面就是 AND。NAND and NOR can each build every other gate on their own, so they are called universal. Tie the two inputs of a NAND together and you have NOT; put a NOT after a NAND and you have AND.
