速查表使用方法 · 考前 20 分钟只看加粗行How to use this sheet · in the last 20 minutes, read only the bold rows

别试图把这一页全部背下来，那是自找压力。真正要闭着眼写得出来的只有五样：① 8 位位权那一行；② 两个文件大小公式；③ 伪代码的 IF / FOR / WHILE / REPEAT 四套框架；④ SQL 的 SELECT … FROM … WHERE …；⑤ 六个门的真值表。这五样占了卷子的大半壁江山。Do not try to memorise the whole page — that only creates pressure. Only five things must be writable with your eyes shut: the eight place values, the two file size formulas, the four pseudocode loop and selection frames, SELECT … FROM … WHERE …, and the six truth tables. Those five carry most of the marks.

剩下的术语当"眼熟题"处理：看到英文能反应出大概意思就够了。遇到不会的题，先回单元 tab 看一遍讲解，再自己合上屏幕写一遍——写一遍比看三遍有用。Treat the rest as recognition practice: recognising the English term is enough. When a question defeats you, go back to the unit tab, read it, then close the screen and write it out — writing once beats reading three times.

外部学习资源 · 含可动手的练习（全部免费）External resources · including hands-on practice (all free)

计算机科学是做出来的，光看讲义学不会 · 这一页按「动手玩 → 官方大纲与真题 → 系统讲解 → 编程实战 → 项目进阶」分组，第一组最重要Computer science is something you do — notes alone will not do. Grouped as hands-on → syllabus & past papers → explanations → coding practice → projects; the first group matters most

怎么搭配着用：第一组是能动手的——拖一拖逻辑门、玩一玩二进制游戏，比看懂定义有用得多。平时：跟本预习站学一个单元 → 去第一组找一个同主题的工具玩十分钟 → 学完两个单元去「二、官方大纲与真题」刷一组真题。先玩明白，再记术语。Paper 2（算法与编程）那一半，请务必真的打开「四、编程实战」里的在线编辑器写几行——看得懂和写得出是两回事。 How to combine them: group 1 is hands-on — dragging logic gates about or playing a binary game teaches far more than reading a definition. Routine: study one unit here → spend ten minutes on a matching tool from group 1 → after two units, do a set of real questions from group 2. Play first, terms second. For the Paper 2 half (algorithms and programming), do open an editor from group 4 and write a few lines — understanding code and writing code are two different skills.
访问提示：下面大多是境外网站。剑桥官网、Code.org、Khan Academy、MathsIsFun、W3Schools 一般能直接打开；Scratch、BBC Bitesize 偶尔转圈，换浏览器或用手机热点再试。YouTube 在国内需要特殊网络，打不开就算了，不是必需的。先跟学校确认考试代号是不是 0478（Cambridge IGCSE Computer Science）——本页链接全部按这个大纲挑的。 Access note: most of these sites are hosted overseas. Cambridge International, Code.org, Khan Academy, MathsIsFun and W3Schools usually open directly; Scratch and BBC Bitesize sometimes hang — try another browser or a phone hotspot. YouTube needs a proxy in mainland China; skip it if it will not load, it is not essential. Check with school that your syllabus code is 0478 (Cambridge IGCSE Computer Science) — every link here was chosen for that syllabus.

一、动手玩（这一组最重要 🎮）1. Hands-on tools (most important)

打开就能玩，不用注册，也不用先学完——建议学第一单元时就来玩一遍Open and play — no sign-up, no prerequisites. Try them while you are still on Unit 1

Cisco Binary Game （二进制游戏 · 单元 1）(binary game · Unit 1) ↗
首选。思科做的免费小游戏：上面出现一个十进制数，你点下面的灯泡把它"点亮成"二进制，越快分越高。玩十分钟，8 位位权那一行不用背就记住了。后面还会升级到多位、十六进制。配合本页单元 1 第 2、3 站。Start here. A free game from Cisco: a denary number appears, you click the lights to build its binary form, and speed scores points. Ten minutes of this and you know the eight place values without rote learning. It progresses to more bits and to hexadecimal. Use with stops 2 and 3 of Unit 1.
learningcontent.cisco.com/games/binary
CircuitVerse （在线逻辑电路实验室 · 单元 10）(online logic circuit lab · Unit 10) ↗
学逻辑门的神器。浏览器里直接拖 NOT / AND / OR / XOR / NAND / NOR 出来连线，接上开关和小灯，一点就能看输出亮不亮；还能自动生成真值表，跟你自己算的对一对。配合本页单元 10——卷子上"画一个电路"的题，你在这里先搭一遍就不慌了。The tool for logic gates. Drag NOT, AND, OR, XOR, NAND and NOR into a browser canvas, wire them to switches and lamps, click and watch the output light up. It also generates the truth table for you, so you can check your own working. Use with Unit 10 — build the circuit here first and exam questions asking you to draw one stop being scary.
circuitverse.org
Logic.ly （逻辑门仿真 · 免安装版）(logic gate simulator, demo) ↗
另一个搭逻辑门的网站，界面更接近课本上画的那种门形状，开关一拨线路上的高低电平会变色，很直观。免费用的是网页演示版，功能够做课本上的所有题。Another gate simulator whose symbols look like the ones in textbooks; flipping a switch colours the wires high or low, which is very visual. The free web demo does everything the syllabus asks.
logic.ly/demo
Scratch （积木式编程 · 单元 7、8）(block-based programming · Units 7, 8) ↗
麻省理工做的免费图形化编程：不用记语法，把积木拖在一起就是程序。它里面的"重复执行""如果…那么""变量""列表"就是本页单元 7 讲的顺序、选择和循环，只是换成了中文积木。英语暂时跟不上时，先用 Scratch 把编程的思路想明白，再回去看伪代码会轻松很多。Free visual programming from MIT: no syntax to memorise, you just snap blocks together. Its repeat, if…then, variables and lists are exactly the sequence, selection and iteration of Unit 7, in block form. If the English is still a barrier, get the thinking straight in Scratch first — the pseudocode will make far more sense afterwards.
scratch.mit.edu
Code.org （免费编程课程 · 零基础入口）(free coding courses · absolute beginner) ↗
全球最大的免费编程入门站，课程按年龄和基础分好了。推荐从 Computer Science Fundamentals（积木式）或 Hour of Code（一小时入门）开始。里面的"循环""条件""函数"动画小课，讲得比任何文字都清楚。The largest free learn-to-code site in the world, with courses sorted by age and experience. Start with Computer Science Fundamentals (block-based) or Hour of Code (one-hour taster). Its short animated lessons on loops, conditionals and functions explain things better than any paragraph.
code.org/learn · hourofcode.com
Hour of Code （一小时编程体验）(Hour of Code) ↗
一堆一小时就能做完的小项目：跟冰雪奇缘画雪花、跟星球大战做个机器人、用代码块控制"愤怒的小鸟"。只想花一小时确认自己喜不喜欢编程，从这里开始最合适。A collection of one-hour projects: draw snowflakes with Frozen, build a droid with Star Wars, guide Angry Birds with code blocks. If you just want one hour to find out whether you like programming, start here.
hourofcode.com
CS Unplugged （不插电的计算机科学 · 单元 1、2、10）(CS Unplugged · Units 1, 2, 10) ↗
不用电脑学计算机科学：用卡牌讲二进制、用绳子打结讲排序、用传纸条讲校验和、用举手投票讲逻辑门。每个活动都是可以几个人一起做的游戏，配有讲解和教学视频。理解不了某个概念时来这里翻一翻，往往一下就通了。Computer science without a computer: cards for binary, knots in rope for sorting, passing notes for checksums, a show of hands for logic gates. Each activity is a game for two or more people, with notes and videos. Whenever a concept will not click, look it up here.
classic.csunplugged.org

二、官方大纲与真题（考剑桥 0478 必看）2. Syllabus and past papers (essential for 0478)

所有复习的终点都是真题。学完两三个单元就该来这里看一眼真实的题目长什么样Every revision route ends at past papers. After two or three units, come here and see what a real question looks like

Cambridge IGCSE Computer Science 0478 官方主页official page ↗
剑桥考试局官方页面。这里能下载到最新大纲（syllabus）——就是考试范围的"宪法"，卷子不会超出它。页面上还有官方的样卷、评分标准（mark scheme）、考生答卷样例。建议打印一份大纲，学完一个单元就在上面打个勾。The official Cambridge International page. Download the current syllabus — the constitution of the exam; nothing outside it will be asked. You also get official specimen papers, mark schemes and exemplar candidate answers. Print the syllabus and tick each topic off as you finish a unit.
cambridgeinternational.org · …computer-science-0478
Cambridge 0478 真题与评分标准（Past papers）past papers & mark schemes ↗
历年真题卷（Paper 1 与 Paper 2）和对应的评分标准。评分标准比答案更重要——它告诉你阅卷人想看到哪几个词。用法建议：先限时做一套，再照着评分标准给自己批改，把"我写了但没得分"的地方抄到本子上，这就是你的提分清单。Past Paper 1 and Paper 2 files with their mark schemes. The mark scheme matters more than the answer — it shows which words the examiner is looking for. Do a paper against the clock, mark it yourself against the scheme, and copy out every point you wrote but got no credit for. That list is your revision plan.
cambridgeinternational.org · …0478/past-papers
PapaCambridge — 0478 真题、笔记与分类练习papers, notes and topic questions ↗
第三方整理的 0478 资源站：历年真题下载，还有按单元分类的题目集（topic-wise）。刚学完单元 1 想只练二进制转换，来这里找对应的分类题就行，不用整套整套地做。A third-party 0478 hub: past paper downloads plus questions sorted by topic. Just finished Unit 1 and only want binary conversions? Pull that topic here instead of doing whole papers.
papacambridge.com · computer-science-0478

三、系统讲解与练习（换个老师再听一遍）3. Full explanations and practice

同一个知识点听三种讲法很正常，哪个让你懂了就用哪个Hearing one idea explained three ways is normal — keep whichever version clicks

Isaac Computer Science （英国官方背景 · 免费）(free, UK government-backed) ↗
讲解质量最高的一家。英国教育部支持、树莓派基金会运营的免费平台，按主题组织：数据表示、算法、编程、网络、逻辑、数据库……每个主题都有讲解 + 例题 + 在线自测，做完立刻判分并给出解析。注册免费。本页任何一个单元没看懂，先来这里搜同一个词。The best explanations of the lot. A free platform run by the Raspberry Pi Foundation with UK government backing, organised by topic: data representation, algorithms, programming, networks, logic, databases. Each topic has notes + worked examples + online self-marking questions with instant feedback. Free to sign up. If any unit here does not land, search the same keyword here.
isaaccomputerscience.org
Teach-ICT （图解式讲解 · 图多、话少）(diagram-heavy notes) ↗
英国老牌计算机教学站，几乎每个概念都配一张图，句子很短。对英语还在适应期的同学特别友好：先看图，再看图下面那两句英文。涵盖 CPU、存储、网络、二进制、数据库等全部主题。A long-running UK teaching site where almost every concept comes with a diagram and short sentences. Very friendly if English is still catching up: read the picture, then the two lines under it. Covers the CPU, storage, networks, binary, databases and more.
teach-ict.com
BBC Bitesize （GCSE 计算机科学 · 短讲解 + 小测）(GCSE computer science · bitesize notes + quizzes) ↗
英国 BBC 的免费学习站，每节都很短，看完立刻有个小测验。注意它按英国本土 GCSE 大纲编排，与剑桥 0478 高度重叠但不完全一样（它多一些计算思维，少一些伪代码细节），当作"换个讲法"用，不要当成唯一依据。The BBC's free study site: very short sections, each followed by a mini quiz. Note it follows the UK GCSE specification, which overlaps heavily with Cambridge 0478 but is not identical (more computational thinking, less pseudocode detail). Use it as a second explanation, not as the only source.
bbc.co.uk/bitesize · computer science
Maths is Fun （二进制数系统 · 讲得最浅的一篇）(binary number system · the gentlest version) ↗
如果本页单元 1 还嫌难，先看这一篇——它用"灯泡亮灭"讲二进制，还带一个可以点的转换器。整站都是这种风格：图多、口气轻松、不假设你懂。顺手看看它的十六进制页。If Unit 1 here still feels hard, read this first — it explains binary with lamps switching on and off and includes a clickable converter. The whole site is like that: pictures, a light tone, no assumptions. Its hexadecimal page is worth a look too.
mathsisfun.com/binary-number-system
Khan Academy （可汗学院 · 计算机科学）(Khan Academy · computing) ↗
免费视频 + 练习，讲得慢、讲得细，还有字幕可以调。适合"看书看不进去"的时候换成视频听一遍。里面的 Algorithms（算法）和 Internet 101 两块和本页单元 7、单元 5 对得上。Free videos and exercises, slow and thorough, with adjustable subtitles. Good for days when reading will not stick. Its Algorithms and Internet 101 sections line up with Units 7 and 5 here.
khanacademy.org/computing/computer-science

四、Paper 2 编程与 SQL 实战（一定要真的写）4. Paper 2 coding and SQL practice (actually type it out)

看得懂伪代码 ≠ 写得出伪代码。这一组是让你亲手敲的地方Understanding pseudocode is not the same as writing it. This group is where you actually type

Online Python （免安装在线编辑器）(online Python editor, no install) ↗
打开网页就能写 Python 并立刻运行，不用装任何软件。Python 不是考试要求的伪代码，但思路完全一样：变量、IF、FOR、WHILE、数组、函数。建议这样练：把本页单元 7 的伪代码例子，在这里用 Python 写一遍跑通——能跑出正确结果，说明你真的懂了那个循环。Write Python in the browser and run it immediately, no installation. Python is not the exam pseudocode, but the thinking is identical: variables, IF, FOR, WHILE, arrays, functions. Try this: rewrite a pseudocode example from Unit 7 in Python and run it — if it gives the right answer, you understand that loop.
online-python.com
Trinket （在线写代码 + 分享）(write and share code online) ↗
同样是浏览器里写 Python（也支持 HTML），好处是可以把作品存下来、分享链接。适合做小项目：写个猜数字游戏、写个成绩统计表，做完发给同学看——有观众的时候学得最快。Another browser Python editor (HTML too) that lets you save and share your work. Good for mini projects: a number-guessing game, a marks calculator. Sharing it with a classmate is the fastest way to learn.
trinket.io
SQLBolt （交互式 SQL 教程 · 单元 9）(interactive SQL tutorial · Unit 9) ↗
学 SQL 最好的免费教程。左边讲一句，右边就让你真的写一句并执行，错了会给提示。从最简单的 SELECT 一路到 WHERE、ORDER BY、GROUP BY，正好覆盖单元 9 的全部考点。一小时能刷完，比看书有用十倍。The best free SQL tutorial. It explains on the left and asks you to write and run a query on the right, with hints when you get it wrong. It goes from plain SELECT through WHERE, ORDER BY and GROUP BY — exactly the Unit 9 content. An hour gets you through it, and that beats ten hours of reading.
sqlbolt.com
W3Schools SQL （SQL 语法速查 + 在线试）(SQL reference + try-it editor) ↗
当字典用：忘了某个句型怎么写就来查，每个语法点都有例子，还有 "Try it Yourself" 可以直接改了跑。它的 SQL Tutorial 章节循序渐进，SQL Quick Reference 一页纸适合考前看。Use it as a dictionary: look up a construct when you forget how to write it; every entry has examples and a "Try it Yourself" box. The SQL Tutorial builds up steadily, and the one-page SQL Quick Reference is perfect before the exam.
w3schools.com/sql
RapidTables （进制转换器 · 用来检查不是用来抄）(number converter — for checking, not copying) ↗
二进制、十进制、十六进制互相转换，还有 ASCII 字符表。正确用法：先自己算，再用它对答案；直接抄等于没练。考场上没有它，但练的时候它是个好老师——算错了立刻能发现错在哪一步。Converts between binary, denary and hexadecimal, and has an ASCII table. Use it the right way: work it out yourself first, then check. Copying from it teaches nothing. It is not available in the exam, but while practising it is a good teacher — you find out immediately which step went wrong.
rapidtables.com/convert/number

五、做项目与进阶（有余力再看）5. Projects and going further

课本之外，让人真正爱上这门课的东西Beyond the textbook — the things that make people actually love the subject

Raspberry Pi Foundation （免费项目教程）(free project pathways) ↗
树莓派基金会的免费项目库，每一步都有图和完整代码：用 Python 画图、做游戏、做爬虫、处理数据、做动画。做完一个项目，你就明白为什么"分解问题"和"写函数"不是考点而是真本事。不一定要买树莓派，纯软件项目在普通电脑上就能做。The Raspberry Pi Foundation's free project library, illustrated step by step with complete code: drawing with Python, making games, scraping data, handling data, animating. Finish one project and you will see that decomposition and functions are real skills, not just syllabus points. You do not need to buy a Pi — the software-only projects run on any computer.
projects.raspberrypi.org/en/pathways/computing
Craig'n'Dave （YouTube 频道 · 英国计算机老师）(YouTube channel · UK computing teachers) ↗
两位英国计算机老师做的频道，讲概念很清楚，还有专门面向 Cambridge IGCSE / A-Level 计算机的播放列表。⚠️ 国内访问需要特殊网络，打不开就跳过，前面几组已经够用了。Two UK computing teachers who explain concepts very clearly, with playlists aimed at Cambridge IGCSE and A-level computer science. ⚠️ Needs a proxy in mainland China — if it will not load, skip it; the groups above are enough.
youtube.com/@craigndave
★ 没有电脑也能练的 12 件事（纸笔就能做）12 things to practise with just paper and a pencil Paper practice纸笔练习

本预习站里的练习其实都不需要电脑。拿一张纸、一支笔，把答案写下来——写一遍比看三遍记得牢。下面按单元列出了最适合纸笔练的项目。None of the exercises on this site actually need a computer. Take paper and a pencil and write the answers out — writing once beats reading three times. Listed here by unit.

  1. 默写位权表：每天在一张纸上写一遍 128 64 32 16 8 4 2 1，写到不用想。（单元 1）Write out the place values: once a day, write 128 64 32 16 8 4 2 1 on a scrap of paper until it is automatic. (Unit 1)
  2. 生日转二进制：把家人的生日（日、月）转成 8 位二进制和两位十六进制，看谁算得快。还可以互相出题。（单元 1）Birthdays in binary: convert family birthdays (day, month) into 8-bit binary and two-digit hex — race each other, then set each other questions. (Unit 1)
  3. 算手机里的照片：找一张手机照片看它的分辨率（如 4032×3024），假设色深 24 位，算算它未压缩有多大，再对比实际文件大小——差多少倍？那就是压缩的功劳。（单元 1）How big is a photo? Check a photo's resolution (say 4032×3024) and, assuming 24-bit colour, work out its uncompressed size; compare with the actual file. The ratio is what compression achieved. (Unit 1)
  4. 画数据包：在纸上画出 header + payload + trailer 三段，标出每一段大概装什么。能画出来说是真懂了。（单元 2）Draw a packet: sketch header, payload and trailer and label roughly what each holds. If you can draw it, you know it. (Unit 2)
  5. 算奇偶校验位：随便写 7 位二进制，加上一位让 1 的个数变成偶数；再让同桌把某一位改掉，看你能否发现。（单元 2）Work out a parity bit: write any 7 bits, add one bit so the number of 1s is even, then get someone to flip a bit and see whether you spot it. (Unit 2)
  6. 演一遍取指译码执行：拿三张纸分别写 PC、MAR、MDR，按单元 3 的步骤一步一步挪动它们，嘴里念出每一步。（单元 3）Act out fetch–decode–execute: label three slips of paper PC, MAR and MDR and move them through the steps of Unit 3, saying each step aloud. (Unit 3)
  7. 给家里的设备分类：把家里所有带芯片的东西列出来（洗衣机、遥控器、电梯…），标出哪些是嵌入式系统，并写出它的输入（传感器）和输出（执行器）。（单元 3、6）Classify devices at home: list everything with a chip inside (washing machine, remote, lift), mark which are embedded systems, and name their inputs (sensors) and outputs (actuators). (Units 3, 6)
  8. 把一天的流程写成伪代码：比如"早上起床"——IF 闹钟响了 THEN 起床 ELSE 继续睡 ENDIF。越无聊越好，写出来你就懂了 IF 和 THEN 为什么要同一行。（单元 7）Write your morning as pseudocode: IF alarm RANG THEN get up ELSE keep sleeping ENDIF. The sillier the example, the better — you will see why IF and THEN belong on one line. (Unit 7)
  9. 手算追踪表：给一段 5 行的循环伪代码，自己画一张表，一列一个变量，一行一次循环，把每次的值填进去。这是 Paper 2 必考题，练 5 道就稳了。（单元 7）Trace by hand: take a five-line loop, draw a trace table with one column per variable and one row per pass, and fill it in. This always comes up in Paper 2 — five goes and it is yours. (Unit 7)
  10. 给同学设计一张表：设计一张 Student(ID, Name, House, Mark) 表，写出主键，再自己想三个问题并写出对应的 SQL：谁是主键？找出 80 分以上的？按分数从高到低排？给每个 House 数人数？（单元 9）Design a table for your class: lay out Student(ID, Name, House, Mark), pick the primary key, then write the SQL for three questions of your own: who is above 80? sort by mark descending? count per house? (Unit 9)
  11. 画六张真值表：拿一张纸画六遍 NOT / AND / OR / XOR / NAND / NOR 的真值表，不看本站自己写；写错的那个就是你真正没掌握的门。（单元 10）Draw the six truth tables: without looking, write out all six — NOT, AND, OR, XOR, NAND, NOR. The one you get wrong is the gate you have not really learnt. (Unit 10)
  12. 卷子对照法：做一套真题后，把自己写的答案和标准答案各抄一遍，用红笔圈出标准答案里有、你却没有的词。这些词就是这门课的行话，攒够 20 个你就入门了。Copy and compare: after a past paper, copy out both your answer and the mark scheme's, then circle in red every word the scheme has that you did not. Those are the subject's key terms — collect twenty and you are in.

最后一句：这门课最怕的是"看懂了"就翻页。合上书，自己写一遍——写不出来就回去再看那一站，这比多刷十道题有用。One last thing: the trap in this subject is turning the page as soon as you "get it". Close the page and write it yourself — if you cannot, go back to that stop. That beats ten more practice questions.

计算机科学不是背出来的，是玩出来的、写出来的 💡Computer science is not memorised — it is played with and written 💡

今天就挑一件：去玩十分钟 Cisco 二进制游戏，或者打开 CircuitVerse 搭一个 AND 门看它亮不亮。动手那十分钟，比多看三页笔记有用。Pick one thing today: ten minutes on the Cisco binary game, or build an AND gate in CircuitVerse and watch it light up. Ten minutes of hands-on beats three more pages of notes.

先懂原理，再记术语，最后才是刷题Understand the idea, learn the term, then practise

这门课一半是背术语（Paper 1），一半是动手写（Paper 2）。配图和「说人话」负责帮你懂；🗣 考场英文怎么说 负责帮你拿 Paper 1 的分；练习题负责帮你拿 Paper 2 的分。Half of this subject is learning the terminology (Paper 1) and half is writing things yourself (Paper 2). The diagrams and "in plain words" boxes make you understand; the 🗣 How to say it in the exam boxes win Paper 1 marks; the practice questions win Paper 2 marks.
